import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGETS = [
  0x001a7900,
  0x001a7980,
  0x001a7a00,
];

function u32le(p) {
  return (
    b[p] |
    (b[p + 1] << 8) |
    (b[p + 2] << 16) |
    (b[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function leb32(v) {
  const out = [];
  do {
    let x = v & 0x7f;
    v >>>= 7;
    if (v) x |= 0x80;
    out.push(x);
  } while (v);
  return out;
}

console.log("=".repeat(60));
console.log("SELECTOR MAP POINTER REFERENCES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(60));

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const bytes = leb32(target);
  const hits = [];

  for (let i = 0; i <= b.length - bytes.length; i++) {
    let ok = true;
    for (let j = 0; j < bytes.length; j++) {
      if (b[i + j] !== bytes[j]) {
        ok = false;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (ok) hits.push(i);
  }

  console.log(`\nTARGET RUNTIME : 0x${target.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
  console.log(`ULEB           : ${bytes.map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")}`);
  console.log(`RAW HITS       : ${hits.length}`);

  for (const p of hits) {
    const prev = p > 0 ? b[p - 1] : -1;

    console.log(
      `  FILE 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} PREV=0x${prev
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(2, "0")}`
    );

    const start = Math.max(0, p - 8);
    const end = Math.min(b.length, p + bytes.length + 12);

    console.log(
      "   " +
        Array.from(b.slice(start, end))
          .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
    );
  }
}

console.log("\n" + "=".repeat(60));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(60));

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = new Set([5220, 5232, 5235]);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND SELECTORS FOR FUNC 1968");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < b.length - 2; i++) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = i;

  for (let n = 0; n < 5 && p < b.length; n++) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 128)) {
      if (targets.has(v >>> 0)) {
        const prev = i > 0 ? b[i - 1] : 0;

        if (prev === 0x41) {
          console.log(
            "CONST HIT @ 0x" +
            i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
            "VALUE", v
          );

          const s = Math.max(0, i - 16);
          const e = Math.min(b.length, p + 24);

          console.log(
            [...b.slice(s, e)]
              .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
              .join(" ")
          );

          hits++;
        }
      }
      break;
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("CONSTANT HITS:", hits);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function encodeULEB(v: number): number[] {
  const out: number[] = [];
  do {
    let b = v & 0x7f;
    v = Math.floor(v / 128);
    if (v !== 0) b |= 0x80;
    out.push(b);
  } while (v !== 0);
  return out;
}

const target = Buffer.from(encodeULEB(TARGET));

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND SELECTOR 0x19534C - EXACT ULEB128");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`ULEB   : ${target.toString("hex")}`);
console.log(`BYTES  : ${target.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i <= wasm.length - target.length; i++) {
  let ok = true;

  for (let j = 0; j < target.length; j++) {
    if (wasm[i + j] !== target[j]) {
      ok = false;
      break;
    }
  }

  if (!ok) continue;

  hits++;

  const start = Math.max(0, i - 32);
  const end = Math.min(wasm.length, i + target.length + 48);

  console.log("");
  console.log(`HIT #${hits}`);
  console.log(`OFFSET : 0x${i.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
  console.log(`RANGE  : 0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${end.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`RAW    : ${wasm.subarray(start, end).toString("hex")}`);
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL ULEB HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findState255880WritersV30.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 0x00255880;

function readULEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(b: Buffer, p: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = b[p++];

        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function readSections(b: Buffer) {
    const sections: any[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < b.length) {
        const id = b[p++];

        const s = readULEB(b, p);
        p = s.next;

        sections.push({
            id,
            payload: p,
            size: s.value,
            end: p + s.value,
        });

        p += s.value;
    }

    return sections;
}

function getSection(sections: any[], id: number) {
    const s = sections.find(x => x.id === id);

    if (!s) {
        throw new Error(`section ${id} not found`);
    }

    return s;
}

function importedFunctions(
    b: Buffer,
    s: any
) {
    let p = s.payload;

    const n = readULEB(b, p);
    p = n.next;

    let count = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const ml = readULEB(b, p);
        p = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = readULEB(b, p);
        p = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = b[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const x = readULEB(b, p);
            p = x.next;
            count++;
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `unexpected import kind ${kind}`
            );
        }
    }

    return count;
}

function getBodies(
    b: Buffer,
    s: any
) {
    let p = s.payload;

    const n = readULEB(b, p);
    p = n.next;

    const result: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

        const size = readULEB(b, p);
        p = size.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

        const locals = readULEB(b, p);
        p = locals.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < locals.value; j++) {
            const count = readULEB(b, p);
            p = count.next;
            p++;
        }

        result.push({
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            codeStart: p,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return result;
}

function hex8(x: number) {
    return (
        "0x" +
        (x >>> 0)
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(8, "0")
    );
}

function decodeForSearch(
    b: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number
) {
    const ins: any[] = [];

    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {

        const abs = p;
        const op = b[p++];

        try {

            if (
                op === 0x20 ||
                op === 0x21 ||
                op === 0x22 ||
                op === 0x23 ||
                op === 0x24
            ) {
                const x = readULEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

            } else if (op === 0x41) {

                const x = readSLEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

                ins.push({
                    abs,
                    op,
                    imm: x.value >>> 0,
                });

                continue;

            } else if (op === 0x42) {

                const x = readSLEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

            } else if (
                op >= 0x28 &&
                op <= 0x3e
            ) {

                const a = readULEB(b, p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = readULEB(b, p);
                p = o.next;

                ins.push({
                    abs,
                    op,
                    offset: o.value >>> 0,
                });

                continue;

            } else if (op === 0x10) {

                const x = readULEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

            } else if (
                op === 0x0c ||
                op === 0x0d
            ) {

                const x = readULEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

            } else if (
                op === 0x02 ||
                op === 0x03 ||
                op === 0x04
            ) {

                // block / loop / if
                p++;

            } else if (op === 0x05) {

                // else

            } else if (op === 0x0e) {

                // br_table
                const n = readULEB(b, p);
                p = n.next;

                for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                    const x = readULEB(b, p);
                    p = x.next;
                }

            }

            ins.push({
                abs,
                op,
            });

        } catch {
            break;
        }
    }

    return ins;
}

function printWindow(
    b: Buffer,
    center: number,
    radius = 35
) {
    const start =
        Math.max(0, center - radius);

    const end =
        Math.min(b.length, center + radius);

    console.log(
        `\n--- WINDOW ${hex8(center)} ---`
    );

    let p = start;

    while (p < end) {

        const pos = p;
        const op = b[p++];

        let text =
            `byte=${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

        try {

            if (op === 0x41) {

                const x = readSLEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

                text =
                    `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex8(x.value)})`;

            } else if (op === 0x20) {

                const x = readULEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

                text = `local.get ${x.value}`;

            } else if (op === 0x21) {

                const x = readULEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

                text = `local.set ${x.value}`;

            } else if (op === 0x22) {

                const x = readULEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

                text = `local.tee ${x.value}`;

            } else if (
                op >= 0x28 &&
                op <= 0x3e
            ) {

                const a = readULEB(b, p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = readULEB(b, p);
                p = o.next;

                text =
                    `memory-op 0x${op
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, "0")}` +
                    ` align=${a.value}` +
                    ` offset=${hex8(o.value)}`;

            } else if (op === 0x10) {

                const x = readULEB(b, p);
                p = x.next;

                text = `call ${x.value}`;

            } else if (op === 0x6a) {

                text = "i32.add";

            } else if (op === 0x6b) {

                text = "i32.sub";

            } else if (op === 0x71) {

                text = "i32.and";

            } else if (op === 0x72) {

                text = "i32.or";

            } else if (op === 0x73) {

                text = "i32.xor";

            } else if (op === 0x74) {

                text = "i32.shl";

            } else if (op === 0x75) {

                text = "i32.shr_s";

            } else if (op === 0x76) {

                text = "i32.shr_u";

            } else if (op === 0x68) {

                text = "i32.ctz";

            } else if (op === 0x36) {

                text = "i32.store";

            } else if (op === 0x28) {

                text = "i32.load";

            }

        } catch {
            text = "decode-error";
        }

        const marker =
            pos === center
                ? " >>> "
                : "     ";

        console.log(
            `${marker}${hex8(pos)}  ${text}`
        );
    }
}

async function main() {

    console.log(
        "Using file: findState255880WritersV30"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        " SEARCH ALL WRITERS OF 0x255880 v30"
    );

    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    const wasm =
        fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(
        `WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
    );

    const sections =
        readSections(wasm);

    const imports =
        importedFunctions(
            wasm,
            getSection(sections, 2)
        );

    const bodies =
        getBodies(
            wasm,
            getSection(sections, 10)
        );

    console.log(
        `Imported functions : ${imports}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Defined functions  : ${bodies.length}`
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        "DIRECT i32.const 0x255880 → i32.store"
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    let writerCount = 0;

    for (let bodyIndex = 0;
         bodyIndex < bodies.length;
         bodyIndex++) {

        const body =
            bodies[bodyIndex];

        const ins =
            decodeForSearch(
                wasm,
                body.codeStart,
                body.bodyEnd
            );

        for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

            const x = ins[i];

            if (
                x.op !== 0x41 ||
                x.imm !== TARGET
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            console.log(
                `\nFUNCTION ${
                    imports + bodyIndex
                }`

            );

            console.log(
                `body index : ${bodyIndex}`
            );

            console.log(
                `CONST     : ${hex8(x.abs)}`
            );

            const nearby =
                ins.slice(
                    i,
                    Math.min(
                        ins.length,
                        i + 10
                    )
                );

            const hasStore =
                nearby.some(
                    y => y.op === 0x36
                );

            if (hasStore) {

                console.log(
                    "STATUS    : POSSIBLE DIRECT WRITER"
                );

                writerCount++;

            } else {

                console.log(
                    "STATUS    : reader / address use"
                );
            }

            printWindow(
                wasm,
                x.abs,
                45
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        `POSSIBLE DIRECT WRITERS : ${writerCount}`
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "NOTE:"
    );

    console.log(
        "A writer is accepted only as a candidate."
    );

    console.log(
        "Final confirmation requires stack/data-flow analysis"
    );

    console.log(
        "of the instructions between i32.const 0x255880"
    );

    console.log(
        "and i32.store."
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(
        "READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );
}

main().catch(err => {

    console.error("");
    console.error("ERROR");
    console.error(err);

    process.exit(1);
});

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findStatePointerWriters.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x0019534c;
const BASE   = 0x00195348;
const IMPORTS = 83;

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value: v >>> 0, end: pos };

    s += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (pos < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        v -= 2 ** s;

      return { value: v | 0, end: pos };
    }
  }

  throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + (x >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function findBodies() {

  let p = 8;
  const bodies = [];

  while (p < b.length) {

    const id = b[p++];

    const r = uleb(p);
    p = r.end;

    const end = p + r.value;

    if (id === 10) {

      const c = uleb(p);
      let q = c.end;

      for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {

        const z = uleb(q);
        q = z.end;

        bodies.push({
          start: q,
          end: q + z.value
        });

        q += z.value;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = findBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATE POINTER WRITER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET :", hex(TARGET));
console.log("BASE   :", hex(BASE));
console.log("============================================================");

let totalStores = 0;
let candidates = 0;

for (let fi = 0; fi < bodies.length; fi++) {

  const func = fi + IMPORTS;
  const body = bodies[fi];

  let p = body.start;

  const groups = uleb(p);
  p = groups.end;

  let localCount = groups.value;

  for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.end;

    p++;
    localCount += n.value;
  }

  const locals = new Map();

  let stack = [];

  function push(x) {
    stack.push(x);
  }

  function pop() {
    return stack.length
      ? stack.pop()
      : { type: "UNKNOWN" };
  }

  function addrKnown(x) {

    if (!x)
      return false;

    if (x.type === "CONST")
      return (
        x.value === TARGET ||
        x.value === BASE
      );

    if (x.type === "ADD") {

      if (
        x.base &&
        x.offset !== undefined &&
        x.base.type === "CONST"
      ) {

        const v =
          (x.base.value + x.offset) >>> 0;

        return (
          v === TARGET ||
          v === BASE
        );
      }
    }

    if (x.type === "LOCAL")
      return false;

    return false;
  }

  let localState = new Map();

  function describe(x) {

    if (!x)
      return "UNKNOWN";

    if (x.type === "CONST")
      return `CONST ${hex(x.value)}`;

    if (x.type === "LOCAL")
      return `L${x.id}`;

    if (x.type === "ADD")
      return `ADD(${describe(x.base)}, ${x.offset})`;

    if (x.type === "LOAD")
      return `LOAD(${describe(x.addr)})`;

    return x.type || "UNKNOWN";
  }

  let found = [];

  while (p < body.end) {

    const at = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // LOCAL.GET
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x20) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      const id = r.value;

      const v =
        localState.get(id) ||
        { type: "LOCAL", id };

      push(v);
      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // LOCAL.SET
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x21) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      const id = r.value;

      const v = pop();

      localState.set(id, v);

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // LOCAL.TEE
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x22) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      const id = r.value;

      const v = stack.length
        ? stack[stack.length - 1]
        : { type: "UNKNOWN" };

      localState.set(id, v);

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // I32.CONST
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x41) {

      const r = sleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      push({
        type: "CONST",
        value: r.value >>> 0
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // I32.ADD
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x6a) {

      const rhs = pop();
      const lhs = pop();

      if (
        lhs?.type === "CONST" &&
        rhs?.type === "CONST"
      ) {

        push({
          type: "CONST",
          value: (lhs.value + rhs.value) >>> 0
        });

      } else if (
        lhs?.type === "CONST" &&
        rhs?.type === "UNKNOWN"
      ) {

        push({
          type: "ADD",
          base: lhs,
          offset: undefined
        });

      } else {

        push({
          type: "ADD",
          base: lhs,
          offset:
            rhs?.type === "CONST"
              ? rhs.value
              : undefined
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // I32.LOAD
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x28) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.end;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.end;

      const addr = pop();

      push({
        type: "LOAD",
        addr,
        offset: o.value
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // I32.STORE
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x36) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.end;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.end;

      const value = pop();
      const addr  = pop();

      totalStores++;

      let hit = false;
      let resolved = "";

      // Direct constant address
      if (addr?.type === "CONST") {

        const absolute =
          (addr.value + o.value) >>> 0;

        if (
          absolute === TARGET ||
          absolute === BASE
        ) {

          hit = true;
          resolved =
            `ABSOLUTE ${hex(absolute)}`;
        }
      }

      // ADD-derived address
      if (!hit && addr?.type === "ADD") {

        if (
          addr.base?.type === "CONST" &&
          addr.offset !== undefined
        ) {

          const absolute =
            (
              addr.base.value +
              addr.offset +
              o.value
            ) >>> 0;

          if (
            absolute === TARGET ||
            absolute === BASE
          ) {

            hit = true;
            resolved =
              `ARITHMETIC ${hex(absolute)}`;
          }
        }
      }

      if (hit) {

        candidates++;

        found.push({
          func,
          at,
          addr: describe(addr),
          offset: o.value,
          value: describe(value),
          resolved
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // I32.STORE8
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x3a) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.end;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.end;

      pop();
      const addr = pop();

      if (addr?.type === "CONST") {

        const absolute =
          (addr.value + o.value) >>> 0;

        if (
          absolute === TARGET ||
          absolute === BASE
        ) {

          candidates++;

          found.push({
            func,
            at,
            addr: describe(addr),
            offset: o.value,
            value: "STORE8",
            resolved:
              `ABSOLUTE ${hex(absolute)}`
          });
        }
      }

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CALL
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x10) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      // Unknown call result.
      // Clear stack because argument provenance
      // cannot safely be propagated through arbitrary calls.
      stack = [];

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // CALL_INDIRECT
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x11) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      p++;

      stack = [];

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // DROP
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (op === 0x1a) {

      pop();
      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Memory opcodes with memarg
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2a ||
      op === 0x2b ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x2e ||
      op === 0x2f ||
      op === 0x30 ||
      op === 0x31 ||
      op === 0x32 ||
      op === 0x33 ||
      op === 0x34 ||
      op === 0x35
    ) {

      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.end;

      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.end;

      pop();

      push({
        type: "LOAD",
        addr: { type: "UNKNOWN" },
        offset: o.value
      });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Generic arithmetic / comparison:
    // consume operands, produce unknown.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (
      op === 0x45 ||
      op === 0x46 ||
      op === 0x47 ||
      op === 0x48 ||
      op === 0x49 ||
      op === 0x4a ||
      op === 0x4b ||
      op === 0x4c ||
      op === 0x4d ||
      op === 0x4e ||
      op === 0x4f ||
      op === 0x50
    ) {

      pop();
      pop();
      push({ type: "UNKNOWN" });

      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x6b ||
      op === 0x6c ||
      op === 0x6d ||
      op === 0x6e ||
      op === 0x6f ||
      op === 0x70 ||
      op === 0x71 ||
      op === 0x72 ||
      op === 0x73 ||
      op === 0x74 ||
      op === 0x75 ||
      op === 0x76 ||
      op === 0x77 ||
      op === 0x78 ||
      op === 0x79 ||
      op === 0x7a
    ) {

      pop();
      pop();
      push({ type: "UNKNOWN" });

      continue;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Control flow
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04
    ) {

      // block / loop / if blocktype
      if (p < body.end) {

        const bt = b[p++];

        if (bt === 0x40)
          continue;

        if (
          bt === 0x7f ||
          bt === 0x7e ||
          bt === 0x7d ||
          bt === 0x7c
        )
          continue;
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05 || op === 0x0b)
      continue;

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

      const r = uleb(p);
      p = r.end;

      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f)
      continue;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Unknown opcode:
    // keep parser synchronized only when possible.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

  }

  if (found.length) {

    console.log("");
    console.log("############################################################");
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log("############################################################");

    for (const x of found) {

      console.log(
        `STORE @ ${hex(x.at)}` +
        `  ${x.resolved}`
      );

      console.log(
        `  ADDRESS : ${x.addr}` +
        ` + offset ${x.offset}`
      );

      console.log(
        `  VALUE   : ${x.value}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TOTAL I32.STORE SCANNED :", totalStores);
console.log("POINTER/ADDRESS CANDIDATES:", candidates);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findStonSchemaV22.ts

import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

const ROOT = path.resolve(
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\node_modules'
);

const SEARCHES = [
    '0x37c096df',
    '37c096df',
    '935368415',

    '0x50c6a654',
    '50c6a654',
    '1355195988',

    '0x178d4519',
    '178d4519',
    '395134233',

    '0x06ecd527',
    '06ecd527',
    '115137831',
];

const MAX_FILE_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

const SKIP_DIRS = new Set([
    '.cache',
    '.git',
    'dist',
    'coverage',
]);

const TEXT_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
    '.ts',
    '.tsx',
    '.js',
    '.mjs',
    '.cjs',
    '.json',
    '.d.ts',
    '.map',
]);

let filesScanned = 0;
let matches = 0;

function shouldSkipDirectory(
    name: string
): boolean {
    return (
        SKIP_DIRS.has(name) ||
        name.startsWith('.')
    );
}

function scanFile(
    filePath: string
) {
    try {
        const stat =
            fs.statSync(filePath);

        if (
            !stat.isFile() ||
            stat.size > MAX_FILE_SIZE
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const ext =
            path.extname(filePath);

        if (
            !TEXT_EXTENSIONS.has(ext)
        ) {
            return;
        }

        const text =
            fs.readFileSync(
                filePath,
                'utf8'
            );

        filesScanned++;

        const lower =
            text.toLowerCase();

        for (
            const search of SEARCHES
        ) {
            const needle =
                search.toLowerCase();

            let position =
                lower.indexOf(needle);

            while (
                position !== -1
            ) {
                matches++;

                const start =
                    Math.max(
                        0,
                        position - 300
                    );

                const end =
                    Math.min(
                        text.length,
                        position + 500
                    );

                const context =
                    text.slice(
                        start,
                        end
                    );

                console.log('');
                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );

                console.log(
                    `MATCH: ${search}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `FILE : ${filePath}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `POS  : ${position}`
                );

                console.log(
                    '------------------------------------------------------------'
                );

                console.log(context);

                console.log(
                    '============================================================'
                );

                position =
                    lower.indexOf(
                        needle,
                        position + needle.length
                    );
            }
        }
    } catch {
        // Ignore unreadable/binary files.
    }
}

function walk(
    dir: string
) {
    let entries:
        fs.Dirent[];

    try {
        entries =
            fs.readdirSync(
                dir,
                {
                    withFileTypes: true,
                }
            );
    } catch {
        return;
    }

    for (
        const entry of entries
    ) {
        if (
            entry.isDirectory()
        ) {
            if (
                shouldSkipDirectory(
                    entry.name
                )
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            walk(
                path.join(
                    dir,
                    entry.name
                )
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (
            entry.isFile()
        ) {
            scanFile(
                path.join(
                    dir,
                    entry.name
                )
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        ' STON.FI v2.2 SDK SCHEMA SEARCH'
    );

    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - LOCAL node_modules ONLY'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `Search root: ${ROOT}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Searching for known STON.fi opcodes...'
    );

    walk(ROOT);

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        'SEARCH FINISHED'
    );

    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    console.log(
        `Files scanned : ${filesScanned}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Matches       : ${matches}`
    );

    console.log('');

    if (matches === 0) {
        console.log(
            'No opcode text found in node_modules.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'Review the matching files above.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT'
    );
}

FILE: scripts\findStore1953.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [0x195350, 0x195354];

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sid = wasm[p++];
        const s = readULEB(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (sid === id) {
            return { start: p, end: p + s.value };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

function parseCode() {
    const sec = findSection(10);
    let p = sec.start;

    const c = readULEB(p);
    p = c.next;

    const bodies: any[] = [];

    for (let ord = 0; ord < c.value; ord++) {
        const size = readULEB(p);
        p = size.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

        const localDecl = readULEB(p);
        p = localDecl.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;
            p++;
        }

        bodies.push({
            globalFunc: ord + 83,
            codeStart: p,
            bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

const bodies = parseCode();

console.log("FIND STORE PATTERNS FOR 0x195350 / 0x195354");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let total = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
    let p = body.codeStart;

    while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        /*
         * Memory stores:
         *
         * 0x36 i32.store
         * 0x37 i64.store
         * 0x38 f32.store
         * 0x39 f64.store
         * 0x3a i32.store8
         * 0x3b i32.store16
         * 0x3c i64.store8
         * 0x3d i64.store16
         * 0x3e i64.store32
         */

        if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readULEB(p);
            p = offset.next;

            /*
             * A direct constant-address store has:
             *
             * i32.const TARGET
             * store
             *
             * The CONST itself is immediately before the store.
             */

            const windowStart = Math.max(body.codeStart, at - 12);

            let foundTarget = false;

            for (let q = windowStart; q < at; q++) {
                if (wasm[q] !== 0x41) continue;

                let r = q + 1;
                let value = 0;
                let shift = 0;

                while (r < at) {
                    const b = wasm[r++];
                    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

                    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
                    shift += 7;
                }

                if (TARGETS.includes(value >>> 0)) {
                    foundTarget = true;

                    console.log("");
                    console.log(
                        `FUNC ${body.globalFunc} STORE @ ` +
                        `0x${at.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `  TARGET CONST = 0x${(value >>> 0).toString(16)} ` +
                        `(${value >>> 0})`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `  ALIGN=${align.value} OFFSET=${offset.value}`
                    );

                    console.log("  BYTES:");

                    const from = Math.max(body.codeStart, q - 8);
                    const to = Math.min(body.bodyEnd, p + 8);

                    let hex = "";

                    for (let x = from; x < to; x++) {
                        hex += wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
                    }

                    console.log(
                        `  0x${from.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. ` +
                        `0x${to.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
                    );
                    console.log(`  ${hex}`);

                    total++;
                }
            }

            if (foundTarget) continue;
        }

        switch (op) {
            case 0x10: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x11: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;

                const y = readULEB(p);
                p = y.next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x20:
            case 0x21:
            case 0x22: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x41:
            case 0x42: {
                let shift = 0;

                while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
                    const b = wasm[p++];
                    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
                    shift += 7;
                    if (shift > 35) break;
                }

                break;
            }

            case 0x43:
                p += 4;
                break;

            case 0x44:
                p += 8;
                break;

            case 0x28:
            case 0x29:
            case 0x2a:
            case 0x2b:
            case 0x2c:
            case 0x2d:
            case 0x2e:
            case 0x2f:
            case 0x36:
            case 0x37:
            case 0x38:
            case 0x39:
            case 0x3a:
            case 0x3b:
            case 0x3c:
            case 0x3d:
            case 0x3e: {
                const a = readULEB(p);
                p = a.next;

                const o = readULEB(p);
                p = o.next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0c:
            case 0x0d: {
                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
                break;
            }

            case 0x0e: {
                const n = readULEB(p);
                p = n.next;

                for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                    const x = readULEB(p);
                    p = x.next;
                }
                break;
            }

            case 0x02:
            case 0x03:
            case 0x04:
                p++;
                break;

            case 0x3f:
            case 0x40:
                p++;
                break;
        }
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log(`TOTAL STORE MATCHES = ${total}`);
console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

FILE: scripts\findStoresNear250FA0.js

const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;

const TARGET = 0x250fa0;
const RANGE = 0x40;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 0;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
    return "0x" +
        (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    const size = s.value;
    pos = s.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const count = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + i,
        start: p,
        end: p + size
    });

    p += size;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode store instructions
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function storeInfo(op, p) {

    if (
        op === 0x36 || // i32.store
        op === 0x3a || // i32.store8
        op === 0x3b    // i32.store16
    ) {

        const a = readULEB(p + 1);
        const o = readULEB(a.next);

        return {
            name:
                op === 0x36 ? "i32.store" :
                op === 0x3a ? "i32.store8" :
                               "i32.store16",
            align: a.value,
            offset: o.value,
            next: o.next
        };
    }

    return null;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Search backward for nearby i32.const
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function findBase(body, storePos) {

    const start = Math.max(body.start, storePos - 40);

    let candidates = [];

    for (let p = start; p < storePos; p++) {

        if (buf[p] !== 0x41)
            continue;

        const r = readSLEB(p + 1);
        const value = r.value >>> 0;

        if (
            value >= TARGET - RANGE &&
            value <= TARGET + RANGE
        ) {
            candidates.push({
                addr: p,
                value
            });
        }
    }

    return candidates;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Main
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STORE SCAN NEAR 0x250FA0");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`RANGE  : +/- ${RANGE} bytes`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let stores = 0;
let candidateStores = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

    for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

        const info = storeInfo(buf[p], p);

        if (!info)
            continue;

        stores++;

        const candidates =
            findBase(body, p);

        if (!candidates.length)
            continue;

        candidateStores++;

        console.log("\n############################################################");
        console.log(`FUNC ${body.func}`);
        console.log(`STORE @ ${hex(p)}`);
        console.log(`TYPE : ${info.name}`);
        console.log(`OFFSET : ${info.offset}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        for (const c of candidates) {

            console.log(
                `BASE CANDIDATE @ ${hex(c.addr)} = ${hex(c.value)}`
            );

            const effective =
                (c.value + info.offset) >>> 0;

            console.log(
                `EFFECTIVE ADDRESS = ${hex(effective)}`
            );
        }

        const from =
            Math.max(body.start, p - 24);

        const to =
            Math.min(body.end, p + 20);

        console.log("\nCONTEXT:");

        for (let q = from; q < to; q += 16) {

            const e = Math.min(to, q + 16);

            const bytes = [];

            for (let z = q; z < e; z++) {
                bytes.push(
                    buf[z]
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(2, "0")
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `${hex(q)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL STORE INSTRUCTIONS : ${stores}`);
console.log(`STORES WITH NEARBY BASE  : ${candidateStores}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findTable1968.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 1968;

let p = 8;

function u() {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;

  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);

  return v >>> 0;
}

let tableStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 9) {
    tableStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!tableStart) {
  throw new Error("TABLE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

let p = 8;

function uleb(): number {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

let table0: number[] = [];

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = uleb();
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 9) {
    const n = uleb();

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const flags = uleb();

      if (flags === 0) {
        if (b[p++] !== 0x41) throw new Error("bad offset expr");
        uleb();
        if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error("bad offset end");
      } else if (flags === 2) {
        uleb();
        if (b[p++] !== 0x41) throw new Error("bad offset expr");
        uleb();
        if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error("bad offset end");
      } else {
        throw new Error("unsupported element flags " + flags);
      }

      const count = uleb();

      for (let j = 0; j < count; j++) {
        const fn = uleb();
        table0.push(fn);
      }
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND FUNC 6618 / 446 IN TABLE 0");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE ENTRIES:", table0.length);

for (const target of [6618, 446]) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("TARGET FUNC:", target);

  let hits = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < table0.length; i++) {
    if (table0[i] === target) {
      console.log(
        "TABLE INDEX:",
        i + 1,
        "FUNC:",
        target
      );
      hits++;
    }
  }

  console.log("HITS:", hits);
}

console.log("");
console.log("DONE");

FILE: scripts\findTable72Producers.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
    };
}

function parseSections() {
    let pos = 8;

    const sections: Record<
        number,
        { start: number; end: number }
    > = {};

    while (pos < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[pos++];

        const s = u32leb(pos);
        pos = s.next;

        const start = pos;
        const end = pos + s.value;

        sections[id] = { start, end };

        pos = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseCodeBodies(section: {
    start: number;
    end: number;
}) {
    let pos = section.start;

    const count = u32leb(pos);
    pos = count.next;

    const bodies: {
        func: number;
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
        instrStart: number;
    }[] = [];

    const imported = 83;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const size = u32leb(pos);
        pos = size.next;

        const bodyStart = pos;
        const bodyEnd = pos + size.value;

        /*
         * locals vector
         */
        const localCount = u32leb(pos);
        pos = localCount.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCount.value; j++) {
            const n = u32leb(pos);
            pos = n.next;

            pos += 1; // valtype
        }

        const instrStart = pos;

        bodies.push({
            func: imported + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            instrStart,
        });

        pos = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function isInside(
    p: number,
    body: {
        bodyStart: number;
        bodyEnd: number;
    }
) {
    return p >= body.bodyStart && p < body.bodyEnd;
}

async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TABLE SLOT 0x72 PRODUCER SEARCH");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("");
    console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length}`);
    console.log("Target function : 4154");
    console.log("Target table slot: 0x72 = 114");
    console.log("i32.const 114 encoding: 41 f2 00");
    console.log("");

    const sections = parseSections();

    const code = sections[10];

    if (!code) {
        throw new Error("Code section not found");
    }

    const bodies = parseCodeBodies(code);

    console.log(`Code bodies : ${bodies.length}`);
    console.log("");

    const hits: {
        offset: number;
        func: number;
    }[] = [];

    /*
     * Search only for the exact instruction byte sequence:
     *
     *   41 f2 00
     *
     * This is NOT a raw 0xf2 search.
     */
    for (const body of bodies) {
        for (
            let p = body.instrStart;
            p + 3 <= body.bodyEnd;
            p++
        ) {
            if (
                wasm[p] === 0x41 &&
                wasm[p + 1] === 0xf2 &&
                wasm[p + 2] === 0x00
            ) {
                hits.push({
                    offset: p,
                    func: body.func,
                });
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" EXACT i32.const 114 HITS");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log(`Hits : ${hits.length}`);
    console.log("");

    for (const h of hits) {
        console.log(
            `Function ${h.func} @ 0x${h.offset
                .toString(16)
                .padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        const start = Math.max(h.offset - 16, 0);
        const end = Math.min(h.offset + 24, wasm.length);

        const hex = Array.from(
            wasm.subarray(start, end)
        )
            .map(
                b =>
                    b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            )
            .join(" ");

        console.log(`  ${hex}`);
        console.log("");
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" IMPORTANT");
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("");
    console.log("These are only PRODUCERS of numeric value 114.");
    console.log("They are NOT yet proven call_indirect callers.");
    console.log("");

    console.log("Next step:");
    console.log("  decode each hit forward to the next real");
    console.log("  call_indirect and perform stack tracking.");
    console.log("");

    console.log("No transaction sent.");
    console.log("No WASM modification.");
    console.log("Read-only / offline.");
}

export { run };

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNC = 7484;

function readULEB(pos) {

  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {

    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {

  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  do {

    b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    shift += 7;

  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * LOCATE ELEMENT SECTION
 * ============================================================
 */

let p = 8;

let elementStart = 0;
let elementEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);

  const payloadStart = size.next;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size.value;

  if (sectionId === 9) {

    elementStart = payloadStart;
    elementEnd = payloadEnd;

    break;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (!elementStart)
  throw new Error("ELEMENT SECTION NOT FOUND");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * HEADER
 * ============================================================
 */

p = elementStart;

const segmentCount = readULEB(p);

p = segmentCount.next;

console.log("FIND TABLE ENTRIES FOR FUNC 7484");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET FUNC :", TARGET_FUNC);

console.log(
  "ELEMENT SECTION :",
  hex(elementStart),
  "..",
  hex(elementEnd)
);

console.log(
  "SEGMENT COUNT :",
  segmentCount.value
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * SEGMENT 0
 *
 * VERIFIED RAW FORMAT:
 *
 * 01
 * 00
 * 41 01 0b
 * a0 46
 * ...
 *
 * flags = 0
 * offset expression follows immediately
 * no table-index field
 * no elemkind field
 * ============================================================
 */

if (segmentCount.value !== 1)
  throw new Error(
    "Unexpected segment count: " +
    segmentCount.value
  );

/*
 * FLAGS
 */

const flags = readULEB(p);

p = flags.next;

console.log(
  "FLAGS :",
  flags.value
);

if (flags.value !== 0)
  throw new Error(
    "Unexpected element segment flags: " +
    flags.value
  );

/*
 * OFFSET EXPRESSION
 *
 * 41 = i32.const
 */

const offsetOpcode = wasm[p++];

if (offsetOpcode !== 0x41) {

  throw new Error(
    "Expected i32.const 0x41, got " +
    hex(offsetOpcode)
  );
}

const offset = readSLEB(p);

p = offset.next;

/*
 * 0b = end
 */

const endOpcode = wasm[p++];

if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {

  throw new Error(
    "Expected end 0x0b, got " +
    hex(endOpcode)
  );
}

console.log(
  "TABLE OFFSET :",
  offset.value
);

p = count.next;

console.log(
  "FUNCTION COUNT :",
  count.value
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/*

 * SEARCH TARGET
 * ============================================================

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const f = readULEB(p);

  if (f.value === TARGET_FUNC) {

    matches.push({

      tableIndex: offset.value + i,

      vectorIndex: i,

      function: f.value,

      rawPosition: p
    });
  }

  p = f.next;
}

  console.log("");

const fs = require("fs");

const FILE = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 8927;

const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function leb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let s = 0;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[pos++];
        v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;

        if (!(x & 0x80)) {
            return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        s += 7;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE REGISTRATION FOR FUNC 8927");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", b.length);
console.log("TARGET    :", TARGET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let pos = 8;
let found = false;

while (pos < b.length) {

    const sectionId = b[pos++];

    const sr = leb(pos);
    const sectionSize = sr.value;
    pos = sr.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    // Element section
    if (sectionId === 9) {

        console.log(
            "ELEMENT SECTION :",
            "0x" + payloadStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
            "..",
            "0x" + payloadEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
        );

        let p = payloadStart;

        const countR = leb(p);
        const segmentCount = countR.value;
        p = countR.next;

        console.log("SEGMENTS :", segmentCount);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

            const flagsR = leb(p);
            const flags = flagsR.value;
            p = flagsR.next;

            let tableIndex = 0;

            // flags 0: active, table 0, offset expression
            // flags 2: active, explicit table index
            if (flags === 2) {
                const tr = leb(p);
                tableIndex = tr.value;
                p = tr.next;
            }

            // Skip/read offset expression.
            // Typical: i32.const <offset> end
            const opcode = b[p++];

            let offset = 0;

            if (opcode === 0x41) {
                const r = leb(p);
                offset = r.value;
                p = r.next;
            } else {
                throw new Error(
                    "Unexpected element offset opcode 0x" +
                    opcode.toString(16)
                );
            }

            if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error("Missing end of offset expression");
            }

            // element kind / expressions
            if (flags === 0 || flags === 1 || flags === 2 || flags === 3) {

                // For function-index segments:
                // flags 0/2 = elemkind byte
                if (flags === 0 || flags === 2) {
                    const elemkind = b[p++];

                    const countR2 = leb(p);
                    const count = countR2.value;
                    p = countR2.next;

                    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

                        const r = leb(p);
                        const func = r.value;
                        p = r.next;

                        if (func === TARGET) {
                            console.log(
                                "FOUND",
                                "TABLE[" + (offset + i) + "]",
                                "-> FUNC",
                                TARGET,
                                "SEGMENT",
                                seg,
                                "TABLE",
                                tableIndex,
                                "ELEMENT INDEX",
                                i
                            );

                            found = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        break;
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (!found) {
    console.log("NO TABLE ENTRY FOUND FOR FUNC", TARGET);
}

console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK   : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

FILE: scripts\findTable8952.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 8952;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    if (pos + i >= buf.length)
      throw new Error("ULEB out of bounds");

    const b = buf[pos + i];

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        next: pos + i + 1
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB too long");
}

function sectionName(id) {
  switch (id) {
    case 0: return "CUSTOM";
    case 1: return "TYPE";
    case 2: return "IMPORT";
    case 3: return "FUNCTION";
    case 4: return "TABLE";
    case 5: return "MEMORY";
    case 6: return "GLOBAL";
    case 7: return "EXPORT";
    case 8: return "START";
    case 9: return "ELEMENT";
    case 10: return "CODE";
    case 11: return "DATA";
    default: return "UNKNOWN";
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND FUNCTION 8952 IN ELEMENT SECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGET    :", TARGET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let pos = 8;
let found = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

  const sectionStart = pos;

  const id = buf[pos++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  pos = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = pos;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

  if (payloadEnd > buf.length)
    throw new Error("Section exceeds WASM");

  console.log(
    "SECTION",
    id,
    sectionName(id),
    "START 0x" + sectionStart.toString(16),
    "SIZE",
    sectionSize
  );

  if (id === 9) {

    console.log("");
    console.log(">>> ELEMENT SECTION FOUND");
    console.log("PAYLOAD : 0x" +
      payloadStart.toString(16) +
      " .. 0x" +
      payloadEnd.toString(16));

    let p = payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    console.log("ELEMENT COUNT :", count);

    for (let e = 0; e < count; e++) {

      const entryStart = p;

      const flagsInfo = readULEB(p);
      const flags = flagsInfo.value;
      p = flagsInfo.next;

      let mode = flags & 3;

      // MVP active/passive/declarative handling
      if (flags === 0) {
        // table index 0 implied
        // offset expression: i32.const + end
        const opcode = buf[p++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
          console.log(
            "ELEMENT", e,
            "unexpected offset opcode 0x" +
            opcode.toString(16)
          );
        }

        const offInfo = readULEB(p);
        p = offInfo.next;

        if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
          console.log("ELEMENT", e, "missing end");
        }

        const nInfo = readULEB(p);
        const n = nInfo.value;
        p = nInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
          const fi = readULEB(p);
          const functionIndex = fi.value;
          p = fi.next;

          if (functionIndex === TARGET) {
            found++;

            console.log("");
            console.log("============================================================");
            console.log("FOUND FUNCTION 8952");
            console.log("ELEMENT       :", e);
            console.log("TABLE INDEX   :", i);
            console.log("ENTRY OFFSET  : 0x" +
              entryStart.toString(16));
            console.log("FUNCTION IDX  :", functionIndex);
            console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
          }
        }

      } else {
        /*
         * For non-MVP element forms we cannot safely skip
         * without decoding the complete element encoding.
         * Stop rather than guessing.
         */
        console.log("");
        console.log("ELEMENT", e);
        console.log("FLAGS :", flags);
        console.log(
          "NON-MVP ELEMENT FORM — STOPPING SAFE SCAN"
        );
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  pos = payloadEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 8952 TABLE HITS :", found);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p:number){
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v |= (x&0x7f)<<s;
    if(!(x&0x80)) return {v:v>>>0,p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

let p=8;
let tableTargets:number[]=[];

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  const start=z.p;
  const end=start+z.v;

  if(id===9){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    console.log("ELEMENT SEGMENTS:",n.v);

    for(let s=0;s<n.v;s++){
      const flags=uleb(q);
      q=flags.p;

      console.log(`SEGMENT ${s} FLAGS=${flags.v}`);

      // active table segment
      if(flags.v===0){
        // offset expression
        const op=b[q++];

        if(op===0x41){
          const x=uleb(q);
          q=x.p;

          const endop=b[q++];

          console.log(`TABLE OFFSET = ${x.v}`);
        }else{
          console.log(`OFFSET OPCODE = 0x${op.toString(16)}`);
        }

        const elemkind=b[q++];

        const count=uleb(q);
        q=count.p;

        for(let i=0;i<count.v;i++){
          const fn=uleb(q);
          q=fn.p;
          tableTargets.push(fn.v);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findTableIndex8689.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const FILE = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGET_INDEX = 8689;

function readULEB(buf, s) {
    let n = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (s.pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[s.pos++];
        n += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return n;

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 49) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function encodeULEB(n) {
    const out = [];

    do {
        let b = n % 128;
        n = Math.floor(n / 128);
        if (n) b |= 128;
        out.push(b);
    } while (n);

    return out;
}

function hex(n) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function getBodies(buf) {
    const s = { pos: 8 };

    while (s.pos < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[s.pos++];
        const size = readULEB(buf, s);
        const start = s.pos;
        const end = start + size;

        if (id === 10) {
            const cs = { pos: start };
            const count = readULEB(buf, cs);
            const bodies = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const bodySize = readULEB(buf, cs);
                const bodyStart = cs.pos;
                const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

                bodies.push({
                    fn: IMPORTS + i,
                    start: bodyStart,
                    end: bodyEnd
                });

                cs.pos = bodyEnd;
            }

            return bodies;
        }

        s.pos = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function dump(buf, start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const q = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [];

        for (let i = p; i < q; i++) {
            bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
    }
}

function findPattern(buf, start, end, pattern) {
    const hits = [];

    for (let p = start; p + pattern.length <= end; p++) {
        let ok = true;

        for (let i = 0; i < pattern.length; i++) {
            if (buf[p + i] !== pattern[i]) {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (ok) hits.push(p);
    }

    return hits;
}

function main() {
    const buf = fs.readFileSync(FILE);
    const bodies = getBodies(buf);

    const enc = encodeULEB(TARGET_INDEX);
    const patterns = [
        {
            name: "i32.const 8689",
            bytes: [0x41, ...enc]
        },
        {
            name: "call_indirect type 0 table 0",
            bytes: [0x11, 0x00, 0x00]
        }
    ];

    console.log("TABLE INDEX 8689 INVESTIGATION");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`TARGET TABLE INDEX = ${TARGET_INDEX}`);
    console.log(`ULEB = ${enc.map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")}`);
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNCTION = 4098`);
    console.log("");

    for (const pattern of patterns) {
        console.log(`PATTERN: ${pattern.name}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        let total = 0;

        for (const body of bodies) {
            const hits = findPattern(
                buf,
                body.start,
                body.end,
                pattern.bytes
            );

            for (const hit of hits) {
                total++;
                console.log(
                    `FUNC ${body.fn} | BODY ${hex(body.start)}..${hex(body.end)}` +
                    ` | HIT ${hex(hit)}`
                );

                const start = Math.max(body.start, hit - 24);
                const end = Math.min(body.end, hit + 40);
                dump(buf, start, end);
                console.log("");
            }
        }

        console.log(`RAW MATCH COUNT = ${total}`);
        console.log("");
    }

    console.log("DONE - NO WASM EXECUTION");
    console.log("Matches are byte-level candidates, not confirmed instruction paths.");
}

try {
    main();
} catch (err) {
    console.error("FAILED:", err);
    process.exitCode = 1;
}
