import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(p:number){
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true){
    const x=b[p++];
    v |= (x&0x7f)<<s;
    if(!(x&0x80)) return {v:v>>>0,p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

function skipBlockType(p:number){
  const x=b[p];
  if(x===0x40 || x===0x7f || x===0x7e || x===0x7d || x===0x7c ||
     x===0x70 || x===0x6f) return p+1;
  return uleb(p).p;
}

function str(p:number){
  const x=uleb(p);
  return x.p+x.v;
}

let imported=0;
let funcs:{fn:number,code:number,end:number}[]=[];

let p=8;

while(p<b.length){
  const id=b[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  const start=z.p;
  const end=start+z.v;

  if(id===2){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      q=str(q);
      q=str(q);
      const kind=b[q++];

      if(kind===0){
        q=uleb(q).p;
        imported++;
      }else if(kind===1){
        q++;
        const fl=uleb(q); q=fl.p;
        q=uleb(q).p;
        if(fl.v&1) q=uleb(q).p;
      }else if(kind===2){
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }else if(kind===3){
        q+=2;
      }
    }
  }

  if(id===10){
    let q=start;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.p;

    for(let i=0;i<n.v;i++){
      const bs=uleb(q);
      const body=bs.p;
      const bend=body+bs.v;

      let x=body;
      const nl=uleb(x);
      x=nl.p;

      for(let j=0;j<nl.v;j++){
        const c=uleb(x);
        x=c.p+1;
      }

      funcs.push({
        fn:imported+i,
        code:x,
        end:bend
      });

      q=bend;
    }
  }

  p=end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("ALL TABLE.SET INSTRUCTIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let count=0;

for(const f of funcs){

  let q=f.code;

  while(q<f.end-1){

    const off=q;
    const op=b[q++];

    try{

      if(op===0x25){
        const x=uleb(q);
        q=x.p;

        console.log(
          `FUNC ${f.fn}  table.get @ 0x${off.toString(16)} table=${x.v}`
        );
      }

      else if(op===0x26){
        const x=uleb(q);
        q=x.p;

        console.log(
          `FUNC ${f.fn}  TABLE.SET @ 0x${off.toString(16)} table=${x.v}`
        );

        console.log("  preceding instructions:");

        // restart and print last ~12 decoded offsets
        let r=f.code;
        const arr:string[]=[];

        while(r<off){
          const a=r;
          const o=b[r++];

          if(o===0x20){
            const x=uleb(r); r=x.p;
            arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} local.get ${x.v}`);
          }
          else if(o===0x21){
            const x=uleb(r); r=x.p;
            arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} local.set ${x.v}`);
          }
          else if(o===0x22){
            const x=uleb(r); r=x.p;
            arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} local.tee ${x.v}`);
          }
          else if(o===0x41){
            const x=uleb(r); r=x.p;
            arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} i32.const ${x.v}`);
          }
          else if(o===0x10){
            const x=uleb(r); r=x.p;
            arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} call ${x.v}`);
          }
          else if(o===0x28 || o===0x29 ||
                  o===0x36 || o===0x37){
            r=uleb(r).p;
            const x=uleb(r); r=x.p;
            arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} mem offset=${x.v}`);
          }
          else if(o===0x6a) arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} i32.add`);
          else if(o===0x6b) arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} i32.sub`);
          else if(o===0x6c) arr.push(`0x${a.toString(16)} i32.mul`);
        }

        for(const x of arr.slice(-12))
          console.log("    "+x);

        count++;
      }

      else if(op===0x20 || op===0x21 || op===0x22){
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }
      else if(op===0x41){
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }
      else if(op===0x28 || op===0x29 ||
              op===0x36 || op===0x37){
        q=uleb(q).p;
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }
      else if(op===0x10){
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }
      else if(op===0x11){
        q=uleb(q).p;
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }
      else if(op===0x0c || op===0x0d){
        q=uleb(q).p;
      }
      else if(op===0x04 || op===0x03 || op===0x02){
        q=skipBlockType(q);
      }

    }catch{
      break;
    }
  }
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [3901, 6521, 8952];
const NEIGHBOR = 20;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND TABLE INDICES FOR TYPE 7 WRAPPERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGETS   :", TARGETS.join(", "));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32LEB(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    const start = p;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: result >>> 0,
                next: p,
                bytes: p - start
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("Invalid U32 LEB128");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readSections(buf) {
    let p = 8;
    const result = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

        result.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: sizeInfo.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return result;
}

function decodeElementSection(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
    const segmentCount = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const segments = [];

    for (let s = 0; s < segmentCount; s++) {

        const segmentStart = p;

        const flagsInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        const flags = flagsInfo.value;
        p = flagsInfo.next;

        /*
         * We expect the classic active element segment:
         *
         * flags = 0
         * table index = 0
         * offset expression = i32.const ...
         * end
         * vec(funcidx)
         *
         * We nevertheless parse the flags explicitly.
         */

        let tableIndex = null;
        let offset = null;

        if (flags === 0) {

            tableIndex = 0;

            const opcode = buf[p++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Unexpected element offset opcode 0x" +
                    opcode.toString(16)
                );
            }

            const offsetInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            offset = offsetInfo.value;
            p = offsetInfo.next;

            const endOpcode = buf[p++];

            if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Unexpected element offset end opcode 0x" +
                    endOpcode.toString(16)
                );
            }
        }

        else if (flags === 2) {

            const tableInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            tableIndex = tableInfo.value;
            p = tableInfo.next;

            const opcode = buf[p++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Unexpected table offset opcode"
                );
            }

            const offsetInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            offset = offsetInfo.value;
            p = offsetInfo.next;

            const endOpcode = buf[p++];

            if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Unexpected table offset end opcode"
                );
            }
        }

        else {
            throw new Error(
                "Unsupported element flags " + flags
            );
        }

        /*
         * For flags 0 and 2 the next field is the function-index vector.
         */

        const vecInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        const functionCount = vecInfo.value;
        p = vecInfo.next;

        const vectorStart = p;

        const entries = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {

            const entryOffset = p;

            const funcInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = funcInfo.next;

            entries.push({
                position: i,
                tableIndex: offset + i,
                function: funcInfo.value,
                rawOffset: entryOffset,
                rawBytes: funcInfo.bytes
            });
        }

        segments.push({
            segment: s,
            segmentStart,
            flags,
            tableIndex,
            offset,
            functionCount,
            vectorStart,
            entries
        });
    }

    return segments;
}

const sections = readSections(wasm);

const elementSection = sections.find(
    x => x.id === 9
);

if (!elementSection) {
    throw new Error("ELEMENT SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log(
    "ELEMENT SECTION :",
    "0x" + elementSection.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
    "..",
    "0x" + elementSection.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
);

console.log(
    "SIZE :",
    elementSection.size
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const segments =
    decodeElementSection(
        wasm,
        elementSection
    );

console.log(
    "SEGMENTS :",
    segments.length
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const found = [];

for (const seg of segments) {

    console.log();
    console.log("SEGMENT", seg.segment);
    console.log("FLAGS :", seg.flags);
    console.log("TABLE :", seg.tableIndex);
    console.log("OFFSET:", seg.offset);
    console.log("FUNCTION COUNT:", seg.functionCount);
    console.log(
        "VECTOR START:",
        "0x" +
        seg.vectorStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    );

    for (const target of TARGETS) {

        const hit =
            seg.entries.find(
                e => e.function === target
            );

        if (!hit) {
            console.log();
            console.log(
                `TARGET FUNC ${target} : NOT FOUND`
            );
            continue;
        }

        found.push({
            target,
            segment: seg.segment,
            tableIndex: hit.tableIndex,
            vectorPosition: hit.position,
            rawOffset: hit.rawOffset,
            entry: hit
        });

        console.log();
        console.log(
            "******** TARGET FOUND ********"
        );

        console.log(
            "FUNCTION        :",
            target
        );

        console.log(
            "VECTOR POSITION :",
            hit.position
        );

        console.log(
            "TABLE INDEX     :",
            hit.tableIndex
        );

        console.log(
            "RAW OFFSET      :",
            "0x" +
            hit.rawOffset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
        );

        console.log(
            "RAW BYTES       :",
            hit.rawBytes
        );

        console.log(
            "*******************************"
        );
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Neighborhoods

console.log();

        segments[item.segment];

    const center =
        item.vectorPosition;

    const from =
        Math.max(
            0,
            center - NEIGHBOR

    const to =
        Math.min(
            seg.entries.length - 1,

    console.log(

  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const TARGETS = [
  2053,
  2101,
  2612
];

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {

    if (pos + i >= b.length)
      return null;

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        end: pos + i + 1
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function getBodies() {

  let p = CODE_START;

  const count = readULEB(p)!;
  p = count.end;

  const bodies: any[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const size = readULEB(p)!;
    p = size.end;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      global: i + 83,
      start: p,
      end: p + size.value
    });

    p += size.value;
  }

  if (p !== CODE_END)
    throw new Error(
      `CODE END MISMATCH ${hex(p)} != ${hex(CODE_END)}`
    );

  return bodies;
}

const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET TABLE INDEX SOURCE SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let total = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {

    /*
       i32.const opcode = 0x41
    */

    if (b[p] !== 0x41)
      continue;

    const x = readULEB(p + 1);

    if (!x)
      continue;

    if (!TARGETS.includes(x.value))
      continue;

    total++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`FUNCTION GLOBAL = ${body.global}`);
    console.log(`BODY            = ${body.ordinal}`);
    console.log(`i32.const       = ${x.value}`);
    console.log(`ADDRESS         = ${hex(p)}`);

    const from = Math.max(
      body.start,
      p - 48
    );

    const to = Math.min(
      body.end,
      x.end + 64
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log(`CONTEXT ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`);

    console.log(
      Array.from(b.slice(from, to))
        .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL TARGET CONSTANTS = ${total}`);
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_TYPE = 3;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Locate code section
 */
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = readULEB(p);

  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

p = codeStart;

const bc = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = bc.value;
p = bc.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE 3 INDIRECT CALL SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET TYPE :", TARGET_TYPE);
console.log("CODE BODIES :", bodyCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {

  const bs = readULEB(p);

  const bodySize = bs.value;
  const bodyStart = bs.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  const funcIndex = IMPORTED + i;

  for (let q = bodyStart; q < bodyEnd - 2; q++) {

    /*
     * call_indirect:
     *
     * 0x11
     * typeidx
     * tableidx
     */
    if (wasm[q] !== 0x11)
      continue;

    const type = readULEB(q + 1);
    const table = readULEB(type.next);

    if (type.value !== TARGET_TYPE)
      continue;

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `FUNC ${funcIndex} @ ${hex(q)} ` +
      `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
    );
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("TYPE 3 INDIRECT CALL COUNT :", hits);

console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

FILE: scripts\findType6618.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

type FuncType = {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
};

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readName(pos: number) {
  const len = readULEB(pos);
  return { next: len.next + len.value };
}

function readLimits(pos: number): number {
  const flags = readULEB(pos);
  let q = flags.next;
  q = readULEB(q).next;
  if (flags.value & 1) q = readULEB(q).next;
  return q;
}

const sections = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

let p = 8;
while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = readULEB(p);
  const start = size.next;
  const end = start + size.value;
  sections.set(id, { start, end });
  p = end;
}

const typeSection = sections.get(1);
const importSection = sections.get(2);
const functionSection = sections.get(3);

if (!typeSection || !importSection || !functionSection) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM section not found");
}

// Read function signatures.
const types: FuncType[] = [];
p = typeSection.start;

const typeCount = readULEB(p);
p = typeCount.next;

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {
  if (b[p++] !== 0x60) throw new Error("Unexpected function type form");

  const pc = readULEB(p);
  p = pc.next;
  const params = Array.from(b.subarray(p, p + pc.value));
  p += pc.value;

  const rc = readULEB(p);
  p = rc.next;
  const results = Array.from(b.subarray(p, p + rc.value));
  p += rc.value;

  types.push({ params, results });
}

// Count imported functions and preserve their type indices.
p = importSection.start;
const ic = readULEB(p);
p = ic.next;

const importedFunctionTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < ic.value; i++) {
  p = readName(p).next; // module
  p = readName(p).next; // field/name

  const kind = b[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {
    const t = readULEB(p);
    importedFunctionTypes.push(t.value);
    p = t.next;
  } else if (kind === 1) {
    p++; // element type
    p = readLimits(p);
  } else if (kind === 2) {
    p = readLimits(p);
  } else if (kind === 3) {
    p += 2; // global value type and mutability
  } else if (kind === 4) {
    // Exception tag import: attribute + type index.
    p++; 
    p = readULEB(p).next;
  } else {
    throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
  }
}

// Read type indices for defined functions.
p = functionSection.start;
const fc = readULEB(p);
p = fc.next;

const definedTypes: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
  const t = readULEB(p);
  definedTypes.push(t.value);
  p = t.next;
}

const TARGET = 6618;
const importedCount = importedFunctionTypes.length;
const ordinal = TARGET - importedCount;

if (TARGET < importedCount) {
  console.log(`Function ${TARGET} is imported.`);
  console.log("Its type index:", importedFunctionTypes[TARGET]);
  process.exit(0);
}

if (ordinal < 0 || ordinal >= definedTypes.length) {
  throw new Error("Target function index out of range");
}

const targetTypeIndex = definedTypes[ordinal];
const indirectTypeIndex = 2;

function fmt(t: FuncType) {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64",
    0x7b: "v128",
    0x70: "funcref",
    0x6f: "externref",
  };

  return `(${t.params.map(x => names[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(", ")}) -> (${t.results.map(x => names[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(", ")})`;
}

console.log("FUNC TYPE COMPATIBILITY CHECK");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("Imported functions :", importedCount);
console.log("Function 6618 ordinal:", ordinal);
console.log("Function 6618 type index:",
 targetTypeIndex);
console.log("Indirect call type index:", indirectTypeIndex);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (!types[targetTypeIndex] || !types[indirectTypeIndex]) {
  throw new Error("Function type index out of range");
}

console.log("Function 6618 signature:", fmt(types[targetTypeIndex]));
console.log("Type 2 signature       :", fmt(types[indirectTypeIndex]));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const a = types[targetTypeIndex];
const c = types[indirectTypeIndex];

const exactMatch =
  a.params.length === c.params.length &&
  a.results.length === c.results.length &&
  a.params.every((v, i) => v === c.params[i]) &&
  a.results.every((v, i) => v === c.results[i]);

console.log("EXACT TYPE MATCH:", exactMatch ? "YES" : "NO");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findType7Callers.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [3901, 6521, 8952];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND CALLERS OF TYPE 7 INDIRECT WRAPPERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGETS   :", TARGETS.join(", "));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: result >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("Invalid U32 LEB128");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readI32(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return {
                value: result | 0,
                next: p
            };
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function sections(buf) {
    let p = 8;
    const out = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = readU32(buf, p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        out.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: s.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return out;
}

function parseImports(buf, section) {
    if (!section) {
        return {
            importedFunctionCount: 0,
            importedFunctionTypes: []
        };
    }

    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const importedFunctionTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const moduleLen = readU32(buf, p);
        p = moduleLen.next;
        p += moduleLen.value;

        const nameLen = readU32(buf, p);
        p = nameLen.next;
        p += nameLen.value;

        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const typeInfo = readU32(buf, p);
            p = typeInfo.next;

            importedFunctionTypes.push(typeInfo.value);
        }

        else if (kind === 1) {
            p += 1;

            const flags = buf[p++];

            const min = readU32(buf, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const max = readU32(buf, p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        }

        else if (kind === 2) {
            const flags = buf[p++];

            const min = readU32(buf, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const max = readU32(buf, p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        }

        else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        }

        else {
            throw new Error("Unknown import kind " + kind);
        }
    }

    return {
        importedFunctionCount: importedFunctionTypes.length,
        importedFunctionTypes
    };
}

function parseFunctionSection(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const types = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const x = readU32(buf, p);
        p = x.next;
        types.push(x.value);
    }

    return types;
}

/*
    Decode enough WASM instructions to identify:

    0x10 call
    0x11 call_indirect
    0xd2 ref.func

    Also decode common instructions so that byte positions remain exact.
*/

function decodeInstruction(buf, p, end) {
    const start = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    const ins = {
        start,
        end: p,
        op,
        mnemonic: "unknown",
        immediate: null
    };

    switch (op) {

        case 0x0b:
            ins.mnemonic = "end";
            break;

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readU32(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "call";
            ins.immediate = {
                func: x.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = readU32(buf, p);
            p = t.next;

            const table = readU32(buf, p);
            p = table.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "call_indirect";
            ins.immediate = {
                type: t.value,
                table: table.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0xd2: {
            const x = readU32(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "ref.func";
            ins.immediate = {
                func: x.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readU32(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "local.get";
            ins.immediate = {
                local: x.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readU32(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "local.set";
            ins.immediate = {
                local: x.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readU32(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "local.tee";
            ins.immediate = {
                local: x.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readI32(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i32.const";
            ins.immediate = {
                value: x.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const a = readU32(buf, p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readU32(buf, p);
            p = o.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i32.load";
            ins.immediate = {
                align: a.value,
                offset: o.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x2d: {
            const a = readU32(buf, p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readU32(buf, p);
            p = o.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i32.load8_u";
            ins.immediate = {
                align: a.value,
                offset: o.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const a = readU32(buf, p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readU32(buf, p);
            p = o.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i32.store";
            ins.immediate = {
                align: a.value,
                offset: o.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const a = readU32(buf, p);
            p = a.next;

            const o = readU32(buf, p);
            p = o.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i64.store";
            ins.immediate = {
                align: a.value,
                offset: o.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x6a:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.add";
            break;

        case 0x6b:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.sub";
            break;

        case 0x46:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.eq";
            break;

        case 0x47:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.ne";
            break;

        case 0x48:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.lt_s";
            break;

        case 0x49:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.lt_u";
            break;

        case 0x4a:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.gt_s";
            break;

        case 0x4b:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.gt_u";
            break;

        case 0x4c:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.le_s";
            break;

        case 0x4d:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.le_u";
            break;

        case 0x4e:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.ge_s";
            break;

        case 0x4f:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.ge_u";
            break;

        case 0x1b:
            ins.mnemonic = "select";
            break;

        case 0x0f:
            ins.mnemonic = "return";
            break;

        case 0x02:
            ins.mnemonic = "block";

            if (p < end) {
                const blockType = buf[p++];

                if (blockType === 0x40) {
                    ins.immediate = {
                        blockType: "empty"
                    };
                } else {
                    ins.immediate = {
                        blockType
                    };
                }
            }

            break;

        case 0x03:
            ins.mnemonic = "loop";

            if (p < end) {
                const blockType = buf[p++];

                ins.immediate = {
                    blockType
                };
            }

            break;

        case 0x04:
            ins.mnemonic = "if";

            if (p < end) {
                const blockType = buf[p++];

                ins.immediate = {
                    blockType
                };
            }

            break;

        case 0x05:
            ins.mnemonic = "else";
            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readU32(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "br";
            ins.immediate = {
                depth: x.value
            };
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readU32(buf, p);
            p = x.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "br_if";
            ins.immediate = {
                depth: x.value
            };
            break;
        }

        default:
            return null;
    }

    ins.end = p;

    if (p > end) {
        return null;
    }

    return ins;
}

function decodeBody(buf, start, end) {
    let p = start;

    const sizeInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadEnd = p + sizeInfo.value;

    if (payloadEnd > end) {
        return null;
    }

    const localsInfo = readU32(buf, p);
    const localDeclCount = localsInfo.value;
    p = localsInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
        const count = readU32(buf, p);
        p = count.next;

        p += 1;
    }

    const instructions = [];

    while (p < payloadEnd) {

        const ins = decodeInstruction(
            buf,
            p,
            payloadEnd
        );

        if (!ins) {
            return {
                instructions,
                failedAt: p,
                payloadEnd
            };
        }

        instructions.push(ins);
        p = ins.end;

        if (ins.mnemonic === "end" && p === payloadEnd) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return {
        instructions,
        failedAt: null,
        payloadEnd
    };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Parse sections
------------------------------------------------------------ */

const secs = sections(wasm);

const importSection = secs.find(x => x.id === 2);
const functionSection = secs.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSection = secs.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!functionSection || !codeSection) {
    throw new Error("FUNCTION or CODE section not found");
}

const imports = parseImports(
    wasm,
    importSection
);

const definedTypes = parseFunctionSection(
    wasm,
    functionSection
);

const functionTypes = [
    ...imports.importedFunctionTypes,
    ...definedTypes
];

console.log(
    "IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :",
    imports.importedFunctionCount
);

console.log(
    "DEFINED FUNCTIONS  :",
    definedTypes.length
);

console.log(
    "TOTAL FUNCTIONS    :",
    functionTypes.length
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Scan every function body
------------------------------------------------------------ */

let p = codeSection.start;

const bodyCountInfo = readU32(buf = wasm, p);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
p = bodyCountInfo.next;

const results = [];

let decodeFailures = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const bodyStart = p;

    const sizeInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyEnd = p + sizeInfo.value;

    const funcIndex =
        imports.importedFunctionCount +
        bodyIndex;

    const decoded =
        decodeBody(
            wasm,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd
        );

    if (!decoded) {
        decodeFailures++;
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    if (decoded.failedAt !== null) {
        decodeFailures++;
    }

    const hits = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < decoded.instructions.length; i++) {

        const ins = decoded.instructions[i];

        if (
            ins.mnemonic === "call" &&
            TARGETS.includes(ins.immediate.func)
        ) {
            hits.push({
                kind: "call",
                target: ins.immediate.func,
                instructionIndex: i,
                offset: ins.start
            });
        }

        if (
            ins.mnemonic === "ref.func" &&
            TARGETS.includes(ins.immediate.func)
        ) {
            hits.push({
                kind: "ref.func",
                target: ins.immediate.func,
                instructionIndex: i,
                offset: ins.start
            });
        }
    }

    if (hits.length > 0) {

        results.push({
            funcIndex,
            bodyIndex,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            typeIndex: functionTypes[funcIndex],
            instructions: decoded.instructions,
            hits
        });
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Report
------------------------------------------------------------ */

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT CALL / REF.FUNC RESULTS");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    "FUNCTIONS WITH DIRECT REFERENCES :",
    results.length
);

console.log(
    "DECODER FAILURES :",
    decodeFailures
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const r of results) {

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `CALLER FUNC ${r.funcIndex} TYPE=${r.typeIndex} BODY=${r.bodyIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
        `BODY : 0x${r.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}` +
        ` .. 0x${r.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    for (const hit of r.hits) {

        console.log();
        console.log(
            `>>> ${hit.kind.toUpperCase()} TARGET=${hit.target}`
        );

        console.log(
            `    OFFSET : 0x${hit.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        const start =
            Math.max(0, hit.instructionIndex - 8);

        const end =
            Math.min(
                r.instructions.length,
                hit.instructionIndex + 5
            );

        console.log("    CONTEXT:");

        for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {

            const ins = r.instructions[i];

            let extra = "";

            if (ins.mnemonic === "call") {
                extra =
                    ` func=${ins.immediate.func}`;
            }

            else if (ins.mnemonic === "ref.func") {
                extra =
                    ` func=${ins.immediate.func}`;
            }

            else if (ins.mnemonic === "local.get") {
                extra =
                    ` local=${ins.immediate.local}`;
            }

            else if (ins.mnemonic === "local.set") {
                extra =
                    ` local=${ins.immediate.local}`;
            }

            else if (ins.mnemonic === "local.tee") {
                extra =
                    ` local=${ins.immediate.local}`;
            }

            else if (ins.mnemonic === "i32.const") {
                extra =
                    ` value=${ins.immediate.value}`;
            }

            else if (
                ins.mnemonic === "i32.load" ||
                ins.mnemonic === "i32.load8_u" ||
                ins.mnemonic === "i32.store" ||
                ins.mnemonic === "i64.store"
            ) {
                extra =
                    ` align=${ins.immediate.align}` +
                    ` offset=${ins.immediate.offset}`;
            }

            else if (ins.mnemonic === "call_indirect") {
                extra =
                    ` type=${ins.immediate.type}` +
                    ` table=${ins.immediate.table}`;
            }

            const marker =
                i === hit.instructionIndex
                    ? " <<< TARGET"
                    : "";

            console.log(
                `      0x${ins.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
                `${ins.mnemonic}${extra}${marker}`
            );
        }
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Per-target summary
------------------------------------------------------------ */

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("PER TARGET SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    const targetHits = [];

    for (const r of results) {
        for (const h of r.hits) {
            if (h.target === target) {
                targetHits.push({
                    caller: r.funcIndex,
                    type: r.typeIndex,
                    kind: h.kind,
                    offset: h.offset
                });
            }
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `TARGET ${target} : ${targetHits.length} DIRECT REFERENCES`
    );

    if (targetHits.length === 0) {
        console.log("  NONE");
    }

    for (const h of targetHits) {
        console.log(
            `  ${h.kind}` +
            ` caller=${h.caller}` +
            ` callerType=${h.type}` +
            ` offset=0x${h.offset.toString(16)}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findType7IndirectCalls.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_TYPE = 7;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let codeSection = null;

while (pos < buf.length) {
  const id = buf[pos++];

  const s = readULEB(pos);
  pos = s.next;

  const start = pos;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeSection = { start, end };
    break;
  }

  pos = end;
}

if (!codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found.");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Enumerate code bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = codeSection.start;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + bodySize;

  bodies.push({ start, end });

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Generic immediate skipper
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function skipImmediate(op, p) {
  // block / loop
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03) {
    return readULEB(p).next;
  }

  // if
  if (op === 0x04) {
    return readULEB(p).next;
  }

  // br / br_if
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    return readULEB(p).next;
  }

  // br_table
  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
    }

    return readULEB(p).next;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    return readULEB(p).next;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    return p;
  }

  // local/global
  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    return readULEB(p).next;
  }

  // memory load/store family
  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    return p;
  }

  // memory.size / memory.grow
  if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
    return readULEB(p).next;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    while (true) {
      const b = buf[p++];
      if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }
    return p;
  }

  // i64.const
  if (op === 0x42) {
    while (true) {
      const b = buf[p++];
      if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }
    return p;
  }

  // f32.const
  if (op === 0x43) return p + 4;

  // f64.const
  if (op === 0x44) return p + 8;

  // ref.null
  if (op === 0xd0) {
    return p + 1;
  }

  // ref.func
  if (op === 0xd2) {
    return readULEB(p).next;
  }

  return p;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const results = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodies.length; bodyIndex++) {
  const body = bodies[bodyIndex];

  let p = body.start;

  // locals declaration
  const localInfo = readULEB(p);
  const localDeclCount = localInfo.value;
  p = localInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    p += 1;
  }

  while (p < body.end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const typeInfo = readULEB(p);
      p = typeInfo.next;

      const tableInfo = readULEB(p);
      p = tableInfo.next;

      if (typeInfo.value === TARGET_TYPE) {
        results.push({
          func: IMPORTED + bodyIndex,
          addr,
          table: tableInfo.value,
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    p = skipImmediate(op, p);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Output
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND TYPE 7 INDIRECT CALLS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("IMPORTED FUNCTIONS :", IMPORTED);
console.log("CODE BODIES :", bodyCount);
console.log("TARGET TYPE :", TARGET_TYPE);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log("TYPE 7 INDIRECT CALL COUNT :", results.length);
console.log("");

for (const r of results) {
  console.log(
    "FUNC",
    r.func,
    "@ 0x" + r.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
    "call_indirect type=7 table=" + r.table
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findType7IndirectWrappers.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 8952;
const TARGET_TYPE = 7;
const TARGET_INDIRECT_TYPE = 4;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE 7 INDIRECT WRAPPER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET FUNC:", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("TARGET TYPE:", TARGET_TYPE);
console.log("INDIRECT TYPE:", TARGET_INDIRECT_TYPE);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32LEB(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let start = p;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: result >>> 0,
                next: p,
                bytes: p - start
            };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("Invalid u32 LEB128");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF in u32 LEB128");
}

function readI32LEB(buf, p) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];

        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                result |= (~0 << shift);
            }

            return {
                value: result | 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        if (shift >= 35) {
            throw new Error("Invalid signed LEB128");
        }
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readVecCount(buf, p) {
    return readU32LEB(buf, p);
}

function skipLimits(buf, p) {
    const flags = buf[p++];

    const min = readU32LEB(buf, p);
    p = min.next;

    if (flags & 0x01) {
        const max = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        p = max.next;
    }

    return p;
}

function readSectionBounds(buf) {
    let p = 8;
    const sections = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        const size = sizeInfo.value;
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + size;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseTypes(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const types = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const form = buf[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        const paramInfo = readVecCount(buf, p);
        const paramCount = paramInfo.value;
        p = paramInfo.next;

        const params = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
            params.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        const resultInfo = readVecCount(buf, p);
        const resultCount = resultInfo.value;
        p = resultInfo.next;

        const results = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
            results.push(buf[p++]);
        }

        types.push({
            params,
            results
        });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseFunctionTypes(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const typeIndices = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const x = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        p = x.next;
        typeIndices.push(x.value);
    }

    return typeIndices;
}

function parseImports(buf, section) {
    let p = section.start;

    const countInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;
    p = countInfo.next;

    const importedFuncTypes = [];
    const imports = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const moduleLen = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        p = moduleLen.next;

        p += moduleLen.value;

        const nameLen = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        p = nameLen.next;

        p += nameLen.value;

        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const typeInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = typeInfo.next;

            importedFuncTypes.push(typeInfo.value);

            imports.push({
                kind: "func",
                typeIndex: typeInfo.value
            });
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            p += 1;
            p = skipLimits(buf, p);
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            p = skipLimits(buf, p);
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        } else {
            throw new Error("Unknown import kind " + kind);
        }
    }

    return {
        importedFuncTypes,
        imports
    };
}

function decodeInstruction(buf, p, end) {
    const start = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    const ins = {
        start,
        end: p,
        op,
        mnemonic: "unknown",
        immediates: {}
    };

    switch (op) {
        case 0x0b:
            ins.mnemonic = "end";
            break;

        case 0x20: {
            const x = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;
            ins.mnemonic = "local.get";
            ins.immediates.local = x.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
            const x = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;
            ins.mnemonic = "local.set";
            ins.immediates.local = x.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
            const x = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;
            ins.mnemonic = "local.tee";
            ins.immediates.local = x.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const x = readI32LEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.const";
            ins.immediates.value = x.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28: {
            const align = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = offset.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i32.load";
            ins.immediates.align = align.value;
            ins.immediates.offset = offset.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x2d: {
            const align = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = offset.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i32.load8_u";
            ins.immediates.align = align.value;
            ins.immediates.offset = offset.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x29: {
            const align = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = offset.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i64.load";
            ins.immediates.align = align.value;
            ins.immediates.offset = offset.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x36: {
            const align = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = offset.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i32.store";
            ins.immediates.align = align.value;
            ins.immediates.offset = offset.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x37: {
            const align = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = offset.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "i64.store";
            ins.immediates.align = align.value;
            ins.immediates.offset = offset.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const x = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;
            ins.mnemonic = "call";
            ins.immediates.func = x.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const typeInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = typeInfo.next;

            const tableInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = tableInfo.next;

            ins.mnemonic = "call_indirect";
            ins.immediates.type = typeInfo.value;
            ins.immediates.table = tableInfo.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x6a:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.add";
            break;

        case 0x6b:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.sub";
            break;

        case 0x46:
            ins.mnemonic = "i32.eq";
            break;

        case 0x1b:
            ins.mnemonic = "select";
            break;

        case 0x02:
            ins.mnemonic = "block";
            p += 1;
            break;

        case 0x03:
            ins.mnemonic = "loop";
            p += 1;
            break;

        case 0x04:
            ins.mnemonic = "if";
            p += 1;
            break;

        case 0x05:
            ins.mnemonic = "else";
            break;

        case 0x0c: {
            const x = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;
            ins.mnemonic = "br";
            ins.immediates.depth = x.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
            const x = readU32LEB(buf, p);
            p = x.next;
            ins.mnemonic = "br_if";
            ins.immediates.depth = x.value;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
            ins.mnemonic = "return";
            break;

        default:
            /*
             * Unknown opcode.
             *
             * We intentionally stop decoding this function rather than
             * pretending that the following bytes are instructions.
             */
            return null;
    }

    ins.end = p;

    if (p > end) {
        return null;
    }

    return ins;
}

function decodeBody(buf, bodyStart, bodyEnd) {
    let p = bodyStart;

    const sizeInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadEnd = p + sizeInfo.value;

    if (payloadEnd > bodyEnd) {
        return null;
    }

    const localDeclInfo = readU32LEB(buf, p);
    const localDeclCount = localDeclInfo.value;
    p = localDeclInfo.next;

    const locals = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
        const n = readU32LEB(buf, p);
        p = n.next;

        const type = buf[p++];

        locals.push({
            count: n.value,
            type
        });
    }

    const instructions = [];

    while (p < payloadEnd) {
        const ins = decodeInstruction(buf, p, payloadEnd);

        if (!ins) {
            return {
                instructions,
                failedAt: p,
                payloadEnd
            };
        }

        instructions.push(ins);
        p = ins.end;

        if (ins.mnemonic === "end" && p === payloadEnd) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return {
        instructions,
        failedAt: null,
        payloadEnd
    };
}

function getOpcodeName(ins) {
    return ins.mnemonic;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Locate sections
------------------------------------------------------------ */

const sections = readSectionBounds(wasm);

const typeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 1);
const importSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);
const functionSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!typeSection || !functionSection || !codeSection) {
    throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

const types = parseTypes(wasm, typeSection);
const functionTypesDefined = parseFunctionTypes(wasm, functionSection);

let importedFunctionTypes = [];

if (importSection) {
    importedFunctionTypes =
        parseImports(wasm, importSection).importedFuncTypes;
}

const functionTypes = [
    ...importedFunctionTypes,
    ...functionTypesDefined
];

console.log("TYPE COUNT :", types.length);
console.log("FUNCTION TYPE ENTRIES :", functionTypes.length);
console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS :", functionTypesDefined.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Decode code section
------------------------------------------------------------ */

let p = codeSection.start;

const bodyCountInfo = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
p = bodyCountInfo.next;

console.log("CODE BODY COUNT :", bodyCount);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const matches = [];
let decodeFailures = 0;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {
    const bodyStart = p;

    const bodySizeInfo = readU32LEB(wasm, p);
    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyEnd = p + bodySizeInfo.value;

    const funcIndex = importedFunctionTypes.length + bodyIndex;
    const typeIndex = functionTypes[funcIndex];

    if (typeIndex !== TARGET_TYPE) {
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    const decoded = decodeBody(wasm, bodyStart, bodyEnd);

    if (!decoded) {
        decodeFailures++;
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    if (decoded.failedAt !== null) {
        decodeFailures++;
    }

    const instructions = decoded.instructions;

    const indirect4 = instructions.filter(
        x =>
            x.mnemonic === "call_indirect" &&
            x.immediates.type === TARGET_INDIRECT_TYPE
    );

    if (indirect4.length === 0) {
        p = bodyEnd;
        continue;
    }

    matches.push({
        funcIndex,
        bodyIndex,
        typeIndex,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        instructions
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   Report
------------------------------------------------------------ */

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("TYPE 7 FUNCTIONS WITH TYPE 4 INDIRECT CALL :", matches.length);
console.log("DECODER FAILURES :", decodeFailures);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const m of matches) {
    console.log();
    console.log(
        `FUNC ${m.funcIndex} TYPE=${m.typeIndex} BODY=${m.bodyIndex}`
    );

    console.log(
        `BODY : 0x${m.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${m.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    let stack = [];

    for (const ins of m.instructions) {
        const off =
            "0x" +
            ins.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");

        let detail = "";

        if (ins.mnemonic === "local.get") {
            detail = ` local=${ins.immediates.local}`;
            stack.push({
                kind: "local",
                value: ins.immediates.local
            });
        }

        else if (ins.mnemonic === "i32.load") {
            detail =
                ` align=${ins.immediates.align}` +
                ` offset=${ins.immediates.offset}`;

            const base = stack.length
                ? stack.pop()
                : null;

            stack.push({
                kind: "load",
                base,
                offset: ins.immediates.offset
            });
        }

        else if (ins.mnemonic === "i32.load8_u") {
            detail =
                ` align=${ins.immediates.align}` +
                ` offset=${ins.immediates.offset}`;

            const base = stack.length
                ? stack.pop()
                : null;

            stack.push({
                kind: "load8",
                base,
                offset: ins.immediates.offset
            });
        }

        else if (ins.mnemonic === "i32.const") {
            detail = ` value=${ins.immediates.value}`;

            stack.push({
                kind: "const",
                value: ins.immediates.value
            });
        }

        else if (ins.mnemonic === "i32.add") {
            const b = stack.pop();
            const a = stack.pop();

            stack.push({
                kind: "add",
                a,
                b
            });
        }

        else if (ins.mnemonic === "call_indirect") {
            detail =
                ` type=${ins.immediates.type}` +
                ` table=${ins.immediates.table}`;

            const tableIndex =
                stack.length
                    ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                    : null;

            console.log(
                `  ${off} ${ins.mnemonic}${detail}`
            );

            console.log(
                "    STACK-BEFORE:",
                JSON.stringify(stack)
            );

            console.log(
                "    TABLE-INDEX-CANDIDATE:",
                JSON.stringify(tableIndex)
            );

            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            `  ${off} ${ins.mnemonic}${detail}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET FUNCTION CHECK");
console.log("============================================================");

const target = matches.find(x => x.funcIndex === TARGET_FUNC);

if (!target) {
    console.log("FUNC 8952 NOT FOUND IN MATCH SET");
} else {
    console.log("FUNC 8952 FOUND : YES");
    console.log("TYPE :", target.typeIndex);
    console.log(
        "BODY :",
        "0x" + target.bodyStart.toString(16),
        "..",
        "0x" + target.bodyEnd.toString(16)
    );
    console.log("INDIRECT TYPE 4 : YES");
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;

let typeStart = -1;
let typeEnd = -1;

while (p < b.length) {

  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  p = z.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + z.v;

  if (id === 1) {
    typeStart = start;
    typeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (typeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Type section not found");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE INDEX SEARCH FOR FUNCTION 5079 v49");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Type section : ${hex(typeStart)} .. ${hex(typeEnd)}`);
console.log("");

let q = typeStart;

const countR = uleb(q);
const count = countR.v;
q = countR.p;

let matches = 0;

for (let typeIndex = 0; typeIndex < count; typeIndex++) {

  const form = b[q++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    console.log(
      `type=${typeIndex} unsupported form=0x${form.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  const paramR = uleb(q);
  const paramCount = paramR.v;
  q = paramR.p;

  const params: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < paramCount; i++)
    params.push(b[q++]);

  const resultR = uleb(q);
  const resultCount = resultR.v;
  q = resultR.p;

  const results: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < resultCount; i++)
    results.push(b[q++]);

  if (
    paramCount === 4 &&
    params.every(x => x === 0x7f) &&
    resultCount === 1 &&
    results[0] === 0x7f
  ) {

    matches++;

    console.log("MATCH");
    console.log(`  type index : ${typeIndex}`);
    console.log(`  params     : 4 × i32`);
    console.log(`  result     : i32`);
    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL MATCHING TYPES : ${matches}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END V49");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 4789; // 0x12b5

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40))
        value -= 2 ** (shift + 7);

      return { value, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p];

  const s = readSLEB(p + 1);
  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
  throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Read defined functions
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let q = codeStart;

const count = readSLEB(q);
q = count.next;

console.log("VALUE 4789 STORE SEARCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET VALUE : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`TARGET HEX   : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`CODE         : ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let hits = 0;

for (let func = 0; func < count.value; func++) {

  const sz = readSLEB(q);
  const bodyStart = sz.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sz.value;

  for (let i = bodyStart; i < bodyEnd; i++) {

    // i32.const opcode
    if (b[i] !== 0x41)
      continue;

    const v = readSLEB(i + 1);

    if (v.value !== TARGET)
      continue;

    hits++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("############################################################");
    console.log(`DEFINED ORDINAL : ${func}`);
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC     : ${func + 83}`);
    console.log(`CONST           : ${hex(i)}`);
    console.log(`VALUE           : ${v.value}`);
    console.log(`BODY            : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd - 1)}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const from = Math.max(bodyStart, i - 24);
    const to = Math.min(bodyEnd, v.next + 48);

    console.log("RAW CONTEXT:");

    for (let x = from; x < to; x += 16) {
      const e = Math.min(x + 16, to);

      console.log(
        `${hex(x)}  ` +
        [...b.slice(x, e)]
          .map(z => z.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
          .join(" ")
      );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Look for store opcodes after the constant
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("NEARBY STORE CANDIDATES:");

    let storeFound = false;

    for (let x = v.next; x < Math.min(v.next + 64, bodyEnd); x++) {

      // i32.store
      if (b[x] === 0x36) {
        storeFound = true;

        console.log(
          `STORE @ ${hex(x)}  ` +
          `opcode=0x36  ` +
          `possible distance=${x - i}`
        );
      }

      // i64.store
      if (b[x] === 0x37) {
        storeFound = true;

        console.log(
          `STORE64 @ ${hex(x)}  ` +
          `opcode=0x37  ` +
          `possible distance=${x - i}`
        );
      }
    }

    if (!storeFound)
      console.log("NONE");

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log("NOTE: CONST 4789 alone does NOT prove a write to S2.");
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL VALUE 4789 HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

const ROOT = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin";

const EXT = new Set([
    ".js",
    ".mjs",
    ".cjs",
    ".ts",
    ".html",
]);

const SKIP = new Set([
    "node_modules",
    ".git",
    "dist",
    "build",
    "coverage",
    ".cache",
    "temp",
]);

const TARGETS = [
    "WebAssembly.instantiate",
    "WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming",
    "WebAssembly.Module",
    "WebAssembly.Instance",
    "imports",
    "a.ga",
    "ga:",
    '"ga"',
    "'ga'",
    "a:",
];

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
    const out: string[] = [];

    let entries: fs.Dirent[];
    try {
        entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
    } catch {
        return out;
    }

    for (const e of entries) {
        if (SKIP.has(e.name)) continue;

        const full = path.join(dir, e.name);

        if (e.isDirectory()) {
            out.push(...walk(full));
        } else if (e.isFile() && EXT.has(path.extname(e.name).toLowerCase())) {
            out.push(full);
        }
    }

    return out;
}

function printContext(lines: string[], hit: number) {
    const from = Math.max(0, hit - 5);
    const to = Math.min(lines.length, hit + 6);

    for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
        console.log(
            `${String(i + 1).padStart(6)} | ${lines[i]}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" WASM IMPORT OBJECT SEARCH v32");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

const files = walk(ROOT);

console.log(`Files scanned: ${files.length}`);
console.log();

let hits = 0;

for (const file of files) {
    let text: string;

    try {
        text = fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8");
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const line = lines[i];

        const matched = TARGETS.filter(t => line.includes(t));

        if (matched.length === 0) continue;

        hits++;

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(file);
        console.log(`line ${i + 1}`);
        console.log(`MATCH: ${matched.join(", ")}`);
        printContext(lines, i);
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL MATCHES: ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

const ROOT = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin";

const EXT = new Set([
    ".js",
    ".mjs",
    ".cjs",
    ".ts",
    ".html",
]);

const SKIP = new Set([
    "node_modules",
    ".git",
    "dist",
    "build",
    "coverage",
    ".cache",
    "temp",
]);

const WASM_PATTERNS = [
    "WebAssembly.instantiate",
    "WebAssembly.instantiateStreaming",
    "WebAssembly.Instance",
    "WebAssembly.Module",
];

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
    const result: string[] = [];

    let entries: fs.Dirent[];

    try {
        entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
    } catch {
        return result;
    }

    for (const entry of entries) {
        if (SKIP.has(entry.name)) continue;

        const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);

        if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            result.push(...walk(full));
        } else if (
            entry.isFile() &&
            EXT.has(path.extname(entry.name).toLowerCase())
        ) {
            result.push(full);
        }
    }

    return result;
}

function printContext(lines: string[], index: number) {
    const start = Math.max(0, index - 12);
    const end = Math.min(lines.length, index + 25);

    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
        console.log(
            `${String(i + 1).padStart(7)} | ${lines[i]}`
        );
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");

console.log("============================================================");

const files = walk(ROOT);

console.log(`Files scanned: ${files.length}`);
console.log();

let total = 0;

for (const file of files) {
    let source: string;

    try {
        source = fs.readFileSync(file, "utf8");
    } catch {
        continue;
    }

    const lines = source.split(/\r?\n/);

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const matches = WASM_PATTERNS.filter(p => lines[i].includes(p));

        if (matches.length === 0) continue;

        total++;

        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(file);
        console.log(`line ${i + 1}`);
        console.log(`MATCH: ${matches.join(", ")}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        printContext(lines, i);
    }
}
