====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findWrapperCallers.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const FILE = path.resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const IMPORTS = 83;
const TARGETS = [4098, 295, 83, 2528, 2529];

function readULEB(buf, s) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (s.pos < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[s.pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) return value;

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 49) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function encodeULEB(value) {
    const bytes = [];

    do {
        let b = value % 128;
        value = Math.floor(value / 128);

        if (value !== 0) b |= 0x80;
        bytes.push(b);
    } while (value !== 0);

    return bytes;
}

function hex(value) {
    return "0x" + value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readBodies(buf) {
    const s = { pos: 8 };

    while (s.pos < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[s.pos++];
        const size = readULEB(buf, s);
        const start = s.pos;
        const end = start + size;

        if (id === 10) {
            const cs = { pos: start };
            const count = readULEB(buf, cs);
            const bodies = [];

            for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {
                const bodySize = readULEB(buf, cs);
                const bodyStart = cs.pos;
                const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

                if (bodyEnd > end) {
                    throw new Error("Function body outside code section");
                }

                bodies.push({
                    fn: IMPORTS + ordinal,
                    start: bodyStart,
                    end: bodyEnd,
                    size: bodySize
                });

                cs.pos = bodyEnd;
            }

            return bodies;
        }

        s.pos = end;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function findRaw(buf, start, end, pattern) {
    const hits = [];

    for (let p = start; p + pattern.length <= end; p++) {
        let matched = true;

        for (let i = 0; i < pattern.length; i++) {
            if (buf[p + i] !== pattern[i]) {
                matched = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (matched) hits.push(p);
    }

    return hits;
}

function dump(buf, start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const lineEnd = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [];

        for (let i = p; i < lineEnd; i++) {
            bytes.push(buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
    }
}

function main() {
    const buf = fs.readFileSync(FILE);
    const bodies = readBodies(buf);

    console.log("WRAPPER CALLER SEARCH");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${buf.length}`);
    console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${bodies.length}`);

    for (const target of TARGETS) {
        const pattern = [0x10, ...encodeULEB(target)];

        console.log("");
        console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC ${target}`);
        console.log(`RAW PATTERN = ${pattern.map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        let total = 0;

        for (const body of bodies) {
            const hits = findRaw(buf, body.start, body.end, pattern);

            for (const hit of hits) {
                total++;

                console.log(
                    `OWNER FUNC ${body.fn}` +
                    ` | BODY ${hex(body.start)}..${hex(body.end)}` +
                    ` | SIZE ${body.size}` +
                    ` | HIT ${hex(hit)}`
                );

                const start = Math.max(body.start, hit - 32);
                const end = Math.min(body.end, hit + 48);

                dump(buf, start, end);
                console.log("");
            }
        }

        console.log(`RAW HIT COUNT = ${total}`);
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("DONE - NO WASM EXECUTION");
    console.log("Raw matches are candidates; validate instruction boundaries.");
}

try {
    main();
} catch (err) {
    console.error("FAILED:", err);
    process.exitCode = 1;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findWrites19534c.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readConst(pos: number) {
  const x = uleb(pos);
  return x;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND WRITES TO 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

// Search exact raw i32.const TARGET followed later by store.
// This intentionally keeps context small.
for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 10; p++) {

  if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) continue;

  const c = readConst(p + 1);

  if (c.value !== TARGET) continue;

  const from = p;
  const to = Math.min(wasm.length, p + 40);

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`CONST AT : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`VALUE    : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);

  let q = c.next;

  while (q < to) {

    const opPos = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
      console.log(
        `STORE    : 0x${opPos.toString(16)} opcode=0x${op.toString(16)}`
      );
      hits++;
      break;
    }

    // skip common immediates
    if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x10 ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x41 ||
      op === 0x42
    ) {
      q = uleb(q).next;
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`POTENTIAL WRITE HITS : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findWrites19534cExact.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function decodeConst(pos: number) {
  if (wasm[pos] !== 0x41) return null;
  const x = uleb(pos + 1);
  return x.value === TARGET ? x.next : null;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND EXACT WRITE CANDIDATES FOR 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 10; p++) {

  if (wasm[p] !== 0x41) continue;

  const afterConst = decodeConst(p);
  if (afterConst === null) continue;

  /*
   * Only accept:
   *
   *   i32.const 0x19534C
   *   i32.store [offset=0]
   *
   * with only harmless stack/local operations between them.
   */

  let q = afterConst;
  let depth = 1;
  let steps = 0;

  while (q < wasm.length && steps < 12) {
    const pos = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const a = uleb(q);
      q = a.next;

      const o = uleb(q);
      q = o.next;

      if (a.value === 2 && o.value === 0) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`CONST : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
        console.log(`STORE : 0x${pos.toString(16)}`);
        console.log("PATTERN: i32.const TARGET -> i32.store offset=0");
        hits++;
      }

      break;
    }

    /*
     * Operations which consume/change the address.
     * Therefore this is NOT a direct write anymore.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x6a || // i32.add
      op === 0x6b || // i32.sub
      op === 0x6c || // mul
      op === 0x6d || // div
      op === 0x74 || // shl
      op === 0x75 || // shr_s
      op === 0x76 || // shr_u
      op === 0x77 || // rotl
      op === 0x78    // rotr
    ) {
      break;
    }

    /*
     * Another const means the target address may no longer
     * be the store address.
     */
    if (op === 0x41 || op === 0x42) break;

    steps++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`EXACT DIRECT WRITES : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\findWrites195354.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x00195354;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND WRITES TO 0x00195354");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payload = pos;
    const end = payload + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = payload;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);

let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const definedCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${definedCount}`);
console.log(`GLOBAL RANGE = 83 .. ${83 + definedCount - 1}`);

console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS = ${hex(TARGET)}`);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

type Hit = {
    func: number;
    ordinal: number;
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    memOffset: number;
    baseConstOffset: number | null;
};

const hits: Hit[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedCount; ordinal++) {

    const globalFunc = 83 + ordinal;

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    let x = bodyStart;

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // local declarations
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    const localInfo = readULEB(x);
    const localGroups = localInfo.value;
    x = localInfo.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {
        const c = readULEB(x);
        x = c.next;
        x++;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Scan body
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    while (x < bodyEnd) {

        const opOffset = x;
        const op = wasm[x++];

        // local/global
        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22 ||
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // memory load/store
        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {

            const align = readULEB(x);
            x = align.next;

            const offsetInfo = readULEB(x);
            const memOffset = offsetInfo.value;
            x = offsetInfo.next;

            // STORE operations:
            // 0x36 i32.store
            // 0x37 i64.store
            // 0x38 f32.store
            // 0x39 f64.store
            // 0x3a i32.store8
            // 0x3b i32.store16
            // 0x3c i64.store8
            // 0x3d i64.store16
            // 0x3e i64.store32

            if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {

                // We can directly identify absolute target
                // when the encoded memory offset itself is TARGET.

                if (memOffset === TARGET) {

                    hits.push({
                        func: globalFunc,
                        ordinal,
                        offset: opOffset,
                        opcode: op,
                        memOffset,
                        baseConstOffset: null
                    });
                }

                // Also detect the common pattern:
                //
                // i32.const TARGET
                // i32.store offset=0
                //
                // This requires looking backward, handled below
                // through a raw byte window.
            }

            continue;
        }

        // constants
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            while (true) {
                const b = wasm[x++];
                if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
            }
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x43) {
            x += 4;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x44) {
            x += 8;
            continue;
        }

        // branch
        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // br_table
        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const c = readULEB(x);
            const count = c.value;
            x = c.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const r = readULEB(x);
                x = r.next;
            }

            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            continue;
        }

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;
            x++;
            continue;
        }

        // prefixed instructions
        if (op === 0xfc) {

            const sub = readULEB(x);
            x = sub.next;

            if (
                sub.value === 8 ||
                sub.value === 9 ||
                sub.value === 10 ||
                sub.value === 11
            ) {
                const a = readULEB(x);
                x = a.next;

                const b = readULEB(x);
                x = b.next;
            }

            continue;
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Second pass: exact byte-pattern search
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("DIRECT MEMORY-OFFSET HITS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const h of hits) {

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${h.func}` +
        ` | ordinal ${h.ordinal}` +
        ` | @ ${hex(h.offset)}` +
        ` | opcode=0x${h.opcode.toString(16)}` +
        ` | offset=${hex(h.memOffset)}`
    );
}

console.log(`COUNT = ${hits.length}`);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("RAW ULEB / I32.CONST PATTERN HITS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let rawHits = 0;

// ULEB encoding of 0x195354
// Search for the exact i32.const immediate sequence:
//
// 41 d4 a6 e5 00
//
// which we already observed in FUNC 91.

const pattern = [0x41, 0xd4, 0xa6, 0xe5, 0x00];

for (let i = codeStart; i <= codeEnd - pattern.length; i++) {

    let match = true;

    for (let j = 0; j < pattern.length; j++) {

        if (wasm[i + j] !== pattern[j]) {
            match = false;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (!match) continue;

    rawHits++;

    // Find containing function
    let q = codeStart;
    const ci = readULEB(q);
    q = ci.next;

    let foundFunc = -1;
    let foundOrdinal = -1;
    let foundBodyStart = -1;
    let foundBodyEnd = -1;

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedCount; ordinal++) {

        const s = readULEB(q);
        const bodySize = s.value;
        q = s.next;

        const start = q;
        const end = start + bodySize;

        if (i >= start && i < end) {

            foundFunc = 83 + ordinal;
            foundOrdinal = ordinal;
            foundBodyStart = start;
            foundBodyEnd = end;
            break;
        }

        q = end;
    }

    console.log(
        `HIT @ ${hex(i)}` +
        ` | FUNC ${foundFunc}` +
        ` | ordinal ${foundOrdinal}`
    );

    const from = Math.max(foundBodyStart, i - 32);
    const to = Math.min(foundBodyEnd, i + 48);

    for (let z = from; z < to; z += 16) {

        const end = Math.min(z + 16, to);

        let bytes = "";

        for (let k = z; k < end; k++) {
            bytes += wasm[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
        }

        console.log(`${hex(z)}  ${bytes}`);
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
}

console.log(`PATTERN COUNT = ${rawHits}`);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\findZeroBaseStore40.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p: number) {
    let v = 0, s = 0, x = 0;
    do {
        x = b[p++];
        v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
        s += 7;
    } while (x & 0x80);
    return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function hx(x: number) {
    return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const r = u(p);
    p = r.next;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = p;
        break;
    }

    p += r.value;
}

p = codeStart;

const cr = u(p);
const count = cr.value;
p = cr.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FIND ZERO-BASE / MEM[0x28] WRITERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let total = 0;

for (let ord = 0; ord < count; ord++) {

    const br = u(p);
    const bodySize = br.value;
    p = br.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    const lr = u(p);
    const groups = lr.value;
    p = lr.next;

    const localTypes: number[] = [];

    for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
        const nr = u(p);
        const n = nr.value;
        p = nr.next;

        const type = b[p++];

        for (let k = 0; k < n; k++)
            localTypes.push(type);
    }

    // Scan instruction stream.
    for (let q = p; q < bodyEnd - 4; q++) {

        // i32.const 0
        if (b[q] === 0x41 && b[q + 1] === 0x00) {

            // Search shortly forward for i32.store offset=40
            for (let z = q + 2; z < Math.min(q + 12, bodyEnd - 4); z++) {

                if (b[z] !== 0x36) continue;

                const r = u(z + 2);

                if (r.value !== 40) continue;

                console.log(
                    "CONST ZERO -> STORE40",
                    "FUNC", ord + 83,
                    "@", hx(z)
                );

                const a = Math.max(bodyStart, q - 4);
                const e = Math.min(bodyEnd, z + 7);

                console.log(
                    " RAW:",
                    [...b.subarray(a, e)]
                        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
                        .join(" ")
                );
                console.log();

                total++;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("ZERO-CONST STORE40 CANDIDATES :", total);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\focus446CallSites.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const path =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(path);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET = 446;

function u32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        v |= (x & 127) << s;
        if (!(x & 128))
            return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
        s += 7;
    }
}

function s32(p) {
    let v = 0, s = 0;
    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];
        v |= (x & 127) << s;
        if (!(x & 128)) {
            if (s < 32 && (x & 64))
                v |= (~0 << (s + 7));
            return { value: v | 0, next: p };
        }
        s += 7;
    }
}

function skipBlockType(p) {
    const x = b[p];
    if (x === 0x40 || x === 0x7f ||
        x === 0x7e || x === 0x7d ||
        x === 0x7c)
        return p + 1;
    return s32(p).next;
}

function skip(p) {
    const op = b[p++];

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04)
        return skipBlockType(p);

    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x0e) {
        let n = u32(p);
        p = n.next;
        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
            p = u32(p).next;
        return u32(p).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x10)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x11) {
        let a = u32(p);
        let c = u32(a.next);
        return c.next;
    }

    if (op >= 0x20 && op <= 0x24)
        return u32(p).next;

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        let a = u32(p);
        return u32(a.next).next;
    }

    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40)
        return p + 1;

    if (op === 0x41)
        return s32(p).next;

    if (op === 0x42) {
        while (true) {
            const x = b[p++];
            if (!(x & 128))
                return p;
        }
    }

    if (op === 0x43)
        return p + 4;

    if (op === 0x44)
        return p + 8;

    return p;
}

function fmt(p) {
    const op = b[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = u32(p + 1);
        return [`local.get ${r.value}`, r.next];
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = u32(p + 1);
        return [`local.set ${r.value}`, r.next];
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = u32(p + 1);
        return [`local.tee ${r.value}`, r.next];
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = s32(p + 1);
        return [`i32.const ${r.value}`, r.next];
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = u32(p + 1);
        return [`call ${r.value}`, r.next];
    }

    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x36) {
        const a = u32(p + 1);
        const c = u32(a.next);
        return [
            `memop 0x${op.toString(16)} align=${a.value} offset=${c.value}`,
            c.next
        ];
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const a = u32(p + 1);
        const c = u32(a.next);
        return [
            `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${c.value}`,
            c.next
        ];
    }

    return [`opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`, p + 1];
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Parse code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
const bodies = [];

while (p < b.length) {

    const section = b[p++];

    const z = u32(p);
    p = z.next;

    const end = p + z.value;

    if (section === 10) {

        const n = u32(p);
        p = n.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {

            const bodyStart = p;

            const sz = u32(p);
            p = sz.next;

            const bodyEnd = p + sz.value;

            const locals = u32(p);
            p = locals.next;

            for (let j = 0; j < locals.value; j++) {
                const count = u32(p);
                p = count.next + 1;
            }

            bodies.push({
                func: IMPORTED + i,
                instr: p,
                end: bodyEnd
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }
    }

    p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find CALL 446 and print focused context.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FOCUSED FUNC 446 CALL SITES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (const f of bodies) {

    let q = f.instr;

    while (q < f.end) {

        const op = b[q];

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const r = u32(q + 1);

            if (r.value === TARGET) {

                hits++;

                console.log("");
                console.log(
                    `FUNC ${f.func}  CALL 446 @ 0x${q.toString(16)}`
                );
                console.log("--------------------------------------------");

                // Show ~12 instructions before and 5 after.
                const ins = [];

                let s = f.instr;

                while (s < q) {
                    const [text, next] = fmt(s);
                    ins.push({
                        pos: s,
                        text,
                        next
                    });
                    s = next;
                }

                const start = Math.max(0, ins.length - 12);

                for (let i = start; i < ins.length; i++) {
                    const x = ins[i];
                    console.log(
                        `0x${x.pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${x.text}`
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  CALL 446`
                );

                let npos = r.next;

                for (let i = 0; i < 5 && npos < f.end; i++) {
                    const [text, next] = fmt(npos);
                    console.log(
                        `0x${npos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${text}`
                    );
                    npos = next;
                }
            }

            q = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        q = skip(q);
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL CALL 446 SITES : ${hits}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\focusFunction3837Tail.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const START = 0x00195d80;
const END   = 0x00195ef1;

function readULEB(buf, pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(buf, pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (b & 0x40)
    value -= 2 ** shift;

  return { value, next: pos };
}

function decodeAt(pos) {
  const addr = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  let x;

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    x = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    return {
      next: x.next,
      text:
        (op === 0x20 ? "local.get " :
         op === 0x21 ? "local.set " :
         "local.tee ") + x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
    x = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    return {
      next: x.next,
      text:
        (op === 0x23 ? "global.get " : "global.set ") +
        x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    x = readSLEB(wasm, pos);
    return {
      next: x.next,
      text: "i32.const " + x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    x = readSLEB(wasm, pos);
    return {
      next: x.next,
      text: "i64.const " + x.value
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x3a ||
    op === 0x3b
  ) {
    const a = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const o = readULEB(wasm, a.next);

    const names = {
      0x28: "i32.load",
      0x29: "i64.load",
      0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
      0x36: "i32.store",
      0x37: "i64.store",
      0x3a: "i32.store8",
      0x3b: "i32.store16"
    };

    return {
      next: o.next,
      text:
        names[op] +
        " align=" + a.value +
        " offset=" + o.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    x = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    return {
      next: x.next,
      text: "call " + x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = readULEB(wasm, pos);
    const table = readULEB(wasm, t.next);

    return {
      next: table.next,
      text:
        "call_indirect type=" +
        t.value +
        " table=" +
        table.value
    };
  }

  const simple = {
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0f: "return"
  };

  if (simple[op]) {
    return {
      next: pos,
      text: simple[op]
    };
  }

  return {
    next: pos,
    text: "opcode=0x" + op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
  };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FOCUS FUNCTION 3837 TAIL");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RANGE : 0x00195d80 .. 0x00195ef1");

let pos = START;

while (pos < END) {
  const r = decodeAt(pos);

  console.log(
    "0x" +
    pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
    "  " +
    r.text
  );

  if (r.next <= pos)
    break;

  pos = r.next;
}

FILE: scripts\focusHighOffset128.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const MIN_FUNC = 8950;
const TARGET_OFFSET = 128;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: result >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  do {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    result |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readName(pos) {
  const x = readULEB(pos);
  return { next: x.next + x.value };
}

/*
 * Sections
 */
let p = 8;

let importSection = null;
let functionSection = null;
let codeSection = null;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const s = readULEB(p);

  const start = s.next;
  const end = start + s.value;

  if (id === 2)
    importSection = { start, end };

  if (id === 3)
    functionSection = { start, end };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSection = { start, end };

  p = end;
}

/*
 * Imported functions
 */
p = importSection.start;

let x = readULEB(p);
const importCount = x.value;
p = x.next;

let importedFunctions = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < importCount; i++) {

  p = readName(p).next;
  p = readName(p).next;

  const kind = wasm[p++];

  if (kind === 0) {

    p = readULEB(p).next;
    importedFunctions++;

  } else if (kind === 1) {

    p++;

    const flags = wasm[p++];

    p = readULEB(p).next;

    if (flags & 1)
      p = readULEB(p).next;

  } else if (kind === 2) {

    const flags = readULEB(p);
    p = flags.next;

    p = readULEB(p).next;

    if (flags.value & 1)
      p = readULEB(p).next;

  } else if (kind === 3) {

    p++;
    p++;
  }
}

/*
 * Function section
 */
p = functionSection.start;

const fc = readULEB(p);
const definedCount = fc.value;
p = fc.next;

const functionTypes = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {

  const t = readULEB(p);

  functionTypes.push(t.value);

  p = t.next;
}

/*
 * Code section
 */
p = codeSection.start;

const bc = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = bc.value;
p = bc.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {

  const bs = readULEB(p);

  const size = bs.value;
  const start = bs.next;
  const end = start + size;

  bodies.push({
    func: importedFunctions + i,
    start,
    end
  });

  p = end;
}

/*
 * Decode
 */
function decodeOne(pos) {

  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  if (op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22) {

    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      local: x.value,
      text:
        op === 0x20
          ? `local.get ${x.value}`
          : op === 0x21
          ? `local.set ${x.value}`
          : `local.tee ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {

    const x = readSLEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      value: x.value,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {

    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      func: x.value,
      text: `call ${x.value}`
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {

    const t = readULEB(pos);
    const table = readULEB(t.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: table.next,
      op,
      type: t.value,
      text:
        `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
    };
  }

  /*
   * Loads
   */
  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {

    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: o.next,
      op,
      offset: o.value,
      text:
        `load opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
    };
  }

  /*
   * Stores
   */
  if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {

    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: o.next,
      op,
      offset: o.value,
      isStore: true,
      text:
        `store opcode=0x${op.toString(16)} ` +
        `align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
    };
  }

  /*
   * branches
   */
  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {

    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      text:
        (op === 0x0c ? "br " : "br_if ") + x.value
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {

    const n = readULEB(pos);
    pos = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++)
      pos = readULEB(pos).next;

    pos = readULEB(pos).next;

    return {
      start,
      end: pos,
      op,
      text: "br_table"
    };
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text:
      `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  };
}

function decodeFunction(body) {

  let p = body.start;

  const locals = readULEB(p);
  p = locals.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {

    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    p++;
  }

  const code = [];

  while (p < body.end) {

    const ins = decodeOne(p);

    code.push(ins);

    p = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x0b)
      break;
  }

  return code;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Main analysis
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("HIGH FUNC OFFSET 128 ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("FUNCTION RANGE :", MIN_FUNC, "..", 9480);
console.log("TARGET STORE OFFSET :", TARGET_OFFSET);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let total = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  if (body.func < MIN_FUNC)
    continue;

  const code = decodeFunction(body);

  for (let i = 0; i < code.length; i++) {

    const ins = code[i];

    if (!ins.isStore || ins.offset !== TARGET_OFFSET)
      continue;

    total++;

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(
      `FUNC ${body.func} @ ${hex(ins.start)}`
    );
    console.log("TYPE :",
      functionTypes[body.func - importedFunctions] ??
      "unknown"
    );
    console.log("STORE :", ins.text);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    /*
     * Print 24 instructions before store.
     */
    const start = Math.max(0, i - 24);

    for (let j = start; j <= i; j++) {

      const x = code[j];

      let marker = "  ";

      if (j === i)
        marker = ">>";

      if (x.op === 0x41 && x.value === 2053)
        marker = "!!";

      console.log(
        marker,
        hex(x.start),
        x.text
      );
    }

    /*
     * Detect direct 2053 nearby.
     */
    let direct2053 = false;

    for (
      let j = Math.max(0, i - 24);
      j <= i;
      j++
    ) {
      if (
        code[j].op === 0x41 &&
        code[j].value === 2053
      ) {
        direct2053 = true;
      }
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
      "DIRECT 2053 NEAR STORE :",
      direct2053 ? "YES" : "NO"
    );
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const sid = wasm[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.next;

        if (sid === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + size.value
            };
        }

        p += size.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

const IMPORTED = 83;

// GLOBAL DEFINITIONS
// ============================================================

const gs = section(6);

let p = gs.start;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

interface G {
    index: number;
    type: number;
    mutable: boolean;
    init: string;
}

const globals: G[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

    const type = wasm[p++];
    const mut = wasm[p++];

    const op = wasm[p++];

    let init = "UNKNOWN";

    if (op === 0x41) {

        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        init = `i32.const ${x.value} (${hex(x.value)})`;

    } else if (op === 0x42) {

        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        init = `i64.const ${x.value}`;

    } else if (op === 0x43) {

        p += 4;
        init = "f32.const";

    } else if (op === 0x44) {

        p += 8;
        init = "f64.const";

    } else if (op === 0x23) {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        init = `global.get ${x.value}`;

    }

    while (p < gs.end && wasm[p] !== 0x0b) {
        p++;
    }

    if (wasm[p] === 0x0b) {
        p++;
    }

    globals.push({
        index: i,
        type,
        mutable: mut === 1,
        init
    });
}

const cs = section(10);

let cp = cs.start;

cp = bodyCount.next;

interface Body {

    start: number;
    end: number;
    instrStart: number;

const bodies: Body[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount.value; ordinal++) {

    const size = uleb(cp);
    cp = size.next;

    const start = cp;
    const end = cp + size.value;

    let p = start;

    const locals = uleb(p);
    p = locals.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;
        p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + ordinal,
        start,
        end,
        instrStart: p
    });

    cp = end;
}

    let sets = 0;

    const getLocations: string[] = [];
    const setLocations: string[] = [];

    for (const body of bodies) {

        let q = body.instrStart;

        while (q < body.end) {

            const off = q;
            const op = wasm[q++];

            if (op === 0x23) {

                const x = uleb(q);
                q = x.next;

                if (x.value === g.index) {

                    gets++;

                    if (getLocations.length < 10) {
                        getLocations.push(
                            `FUNC ${body.func} @ ${hex(off)}`
                        );
                    }
                }

                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x24) {

                const x = uleb(q);
                q = x.next;

                if (x.value === g.index) {

                    sets++;

                    if (setLocations.length < 20) {
                        setLocations.push(
                            `FUNC ${body.func} @ ${hex(off)}`
                        );
                    }
                }

                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x41) {
                q = sleb(q).next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x42) {
                q = sleb(q).next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x43) {
                q += 4;
                continue;

            if (op === 0x44) {
                q += 8;
                continue;

                op === 0x20 ||
                op === 0x21 ||
                op === 0x22

                q = uleb(q).next;
                continue;

            if (op === 0x10) {
                q = uleb(q).next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x11) {
                q = uleb(q).next;
                q++;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
                q = uleb(q).next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x0e) {

                const n = uleb(q);
                q = n.next;

                for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                    q = uleb(q).next;
                }

                continue;
            }

            if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
                q = uleb(q).next;
                q = uleb(q).next;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
                q++;
                continue;
            }

            if (op === 0xfc || op === 0xfd) {
                q = uleb(q).next;
                continue;
            }
        }
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`GLOBAL ${g.index}`);
    console.log(`TYPE        = ${g.type}`);
    console.log(`MUTABLE     = YES`);
    console.log(`INITIALIZER = ${g.init}`);
    console.log(`GET COUNT   = ${gets}`);
    console.log(`SET COUNT   = ${sets}`);
    console.log("");

    console.log("SET LOCATIONS");
    for (const x of setLocations) {
        console.log(`  ${x}`);
    }

    if (sets > setLocations.length) {
        console.log(
            `  ... ${sets - setLocations.length} more`
        );
    }

    console.log("");

    console.log("GET LOCATIONS (first 10)");
    for (const x of getLocations) {
        console.log(`  ${x}`);
    }

    console.log("");
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_TYPE = 7;
const CONTEXT = 12;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let codeSection = null;

while (pos < buf.length) {
  const id = buf[pos++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
  pos = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = pos;
  const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeSection = { start, end };
    break;
  }

  pos = end;
}

if (!codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found.");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// BODY INDEX
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = codeSection.start;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

  bodies.push({ start, end });

  p = end;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SIMPLE DECODER
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function decodeOne(op, p) {
  let text = `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    return {
      text: `local.get ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    return {
      text: `local.set ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    return {
      text: `local.tee ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x23) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    return {
      text: `global.get ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x24) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    return {
      text: `global.set ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    return {
      text: `call ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = readULEB(p);
    p = t.next;

    const table = readULEB(p);
    p = table.next;

    return {
      text: `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
      next: p
    };
  }

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = readULEB(p);
    p = offset.next;

    let name = "memory";

    if (op === 0x28) name = "i32.load";
    else if (op === 0x29) name = "i64.load";
    else if (op === 0x2c) name = "i32.load8_s";
    else if (op === 0x2d) name = "i32.load8_u";
    else if (op === 0x36) name = "i32.store";
    else if (op === 0x37) name = "i64.store";
    else if (op === 0x3a) name = "i32.store8";

    return {
      text: `${name} align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`,
      next: p
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = readSLEB(p);

    return {
      text: `i32.const ${r.value}`,
      next: r.next
    };
  }

  const names = {
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u"
  };

  if (names[op]) {
    return {
      text: names[op],
      next: p
    };
  }

  return {
    text,
    next: p
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SCAN EACH FUNCTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const results = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodies.length; bodyIndex++) {
  const body = bodies[bodyIndex];

  let p = body.start;

  // locals
  const localInfo = readULEB(p);
  const localDeclCount = localInfo.value;
  p = localInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
    p = readULEB(p).next;
    p++;
  }

  const decoded = [];

  while (p < body.end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    const d = decodeOne(op, p);

    p = d.next;

    decoded.push({
      addr,
      text: d.text
    });
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < decoded.length; i++) {
    if (
      decoded[i].text ===
      "call_indirect type=7 table=0"
    ) {
      results.push({
        func: IMPORTED + bodyIndex,
        index: i,
        decoded
      });
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// OUTPUT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FOCUS TYPE 7 DISPATCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TYPE 7 DISPATCHES :", results.length);
console.log("CONTEXT :", CONTEXT);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const r of results) {
  const start = Math.max(0, r.index - CONTEXT);
  const end = Math.min(
    r.decoded.length,
    r.index + 1
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(
    "FUNC",
    r.func,
    "@ 0x" +
      r.decoded[r.index].addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
  );
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
    const x = r.decoded[i];

    console.log(
      (i === r.index ? ">> " : "   ") +
      "0x" +
      x.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
      x.text
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\func119Signature.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return [value >>> 0, pos];

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSections() {
  let pos = 8;
  let typeSection = null;
  let functionSection = null;
  let codeSection = null;

  while (pos < b.length) {
    const id = b[pos++];

    const [sectionSize, next] = uleb(pos);
    pos = next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = pos + sectionSize;

    if (id === 1)
      typeSection = [start, end];

    if (id === 3)
      functionSection = [start, end];

    if (id === 10)
      codeSection = [start, end];

    pos = end;
  }

  return { typeSection, functionSection, codeSection };
}

function readTypes(start, end) {
  let pos = start;

  const [count, next] = uleb(pos);
  pos = next;

  const types = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const form = b[pos++];

    const [paramCount, nextParam] = uleb(pos);
    pos = nextParam;

    const params = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++)
      params.push(b[pos++]);

    const [resultCount, nextResult] = uleb(pos);
    pos = nextResult;

    const results = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++)
      results.push(b[pos++]);

    types.push({
      form,
      params,
      results
    });
  }

  return types;
}

function getFunctionTypeIndex(functionSection, targetFunc) {
  let pos = functionSection[0];

  const [count, next] = uleb(pos);
  pos = next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const [typeIndex, nextType] = uleb(pos);
    pos = nextType;

    const func = 83 + i;

    if (func === targetFunc)
      return typeIndex;
  }

  return null;
}

function getFunctionBody(codeSection, targetFunc) {
  let pos = codeSection[0];

  const [count, next] = uleb(pos);
  pos = next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const [bodySize, nextSize] = uleb(pos);
    pos = nextSize;

    const func = 83 + i;

    if (func === targetFunc)
      return {
        start: pos,
        end: pos + bodySize,
        size: bodySize
      };

    pos += bodySize;
  }

  return null;
}

const sections = readSections();

const types = readTypes(...sections.typeSection);

const targetFunc = 119;

const typeIndex = getFunctionTypeIndex(
  sections.functionSection,
  targetFunc
);

const body = getFunctionBody(
  sections.codeSection,
  targetFunc
);

console.log("FUNC 119 SIGNATURE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE INDEX :", typeIndex);

const type = types[typeIndex];

console.log(
  "PARAM COUNT:",
  type.params.length
);

console.log(
  "PARAMS     :",
  type.params.map(x => `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(", ")
);

console.log(
  "RESULT COUNT:",
  type.results.length
);

console.log(
  "RESULTS    :",
  type.results.map(x => `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(", ")
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

console.log(
  "BODY       : 0x" +
  body.start.toString(16) +
  " .. 0x" +
  body.end.toString(16)
);

console.log("SIZE       :", body.size);

console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\getFunc87Type.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function leb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;
let typeStart = 0, typeSize = 0;
let importStart = 0, importSize = 0;
let funcStart = 0, funcSize = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const r = leb(p);
  const size = r.value;
  p = r.next;

  if (id === 1) {
    typeStart = p;
    typeSize = size;
  }

  if (id === 2) {
    importStart = p;
    importSize = size;
  }

  if (id === 3) {
    funcStart = p;
    funcSize = size;
  }

  p += size;
}

console.log("FUNC 87 TYPE RESOLUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let q = funcStart;
const n = leb(q);
q = n.next;

let typeIndex87 = -1;

for (let i = 0; i <= 87; i++) {
  const r = leb(q);

  if (i === 87) {
    typeIndex87 = r.value;
    break;
  }

  q = r.next;
}

console.log(`FUNC 87 TYPE INDEX : ${typeIndex87}`);

console.log("");
console.log("TYPE SECTION");
console.log("============================================================");

q = typeStart;
const tc = leb(q);
q = tc.next;

for (let i = 0; i < tc.value; i++) {
  const form = wasm[q++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    console.log(`TYPE ${i}: form=0x${form.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  const pc = leb(q);
  q = pc.next;

  const params: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
    params.push(wasm[q++]);
  }

  const rc = leb(q);
  q = rc.next;

  const results: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
    results.push(wasm[q++]);
  }

  if (i === typeIndex87) {
    console.log(`TYPE ${i} <-- FUNC 87`);
    console.log(`PARAM COUNT  : ${params.length}`);
    console.log(
      `PARAM TYPES  : ${params.map(x => "0x" + x.toString(16)).join(", ")}`
    );
    console.log(`RESULT COUNT : ${results.length}`);
    console.log(
      `RESULT TYPES : ${results.map(x => "0x" + x.toString(16)).join(", ")}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

function friendly(address: Address) {

        urlSafe: true,
        bounceable: true,
        testOnly: true,
    });
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

FILE: scripts\getPoolAddressV22ReadOnly.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER =
    'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x';

const ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQA-oZKiTGR0Aes0yV7uck9-OLrLRJCwRMdwDnUgLU5EJYu0';

const PTON_ROUTER_WALLET =
    'EQBTYCx7TGgVgaIr3tuJ3r_91E6FUBBWLtT73lTYYmrIc5gb';

function addressSlice(address: Address) {
    return {
        type: 'slice' as const,
        cell: beginCell()
            .storeAddress(address)
            .endCell(),
    };
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI GET POOL ADDRESS');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    const router =
        Address.parse(ROUTER);

    const token0 =
        Address.parse(ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET);

    const token1 =
        Address.parse(PTON_ROUTER_WALLET);

    const latest =
        await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        `MASTERCHAIN SEQNO: ${latest.last.seqno}`,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`ROUTER : ${router.toString()}`);
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `TOKEN0 : ${token0.toString()}`,
    );
    console.log(
        `TOKEN1 : ${token1.toString()}`,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calling get_pool_address...');

    const result = await client.runMethod(
        latest.last.seqno,
        router,
        'get_pool_address',
        [
            addressSlice(token0),
            addressSlice(token1),
        ],
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`EXIT CODE: ${result.exitCode}`);

    if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('GETTER FAILED');
        console.log('');
        console.log('RESULT RAW:');
        console.log(result.resultRaw);
        return;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('GETTER SUCCESS');

    try {
        const poolAddress =
            result.reader.readAddress();

        console.log('');
        console.log('POOL ADDRESS');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log(
            poolAddress.toString(),
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('POOL ADDRESS RAW');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log(
            poolAddress.toRawString(),
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Could not decode returned address:',
        );

        console.log(
            e instanceof Error
                ? e.message
                : String(e),
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('RESULT RAW:');
        console.log(result.resultRaw);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINISHED - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const routerAddress = Address.parse(
        'kQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWk0v'
    );

    const state = await provider.provider(routerAddress).getState();

    if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
        throw new Error('Router is not active');
    }

    if (!state.state.code) {
        throw new Error('Router code is missing');
    }

    const code = Cell.fromBoc(state.state.code)[0];

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROUTER HASH TEST =====');
    console.log('Router:', routerAddress.toString());
    console.log('');
    console.log('Cell hash:');
    console.log(code.hash().toString('hex'));
    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC length:', code.toBoc().length);
    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC hex:');
    console.log(code.toBoc().toString('hex'));
    console.log('============================');
}

FILE: scripts\getRouterJettonWallets.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ENDPOINT =
    'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com';

const ROUTER =
    'EQBQ_UBQvR9ryUjKDwijtoiyyga2Wl-yJm6Y8gl0k-HDh_5x';

const ROOSTER_MASTER =
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx';

const PTON_MASTER =
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S';

async function getWallet(
    client: TonClient4,
    master: Address,
    router: Address,
    seqno: number,
): Promise<Address> {
    const result = await client.runMethod(
        seqno,
        master,
        'get_wallet_address',
        [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: (() => {
                    const { beginCell } = require('@ton/core');

                    return beginCell()
                        .storeAddress(router)
                        .endCell();
                })(),
            },
        ],
    );

    if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `get_wallet_address failed: ${result.exitCode}`,
        );
    }

    return result.reader.readAddress();
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER JETTON WALLET DISCOVERY');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: ENDPOINT,
    });

    const router =
        Address.parse(ROUTER);

    const rooster =
        Address.parse(ROOSTER_MASTER);

    const pton =
        Address.parse(PTON_MASTER);

    const latest =
        await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(
        `MASTERCHAIN SEQNO: ${latest.last.seqno}`,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`ROUTER: ${router.toString()}`);
    console.log('');

    const roosterWallet =
        await getWallet(
            client,
            rooster,
            router,
            latest.last.seqno,
        );

    console.log('ROOSTER ROUTER WALLET');
    console.log('----------------------------------------');
    console.log(
        roosterWallet.toString(),
    );

    console.log('');

    const ptonWallet =
        await getWallet(
            client,
            pton,
            router,
            latest.last.seqno,
        );

    console.log('PTON ROUTER WALLET');
    console.log('----------------------------------------');
    console.log(
        ptonWallet.toString(),
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINISHED - READ ONLY');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\getRouterPoolV22.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const OLD_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQB2Ijt3RHMCfzYBUH44pPD04IYZOj8Ol4RsyNDgQhWwMvd_'
);

const TARGET = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('===== STON V2.2 POOL + LP ACCOUNT CHECK =====');
    console.log('');

    const router = DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);
    const routerProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);

    console.log('Getting Pool from Router...');
    console.log('');

    const pool = await router.getPool(
        routerProvider,
        {
            token0: ROOSTER,
            token1: PTON,
        }
    );

    console.log('===== POOL IDENTIFICATION =====');
    console.log('Pool returned by Router:');
    console.log(pool.address.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'Matches expected Pool:',
        pool.address.equals(TARGET)
    );

    console.log(
        'Matches old EQB2 Pool:',
        pool.address.equals(OLD_POOL)
    );

    console.log('');

    const poolProvider = provider.provider(pool.address);

    const deployed = await provider.isContractDeployed(pool.address);

    console.log('===== POOL STATE =====');
    console.log('Deployed:', deployed);

    if (!deployed) {
        console.log('Pool is not deployed.');
        return;
    }

    const state = await provider.getContractState(pool.address);

    console.log('State:', state.state);
    console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('===== POOL DATA =====');

    const poolData = await pool.getPoolData(poolProvider);

    console.log('isLocked:', poolData.isLocked);
    console.log(
        'routerAddress:',
        poolData.routerAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'totalSupplyLP:',
        poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'reserve0 ROOSTER nano:',
        poolData.reserve0.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'reserve0 ROOSTER:',
        Number(poolData.reserve0) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'reserve1 pTON nano:',
        poolData.reserve1.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'reserve1 pTON:',
        Number(poolData.reserve1) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'token0WalletAddress:',
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'token1WalletAddress:',
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'lpFee:',
        poolData.lpFee.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'protocolFee:',
        poolData.protocolFee.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log('===== USER LP ACCOUNT =====');

    console.log('User:');
    console.log(USER.toString());
    console.log('');

    const lpAccountAddress = await pool.getLpAccountAddress(
        poolProvider,
        {
            ownerAddress: USER,
        }
    );

    console.log('LP Account Address:');
    console.log(lpAccountAddress.toString());
    console.log('');

    const lpDeployed = await provider.isContractDeployed(
        lpAccountAddress
    );

    console.log('LP Account deployed:', lpDeployed);

    if (lpDeployed) {
        const lpState = await provider.getContractState(
            lpAccountAddress
        );

        console.log('LP Account state:', lpState.state);
        console.log(
            'LP Account balance:',
            lpState.balance.toString()
        );

        try {
            const lpAccount = await pool.getLpAccount(
                poolProvider,
                {
                    ownerAddress: USER,
                }
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log('LP Account object created successfully.');
            console.log(
                'LP Account address from object:',
                lpAccount.address.toString()
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('LP Account object read error:');
            console.log(String(e));
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== END =====');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { RoosterCoin } from '../wrappers/RoosterCoin';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const masterAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const secondOwner = Address.parse(
        '0QCcuKtKTEvFaHvh0eKWal4G2AvhHW_Y89iCb8HIBX2dydxp'
    );

    const master = provider.open(
        RoosterCoin.createFromAddress(masterAddress)
    );

    const jettonWalletAddress = await master.getWalletAddress(
    secondOwner
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== SECOND ROOSTER WALLET =====');
    console.log('Master:', masterAddress.toString());
    console.log('Owner:', secondOwner.toString());
    console.log('ROOSTER Jetton Wallet:', jettonWalletAddress.toString());
    console.log('=================================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { JettonWallet } from '../wrappers/JettonWallet';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const secondJettonWallet = Address.parse(
        'EQBZ4Je83VkDMcwnXOPiGzvJL38ihcROO25NCL0PjcdypEbn'
    );

    const wallet = provider.open(
        JettonWallet.createFromAddress(secondJettonWallet)
    );

    const data = await wallet.getWalletData();

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== SECOND ROOSTER WALLET =====');
    console.log('Jetton Wallet:', secondJettonWallet.toString());
    console.log('Owner:', data.ownerAddress.toString());
    console.log('Minter:', data.minterAddress.toString());
    console.log('Balance (nano):', data.jettonBalance.toString());
    console.log(
        'Balance (ROOSTER):',
        Number(data.jettonBalance) / 1000000000
    );
    console.log('==================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\getWalletAddress.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const roosterCoinAddress = Address.parse(
        'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
    );

    const ownerAddress = provider.sender().address;

    if (!ownerAddress) {
        throw new Error('Wallet address not available.');
    }

    const contract = provider.provider(roosterCoinAddress);

    const result = await contract.get('get_wallet_address', [
        {
            type: 'slice',
            cell: beginCell()
                .storeAddress(ownerAddress)
                .endCell(),
        },
    ]);

    const jettonWalletAddress = result.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('Rooster Coin Master:');
    console.log(roosterCoinAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Owner Wallet:');
    console.log(ownerAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Jetton Wallet:');
    console.log(jettonWalletAddress.toString());
}
