FILE: scripts\guardedSendLiveLiquidityV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;
const MIN_LP_OUT = 300329052918n;

const GAS_AMOUNT = 300000000n;
const FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT = 240000000n;

const TRANSFER_OPCODE = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE = 0x37c096df;

function assertEqual(
    label: string,
    actual: string,
    expected: string
) {
    if (actual !== expected) {
        throw new Error(
            `${label} FAILED\nExpected: ${expected}\nActual:   ${actual}`
        );
    }

    console.log(`${label}: PASS`);
}

function assertBigInt(
    label: string,
    actual: bigint,
    expected: bigint
) {
    if (actual !== expected) {
        throw new Error(
            `${label} FAILED\nExpected: ${expected}\nActual:   ${actual}`
        );
    }

    console.log(`${label}: PASS`);
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('=================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 GUARDED LIVE SEND');
    console.log(' REAL TRANSACTION POSSIBLE');
    console.log(' FINAL CONFIRMATION REQUIRED');
    console.log('=================================================');

    const sender = provider.sender();

    if (!sender.address) {
        throw new Error(
            'Connected wallet address is unavailable.'
        );
    }

    const senderAddress = sender.address;

    console.log('\n===== 1. WALLET =====');
    console.log('Connected wallet:');
    console.log(senderAddress.toString());

    assertEqual(
        'Connected wallet',
        senderAddress.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== 2. USER ROOSTER WALLET =====');

    const userJettonWalletResult =
        await provider.provider(
            ROOSTER_MASTER
        ).get('get_wallet_address', [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(USER)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]);

    const derivedUserWallet =
        userJettonWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log(
        'Derived user ROOSTER wallet:',
        derivedUserWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected user ROOSTER wallet:',
        EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    assertEqual(
        'ROOSTER wallet',
        derivedUserWallet.toRawString(),
        EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET.toRawString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== 3. ROUTER pTON WALLET =====');

    const routerPtonWalletResult =
        await provider.provider(
            PTON_MASTER
        ).get('get_wallet_address', [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(ROUTER)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]);

    const routerPtonWallet =
        routerPtonWalletResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log(
        'Derived router pTON wallet:',
        routerPtonWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected router pTON wallet:',
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    assertEqual(
        'pTON router wallet',
        routerPtonWallet.toRawString(),
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET.toRawString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== 4. ROOSTER BALANCE =====');

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     * get_wallet_data MUST be called on the USER'S
     * ROOSTER JETTON WALLET, not on the Jetton Master.
     */

    const balanceResult =
        await provider.provider(
            derivedUserWallet
        ).get('get_wallet_data', []);

    const balance =
        balanceResult.stack.readBigNumber();

    console.log(
        'User ROOSTER wallet:',
        derivedUserWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Current ROOSTER nano balance:',
        balance.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Current ROOSTER balance:',
        Number(balance) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'Required ROOSTER nano:',
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Required ROOSTER:',
        Number(ROOSTER_AMOUNT) / 1e9
    );

    if (balance < ROOSTER_AMOUNT) {
        throw new Error(
            `Insufficient ROOSTER balance.\n` +
            `Required: ${ROOSTER_AMOUNT}\n` +
            `Available: ${balance}`
        );
    }

    console.log('ROOSTER balance: PASS');

    console.log('\n===== 5. FRESH DEADLINE =====');

    const now =
        Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

    const deadline =
        now + 900;

    console.log(
        'Current Unix time:',
        now
    );

    console.log(
        'Fresh deadline:',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Lifetime: 900 seconds'
    );

    if (deadline <= now) {
        throw new Error(
            'Deadline validation FAILED.'
        );
    }

    console.log('Deadline: PASS');

    console.log('\n===== 6. BUILD FRESH SDK TRANSACTION =====');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    /*
     * SDK 2.7.0 requires the router provider
     * as the first argument.
     */

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    const txParams =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
                sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
                deadline,
                gasAmount: GAS_AMOUNT,
                forwardGasAmount: FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT,
                transferExcessAddress: USER,
            }
        );

    console.log(
        'SDK transaction built: PASS'
    );

    console.log('\n===== 7. DESTINATION CHECK =====');

    assertEqual(
        'Destination',
        txParams.to.toRawString(),
        EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET.toRawString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== 8. GAS CHECK =====');

    assertBigInt(
        'Gas',
        BigInt(txParams.value),
        GAS_AMOUNT
    );

    console.log('\n===== 9. BODY STRUCTURE =====');

    const body = txParams.body;

    if (!body) {
        throw new Error(
            'SDK transaction body is missing. Aborting before send.'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'Bits:',
        body.bits.length
    );

    console.log(
        'Refs:',
        body.refs.length
    );

    if (body.bits.length !== 720) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected body bits: ${body.bits.length}`
        );
    }

    if (body.refs.length !== 1) {
        throw new Error(
            `Unexpected body refs: ${body.refs.length}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'Body structure: PASS'
    );

    console.log('\n===== 10. BODY CONTENT =====');

    const parser =
        body.beginParse();

    const transferOpcode =
        parser.loadUint(32);

    const queryId =
        parser.loadUintBig(64);

    const amount =
        parser.loadCoins();

    const destination =
        parser.loadAddress();

    const responseDestination =
        parser.loadAddress();

    const customPayloadPresent =
        parser.loadBit();

    if (customPayloadPresent) {
        parser.loadRef();
    }

    const forwardAmount =
        parser.loadCoins();

    const forwardPayload =
        parser.loadRef();

    const provideParser =
        forwardPayload.beginParse();

    const provideOpcode =
        provideParser.loadUint(32);

    const routerWallet =
        provideParser.loadAddress();

    const refundAddress =
        provideParser.loadAddress();

    const excessesAddress =
        provideParser.loadAddress();

   const encodedDeadline =
    provideParser.loadUintBig(64);

const provideBody =
    provideParser.loadRef();

const provideBodyParser =
    provideBody.beginParse();

const minLpOut =
    provideBodyParser.loadCoins();

const receiver =
    provideBodyParser.loadAddress();

const bothPositive =
    provideBodyParser.loadBit();

const forwardGas =
    provideBodyParser.loadCoins();

const provideCustomPayloadPresent =
    provideBodyParser.loadBit();

if (provideCustomPayloadPresent) {
    provideBodyParser.loadRef();
}

    console.log(
        'Transfer opcode:',
        `0x${transferOpcode.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
        'Query ID:',
        queryId.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Amount:',
        amount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Destination:',
        destination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Response destination:',
        responseDestination.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward TON:',
        forwardAmount.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Provide LP opcode:',
        `0x${provideOpcode.toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log(
        'Router wallet:',
        routerWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Refund:',
        refundAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Excesses:',
        excessesAddress.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline:',
        encodedDeadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'minLpOut:',
        minLpOut.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Receiver:',
        receiver.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'bothPositive:',
        bothPositive
    );

    console.log(
        'Custom gas:',
        forwardGas.toString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== 11. FINAL CONTENT GUARDS =====');

    assertEqual(
        'Transfer opcode',
        `0x${transferOpcode.toString(16)}`,
        `0x${TRANSFER_OPCODE.toString(16)}`
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'Query ID',
        queryId,
        0n
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'ROOSTER amount',
        amount,
        ROOSTER_AMOUNT
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Destination',
        destination.toRawString(),
        ROUTER.toRawString()
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Response destination',
        responseDestination.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'Forward TON',
        forwardAmount,
        FORWARD_GAS_AMOUNT
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Provide LP opcode',
        `0x${provideOpcode.toString(16)}`,
        `0x${PROVIDE_LP_OPCODE.toString(16)}`
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Router pTON wallet',
        routerWallet.toRawString(),
        EXPECTED_ROUTER_PTON_WALLET.toRawString()
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Refund address',
        refundAddress.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Excesses address',
        excessesAddress.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'Deadline',
        encodedDeadline,
        BigInt(deadline)
    );

    assertBigInt(
        'minLpOut',
        minLpOut,
        MIN_LP_OUT
    );

    assertEqual(
        'Receiver',
        receiver.toRawString(),
        USER.toRawString()
    );

    if (bothPositive !== true) {
        throw new Error(
            'bothPositive FAILED'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'bothPositive: PASS'
    );

    assertBigInt(
    'Forward gas',
    forwardGas,
    0n
);

if (provideCustomPayloadPresent !== false) {
    throw new Error(
        'Provide custom payload must be false.'
    );
}

console.log(
    'Provide custom payload: PASS'
);

    console.log('\n=================================================');
    console.log(' ALL GUARDS PASSED');
    console.log('=================================================');

    console.log(
        '\nIMPORTANT TRANSACTION SUMMARY'
    );

    console.log(
        '-----------------------------------------------'
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER amount : 200073.535827652 ROOSTER'
    );

    console.log(
        'Minimum LP     : 300.329052918 LP'
    );

    console.log(
        'Gas            : 0.30 TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Forward gas    : 0.24 TON'
    );

    console.log(
        'Deadline       :',
        deadline.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Router         :',
        ROUTER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'User           :',
        USER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'User ROOSTER wallet:',
        derivedUserWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        '\nNO TRANSACTION HAS BEEN SENT YET.'
    );

    console.log(
        '\nThe next line will request explicit confirmation.'
    );

    const answer =
    await provider.ui().prompt(
        'SEND THIS REAL TESTNET TRANSACTION? Confirm to continue:'
    );

if (answer !== true) {
    console.log(
        '\nSEND CANCELLED.'
    );

    console.log(
        'Confirmation was not accepted.'
    );

    console.log(
        'No transaction was sent.'
    );

    return;
}

console.log(
    '\n===== FINAL CONFIRMATION ACCEPTED ====='
);

console.log(
    'Explicit confirmation received.'
);

console.log(
    'Preparing wallet send...'
);

const sendBody = txParams.body;

if (!sendBody) {
    throw new Error(
        'Transaction body is missing. SEND ABORTED.'
    );
}

await sender.send({
    to: Address.parse(txParams.to.toString()),
    value: BigInt(txParams.value),
    body: sendBody,
});

    console.log(
        '\n================================================='
    );

        ' TRANSACTION SUBMITTED TO WALLET'
    );

    console.log(
        '================================================='
    );

    console.log(
        'Tonkeeper/connected wallet should now show the confirmation.'
    );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\identifyFunc1974Callers.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNC = 1974;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const TARGET_CALLS = new Set([
  0x002314c7,
  0x002314d0,
  0x002314e9,
]);

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function readName(pos: number): { text: string; next: number } {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  const end = n.next + n.value;

  let s = "";
  for (let i = n.next; i < end; i++) {
    s += String.fromCharCode(wasm[i]);
  }

  return {
    text: s,
    next: end,
  };
}

type Body = {
  ordinal: number;
  globalFunc: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  instrStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  size: number;
};

function parseCodeSection(): Body[] {
  /*
   * Locate section id 10 (Code).
   */
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    const payloadStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
      let q = payloadStart;

      const countInfo = uleb(q);
      const count = countInfo.value;
      q = countInfo.next;

      const bodies: Body[] = [];

      for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {
        const bodySizeInfo = uleb(q);
        const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
        const bodyStart = bodySizeInfo.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

        /*
         * Function body:
         *
         * vec(locals)
         * expression
         *
         * We only need to skip the local declaration vector
         * to locate the first instruction.
         */
        let r = bodyStart;

        const localCountInfo = uleb(r);
        const localCount = localCountInfo.value;
        r = localCountInfo.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
          const n = uleb(r);
          r = n.next;

          // valtype is one byte
          r += 1;
        }

        bodies.push({
          ordinal,
          globalFunc: ordinal + IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS,
          bodyStart,
          instrStart: r,
          bodyEnd,
          size: bodySize,
        });

        q = bodyEnd;
      }

      return bodies;
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

const bodies = parseCodeSection();

console.log("IDENTIFY FUNC1974 CALLERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(70));
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS}`);
console.log(`CODE BODIES = ${bodies.length}`);
console.log();

const targetBody = bodies.find(x => x.globalFunc === TARGET_FUNC);

if (!targetBody) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} body not found`);
}

console.log(
  `FUNC1974 BODY = ${hex(targetBody.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(targetBody.bodyEnd)}`
);
console.log(
  `FUNC1974 INSTR = ${hex(targetBody.instrStart)}`
);
console.log();

type Instr = {
  addr: number;
  op: number;
  name: string;
  imm?: number;
};

function decode(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
  const out: Instr[] = [];
  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    try {
      switch (op) {

        case 0x00:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "unreachable" });
          break;

        case 0x01:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "nop" });
          break;

        case 0x02:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "block" });
          p += 1;
          break;

        case 0x03:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "loop" });
          p += 1;
          break;

        case 0x04:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "if" });
          p += 1;
          break;

        case 0x05:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "else" });
          break;

        case 0x0b:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "end" });
          break;

        case 0x0c: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "br", imm: x.value });
          break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "br_if", imm: x.value });
          break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "return" });
          break;

        case 0x10: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "call", imm: x.value });
          break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          p += 1;
          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "call_indirect",
            imm: x.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x1a:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "drop" });
          break;

        case 0x20: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "local.get",
            imm: x.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "local.set",
            imm: x.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "local.tee",
            imm: x.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "global.get",
            imm: x.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.next;
          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "global.set",
            imm: x.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x28: {
          const align = uleb(p);
          p = align.next;

          const offset = uleb(p);
          p = offset.next;

          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "i32.load",
            imm: offset.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x2d: {
          const align = uleb(p);
          p = align.next;

          const offset = uleb(p);
          p = offset.next;

          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "i32.load8_u",
            imm: offset.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x36: {
          const align = uleb(p);
          p = align.next;

          const offset = uleb(p);
          p = offset.next;

          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "i32.store",
            imm: offset.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x3a: {
          const align = uleb(p);
          p = align.next;

          const offset = uleb(p);
          p = offset.next;

          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "i32.store8",
            imm: offset.value,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
          const x = sleb(p);
          p = x.next;

          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: "i32.const",
            imm: x.value >>> 0,
          });
          break;
        }

        case 0x45:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.eqz" });
          break;

        case 0x46:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.eq" });
          break;

        case 0x47:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.ne" });
          break;

        case 0x4b:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.ge_u" });
          break;

        case 0x6a:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.add" });
          break;

        case 0x6b:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.sub" });
          break;

        case 0x71:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.and" });
          break;

        case 0x72:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.or" });
          break;

        case 0x74:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.shl" });
          break;

        case 0x7c:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.add" });
          break;

        case 0xa7:
          out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.wrap_i64" });
          break;

        default:
          out.push({
            addr,
            op,
            name: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
          });
          break;
      }
    } catch {
      out.push({
        addr,
        op,
        name: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
      });
      break;
    }
  }

  return out;
}

console.log("SEARCHING EXACT CALL LOCATIONS");
console.log();

let found = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

  const ins = decode(body.instrStart, body.bodyEnd);

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {

    const x = ins[i];

    if (
      x.name === "call" &&
      x.imm === TARGET_FUNC &&
      TARGET_CALLS.has(x.addr)
    ) {

      found++;

      console.log("=".repeat(70));
      console.log(`CALL #${found}`);
      console.log(`CALL PC       = ${hex(x.addr)}`);
      console.log(`CALL TARGET   = FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
      console.log(`CALLER ORDINAL= ${body.ordinal}`);
      console.log(`CALLER GLOBAL = FUNC ${body.globalFunc}`);
      console.log(
        `CALLER BODY   = ${hex(body.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(body.bodyEnd)}`
      );
      console.log(
        `CALLER INSTR  = ${hex(body.instrStart)}`
      );
      console.log();

      const lo = Math.max(0, i - 25);
      const hi = Math.min(ins.length, i + 5);

      console.log("PRE-CALL INSTRUCTIONS");
      console.log("-".repeat(70));

      for (let j = lo; j < hi; j++) {
        const z = ins[j];

        let extra = "";

        if (
          z.name === "local.get" ||
          z.name === "local.set" ||
          z.name === "local.tee"
        ) {
          extra = ` local${z.imm}`;
        } else if (z.name === "i32.const") {
          extra = ` ${hex(z.imm!)}`;
        } else if (
          z.name === "call" ||
          z.name === "br" ||
          z.name === "br_if"
        ) {
          extra = ` ${z.imm}`;
        } else if (
          z.name === "i32.load" ||
          z.name === "i32.store" ||
          z.name === "i32.load8_u" ||
          z.name === "i32.store8"
        ) {
          extra = ` offset=${hex(z.imm ?? 0)}`;
        }

        console.log(
          `${hex(z.addr)}  ${z.name}${extra}`
        );
      }

      console.log();

      /*
       * Search backward from the CALL for constants and selector
       * addresses in the same caller body.
       */
      console.log("BACKWARD CONSTANT / TARGET SCAN");
      console.log("-".repeat(70));

      let targetConstFound = false;

      for (
        let j = Math.max(0, i - 60);
        j < i;
        j++
      ) {
        const z = ins[j];

        if (z.name === "i32.const") {

          if (
            z.imm === 0x0019534c ||
            z.imm === 0x00195350 ||
            z.imm === 0x00195354 ||
            z.imm === 0x00195358
          ) {
            console.log(
              `>>> TARGET ADDRESS CONST ${hex(z.imm)} @ ${hex(z.addr)}`
            );
            targetConstFound = true;
          }

          if (
            z.imm === 4788 ||
            z.imm === 4789 ||
            z.imm === 4790 ||
            z.imm === 4791
          ) {
            console.log(
              `selector-like CONST ${z.imm} @ ${hex(z.addr)}`
            );
          }
        }
      }

      if (!targetConstFound) {
        console.log("NO TARGET ADDRESS CONSTANT IN PRE-CALL WINDOW");
      }

      console.log();

      /*
       * Print raw bytes around the call for independent verification.
       */
      const rawStart = Math.max(body.instrStart, x.addr - 48);
      const rawEnd = Math.min(body.bodyEnd, x.addr + 12);

      console.log(
        `RAW ${hex(rawStart)} .. ${hex(rawEnd)}`
      );

      for (let p = rawStart; p < rawEnd; p += 16) {
        const e = Math.min(rawEnd, p + 16);
        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
          bytes.push(
            wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
          );
        }

        console.log(
          `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
      }

      console.log();
    }
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNC = 2057;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function decode(pos: number): { text: string; next: number } {
  const op = buf[pos++];

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00:
      return { text: "unreachable", next: pos };

    case 0x01:
      return { text: "nop", next: pos };

    case 0x02: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `block ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x03: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `loop ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x04:
      return { text: "if", next: pos };

    case 0x05:
      return { text: "else", next: pos };

    case 0x0b:
      return { text: "end", next: pos };

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `br ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `br_if ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x0f:
      return { text: "return", next: pos };

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `call ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      const y = readULEB(x.next);
      return {
        text: `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${y.value}`,
        next: y.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.get ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.set ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.tee ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `global.get ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `global.set ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);
      return {
        text: `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);
      return {
        text: `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const o = readULEB(a.next);
      return {
        text: `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`,
        next: o.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x3f:
      return { text: "memory.size", next: pos + 1 };

    case 0x40:
      return { text: "memory.grow", next: pos + 1 };

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      return { text: `i32.const ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      return { text: `i64.const ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x45:
      return { text: "i32.eqz", next: pos };

    case 0x46:
      return { text: "i32.eq", next: pos };

    case 0x47:
      return { text: "i32.ne", next: pos };

    case 0x4b:
      return { text: "i32.gt_u", next: pos };

    case 0x4f:
      return { text: "i32.ge_u", next: pos };

    case 0x50:
      return { text: "i64.eqz", next: pos };

    case 0x6a:
      return { text: "i32.add", next: pos };

    case 0x6b:
      return { text: "i32.sub", next: pos };

    case 0x71:
      return { text: "i32.and", next: pos };

    case 0x74:
      return { text: "i32.shl", next: pos };

    case 0xa7:
      return { text: "i32.wrap_i64", next: pos };

    case 0xad:
      return { text: "i64.extend_i32_u", next: pos };

    default:
      return {
        text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        next: pos,
      };
  }
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

const functions: {
  ordinal: number;
  globalFunc: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const s = readULEB(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  functions.push({
    ordinal,
    globalFunc: ordinal + IMPORTED,
    start,
    end,
  });

  p = end;
}

console.log("IDENTIFY FUNC2057 CALLERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log("");

let hits = 0;

for (const f of functions) {
  let q = f.start;

  const ins: {
    pos: number;
    text: string;
  }[] = [];

  while (q < f.end) {
    const pos = q;
    const d = decode(q);

    ins.push({
      pos,
      text: d.text,
    });

    if (d.next <= q) break;
    q = d.next;
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
    if (ins[i].text !== `call ${TARGET_FUNC}`) continue;

    hits++;

    console.log(
      `CALL @0x${ins[i].pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    console.log(
      `  CALLER GLOBAL = ${f.globalFunc}`
    );
    console.log(
      `  CALLER ORDINAL = ${f.ordinal}`
    );
    console.log(
      `  BODY = 0x${f.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${f.end
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );

    console.log("  PRE-CALL INSTRUCTIONS:");

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 12);

    for (let k = from; k < i; k++) {
      console.log(
        `    0x${ins[k].pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins[k].text}`
      );
    }

    console.log("");
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL DIRECT CALLS = ${hits}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET = 0x93e16;

function readU32LE(p: number): number {
  return (
    buf[p] |
    (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
    (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
    (buf[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readName(p: number): { name: string; next: number } {
  const len = readULEB(p);
  p = len.next;

  const end = p + len.value;
  let s = "";

  while (p < end) {
    s += String.fromCharCode(buf[p++]);
  }

  return { name: s, next: p };
}

function findCodeSection(): { start: number; end: number } {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const payloadEnd = p + size;

    if (id === 10) {
      return {
        start: payloadStart,
        end: payloadEnd,
      };
    }

    p = payloadEnd;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log("IDENTIFY FUNCTION AT ADDRESS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET = 0x${TARGET.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${functionCount}`);
console.log("");

let found = false;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount; ordinal++) {
  const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
  p = bodySizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  if (TARGET >= bodyStart && TARGET < bodyEnd) {
    const globalFunc = ordinal + 83;

    console.log("MATCH FOUND");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${ordinal}`);
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${globalFunc}`);
    console.log(
      `BODY          = 0x${bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${bodyEnd
        .toString(16)
        .padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    console.log(`BODY SIZE     = ${bodySize}`);
    console.log(
      `TARGET OFFSET = 0x${(TARGET - bodyStart).toString(16)}`
    );

    console.log("");
    console.log("RAW BODY PREFIX");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const end = Math.min(bodyEnd, bodyStart + 160);

    for (let q = bodyStart; q < end; q += 16) {
      const lineEnd = Math.min(q + 16, end);

      const hex: string[] = [];

      for (let r = q; r < lineEnd; r++) {
        hex.push(buf[r].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
      }

      console.log(
        `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${hex.join(" ")}`
      );
    }

    found = true;
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (!found) {
  console.log("NO FUNCTION BODY CONTAINS TARGET");
}

console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TARGET_RAW =
    '0:F900B682B84A89ECC8BDAA55CE03B04732B4EAF1ED9F67907FB5E58DD7B319AB';

const DERIVED_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQB2Ijt3RHMCfzYBUH44pPD04IYZOj8Ol4RsyNDgQhWwMvd_'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('===== LIQUIDITY TARGET IDENTIFICATION =====');
    console.log('');

    const target = Address.parseRaw(TARGET_RAW);

    console.log('TARGET RAW:');
    console.log(TARGET_RAW);
    console.log('');

    console.log('TARGET FRIENDLY:');
    console.log(target.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('DERIVED POOL:');
    console.log(DERIVED_POOL.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('ADDRESS COMPARISON:');
    console.log(
        target.equals(DERIVED_POOL)
            ? 'SAME ADDRESS'
            : 'DIFFERENT ADDRESSES'
    );
    console.log('');

    async function inspect(name: string, address: Address) {
        console.log(`===== ${name} =====`);
        console.log('Address:', address.toString());

        try {
            const deployed = await provider.isContractDeployed(address);
            console.log('Deployed:', deployed);
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('Deployment check error:', String(e));
        }

        try {
            const state = await provider.getContractState(address);

            console.log('State type:', state.state);
            console.log('Balance:', state.balance.toString());
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('Contract state error:', String(e));
        }

        console.log('');
    }

    await inspect('TARGET F900...', target);
    await inspect('DERIVED POOL EQB2...', DERIVED_POOL);

    console.log('===== IDENTIFICATION COMPLETE =====');
}

FILE: scripts\identifyPoolOutgoingDestinationV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

const DESTINATION = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const TARGET_OPCODE = '801e33f2';

const SDK = path.resolve(
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\node_modules\\@ston-fi\\sdk'
);

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function walk(dir: string): string[] {
    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return [];

    const result: string[] = [];

    for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, {
        withFileTypes: true,
    })) {
        const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);

        if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            result.push(...walk(full));
        } else {
            result.push(full);
        }
    }

    return result;
}

function isText(file: string): boolean {
    return [
        '.js',
        '.cjs',
        '.mjs',
        '.map',
        '.d.ts',
    ].includes(path.extname(file).toLowerCase());
}

function showValue(label: string, value: any) {
    console.log(`${label}:`);

    try {
        console.log(value.toString());
    } catch {
        console.log('<unavailable>');
    }
}

async function main() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 POOL OUTGOING DESTINATION IDENTIFIER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Destination:');
    console.log(DESTINATION.toString());

    console.log('');

    // ============================================================
    // 1. Search local SDK for 0x801e33f2
    // ============================================================

    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('LOCAL SDK OPCODE SEARCH');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    const files = walk(SDK).filter(isText);

    let matches = 0;

    for (const file of files) {
        let source: string;

        try {
            source = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        const lower = source.toLowerCase();

        let pos = 0;

        while (true) {
            const found = lower.indexOf(
                TARGET_OPCODE,
                pos
            );

            if (found === -1) break;

            matches++;

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `FILE: ${path.relative(SDK, file)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `OFFSET: ${found}`
            );

            const start = Math.max(
                0,
                found - 400
            );

            const end = Math.min(
                source.length,
                found + 800
            );

            console.log(
                source
                    .slice(start, end)
                    .replace(/\r?\n/g, ' ')
                    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
            );

            pos =
                found +
                TARGET_OPCODE.length;

            if (matches >= 30) break;
        }

        if (matches >= 30) break;
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `SDK opcode matches: ${matches}`
    );

    // ============================================================
    // 2. Query destination
    // ============================================================

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('DESTINATION get_wallet_data');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: TONCENTER,
    });

    try {
        const result =
            await client.runMethod(
                DESTINATION,
                'get_wallet_data'
            );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'get_wallet_data succeeded.'
        );

        console.log(
            `gas_used: ${result.gas_used}`
        );

        const stack = result.stack;

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'TupleReader detected.'
        );

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Read standard Jetton Wallet get_wallet_data fields
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Attempting standard Jetton Wallet layout:'
        );

        console.log(
            'balance, owner, jetton_master, jetton_wallet_code'
        );

        try {
            const balance =
                stack.readBigNumber();

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `balance: ${balance}`
            );

            console.log(
                `balance decimal: ${balance.toString()}`
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `balance read failed: ${e}`
            );
        }

        try {
            const owner =
                stack.readAddress();

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'owner:'
            );
            console.log(
                owner.toString()
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `owner read failed: ${e}`
            );
        }

        try {
            const master =
                stack.readAddress();

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'jetton_master:'
            );
            console.log(
                master.toString()
            );
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `master read failed: ${e}`
            );
        }

        try {
            const code =
                stack.readCell();

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'jetton_wallet_code: CELL'
            );

            try {
                console.log(
                    `bits: ${code.bits.length}`
                );
            } catch {}

            try {
                console.log(
                    `refs: ${code.refs.length}`
                );
            } catch {}

            try {
                console.log(
                    'BOC(base64):'
                );

                console.log(
                    code
                        .toBoc()
                        .toString('base64')
                );
            } catch {}
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `code read failed: ${e}`
            );
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Remaining stack information
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'Remaining TupleReader items:'
        );

        try {
            console.log(
                `remaining: ${stack.remaining}`
            );
        } catch {
            console.log(
                'remaining: <property unavailable>'
            );
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'get_wallet_data FAILED.'
        );

        console.log(e);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' IDENTIFICATION COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    await main();
}

FILE: scripts\identifyPoolTokens.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL =
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf';

const TOKEN0_WALLET =
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq';

const TOKEN1_WALLET =
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3';

async function readJettonWallet(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    label: string,
    walletAddress: string,
) {
    console.log(`\n===== ${label} =====`);
    console.log(`Wallet: ${walletAddress}`);

    const wallet = Address.parse(walletAddress);

    try {
        const result = await provider.provider(wallet).get(
            'get_wallet_data',
            [],
        );

        console.log('\nget_wallet_data result:');
        console.log(result.stack);

        console.log('\nDecoded values:');

        const balance = result.stack.readBigNumber();
        const ownerAddress = result.stack.readAddress();
        const jettonMaster = result.stack.readAddress();
        const walletCode = result.stack.readCell();

        console.log(`balance: ${balance.toString()}`);
        console.log(`ownerAddress: ${ownerAddress.toString()}`);
        console.log(`jettonMaster: ${jettonMaster.toString()}`);
        console.log(`walletCode: ${walletCode}`);
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            'ERROR:',
            e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e),
        );
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('===== IDENTIFY POOL TOKENS =====');
    console.log(`Pool: ${POOL}`);

    await readJettonWallet(
        provider,
        'TOKEN0 WALLET',
        TOKEN0_WALLET,
    );

    await readJettonWallet(
        provider,
        'TOKEN1 WALLET',
        TOKEN1_WALLET,
    );

    console.log('\n===== DONE =====');
}

FILE: scripts\inlineFunction87Into122V1.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const START = 0x0001226d;
const END = 0x0001295c;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION 122 + FUNCTION 87 INLINE ANALYSIS v1");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(
  `Range     : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`
);
console.log();

type Value = string;

interface Slot {
  id: number;
  arg1: Value;
  arg3: Value;
  callNo: number;
  pc: number;
}

function slotName(n: number): string {
  return `S${n}`;
}

function clean(x: string): string {
  return x.replace(/\s+/g, "");
}

/*
 * Function 87 was fully decoded previously.
 *
 * Inputs:
 *   local0 = destination
 *   local1 = arg1
 *   local2 = arg2
 *   local3 = arg3
 *   local4 = arg4
 *
 * In Function 122:
 *   arg2 = 0
 *   arg4 = 0
 *
 * Function 87 finally performs:
 *
 *   STORE64 [local0 + 8]
 *   STORE64 [local0]
 *
 * The symbolic expressions below reproduce the two output
 * expressions while keeping them readable.
 */

function function87Outputs(
  a: Value,
  b: Value
): { lo: Value; hi: Value } {

  /*
   * Reconstruct the symbolic state of Function 87.
   *
   * From the decoded Function 87:
   *
   * local1 =
   *   (((local1 | 0xffffffff)
   *      / (local3 rotl 32))
   *      - (...))
   *
   * local3 =
   *   ((((local3 | 0xffffffff)
   *       / (local1 | 0xffffffff))
   *       rotl 32)
   *       - ((local3 | 0xffffffff)
   *          / (local1 rotl 32)))
   *
   * local5 =
   *   (local3 | 0xffffffff)
   *   / (local1 | 0xffffffff)
   *
   * The exact signed-i64 semantics are preserved symbolically.
   */

  const A = `(${a})`;
  const B = `(${b})`;

  const Aor = `(${A} | 0xffffffff)`;
  const Bor = `(${B} | 0xffffffff)`;

  const Brot = `rotl32(${B})`;
  const Arot = `rotl32(${A})`;

  const q1 = `(${Aor} / ${Brot})`;

  const l1 = `(${q1} - (${Aor} / ${Brot}))`;

  /*
   * Keep the intermediate names explicit rather than
   * expanding them recursively.
   *
   * This is intentional: the next analysis stage will
   * resolve exact expression dependencies.
   */

  const q = `(${Bor} / ${Aor})`;

  const l3 = `(rotl32(${q}) - (${Bor} / ${Arot}))`;

  const l1r = `rotl32(${l1})`;
  const l3r = `rotl32(${l3})`;

  const outLo = `((${l1r} * ${B}) | ${l3r})`;
  const outHi = `((${l3r} * ${A}) | ${l1r})`;

  return {
    lo: outLo,
    hi: outHi,
  };
}

/*
 * These are the 30 Function-87 calls already recovered
 * from Function 122.
 *
 * S0 is CALL #2.
 */

console.log(" SLOT MAP");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const c of calls) {
  const out = function87Outputs(c.arg1, c.arg3);

  console.log();
  console.log(
    `CALL #${c.callNo} @ 0x${c.pc.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} -> ${slotName(c.id)}`

const seen = new Set(calls.map((x) => x.id));

for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
  console.log(
    `${slotName(i).padEnd(4)} : ${seen.has(i) ? "CALL PRESENT" : "MISSING"}`
  );
}

console.log();
console.log(`Total CALL 87 : ${calls.length}`);
console.log(`Unique slots  : ${seen.size}`);
console.log();

if (seen.size === 30) {
  console.log("STATUS : ALL S0..S29 RESOLVED");
} else {
  console.log("STATUS : SLOT COVERAGE INCOMPLETE");
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const values = [
  141606,
  144293,
  149912,
  149932,
];

function asciiAt(p: number, n = 80) {
  let s = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < n && p + i < wasm.length; i++) {
    const b = wasm[p + i];
    s += b >= 32 && b <= 126 ? String.fromCharCode(b) : ".";
  }
  return s;
}

function hexAt(p: number, n = 32) {
  return Array.from(wasm.subarray(p, p + n))
    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
    .join(" ");
}

  for (const base of [v - 32, v, v + 32]) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(`OFFSET 0x${base.toString(16)}`);
    console.log(`HEX    ${hexAt(base)}`);
    console.log(`ASCII  ${asciiAt(base)}`);
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
    s += 7;
  }
}

const targets = [84, 85, 86, 89, 91, 96, 101, 134, 2413, 259];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 HELPER FAMILY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of targets) {
  let found = false;

  for (let p = 0; p < wasm.length - 1; p++) {
    if (wasm[p] !== 0x10) continue;

    const x = uleb(p + 1);
    if (x.value !== target) continue;

    // Show first occurrence and surrounding bytes only.
    console.log("");
    console.log(`CALL FUNC_${target} @ 0x${p.toString(16)}`);

    const s = Math.max(0, p - 24);
    const e = Math.min(wasm.length, p + 12);

    console.log(
      Array.from(wasm.subarray(s, e))
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ")
    );

    found = true;
    break;
  }

  if (!found) {
    console.log(`FUNC_${target} : no call found`);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspect19534C.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 0x19534c;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0, shift = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("INSPECT 0x19534C DATA / MEMORY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log(`TARGET : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log();

let p = 8;
let hit = false;
let dataCount = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const si = uleb(p);
    p = si.next;

    const end = p + si.value;

    if (sectionId !== 11) {
        p = end;
        continue;
    }

    const ci = uleb(p);
    const count = ci.value;
    p = ci.next;

    dataCount = count;

    console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS : ${count}`);
    console.log();

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {

        const fi = uleb(p);
        const flags = fi.value;
        p = fi.next;

        let offset = null;

        if (flags === 0) {

            const opcode = wasm[p++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                console.log(`SEG ${seg}: unsupported offset opcode`);
                process.exit(1);
            }

            const oi = uleb(p);
            offset = oi.value;
            p = oi.next;

            if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) {
                console.log(`SEG ${seg}: bad offset end`);
                process.exit(1);
            }

        } else if (flags === 2) {

            const mi = uleb(p);
            const memoryIndex = mi.value;
            p = mi.next;

            const opcode = wasm[p++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                console.log(`SEG ${seg}: unsupported offset opcode`);
                process.exit(1);
            }

            const oi = uleb(p);
            offset = oi.value;
            p = oi.next;

            p++; // end expression

        } else if (flags === 1) {

            const li = uleb(p);
            p = li.next + li.value;
            continue;

        } else {
            console.log(`SEG ${seg}: flags=${flags}`);
            process.exit(1);
        }

        const li = uleb(p);
        const len = li.value;
        p = li.next;

        const dataStart = p;
        const dataEnd = p + len;

        const segEnd = offset + len;

        // Check whether TARGET falls anywhere near this segment.
        if (
            TARGET >= offset - 32 &&
            TARGET <= segEnd + 32
        ) {

            hit = true;

            console.log("############################################################");
            console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg}`);
            console.log("############################################################");
            console.log(`OFFSET : ${hex(offset)}`);
            console.log(`SIZE   : ${len}`);
            console.log(`END    : ${hex(segEnd)}`);
            console.log();

            const from = Math.max(offset, TARGET - 32);
            const to = Math.min(segEnd, TARGET + 32);

            for (let addr = from; addr < to; addr++) {

                const idx = dataStart + (addr - offset);
                const b = wasm[idx];

                const mark =
                    addr >= TARGET && addr < TARGET + 4
                        ? "  <=== TARGET"
                        : "";

                console.log(
                    `${hex(addr)}  ${b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}${mark}`
                );
            }

            console.log();

            // If the target is fully contained, decode LE values.
            if (TARGET >= offset && TARGET + 4 <= segEnd) {

                const idx = dataStart + (TARGET - offset);

                const b0 = wasm[idx];
                const b1 = wasm[idx + 1];
                const b2 = wasm[idx + 2];
                const b3 = wasm[idx + 3];

                const value =
                    (b0 |
                    (b1 << 8) |
                    (b2 << 16) |
                    (b3 << 24)) >>> 0;

                console.log("TARGET VALUE");
                console.log(`RAW  : ${[b0,b1,b2,b3]
                    .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
                    .join(" ")}`);

                console.log(`I32  : ${value}`);
                console.log(`HEX  : ${hex(value)}`);
                console.log();

                console.log("RESULT:");
                console.log(`MEM[0x19534C] = ${value} (${hex(value)})`);
            }
        }

        p = dataEnd;
    }

    break;
}

console.log();

if (!hit) {
    console.log("NO DATA SEGMENT NEAR TARGET.");
    console.log("Therefore the selector is almost certainly initialized dynamically.");
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspect2774Signature.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const TARGETS = [2774, 4815];

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos,
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readName(pos: number): {
    value: string;
    next: number;
} {
    const n = readU32Leb(pos);
    const end = n.next + n.value;

    return {
        value: Buffer.from(
            wasm.subarray(n.next, end),
        ).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function findSection(idWanted: number): {
    start: number;
    size: number;
} {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = readU32Leb(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (id === idWanted) {
            return {
                start: p,
                size: s.value,
            };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error(
        `Section ${idWanted} not found`,
    );
}

function valtype(v: number): string {
    switch (v) {
        case 0x7f:
            return "i32";

        case 0x7e:
            return "i64";

        case 0x7d:
            return "f32";

        case 0x7c:
            return "f64";

        case 0x70:
            return "funcref";

        case 0x6f:
            return "externref";

        default:
            return `0x${v.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

function parseTypes(): {
    params: string[];
    results: string[];
}[] {
    const sec = findSection(1);

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = readU32Leb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const types = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unexpected type form at ${p - 1}`,
            );
        }

        const pc = readU32Leb(p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
            params.push(
                valtype(wasm[p++]),
            );
        }

        const rc = readU32Leb(p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results: string[] = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
            results.push(
                valtype(wasm[p++]),
            );
        }

        types.push({
            params,
            results,
        });
    }

    return types;
}

function getDefinedFunctionTypes(): number[] {
    const sec = findSection(3);

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = readU32Leb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const types: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const x = readU32Leb(p);
        p = x.next;

        types.push(x.value);
    }

    return types;
}

function getImportFunctionTypes(): number[] {
    const sec = findSection(2);

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = readU32Leb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const result: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const module = readName(p);
        p = module.next;

        const name = readName(p);
        p = name.next;

        const kind = wasm[p++];

        if (kind === 0x00) {
            const type = readU32Leb(p);
            p = type.next;

            result.push(type.value);
        } else if (kind === 0x01) {
            p++;

            const flags = wasm[p++];

            const min = readU32Leb(p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const max = readU32Leb(p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 0x02) {
            const flags = wasm[p++];

            const min = readU32Leb(p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const max = readU32Leb(p);
                p = max.next;
            }

            p++; // memory limits encoding
        } else if (kind === 0x03) {
            p += 2;
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unknown import kind ${kind}`,
            );
        }
    }

    return result;
}

function getFunctionType(
    funcIndex: number,
    types: ReturnType<typeof parseTypes>,
    importedTypes: number[],
    definedTypes: number[],
) {
    let typeIndex: number;

    if (funcIndex < IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS) {
        typeIndex = importedTypes[funcIndex];
    } else {
        const definedIndex =
            funcIndex - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

        typeIndex = definedTypes[definedIndex];
    }

    return {
        typeIndex,
        type: types[typeIndex],
    };
}

export async function run() {
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " FUNCTION SIGNATURE INSPECTION",
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        `WASM size : ${wasm.length}`,
    );

    const types = parseTypes();
    const importedTypes =
        getImportFunctionTypes();
    const definedTypes =
        getDefinedFunctionTypes();

    console.log(
        `Types          : ${types.length}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `Imported funcs  : ${importedTypes.length}`,
    );

    console.log(
        `Defined funcs   : ${definedTypes.length}`,
    );

    console.log();

    for (const func of TARGETS) {
        const info = getFunctionType(
            func,
            types,
            importedTypes,
            definedTypes,
        );

        console.log(
            "------------------------------------------------------------",
        );

        console.log(
            `Function ${func}`,
        );

        console.log(
            `Type index : ${info.typeIndex}`,
        );

        console.log(
            `Parameters : ${info.type.params.length}`,
        );

        info.type.params.forEach(
            (x, i) => {
                console.log(
                    `  param[${i}] : ${x}`,
                );
            },
        );

        console.log(
            `Results    : ${info.type.results.length}`,
        );

        info.type.results.forEach(
            (x, i) => {
                console.log(
                    `  result[${i}] : ${x}`,
                );
            },
        );

        console.log();
    }

    const target =
        getFunctionType(
            2774,
            types,
            importedTypes,
            definedTypes,
        );

    const caller =
        getFunctionType(
            4815,
            types,
            importedTypes,
            definedTypes,
        );

    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " CALL COMPATIBILITY CHECK",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    console.log(
        `2774 expects ${target.type.params.length} arguments.`,
    );

    console.log(
        `4815 pushes six locals immediately before call 2774.`,
    );

    console.log();

    if (
        target.type.params.length === 6
    ) {
        console.log(
            "RESULT: Function 2774 has exactly 6 parameters.",
        );

        console.log(
            "Therefore the six local.get instructions map directly:",
        );

        console.log(
            "  2774.local0 <- 4815.local0",
        );

        console.log(
            "  2774.local1 <- 4815.local1",
        );

        console.log(
            "  2774.local2 <- 4815.local2",
        );

        console.log(
            "  2774.local3 <- 4815.local3",
        );

        console.log(
            "  2774.local4 <- 4815.local4",
        );

        console.log(
            "  2774.local5 <- 4815.local5",
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            "2774 does NOT have exactly six parameters.",
        );

        console.log(
            "We must reconstruct the stack argument mapping.",
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log(
        " TARGET: 2774.local2",
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================",
    );
    console.log();

    if (
        target.type.params.length >= 3
    ) {
        console.log(
            `2774.local2 type = ${target.type.params[2]}`,
        );

        console.log(
            "This is the value that is written to [local2 + 0].",
        );

        console.log(
            "Next step: trace 4815.local2 backward.",
        );
    }

    console.log();
}
