import fs from "node:fs";
import wabtInit from "wabt";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wabt = await wabtInit();
const bytes = fs.readFileSync(file);
const mod = wabt.readWasm(bytes, { readDebugNames: true });

mod.generateNames();
mod.applyNames();

const wat = mod.toText({
  foldExprs: false,
  inlineExport: false
});

const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
const start = lines.findIndex(line =>
  /^\s*\(func\s+\$func6618\b/.test(line)
);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 6618 WAT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

if (start < 0) {
  console.log("Function name func6618 not found.");
  console.log("Lines mentioning 6618:");
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    if (lines[i].includes("6618")) {
      console.log(`${i + 1}: ${lines[i]}`);
    }
  }
  process.exitCode = 1;
} else {
  let end = start + 1;

  while (end < lines.length &&
         !/^\s*\(func\b/.test(lines[end])) {
    end++;
  }

  console.log(`WAT LINES: ${start + 1} .. ${end}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
    console.log(`${String(i + 1).padStart(6)} | ${lines[i]}`);
  }
}

mod.destroy();

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspect7006DataInit.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const wasmPath = path.resolve("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const outputPath = path.resolve("scripts/inspect7006DataInit_output.txt");
const TARGET = 0x256aac;
const NEAR = 0x1000;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const lines = [];

function log(s = "") {
    lines.push(String(s));
}

function readU32(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos.offset < wasm.length) {
        const byte = wasm[pos.offset++];
        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) {
            return value >>> 0;
        }

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF reading ULEB128");
}

function readS32(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    do {
        if (pos.offset >= wasm.length) throw new Error("Unexpected EOF reading SLEB128");

        byte = wasm[pos.offset++];
        result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid SLEB128");
    } while (byte & 0x80);

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return result | 0;
}

function readName(pos) {
    const len = readU32(pos);
    const start = pos.offset;
    pos.offset += len;

    if (pos.offset > wasm.length) throw new Error("Invalid name length");
    return wasm.toString("utf8", start, start + len);
}

function readOffsetExpr(pos) {
    const opcode = wasm[pos.offset++];

    if (opcode === 0x41) {
        const value = readS32(pos);
        const end = wasm[pos.offset++];

        if (end !== 0x0b) throw new Error("Unexpected i32.const expression ending");
        return { kind: "i32.const", value: value >>> 0 };
    }

    if (opcode === 0x23) {
        const index = readU32(pos);
        const end = wasm[pos.offset++];

        if (end !== 0x0b) throw new Error("Unexpected global.get expression ending");
        return { kind: "global.get", index };
    }

    throw new Error(
        `Unsupported data offset expression opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)} ` +
        `at file offset 0x${(pos.offset - 1).toString(16)}`
    );
}

function hex(n) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function bytesHex(data) {
    return [...data].map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ");
}

if (wasm.length < 8 ||
    wasm[0] !== 0x00 ||
    wasm[1] !== 0x61 ||
    wasm[2] !== 0x73 ||
    wasm[3] !== 0x6d) {
    throw new Error("File does not have a valid WASM magic header.");
}

log("ROOSTER WASM — DATA INITIALIZATION INSPECTION");
log("READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION");
log(`FILE: ${wasmPath}`);
log(`SIZE: ${wasm.length}`);
log(`TARGET MEMORY ADDRESS: ${hex(TARGET)} (${TARGET})`);
log("=".repeat(78));

const sections = [];
let cursor = 8;

while (cursor < wasm.length) {
    const pos = { offset: cursor };
    const id = wasm[pos.offset++];
    const size = readU32(pos);
    const start = pos.offset;
    const end = start + size;

    if (end > wasm.length) {
        throw new Error(`Section ${id} exceeds file boundary.`);
    }

    sections.push({ id, start, end, size });
    cursor = end;
}

log(`SECTIONS FOUND: ${sections.length}`);

const dataSection = sections.find(s => s.id === 11);

if (!dataSection) {
    log("\nNo data section (section 11) exists in this WASM file.");
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join("\n"), "utf8");
    console.log(lines.join("\n"));
    process.exit(0);
}

log(`DATA SECTION: file offsets ${hex(dataSection.start)} .. ${hex(dataSection.end)}`);
log(`DATA SECTION SIZE: ${dataSection.size}`);
log("-".repeat(78));

const pos = { offset: dataSection.start };
const segmentCount = readU32(pos);
const segments = [];
let unsupportedSegments = 0;

log(`DATA SEGMENT COUNT: ${segmentCount}`);

for (let index = 0; index < segmentCount; index++) {
    const segmentStart = pos.offset;
    const flags = readU32(pos);

    let mode;
    let memoryIndex = 0;
    let offsetExpr = null;

    if (flags === 0) {
        mode = "active-memory-0";
        offsetExpr = readOffsetExpr(pos);
    } else if (flags === 1) {
        mode = "passive";
    } else if (flags === 2) {
        mode = "active-explicit-memory";
        memoryIndex = readU32(pos);
        offsetExpr = readOffsetExpr(pos);
    } else {
        throw new Error(`Unknown data segment flags ${flags} at ${hex(segmentStart)}`);
    }

    const length = readU32(pos);
    const dataStart = pos.offset;
    const dataEnd = dataStart + length;

    if (dataEnd > dataSection.end) {
        throw new Error(`Segment ${index} data exceeds data section.`);
    }

    const bytes = wasm.subarray(dataStart, dataEnd);
    pos.offset = dataEnd;

    const segment = {
        index,
        mode,
        memoryIndex,
        offsetExpr,
        length,
        bytes,
        dataStart,
        dataEnd,
        fileOffset: segmentStart
    };

    segments.push(segment);

    if (mode === "passive" || offsetExpr?.kind !== "i32.const") {
        unsupportedSegments++;
    }
}

log(`PARSED SEGMENTS: ${segments.length}`);
log(`Passive or non-constant-offset segments: ${unsupportedSegments}`);

const active = segments.filter(
    s => s.offsetExpr?.kind === "i32.const" &&
        s.mode !== "passive"
);

let targetCovered = false;
let nearbyCount = 0;

for (const s of active) {
    const base = s.offsetExpr.value >>> 0;
    const end = base + s.length;
    const targetEnd = TARGET + 4;

    const overlaps = base < targetEnd && end > TARGET;
    const nearby = base <= TARGET + NEAR && end >= TARGET - NEAR;

    if (nearby) nearbyCount++;

    if (!overlaps && !nearby) continue;

    log("\n" + "-".repeat(78));
    log(`SEGMENT ${s.index}`);
    log(`MODE: ${s.mode}`);
    log(`MEMORY INDEX: ${s.memoryIndex}`);
    log(`BASE: ${hex(base)} (${base})`);
    log(`LENGTH: ${s.length}`);
    log(`END EXCLUSIVE: ${hex(end)}`);
    log(`WASM FILE DATA: ${hex(s.dataStart)} .. ${hex(s.dataEnd)}`);
    log(`TARGET OVERLAPS SEGMENT: ${overlaps ? "YES" : "NO"}`);

    if (overlaps) {
        targetCovered = true;

        const relative = TARGET - base;
        const from = Math.max(0, relative - 16);
        const to = Math.min(s.length, relative + 32);
        const snippet = s.bytes.subarray(from, to);

        log(`TARGET OFFSET WITHIN SEGMENT: ${relative}`);
        log(`BYTES AROUND TARGET [${from}..${to}):`);
        log(bytesHex(snippet));

        if (relative >= 0 && relative + 4 <= s.length) {
            const value = new DataView(
                s.bytes.buffer,
                s.bytes.byteOffset + relative,
                4
            ).getUint32(0, true);

            log(`INITIAL LITTLE-ENDIAN I32 AT TARGET: ${value} (${hex(value)})`);
            log(`EQUALS TABLE SLOT 4769: ${value === 4769 ? "YES" : "NO"}`);
        } else {
            log("TARGET 4-BYTE VALUE: only partially covered by this segment.");
        }
    }
}

log("\n" + "=".repeat(78));
log("SUMMARY");
log(`Active segments with constant offsets: ${active.length}`);
log(`Segments within +/- ${hex(NEAR)} of target: ${nearbyCount}`);
log(`Target covered by an active constant-offset data segment: ${targetCovered}`);

if (targetCovered) {
    log("Interpretation: the segment bytes can establish the initial memory value.");
} else {
    log("Interpretation: no active constant-offset segment directly covers the target.");
    log("This does not rule out global-based offsets, runtime initialization, or indirect writes.");
}

log("\nNo WASM code was executed and no network or transaction was used.");

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join("\n"), "utf8");
console.log(lines.slice(0, 220).join("\n"));
console.log(`\nFull report: ${outputPath}`);

FILE: scripts\inspect7006DispatchPaths.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import os from "node:os";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const wasmPath = path.resolve("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const decoderPath = path.resolve("scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const outputPath = path.resolve("scripts/inspect7006DispatchPaths_output.txt");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const source = fs.readFileSync(decoderPath, "utf8");
const output = [];

function log(s = "") {
    output.push(String(s));
}

function readU32(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos.i < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[pos.i++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return result >>> 0;

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readName(pos) {
    const length = readU32(pos);
    const start = pos.i;
    pos.i += length;
    if (pos.i > wasm.length) throw new Error("Invalid name");
    return wasm.toString("utf8", start, start + length);
}

function getSection(idWanted) {
    let i = 8;

    while (i < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[i];
        const pos = { i: i + 1 };
        const size = readU32(pos);
        const start = pos.i;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > wasm.length) throw new Error("Invalid section boundary");
        if (id === idWanted) return { start, end, size };

        i = end;
    }

    return null;
}

function getExports() {
    const section = getSection(7);
    if (!section) return [];

    const pos = { i: section.start };
    const count = readU32(pos);
    const result = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const name = readName(pos);
        const kind = wasm[pos.i++];
        const index = readU32(pos);
        if (kind === 0) result.push({ name, index });
    }

    return result;
}

const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const markerIndex = source.indexOf(marker);

if (markerIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error("Decoder bootstrap marker not found.");
}

const tempPath = path.join(os.tmpdir(), "rooster_decoder_dispatch_paths.mjs");

fs.writeFileSync(
    tempPath,
    source.slice(0, markerIndex) +
    "\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n",
    "utf8"
);

const { bodies, decodeFunction } =
    await import(pathToFileURL(tempPath).href + "?dispatchPaths");

const exports = getExports();
const cache = new Map();

function decode(func) {
    if (cache.has(func)) return cache.get(func);

    try {
        const result = decodeFunction(func);
        cache.set(func, result);
        return result;
    } catch (error) {
        cache.set(func, null);
        return null;
    }
}

function textOf(ins) {
    return String(ins?.text ?? "");
}

function offsetOf(ins) {
    const n = ins?.start ?? ins?.offset ?? ins?.address;
    return Number.isInteger(n) ? "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") : "offset=?";
}

function printInstruction(func, index, ins) {
    log(
        `FUNC ${func} #${String(index).padStart(5)} ` +
        `${offsetOf(ins)} | ${textOf(ins)}`
    );
}

function printContext(func, instructions, index, radius = 12) {
    log(`\n--- FUNC ${func}, instruction #${index} ---`);

    const first = Math.max(0, index - radius);
    const last = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, index + radius);

    for (let i = first; i <= last; i++) {
        printInstruction(func, i, instructions[i]);
    }
}

const focusFunctions = [98, 203, 2616, 3837, 7006, 6991];

log("ROOSTER WASM — INDIRECT DISPATCH PATHS");
log("READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION");
log(`WASM SIZE: ${wasm.length}`);
log(`DECODER BODY COUNT: ${bodies.size}`);
log("=".repeat(78));

log("\n1. INDIRECT CALLS IN FOCUS FUNCTIONS");

for (const func of focusFunctions) {
    const decoded = decode(func);

    if (!decoded || !Array.isArray(decoded.instructions)) {
        log(`\nFUNC ${func}: decode failed`);
        continue;
    }

    const instructions = decoded.instructions;
    log(`\nFUNC ${func}: ${instructions.length} instructions`);

    const hits = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const t = textOf(instructions[i]);

        if (/call_indirect/i.test(t) ||
            /2452140|0x256aac|4769|0x12a1/i.test(t)) {
            hits.push(i);
        }
    }

    if (hits.length === 0) {
        log("No indirect-call or target-constant matches.");
    }

    const printed = new Set();

    for (const hit of hits) {
        const first = Math.max(0, hit - 12);
        const last = Math.min(instructions.length - 1, hit + 12);

        log(`\n>>> MATCH at instruction ${hit}: ${textOf(instructions[hit])}`);

        for (let i = first; i <= last; i++) {
            if (printed.has(i)) continue;
            printed.add(i);
            printInstruction(func, i, instructions[i]);
        }
    }
}

log("\n\n2. DIRECT CALLERS OF FUNCTIONS 98 AND 2616");

const soughtCallers = new Set([98, 2616]);
const callerHits = new Map([[98, []], [2616, []]]);
let scanned = 0;
let failed = 0;

for (const func of bodies.keys()) {
    const decoded = decode(func);

    if (!decoded || !Array.isArray(decoded.instructions)) {
        failed++;
        continue;
    }

    scanned++;

    const instructions = decoded.instructions;

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const t = textOf(instructions[i]);
        const m = t.match(/^call\s+(\d+)\b/);

        if (!m) continue;

        const target = Number(m[1]);
        if (!soughtCallers.has(target)) continue;

        callerHits.get(target).push({ func, index: i, instructions });
    }
}

for (const target of soughtCallers) {
    const hits = callerHits.get(target);

    log(`\nTARGET FUNC ${target}: ${hits.length} direct call sites`);

    for (const hit of hits.slice(0, 80)) {
        log(`\nCaller FUNC ${hit.func}`);
        printContext(hit.func, hit.instructions, hit.index, 8);
    }

    if (hits.length > 80) {
        log(`Output capped at 80 call sites; ${hits.length - 80} omitted.`);
    }
}

log("\n\n3. ALL DIRECT REFERENCES TO TARGET ADDRESS");

let addressRefs = 0;
let valueRefs = 0;

for (const func of bodies.keys()) {
    const decoded = decode(func);
    if (!decoded || !Array.isArray(decoded.instructions)) continue;

    const ins = decoded.instructions;

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
        const t = textOf(ins[i]).toLowerCase();

        if (t.includes("2452140") || t.includes("0x256aac")) {
            addressRefs++;
            log(`ADDRESS REF: FUNC ${func}, #${i}, ${offsetOf(ins[i])} | ${textOf(ins[i])}`);
            printContext(func, ins, i, 5);
        }

        if (/\b4769\b|0x12a1/.test(t)) {
            valueRefs++;
            log(`VALUE REF: FUNC ${func}, #${i}, ${offsetOf(ins[i])} | ${textOf(ins[i])}`);
        }
    }
}

log(`\nADDRESS REFERENCE COUNT: ${addressRefs}`);
log(`4769 / 0x12a1 TEXT MATCH COUNT: ${valueRefs}`);

log("\n\n4. SUMMARY");
log(`Functions decoded successfully: ${scanned}`);
log(`Decode failures: ${failed}`);
log("- A numeric match alone does not prove the indirect call selects slot 4769.");
log("- This analysis is static; the WASM was not executed.");

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, output.join("\n"), "utf8");

console.log(output.slice(0, 300).join("\n"));
console.log(`\nFull output saved: ${outputPath}`);

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import os from "node:os";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const wasmPath = path.resolve("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const decoderPath = path.resolve("scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const outputPath = path.resolve("scripts/inspect7006ExportPaths_output.txt");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const source = fs.readFileSync(decoderPath, "utf8");
const output = [];

function log(s = "") {
    output.push(String(s));
}

function readU32(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos.i < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[pos.i++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return result >>> 0;

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB128");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function readName(pos) {
    const length = readU32(pos);
    const start = pos.i;
    pos.i += length;

    if (pos.i > wasm.length) throw new Error("Invalid name length");

    return wasm.toString("utf8", start, start + length);
}

function findSection(sectionId) {
    let i = 8;

    while (i < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[i];
        const pos = { i: i + 1 };
        const size = readU32(pos);
        const start = pos.i;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > wasm.length) throw new Error("Invalid section size");

        if (id === sectionId) return { start, end, size };

        i = end;
    }

    return null;
}

function readExports() {
    const section = findSection(7);
    if (!section) return [];

    const pos = { i: section.start };
    const count = readU32(pos);
    const result = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const name = readName(pos);
        const kind = wasm[pos.i++];
        const index = readU32(pos);

        if (kind === 0) result.push({ name, index });
    }

    return result;
}

const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";

if (markerIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error("Could not find decoder bootstrap marker.");
}

    decoder = await import(pathToFileURL(tempPath).href + "?inspect7006");

    process.exit(1);

const targets = new Set([
    7006,
    4769,
    2452140,
    0x256aac
]);

function serialized(ins) {
    try {
        return JSON.stringify(ins, (_, value) =>
            typeof value === "bigint" ? value.toString() : value
        );
    } catch {
        return String(ins);
    }
}

function formatOffset(ins) {
    const value = ins?.offset ?? ins?.pc ?? ins?.address;
    if (typeof value === "number") {
        return `0x${value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`;
    }
    return "offset=?";
}

function inspectFunction(funcIndex, label) {
    let decoded;

    try {
        decoded = decodeFunction(funcIndex);
    } catch (error) {
        log(`\nFUNC ${funcIndex} [${label}] DECODE ERROR`);
        log(String(error?.message ?? error));
        return;
    }

    if (!decoded || !Array.isArray(decoded.instructions)) {
        log(`\nFUNC ${funcIndex} [${label}]`);
        log("Unexpected decoder result shape.");
        log(JSON.stringify(decoded).slice(0, 2000));
        return;
    }

    const instructions = decoded.instructions;

    log(`\nFUNC ${funcIndex} [${label}]`);
    log(`BODY: ${JSON.stringify(decoded.body)}`);
    log(`INSTRUCTIONS: ${instructions.length}`);

    // Show initial instructions to expose function setup and argument handling.
    log("-- FIRST INSTRUCTIONS --");
    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(8, instructions.length); i++) {
        log(`${String(i).padStart(4)} ${formatOffset(instructions[i])} ${serialized(instructions[i])}`);
    }

    // Print call instructions and instructions mentioning our target values.
    const hits = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
        const s = serialized(instructions[i]).toLowerCase();

        const isCall = /call_indirect|call/.test(s);
        const hasTarget = [...targets].some(n =>
            s.includes(String(n)) ||
            s.includes("0x" + n.toString(16))
        );

        if (isCall || hasTarget) hits.push(i);
    }

    const shown = new Set();

    log("-- CALL / TARGET CONTEXT --");

    for (const index of hits) {
        for (let j = Math.max(0, index - 2);
             j <= Math.min(instructions.length - 1, index + 2);
             j++) {
            if (shown.has(j)) continue;
            shown.add(j);

            log(
                `${String(j).padStart(5)} ` +
                `${formatOffset(instructions[j])} ` +
                `${serialized(instructions[j])}`
            );
        }
        log("--");
    }

    if (hits.length === 0) log("No call or target-value matches.");
}

const selected = new Map();

for (const e of exports) {
    selected.set(e.index, `export:${e.name}`);
}

for (const func of [83, 98, 203, 2616, 3837, 7006]) {
    if (!selected.has(func)) selected.set(func, "trace-candidate");
}

for (const [funcIndex, label] of selected) {
    inspectFunction(funcIndex, label);
}

log("\nINTERPRETATION LIMITS");
log("- A call_indirect type match alone does not prove table slot 4769 is selected.");
log("- Numeric matches are heuristic; instruction structure and dataflow must confirm them.");
log("- No WASM code was executed; no network or transaction was used.");

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, output.join("\n"), "utf8");

console.log(output.slice(0, 260).join("\n"));
console.log(`\nFull output saved: ${outputPath}`);

import fs from 'node:fs';

const sourcePath = 'scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs';

    "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";

    throw new Error(

    );

    decoderSource + '\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n';

    'data:text/javascript;base64,' +
    Buffer.from(moduleSource, 'utf8').toString('base64');

const { bodies, decodeFunction } = await import(moduleUrl);

function hex(value) {
    return '0x' + value.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

for (const func of [2484, 3990]) {
    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(76));

    if (!body) {
        console.log(`FUNCTION BODY NOT FOUND: ${func}`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log(`BODY START: ${hex(body.start)}`);
    console.log(`BODY END:   ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`BODY SIZE:  ${body.end - body.start} bytes`);
    console.log('-'.repeat(76));

    try {
        const result = decodeFunction(func);
        const instructions = result.instructions;

        console.log(`INSTRUCTION COUNT: ${instructions.length}`);
        console.log('-'.repeat(76));

        for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
            const ins = instructions[i];

            console.log(

                `${hex(ins.start)}  ` +
                `${ins.raw.padEnd(38)} ` +
                `${ins.text}`
            );
        }

        console.log('-'.repeat(76));
        console.log('TARGET ADDRESS REFERENCES');

        const targets = [
            0x256AAC,
            0x256AB0,
            0x256ACC,
            0x256ACE,
        ];

        let found = false;

        for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
            const ins = instructions[i];

            for (const target of targets) {
                if (
                    ins.text.toLowerCase().includes(
                        target.toString(16)
                    )
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        `INS #${i} @ ${hex(ins.start)}: ` +
                        `${ins.text} [target ${hex(target)}]`
                    );
                    found = true;
                }
            }
        }

        if (!found) {
            console.log('No textual references to listed addresses.');
        }
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(`DECODE ERROR: ${error.message}`);
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspect7006Startup.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";

const wasmPath = path.resolve("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const decoderPath = path.resolve("scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const outputPath = path.resolve("scripts/inspect7006Startup_output.txt");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const decoderSource = fs.readFileSync(decoderPath, "utf8");
const lines = [];

function log(s = "") {
    lines.push(String(s));
}

function u32(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (pos.i < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[pos.i++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return result >>> 0;
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("Invalid ULEB");
    }

    throw new Error("Unexpected EOF");
}

function name(pos) {
    const len = u32(pos);
    const start = pos.i;
    pos.i += len;
    if (pos.i > wasm.length) throw new Error("Invalid name");
    return wasm.toString("utf8", start, start + len);
}

function sectionList() {
    const result = [];
    let i = 8;

    while (i < wasm.length) {
        const pos = { i: i + 1 };
        const id = wasm[i];
        const size = u32(pos);
        const start = pos.i;
        const end = start + size;

        if (end > wasm.length) throw new Error("Invalid section boundary");

        result.push({ id, start, end, size });
        i = end;
    }

    return result;
}

function parseExports(section) {
    const pos = { i: section.start };
    const count = u32(pos);
    const exports = [];

    for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
        const exportName = name(pos);
        const kind = wasm[pos.i++];
        const index = u32(pos);
        exports.push({ name: exportName, kind, index });
    }

    return exports;
}

function parseStart(section) {
    const pos = { i: section.start };
    return u32(pos);
}

const sections = sectionList();
const byId = new Map(sections.map(s => [s.id, s]));
const exports = byId.has(7) ? parseExports(byId.get(7)) : [];
const startFunction = byId.has(8) ? parseStart(byId.get(8)) : null;

const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const markerIndex = decoderSource.indexOf(marker);

if (markerIndex < 0) throw new Error("Decoder bootstrap marker missing.");

const importStart = decoderSource.indexOf("const bodies = new Map()");
const bodyMapText = decoderSource.slice(
    importStart >= 0 ? importStart : 0,
    markerIndex
);

log("ROOSTER WASM — STARTUP AND EXPORT INSPECTION");
log("READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION");
log(`WASM SIZE: ${wasm.length}`);
log(`SECTIONS: ${sections.length}`);
log(`START FUNCTION: ${startFunction === null ? "none declared" : startFunction}`);
log("=".repeat(78));

log("\nEXPORTS THAT MAY RELATE TO INITIALIZATION");
for (const e of exports) {
    if (e.kind !== 0) continue;

    if (/start|init|ctor|main|runtime|initialize|setup|wasm/i.test(e.name)) {
        log(`function ${e.index}  ${e.name}`);
    }
}

log("\nALL FUNCTION EXPORTS");
for (const e of exports.filter(x => x.kind === 0)) {
    log(`function ${e.index}  ${e.name}`);
}

log("\nRELEVANT SECTION LOCATIONS");
for (const s of sections) {
    log(`section ${String(s.id).padStart(2)} | start=0x${s.start.toString(16)} | size=${s.size}`);
}

log("\nDECODER BOOTSTRAP MARKERS");
for (const term of [
    "const bodies = new Map()",
    "bodies.set(83 + ordinal",
    "function decodeFunction(func)",
    "WebAssembly",
    "instantiate"
]) {
    const idx = decoderSource.indexOf(term);
    log(`${term}: ${idx < 0 ? "not found" : `source offset ${idx}`}`);
}

log("\nNOTES");
log("- Export kind 0 means function; other export kinds are not listed above.");
log("- A missing start section does not mean the module has no initialization.");
log("- Emscripten-style constructors may be called through an exported function.");
log("- This script parses metadata only; it does not execute WASM.");

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join("\n"), "utf8");
console.log(lines.join("\n"));
console.log(`\nSaved: ${outputPath}`);

FILE: scripts\inspect7481Family.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGETS = [7481, 7482, 7483, 7484, 7485];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT FUNCTION FAMILY 7481..7485");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", buf.length);
console.log("TARGETS   :", TARGETS.join(", "));
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function uleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: result, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function sleb(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        result |= (~0 << shift);
      }

      return {
        value: result | 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    if (shift > 35) return null;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decodeOne(pos, end) {
  const start = pos;

  if (pos >= end) return null;

  const op = buf[pos++];

  const ins = {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
  };

  // local.get / set / tee
  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;

    const n =
      op === 0x20 ? "local.get" :
      op === 0x21 ? "local.set" :
      "local.tee";

    ins.text = `${n} local=${a.value}`;
    ins.local = a.value;

    return ins;
  }

  // i32.const
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const a = sleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.value = a.value;
    ins.text = `i32.const ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  // i64.const
  if (op === 0x42) {
    const a = sleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.value = a.value;
    ins.text = `i64.const ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  // call
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.callTarget = a.value;
    ins.text = `call ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  // call_indirect
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.text =
      `call_indirect type=${a.value} table=${b.value}`;

    ins.indirectType = a.value;

    return ins;
  }

  // i32.load
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text =
      `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    ins.isLoad = true;

    return ins;
  }

  // i64.load
  if (op === 0x29) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text =
      `i64.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    ins.isLoad = true;

    return ins;
  }

  // i32.load8_u
  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text =
      `i32.load8_u align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    ins.isLoad = true;

    return ins;
  }

  // i32.store
  if (op === 0x36) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text =
      `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    ins.isStore = true;

    return ins;
  }

  // i64.store
  if (op === 0x37) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text =
      `i64.store align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    ins.isStore = true;

    return ins;
  }

  // store8
  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    const b = uleb(a.next);
    if (!b) return null;

    ins.end = b.next;
    ins.offset = b.value;
    ins.text =
      `i32.store8 align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`;

    ins.isStore = true;

    return ins;
  }

  // add/sub
  if (op === 0x6a) {
    ins.text = "i32.add";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x6b) {
    ins.text = "i32.sub";
    return ins;
  }

  // comparisons
  const simple = {
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",
    0x67: "i32.clz"
  };

  if (simple[op]) {
    ins.text = simple[op];
    return ins;
  }

  // control flow
  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
    if (pos >= end) return null;

    const blockType = buf[pos++];

    ins.end = pos;
    ins.text =
      op === 0x02 ? `block type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}` :
      op === 0x03 ? `loop type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}` :
      `if type=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    ins.text = "else";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    ins.text = "end";
    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const a = uleb(pos);
    if (!a) return null;

    ins.end = a.next;
    ins.text =
      `${op === 0x0c ? "br" : "br_if"} ${a.value}`;

    return ins;
  }

  if (op === 0x0f) {
    ins.text = "return";
    return ins;
  }

  return ins;
}

/*
 * Find code section.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const sectionId = buf[p++];

  const sizeInfo = uleb(p);
  if (!sizeInfo) {
    throw new Error("Bad section size");
  }

  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

/*
 * Locate requested bodies.
 */
let q = codeStart;

const countInfo = uleb(q);
q = countInfo.next;

const bodyCount = countInfo.value;

const targets = new Map();

for (let func = 0; func < bodyCount; func++) {
  const sizeInfo = uleb(q);

  if (!sizeInfo) {
    throw new Error("Bad function body size");
  }

  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  if (TARGETS.includes(func)) {
    targets.set(func, {
      func,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      bodySize
    });
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("CODE BODY COUNT :", bodyCount);
console.log("FOUND TARGETS   :", targets.size);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const func of TARGETS) {
  const target = targets.get(func);

  if (!target) {
    console.log("FUNC", func, "NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("FUNC", func);
  console.log("BODY", hex(target.bodyStart), "..", hex(target.bodyEnd));
  console.log("SIZE", target.bodySize);
  console.log("============================================================");

  let x = target.bodyStart;

  const localInfo = uleb(x);

  if (!localInfo) {
    console.log("LOCAL DECLARATION FAILURE");
    continue;
  }

  const localGroups = localInfo.value;
  x = localInfo.next;

  for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
    const n = uleb(x);
    if (!n) break;

    x = n.next + 1;
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (x < target.bodyEnd) {
    const ins = decodeOne(x, target.bodyEnd);

    if (!ins) {
      console.log("DECODER FAILURE @", hex(x));
      break;
    }

    instructions.push(ins);

    x = ins.end;
  }

  /*
   * Important loads/stores.
   */
  console.log("");
  console.log("RELEVANT MEMORY OPS:");

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];

    if (
      (ins.isLoad || ins.isStore) &&
      [0, 4, 8, 12, 32, 36, 40, 64, 80, 120, 128].includes(ins.offset)
    ) {
      console.log(
        hex(ins.start),
        ins.text
      );
    }
  }

  /*
   * Indirect calls.
   */
  console.log("");
  console.log("INDIRECT CALLS:");

  for (const ins of instructions) {
    if (ins.indirectType !== undefined) {
      console.log(
        hex(ins.start),
        ins.text
      );
    }
  }

  /*
   * Direct calls to interesting functions.
   */
  console.log("");
  console.log("CALLS TO FAMILY / RELATED:");

  for (const ins of instructions) {
    if (
      ins.callTarget !== undefined &&
      (
        TARGETS.includes(ins.callTarget) ||
        [248, 446, 344, 479].includes(ins.callTarget)
      )
    ) {
      console.log(
        hex(ins.start),
        ins.text
      );
    }
  }

  /*
   * Constants 4275 / 2053 and nearby constants.
   */
  console.log("");
  console.log("DISPATCH CONSTANT CHECK:");

  let foundConstant = false;

  for (const ins of instructions) {
    if (
      ins.text === "i32.const 4275" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 2053" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 7484" ||
      ins.text === "i32.const 8952"
    ) {
      console.log(
        hex(ins.start),
        ins.text,
        "<=== TARGET"
      );

      foundConstant = true;
    }
  }

  if (!foundConstant) {
    console.log("NO TARGET CONSTANTS");
  }

  /*
   * Print compact instruction listing.
   */
  console.log("");
  console.log("FULL INSTRUCTION LIST:");

  for (const ins of instructions) {
    console.log(
      hex(ins.start),
      ins.text
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspect7969Data.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FILE_OFFSET = 0x004d7c64;
const TARGET_VALUE = 7969;

function readULEB(
    wasm: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const b = wasm[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: p };

        shift += 7;
    }

    throw new Error("ULEB EOF");
}

function readSLEB(
    wasm: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (-1 << shift);

    return {
        value,
        next: p,
    };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function u32(
    wasm: Buffer,
    p: number
): number {
    return (
        wasm[p] |
        (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function findDataSection(wasm: Buffer) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const size = readULEB(wasm, p);

        const payloadStart = size.next;
        const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size.value;

        if (id === 11) {
            return {
                payloadStart,
                payloadEnd,
            };
        }

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("Data section not found");
}

function inspectBytes(
    wasm: Buffer,
    start: number,
    end: number,
    target: number
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" RAW DATA WINDOW");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const before = 128;
    const after = 128;

    const a = Math.max(start, target - before);
    const b = Math.min(end, target + after + 4);

    console.log(
        `Window: ${hex(a)} .. ${hex(b)}`
    );

    console.log("");

    for (
        let p = a;
        p + 4 <= b;
        p += 4
    ) {
        const value = u32(wasm, p);

        const mark =
            p === target
                ? "  <<< TARGET 7969"
                : "";

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${value.toString().padStart(10)}  ${hex(value)}${mark}`
        );
    }
}

function inspectDataSegments(
    wasm: Buffer
) {
    const sec = findDataSection(wasm);

    let p = sec.payloadStart;

    const countInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
    const count = countInfo.value;

    p = countInfo.next;

    console.log(
        `Data segments: ${count}`
    );

    console.log("");

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
        const flagsInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);
        const flags = flagsInfo.value;

        p = flagsInfo.next;

        let offset = 0;
        let memoryIndex = 0;

        if (flags === 0) {
            if (wasm[p] !== 0x41)
                throw new Error(
                    `Segment ${seg}: unsupported offset`
                );

            p++;

            const off = readSLEB(wasm, p);

            offset = off.value >>> 0;
            p = off.next;

            if (wasm[p] !== 0x0b)
                throw new Error(
                    `Segment ${seg}: missing end`
                );

            p++;
        }
        else if (flags === 2) {
            const mem = readULEB(wasm, p);

            memoryIndex = mem.value;
            p = mem.next;

            if (wasm[p] !== 0x41)
                throw new Error(
                    `Segment ${seg}: unsupported offset`
                );

            p++;

            const off = readSLEB(wasm, p);

            offset = off.value >>> 0;
            p = off.next;

            if (wasm[p] !== 0x0b)
                throw new Error(
                    `Segment ${seg}: missing end`
                );

            p++;
        }

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(wasm, p);

        const size = sizeInfo.value;
        const dataStart = sizeInfo.next;
        const dataEnd = dataStart + size;

        if (
            TARGET_FILE_OFFSET >= dataStart &&
            TARGET_FILE_OFFSET < dataEnd
        ) {
            const runtimeAddress =
                offset +
                (TARGET_FILE_OFFSET - dataStart);

            console.log(
                "============================================================"
            );
            console.log(
                " TARGET 7969 FOUND IN DATA SEGMENT"
            );
            console.log(
                "============================================================"
            );

            console.log(
                `Segment       : ${seg}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Flags         : ${flags}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Memory index  : ${memoryIndex}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Data start    : ${hex(dataStart)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Data end      : ${hex(dataEnd)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Segment size  : ${size}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Runtime start : ${hex(offset)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `File target   : ${hex(TARGET_FILE_OFFSET)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Runtime target: ${hex(runtimeAddress)}`
            );

            console.log("");

            const relative =
                TARGET_FILE_OFFSET - dataStart;

            console.log(
                `Relative      : ${hex(relative)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Value         : ${u32(wasm, TARGET_FILE_OFFSET)}`
            );

            console.log(
                `Value hex     : ${hex(
                    u32(wasm, TARGET_FILE_OFFSET)
                )}`
            );

            inspectBytes(
                wasm,
                dataStart,
                dataEnd,
                TARGET_FILE_OFFSET
            );

            return;
        }

        p = dataEnd;
    }

    console.log(
        "TARGET NOT FOUND INSIDE DATA SEGMENTS"
    );
}

function scanNearbyFunctionLikeValues(
    wasm: Buffer
) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" NEARBY VALUES THAT LOOK LIKE FUNCTION/TABLE INDICES");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const start =
        Math.max(0, TARGET_FILE_OFFSET - 512);

    const end =
        Math.min(
            wasm.length,
            TARGET_FILE_OFFSET + 512
        );

    for (
        let p = start;
        p + 4 <= end;
        p += 4
    ) {
        const v = u32(wasm, p);

        if (
            v === TARGET_VALUE ||
            (v >= 4000 && v <= 10000)
        ) {
            console.log(
                `${hex(p)} -> ${v} (${hex(v)})`
            );
        }
    }
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" INSPECT TABLE INDEX 7969 DATA");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    console.log(
        `WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target file offset : ${hex(TARGET_FILE_OFFSET)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target value       : ${TARGET_VALUE}`
    );

    inspectDataSegments(wasm);

    scanNearbyFunctionLikeValues(wasm);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspect8952StaticDescriptors.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    1613172,
    1613200,
    1613248,
    1613268,
    1613316,
    1613344,
];

const WINDOW_BEFORE = 32;
const WINDOW_AFTER = 64;

function u8(buf, p) {
    return buf[p];
}

function u16(buf, p) {
    return buf[p] | (buf[p + 1] << 8);
}

function u32(buf, p) {
    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function i32(buf, p) {
    return u32(buf, p) | 0;
}

function hex(n, width = 8) {
    return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function ascii(buf, start, end) {
    let s = "";
    for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
        const c = buf[p];
        s += c >= 32 && c <= 126 ? String.fromCharCode(c) : ".";
    }
    return s;
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT 8952 STATIC DESCRIPTORS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET RANGE : 1613172 .. 1613344");
console.log();

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    const start = Math.max(0, target - WINDOW_BEFORE);
    const end = Math.min(wasm.length, target + WINDOW_AFTER);

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`TARGET : ${target} (0x${hex(target)})`);
    console.log(
        `WINDOW : ${start} (0x${hex(start)}) .. ${end - 1} (0x${hex(end - 1)})`
    );
    console.log();

    console.log("BYTE DUMP");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const lineEnd = Math.min(p + 16, end);

        let bytes = "";
        for (let q = p; q < lineEnd; q++) {
            bytes += wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
        }

        while (bytes.length < 16 * 3) {
            bytes += "   ";
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${hex(p)}  ${bytes} | ${ascii(wasm, p, lineEnd)}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("U32 VALUES AT TARGET + OFFSETS");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const offsets = [
        -16, -12, -8, -4,
         0,   4,  8, 12,
        16,  20, 24, 28,
        32,  36, 40, 44,
        48,  52, 56, 60
    ];

    for (const off of offsets) {
        const p = target + off;

        if (p < 0 || p + 4 > wasm.length) continue;

        console.log(
            `OFFSET ${off.toString().padStart(4)}  ` +
            `ADDR 0x${hex(p)}  ` +
            `U32 ${u32(wasm, p).toString().padStart(10)}  ` +
            `0x${hex(u32(wasm, p))}  ` +
            `I32 ${i32(wasm, p)}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET CONSTANTS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    console.log(
        `${target.toString().padEnd(10)} ` +
        `0x${hex(target)}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

import { Address, TonClient4 } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ACCOUNT =
    '0:9220C181A6CFEACD11B7B8F62138DF1BB9CC82B6ED2661D2F5FAEE204B3EFB20';

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
    console.log(' STON.FI / pTON ACCOUNT IDENTIFICATION');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));

    const address = Address.parse(ACCOUNT);

    const client = new TonClient4({
        endpoint: 'https://sandbox-v4.tonhubapi.com',
    });

    console.log('\nACCOUNT:');
    console.log(ACCOUNT);

    console.log('\nGetting latest block...');

    const last = await client.getLastBlock();

    console.log(`block seqno: ${last.last.seqno}`);

    console.log('\nRunning get_wallet_data...');

    try {
        const result = await client.runMethod(
            last.last.seqno,
            address,
            'get_wallet_data'
        );

        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
        console.log('GET METHOD RESULT');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));

        console.log(`exitCode: ${result.exitCode}`);

        if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
            console.log('\nGET METHOD FAILED');
            console.log(result.resultRaw);
            return;
        }

        const reader = result.reader;

        const balance = reader.readBigNumber();
        const owner = reader.readAddress();
        const master = reader.readAddress();
        const code = reader.readCell();

        console.log('\nbalance:');
        console.log(balance.toString());

        console.log('\nowner:');
        console.log(owner?.toString() ?? 'null');

        console.log('\nmaster:');
        console.log(master?.toString() ?? 'null');

        console.log('\ncode hash:');
        console.log(code.hash().toString('hex'));

        console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
        console.log('COMPARISON');
        console.log('='.repeat(70));

        console.log('\nKnown pTON master:');
        console.log(
            'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
        );

        console.log('\nKnown Router pTON wallet:');
        console.log(
            'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
        );

        console.log('\nKnown user wallet:');
        console.log(
            '0:F19F930B6C5C95EB8F7E381BE242B329E4AA968C61CD57014CE864DE68B97C37'
        );

    } catch (error) {
        console.log('\nget_wallet_data FAILED');

        console.log(
            error instanceof Error
                ? error.stack ?? error.message
                : String(error)
        );
    }

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
    console.log('DONE');
    console.log('='.repeat(70));
}

    const end = addr + 16;

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 8) {

        console.log(
            mark,

            "0x" + bytes[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );
    }

    console.log("");
}

FILE: scripts\inspectActualOperandParserV22.ts

import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOT =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\node_modules\\@tact-lang\\opcode\\dist';

function inspect(file: string, patterns: string[]) {
    console.log();
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(`FILE: ${file}`);
    console.log('============================================================');

    if (!fs.existsSync(file)) {
        console.log('[MISSING]');
        return;
    }

    const lines = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/);

    for (const pattern of patterns) {
        let count = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
            if (!lines[i].toLowerCase().includes(pattern.toLowerCase())) {
                continue;
            }

            count++;

            console.log();
            console.log(`PATTERN: ${pattern}`);
            console.log(`--- lines ${Math.max(1, i - 8)}-${Math.min(lines.length, i + 35)} ---`);

            const start = Math.max(0, i - 8);
            const end = Math.min(lines.length, i + 35);

            for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {
                console.log(
                    `${String(j + 1).padStart(5, ' ')} | ${lines[j]}`
                );
            }
        }

        console.log(`Matches: ${count}`);
    }
}

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ACTUAL OPERAND PARSER INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' LOCAL NODE_MODULES ONLY');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('========================================================================');

    const operandLoader = path.join(
        ROOT,
        'decompiler',
        'operand-loader.js'
    );

    const prefixMatcher = path.join(
        ROOT,
        'utils',
        'prefix-matcher.js'
    );

    inspect(operandLoader, [
        'function parseInstruction',
        'type === "uint"',
        'type === "int"',
        'type === "ref"',
        'type === "slice"',
        'type === "subslice"',
        'loadBits',
        'loadUint',
        'loadRef',
        'loadSlice',
        'loadUintBig',
        'loadInt',
    ]);

    inspect(prefixMatcher, [
        'class PrefixMatcher',
        'loadPrefix',
        'prefix',
        'match',
        'mnemonic',
        'PUSHCONT',
    ]);

    console.log();
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('========================================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectAddressUsers.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF');
        const byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if (!(byte & 0x80)) return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    }
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error('Code section not found');

const count = uleb(codeStart);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    bodies.set(83 + ordinal, { start, end, size: size.value });
    pos = end;
}

for (const func of [1184, 1776]) {
    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC ${func}`);

    const body = bodies.get(func);

    if (!body) {
        console.log('BODY NOT FOUND');
        continue;
    }

    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE = ${body.size}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p += 16) {
        const end = Math.min(p + 16, body.end);
        const bytes = [...wasm.subarray(p, end)]
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ');

        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes}`);
    }
}

console.log('\nDONE - READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectAddressWriters.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF');
        const byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    }
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error('Code section not found');

const count = uleb(codeStart);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    bodies.set(83 + ordinal, {
        start,
        end,
        size: size.value
    });

    pos = end;
}

function dump(start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const stop = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [...wasm.subarray(p, stop)]
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ');

        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes}`);
    }
}

const targets = [1768, 1777, 9480];

console.log('ADDRESS WRITER INSPECTION');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${count.value}`);

for (const func of targets) {
    const body = bodies.get(func);

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC ${func}`);

    if (!body) {
        console.log('BODY NOT FOUND');
        continue;
    }

    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE = ${body.size}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    dump(body.start, body.end);
}

console.log('\nDONE - READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectBulkCallerBodies.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
  { func: 2900, pc: 0x000e775e, target: 1907, name: "memory.copy" },
  { func: 7983, pc: 0x00276a77, target: 480, name: "memory.fill" },
  { func: 7984, pc: 0x00276a96, target: 480, name: "memory.fill" },
  { func: 8364, pc: 0x002b8b8d, target: 480, name: "memory.fill" },
  { func: 8408, pc: 0x002bba0c, target: 480, name: "memory.fill" },
  { func: 8410, pc: 0x002bbac6, target: 480, name: "memory.fill" },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readU32(p: number): number {
  return (
    wasm[p] |
    (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let byte = 0;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    byte = wasm[p++];
    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size;

    if (sectionId === id) {
      return { start, end, size };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function parseCodeBodies() {
  const sec = findSection(10);

  if (!sec) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  }

  let p = sec.start;
  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  const bodies: {
    ordinal: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    bodyStart: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd,
      bodyStart,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return { sec, bodies };
}

function opcodeName(op: number) {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",

    0xfc: "PREFIX_FC",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeOne(p: number) {
  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  let text = opcodeName(op);
  let imm: number | undefined;

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      imm = r.value;
      text += ` FUNC${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const type = readULEB(p);
      p = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      text += ` type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22:
    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x3a: {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text += ` align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      imm = r.value;
      text += ` ${r.value} (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = readULEB(p);
      p = sub.next;

      if (sub.value === 10) {
        text = "memory.copy";

        const dst = readULEB(p);
        p = dst.next;

        const src = readULEB(p);
        p = src.next;

        text += ` mem${dst.value} <- mem${src.value}`;
      } else if (sub.value === 11) {
        text = "memory.fill";

        const mem = readULEB(p);
        p = mem.next;

        text += ` mem${mem.value}`;
      } else if (sub.value === 8) {
        text = "memory.init";

        const data = readULEB(p);
        p = data.next;

        const mem = readULEB(p);
        p = mem.next;

        text += ` data=${data.value} mem=${mem.value}`;
      } else if (sub.value === 9) {
        text = "data.drop";

        const data = readULEB(p);
        p = data.next;

        text += ` data=${data.value}`;
      } else {
        text += ` sub=${sub.value}`;
      }

      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: p,
    op,
    text,
    imm,
  };
}

function decodeBody(bodyStart: number, bodyEnd: number) {
  let p = bodyStart;

  const localCountInfo = readULEB(p);
  const localCount = localCountInfo.value;
  p = localCountInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;
    p += 1;
  }

  const instructions: ReturnType<typeof decodeOne>[] = [];

  while (p < bodyEnd) {
    const ins = decodeOne(p);
    instructions.push(ins);
    p = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x0b) break;
  }

  return instructions;
}

console.log("INSPECT BULK CALLER BODIES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const { sec, bodies } = parseCodeBodies();

console.log(
  `CODE SECTION = ${hex(sec.start)} .. ${hex(sec.end)}`
);
console.log(`CODE BODIES  = ${bodies.length}`);
console.log();

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const body = bodies[target.func];

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${target.func} NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  const instructions = decodeBody(body.bodyStart, body.end);

  let idx = instructions.findIndex(
    x => x.start === target.pc
  );

  if (idx < 0) {
    console.log();
    console.log(`FUNC ${target.func}`);
    console.log(`CALL PC ${hex(target.pc)} NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  const from = Math.max(0, idx - 40);
  const to = Math.min(instructions.length, idx + 13);

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${target.func}`);
  console.log(`CALL PC     = ${hex(target.pc)}`);
  console.log(`TARGET FUNC = ${target.target}`);
  console.log(`TARGET      = ${target.name}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY        = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );
  console.log(
    `CONTEXT     = instructions ${from} .. ${to - 1}`
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];

    const marker =
      i === idx ? "  <<< CALL" :
      i === idx - 1 ? "  <<< BEFORE" :
      "             ";

    console.log(
      `${marker} ${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.text}`
    );
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  if (target.name === "memory.fill") {
    console.log(
      "EXPECTED STACK ORDER: destination, value, length"
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      "EXPECTED STACK ORDER: destination, source, length"
    );
  }

  console.log("============================================================");
}

console.log();
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectBulkFunctions.ts
