import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
  { func: 480, name: "FUNC480" },
  { func: 1907, name: "FUNC1907" },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p,
  };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

    if (sectionId === id) {
      return {
        start,
        end,
        size: sizeInfo.value,
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function parseBodies() {
  const sec = findSection(10);

  if (!sec) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  }

  let p = sec.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      start,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return {
    section: sec,
    bodies,
  };
}

function name(op: number) {
  const m: Record<number, string> = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",

    0x41: "i32.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",

    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",

    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",

    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",

    0xfc: "PREFIX_FC",
  };

  return m[op] ?? `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeOne(p: number) {
  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  let text = name(op);

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02:
    case 0x03:
    case 0x04: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0c:
    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` FUNC${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const type = readULEB(p);
      p = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      text += ` type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22:
    case 0x23:
    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text += ` ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x3a: {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text +=
        ` align=${align.value}` +
        ` offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;

      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      text +=
        ` ${r.value}` +
        ` (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;

      break;
    }

    case 0xfc: {
      const sub = readULEB(p);
      p = sub.next;

      if (sub.value === 8) {
        const data = readULEB(p);
        p = data.next;

        const mem = readULEB(p);
        p = mem.next;

        text =
          `memory.init data=${data.value}` +
          ` mem=${mem.value}`;
      }

      else if (sub.value === 9) {
        const data = readULEB(p);
        p = data.next;

        text =
          `data.drop data=${data.value}`;
      }

      else if (sub.value === 10) {
        const dst = readULEB(p);
        p = dst.next;

        const src = readULEB(p);
        p = src.next;

        text =
          `memory.copy mem${dst.value}` +
          ` <- mem${src.value}`;
      }

      else if (sub.value === 11) {
        const mem = readULEB(p);
        p = mem.next;

        text =
          `memory.fill mem${mem.value}`;
      }

      else {
        text += ` sub=${sub.value}`;
      }

      break;
    }

    default:
      break;
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: p,
    op,
    text,
  };
}

function decodeBody(start: number, end: number) {
  let p = start;

  const localCount = readULEB(p);
  p = localCount.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    // local type
    p++;
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const ins = decodeOne(p);

    instructions.push(ins);

    p = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x0b) {
      break;
    }
  }

  return instructions;
}

console.log("INSPECT BULK FUNCTIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const parsed = parseBodies();

console.log(
  `CODE SECTION = ${hex(parsed.section.start)} .. ${hex(parsed.section.end)}`
);

console.log(`CODE BODIES  = ${parsed.bodies.length}`);

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const body = parsed.bodies[target.func];

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`\nFUNC ${target.func} NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`${target.name}  GLOBAL FUNC = ${target.func}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );
  console.log("============================================================");

  const instructions = decodeBody(body.start, body.end);

  for (const ins of instructions) {
    console.log(
      `${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.text}`
    );
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const bulk = instructions.filter(
    x =>
      x.text.startsWith("memory.fill") ||
      x.text.startsWith("memory.copy") ||
      x.text.startsWith("memory.init") ||
      x.text.startsWith("data.drop")
  );

  console.log(`BULK MEMORY OPS = ${bulk.length}`);

  for (const x of bulk) {
    console.log(
      `  ${hex(x.start)}  ${x.text}`
    );
  }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET SELECTOR RANGE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("0x19534c .. 0x195358");
console.log();
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectBulkOperands.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
  { func: 480, pc: 0x2387e, name: "memory.fill" },
  { func: 480, pc: 0x238a8, name: "memory.fill" },
  { func: 480, pc: 0x23caa, name: "memory.fill" },
  { func: 480, pc: 0x23d21, name: "memory.fill" },
  { func: 480, pc: 0x2492d, name: "memory.fill" },

  { func: 1907, pc: 0x8f5aa, name: "memory.copy" },
  { func: 1907, pc: 0x8f776, name: "memory.copy" },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p,
  };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    if (sectionId === id) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function parseBodies() {
  const sec = findSection(10);

  if (!sec) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
  }

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    bodies.push({
      ordinal: i,
      start,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return {
    section: sec,
    bodies,
  };
}

function decodeOne(p: number) {
  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  let text = `opcode_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  switch (op) {
    case 0x02:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `block ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x03:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `loop ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x04:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `if ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x05:
      text = "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      text = "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `br ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x0d:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x0f:
      text = "return";
      break;

    case 0x10:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `call FUNC${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x11:
      {
        const type = readULEB(p);
        p = type.next;

        const table = readULEB(p);
        p = table.next;

        text =
          `call_indirect type=${type.value}` +
          ` table=${table.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x1a:
      text = "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x21:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x22:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x23:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x24:
      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x28:
    case 0x29:
    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
    case 0x36:
    case 0x37:
    case 0x3a:
      {
        const align = readULEB(p);
        p = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(p);
        p = offset.next;

        text =
          `memop align=${align.value}` +
          ` offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x41:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text =
          `i32.const ${r.value}` +
          ` (${hex(r.value >>> 0)})`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x42:
      {
        const r = readSLEB(p);
        p = r.next;

        text = `i64.const ${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x45:
      text = "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      text = "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      text = "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      text = "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      text = "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x6c:
      text = "i32.mul";
      break;

    case 0xa7:
      text = "i32.wrap_i64";
      break;

    case 0xfc:
      {
        const sub = readULEB(p);
        p = sub.next;

        if (sub.value === 8) {
          const data = readULEB(p);
          p = data.next;

          const mem = readULEB(p);
          p = mem.next;

          text =
            `memory.init data=${data.value}` +
            ` mem=${mem.value}`;
        }

        else if (sub.value === 9) {
          const data = readULEB(p);
          p = data.next;

          text = `data.drop data=${data.value}`;
        }

        else if (sub.value === 10) {
          const dst = readULEB(p);
          p = dst.next;

          const src = readULEB(p);
          p = src.next;

          text =
            `memory.copy mem${dst.value}` +
            ` <- mem${src.value}`;
        }

        else if (sub.value === 11) {
          const mem = readULEB(p);
          p = mem.next;

          text = `memory.fill mem${mem.value}`;
        }

        else {
          text = `PREFIX_FC sub=${sub.value}`;
        }
      }
      break;
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: p,
    op,
    text,
  };
}

function decodeBody(start: number, end: number) {
  let p = start;

  const locals = readULEB(p);
  p = locals.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;
    p++;
  }

  const instructions = [];

  let depth = 1;

  while (p < end) {
    const ins = decodeOne(p);

    instructions.push(ins);

    p = ins.end;

    if (
      ins.op === 0x02 ||
      ins.op === 0x03 ||
      ins.op === 0x04
    ) {
      depth++;
    }

    else if (ins.op === 0x0b) {
      depth--;

      if (depth === 0) {
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  return instructions;
}

console.log("INSPECT BULK OPERANDS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const parsed = parseBodies();

console.log(
  `CODE SECTION = ${hex(parsed.section.start)} .. ${hex(parsed.section.end)}`
);

console.log(`CODE BODIES  = ${parsed.bodies.length}`);

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  const body = parsed.bodies[target.func];

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${target.func} NOT FOUND`);
    continue;
  }

  const instructions = decodeBody(
    body.start,
    body.end
  );

  const index = instructions.findIndex(
    x => x.start === target.pc
  );

  console.log();
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${target.func}`);
  console.log(`BULK PC     = ${hex(target.pc)}`);
  console.log(`OPERATION   = ${target.name}`);
  console.log(`BODY        = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  if (index < 0) {
    console.log("TARGET PC NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  const from = Math.max(0, index - 25);
  const to = Math.min(
    instructions.length,
    index + 4
  );

  for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];

    const marker =
      i === index
        ? " <<< TARGET"
        : "";

    console.log(
      `${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.text}${marker}`
    );
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  if (target.name === "memory.fill") {
    console.log(
      "OPERAND ORDER = destination, value, length"
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      "OPERAND ORDER = destination, source, length"
    );
  }
}

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const targets = [
  0x0031bbab,
  0x0031bdc5,
  0x0031c0f0,
  0x0031c588
];

function dump(offset) {
  const start = Math.max(0, offset - 40);
  const end = Math.min(buf.length, offset + 40);

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(
    "OFFSET : 0x" + offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
  );
  console.log("============================================================");

  for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
    let hex = "";

    for (let j = 0; j < 16 && p + j < end; j++) {
      const x = p + j;

      if (x === offset) hex += "[";
      hex += buf[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
      if (x === offset + 1) hex += "]";

      hex += " ";
    }

    console.log(
      "0x" + p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
      hex
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("TARGET BYTES:");
  console.log(
    buf[offset].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
    buf[offset + 1].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT CALL 96 CANDIDATES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const x of targets) {
  dump(x);
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectCallbackFamily.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([
  8950,
  8951,
  8952,
  8953,
  8954,
  100,
  117
]);

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte;

  while (true) {
    byte = buf[pos++];
    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(byte & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function readName(pos) {
  const n = readULEB(pos);
  const start = n.next;
  const end = start + n.value;
  return {
    value: buf.subarray(start, end).toString(),
    next: end
  };
}

function skipLimits(pos) {
  const flags = u8(pos++);

  const min = readULEB(pos);
  pos = min.next;

  if (flags & 1) {
    const max = readULEB(pos);
    pos = max.next;
  }

  return pos;
}

function skipTableType(pos) {
  pos++; // element type
  return skipLimits(pos);
}

function skipMemType(pos) {
  pos++; // flags / limits handled below
  return pos;
}

function skipGlobalType(pos) {
  pos++; // valtype
  pos++; // mutability
  return pos;
}

function skipInitExpr(pos) {
  while (true) {
    const op = u8(pos++);

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      return pos;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      pos = readSLEB(pos).next;
    } else if (op === 0x42) {
      let shift = 0;
      while (true) {
        const b = u8(pos++);
        shift += 7;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
      }
    } else if (op === 0x43) {
      pos += 4;
    } else if (op === 0x44) {
      pos += 8;
    } else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0xd2) {
      pos = readULEB(pos).next;
    } else {
      // Simple init expression opcode.
    }
  }
}

function parseSections() {
  let p = 8;

  let types = [];
  let funcTypeIndices = [];
  let codeBodies = [];

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = u8(p++);
    const sz = readULEB(p);
    const sectionSize = sz.value;
    p = sz.next;

    const end = p + sectionSize;

    if (id === 1) {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const form = u8(p++);

        if (form !== 0x60) {
          throw new Error("Unexpected type form at " + p.toString(16));
        }

        const np = readULEB(p);
        p = np.next;

        const params = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < np.value; j++) {
          params.push(u8(p++));
        }

        const nr = readULEB(p);
        p = nr.next;

        const results = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < nr.value; j++) {
          results.push(u8(p++));
        }

        types.push({ params, results });
      }
    }

    else if (id === 3) {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const t = readULEB(p);
        p = t.next;
        funcTypeIndices.push(t.value);
      }
    }

    else if (id === 10) {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const bodySize = readULEB(p);
        const size = bodySize.value;
        p = bodySize.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + size;

        codeBodies.push({
          bodyStart,
          bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return {
    types,
    funcTypeIndices,
    codeBodies
  };
}

function opcodeName(op) {
  const m = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",

    0x3a: "i32.store8",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul",

    0xd0: "ref.null",
    0xd2: "ref.func"
  };

  return m[op] || `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeBody(start, end) {
  let p = start;

  const localGroups = readULEB(p);
  p = localGroups.next;

  const locals = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;

    const type = u8(p++);

    locals.push({
      count: n.value,
      type
    });
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (p < end) {
    const offset = p;
    const op = u8(p++);

    const item = {
      offset,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op)
    };

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const v = readSLEB(p);
      item.value = v.value;
      p = v.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x42) {
      let shift = 0;
      let value = 0;

      while (true) {
        const b = u8(p++);
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

        shift += 7;
      }

      item.value = value;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      item.func = x.value;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      item.type = t.value;
      item.table = table.value;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22
    ) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      item.local = x.value;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x23 ||
      op === 0x24
    ) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      item.global = x.value;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x3a
    ) {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offsetImm = readULEB(p);
      p = offsetImm.next;

      item.align = align.value;
      item.offsetImm = offsetImm.value;
    }

    instructions.push(item);

    if (op === 0x0b && p >= end) break;
  }

  return {
    locals,
    instructions
  };
}

const parsed = parseSections();

const importedFunctionCount = 83;

function getFunctionType(funcId) {
  if (funcId < importedFunctionCount) {
    return null;
  }

  const definedIndex = funcId - importedFunctionCount;
  const typeIndex = parsed.funcTypeIndices[definedIndex];

  return {
    typeIndex,
    type: parsed.types[typeIndex]
  };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALLBACK FAMILY INSPECTION");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

for (const funcId of [...TARGETS].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
  const definedIndex = funcId - importedFunctionCount;
  const body = parsed.codeBodies[definedIndex];
  const ft = getFunctionType(funcId);

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`FUNC ${funcId}`);

  if (!body) {
    console.log("BODY : NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    `BODY : 0x${body.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${body.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
  );

  if (ft?.type) {
    const p = ft.type.params.map(x => `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(",");
    const r = ft.type.results.map(x => `0x${x.toString(16)}`).join(",");

    console.log(`TYPE : ${ft.typeIndex}`);
    console.log(`SIG  : (${p}) -> (${r})`);
  } else {
    console.log("TYPE : unknown");
  }

  const decoded = decodeBody(body.bodyStart, body.bodyEnd);

  console.log(
    `LOCALS GROUPS : ${decoded.locals.length}`
  );

  if (decoded.locals.length) {
    console.log(
      "LOCALS : " +
      decoded.locals
        .map(x => `${x.count}x0x${x.type.toString(16)}`)
        .join(", ")
    );
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS:");

  let important = 0;

  for (const ins of decoded.instructions) {
    if (
      ins.name === "call" ||
      ins.name === "call_indirect" ||
      ins.name === "i32.const" ||
      ins.name === "i32.load" ||
      ins.name === "i32.load8_u" ||
      ins.name === "i32.store" ||
      ins.name === "i64.load" ||
      ins.name === "i64.store" ||
      ins.name === "local.get" ||
      ins.name === "local.set" ||
      ins.name === "local.tee" ||
      ins.name === "i32.add" ||
      ins.name === "i32.sub" ||
      ins.name === "i32.eq"
    ) {
      let s =
        `0x${ins.offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ${ins.name}`;

      if (ins.value !== undefined) {
        s += ` ${ins.value}`;
      }

      if (ins.func !== undefined) {
        s += ` FUNC=${ins.func}`;
      }

      if (ins.type !== undefined) {
        s += ` TYPE=${ins.type} TABLE=${ins.table}`;
      }

      if (ins.local !== undefined) {
        s += ` LOCAL=${ins.local}`;
      }

      if (ins.offsetImm !== undefined) {
        s += ` OFFSET=${ins.offsetImm}`;
      }

      console.log(s);
      important++;
    }
  }

  console.log();
  console.log(
    `DIRECT CALLS : ${decoded.instructions.filter(x => x.name === "call").length}`
  );

  for (const ins of decoded.instructions) {
    if (ins.name === "call") {
      console.log(`  -> FUNC ${ins.func}`);
    }
  }

  console.log(
    `INDIRECT CALLS : ${decoded.instructions.filter(x => x.name === "call_indirect").length}`
  );

  for (const ins of decoded.instructions) {
    if (ins.name === "call_indirect") {
      console.log(
        `  -> TYPE ${ins.type}, TABLE ${ins.table}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectCallers248.js

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 248;
const CONTEXT = 40;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

function skipBlockType(pos) {
  const b = wasm[pos];

  if (
    b === 0x40 ||
    b === 0x7f ||
    b === 0x7e ||
    b === 0x7d ||
    b === 0x7c ||
    b === 0x7b ||
    b === 0x70 ||
    b === 0x6f
  ) {
    return pos + 1;
  }

  return readSLEB(pos).next;
}

function skipMemArg(pos) {
  const align = readULEB(pos);
  const offset = readULEB(align.next);

  return {
    next: offset.next,
    align: align.value,
    offset: offset.value
  };
}

function decodeInstructions(start, end) {
  const instructions = [];

  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const opPos = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    try {
      let text = `0x${opPos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  `;

      switch (op) {
        case 0x00:
          text += "unreachable";
          break;

        case 0x01:
          text += "nop";
          break;

        case 0x02:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text += "block";
          break;

        case 0x03:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text += "loop";
          break;

        case 0x04:
          p = skipBlockType(p);
          text += "if";
          break;

        case 0x05:
          text += "else";
          break;

        case 0x0b:
          text += "end";
          break;

        case 0x0c: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `br ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0d: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `br_if ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0e: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;

          const labels = [];

          for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            labels.push(x.value);
          }

          const def = readULEB(p);
          p = def.next;

          text += `br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${def.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x0f:
          text += "return";
          break;

        case 0x10: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `call ${r.value}`;

          if (r.value === TARGET) {
            text += "  <<< TARGET CALL";
          }

          break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
          const type = readULEB(p);
          p = type.next;

          const table = readULEB(p);
          p = table.next;

          text += `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x1a:
          text += "drop";
          break;

        case 0x1b:
          text += "select";
          break;

        case 0x20: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `local.get local=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x21: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `local.set local=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x22: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `local.tee local=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x23: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `global.get global=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x24: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `global.set global=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x25: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `table.get table=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x26: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `table.set table=${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2a:
        case 0x2b:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x2e:
        case 0x2f:
        case 0x30:
        case 0x31:
        case 0x32:
        case 0x33:
        case 0x34:
        case 0x35:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x38:
        case 0x39:
        case 0x3a:
        case 0x3b:
        case 0x3c:
        case 0x3d:
        case 0x3e: {
          const mem = skipMemArg(p);
          p = mem.next;

          const names = {
            0x28: "i32.load",
            0x29: "i64.load",
            0x2a: "f32.load",
            0x2b: "f64.load",
            0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
            0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
            0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
            0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
            0x30: "i64.load8_s",
            0x31: "i64.load8_u",
            0x32: "i64.load16_s",
            0x33: "i64.load16_u",
            0x34: "i64.load32_s",
            0x35: "i64.load32_u",
            0x36: "i32.store",
            0x37: "i64.store",
            0x38: "f32.store",
            0x39: "f64.store",
            0x3a: "i32.store8",
            0x3b: "i32.store16",
            0x3c: "i64.store8",
            0x3d: "i64.store16",
            0x3e: "i64.store32"
          };

          text += `${names[op]} align=${mem.align} offset=${mem.offset}`;

          break;
        }

        case 0x3f: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `memory.size ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x40: {
          const r = readULEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `memory.grow ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `i32.const ${r.value}`;

          if (r.value === 4275) {
            text += "  <<< 4275";
          }

          break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
          const r = readSLEB(p);
          p = r.next;
          text += `i64.const ${r.value}`;
          break;
        }

        case 0x43:
          p += 4;
          text += "f32.const";
          break;

        case 0x44:
          p += 8;
          text += "f64.const";
          break;

        default:
          text += `UNKNOWN opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

          instructions.push({
            pos: opPos,
            text
          });

          return instructions;
      }

      instructions.push({
        pos: opPos,
        text
      });
    } catch {
      return instructions;
    }
  }

  return instructions;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate Code Section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;
let bodies = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {
  const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

  const secSize = readULEB(pos);
  const size = secSize.value;
  pos = secSize.next;

  const secStart = pos;
  const secEnd = secStart + size;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    const count = readULEB(pos);
    pos = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const bodySize = readULEB(pos);
      pos = bodySize.next;

      const bodyStart = pos;
      const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

      bodies.push({
        index: i,
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd
      });

      pos = bodyEnd;
    }

    break;
  }

  pos = secEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find callers
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const callers = [];

for (const body of bodies) {
  const instructions = decodeInstructions(body.start, body.end);

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    if (
      instructions[i].text.includes(
        `call ${TARGET}`
      )
    ) {
      callers.push({
        func: body.index,
        instructions,
        index: i
      });
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET FUNC :", TARGET);
console.log("CALLERS :", callers.length);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const hit of callers) {
  console.log("");

  console.log(
    `FUNC ${hit.func} -> CALL 248 @ 0x${hit.instructions[hit.index].pos
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(8, "0")}`
  );
  console.log("============================================================");

  const start = Math.max(0, hit.index - CONTEXT);
  const end = Math.min(
    hit.instructions.length,
    hit.index + 1
  );

  for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
    const marker = i === hit.index ? "  >>>" : "     ";
    console.log(marker + hit.instructions[i].text);
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const CALLS = [
    0x000d4392,
    0x002585c5,
];

const BEFORE = 160;
const AFTER = 48;

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dump(addr: number) {
    const start = Math.max(0, addr - BEFORE);
    const end = Math.min(b.length, addr + AFTER);

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`CALL @ ${hex(addr)}`);
    console.log(`Range ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const q = Math.min(p + 16, end);

        const bytes = [];
        const ascii = [];

        for (let i = p; i < q; i++) {
            bytes.push(b[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
            ascii.push(
                b[i] >= 0x20 && b[i] <= 0x7e
                    ? String.fromCharCode(b[i])
                    : "."
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.join(" ").padEnd(47, " ")}  ${ascii.join("")}`
        );
    }
}

console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const addr of CALLS) {
    dump(addr);
}

console.log(" END");

import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const CODE_START = 0x00006e7d;
const CODE_END   = 0x00324ef5;

const TARGET_TYPE = 4;

const TARGETS = [
  0x002bacae,
  0x002bbfa9,
  0x002bf7e6,
  0x002ced71,
  0x0030211a,
  0x0030229c,
];

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];

    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op: number) {

  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x74: "i32.shl",

    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0f: "return",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL_INDIRECT CANDIDATE INSPECTOR");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {

  console.log("");
  console.log(`TARGET @ ${hex(target)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let found = false;

  for (let i = target; i >= CODE_START; i--) {

    if (b[i] !== 0x11)
      continue;

    try {

      const typeR = uleb(i + 1);
      const tableR = uleb(typeR.p);

      if (
        typeR.v !== TARGET_TYPE ||
        tableR.v !== 0
      )
        continue;

      if (i !== target)
        continue;

      found = true;

      const from = Math.max(CODE_START, i - 100);

      let p = from;

      while (p < i) {

        const pos = p;
        const op = b[p++];

        console.log(
          `${hex(pos)}  ${opcodeName(op)}`
        );

        /*
           Decode only common immediates needed for inspection.
        */

        if (
          op === 0x20 ||
          op === 0x21 ||
          op === 0x22 ||
          op === 0x23 ||
          op === 0x24 ||
          op === 0x10 ||
          op === 0x0c ||
          op === 0x0d
        ) {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.p;
          console.log(`           imm=${x.v}`);
        }
        else if (op === 0x41) {
          const x = uleb(p);
          p = x.p;
          console.log(`           imm=${x.v}`);
        }
        else if (
          op === 0x28 ||
          op === 0x29 ||
          op === 0x2c ||
          op === 0x2d ||
          op === 0x36 ||
          op === 0x37
        ) {
          const align = uleb(p);
          p = align.p;

          const offset = uleb(p);
          p = offset.p;

          console.log(
            `           offset=${offset.v}`
          );
        }
      }

      console.log(
        `${hex(i)}  call_indirect type=4 table=0`
      );

    } catch {
      // ignore
    }

    break;
  }

  if (!found)
    console.log("NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function uleb(pos:number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value: v, next: p };
}

function readVec(pos:number) {
  const n = uleb(pos);
  return { count:n.value, pos:n.next };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CALL_INDIRECT TYPE 3");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let p = 0;

// WASM header
if (wasm[0] !== 0x00 || wasm[1] !== 0x61 || wasm[2] !== 0x73 || wasm[3] !== 0x6d) {
  throw new Error("Not WASM");
}

p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const sectionId = wasm[p++];
  const sz = uleb(p);
  p = sz.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sz.value;

  if (sectionId === 1) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;

    console.log(`TYPE SECTION COUNT : ${n.value}`);
    console.log("");

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const form = wasm[p++];

      if (form !== 0x60) {
        console.log(`TYPE ${i}: form=0x${form.toString(16)}`);
        continue;
      }

      const pc = uleb(p);
      p = pc.next;

      const params:number[] = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
        params.push(wasm[p++]);
      }

      const rc = uleb(p);
      p = rc.next;

      const results:number[] = [];
      for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
        results.push(wasm[p++]);
      }

      if (i === 3) {
        const name = (x:number) => ({
          0x7f:"i32",
          0x7e:"i64",
          0x7d:"f32",
          0x7c:"f64",
          0x7b:"v128"
        } as any)[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`;

        console.log("TARGET TYPE INDEX : 3");
        console.log(
          `PARAMS  : ${params.map(name).join(", ") || "(none)"}`
        );
        console.log(
          `RESULTS : ${results.map(name).join(", ") || "(none)"}`
        );
      }
    }

    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

async function main() {
    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {
        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {
        console.log('===== GET CONFIG =====');

        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('Masterchain seqno:');
        console.log(master.last.seqno);

        const result = await client.getConfig(master.last);

        const config18 = result.config.get(18);

        if (!config18) {
            console.log('Config 18 NOT FOUND');
            return;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== CONFIG 18 ROOT =====');

        console.log('Bits:', config18.bits.length);
        console.log('Refs:', config18.refs.length);
        console.log('Type:', config18.type);
        console.log(
            'Hash:',
            config18.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        for (let i = 0; i < config18.refs.length; i++) {
            const ref = config18.refs[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(`===== CONFIG 18 REF ${i} =====`);

            console.log('Bits:', ref.bits.length);
            console.log('Refs:', ref.refs.length);
            console.log('Type:', ref.type);

            console.log('Hash:');
            console.log(
                ref.hash().toString('hex')
            );

            console.log('Bits string:');
            console.log(
                ref.bits.toString()
            );

            console.log('BOC:');
            console.log(
                Buffer.from(
                    ref.toBoc({ idx: false })
                ).toString('base64')
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== DONE =====');

    } finally {
        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {
    const part1 = int & 255;
    const part2 = (int >> 8) & 255;
    const part3 = (int >> 16) & 255;
    const part4 = (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
});

export async function run() {
    await main();
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteRoundRobinEngine,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

async function main() {
    const servers = [
        {
            ip: -1468571697,
            port: 27787,
            key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
        },
        {
            ip: -1468575011,
            port: 51088,
            key: 'Sy5ghr3EahQd/1rDayzZXt5+inlfF+7kLfkZDJcU/ek=',
        },
    ];

    const engines: LiteSingleEngine[] = [];

    for (const server of servers) {
        engines.push(
            new LiteSingleEngine({
                host: `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`,
                publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
                reconnectTimeout: 3000,
            })
        );
    }

    const engine = new LiteRoundRobinEngine(engines);

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {
        console.log('===== GET CONFIG 43 =====');

        const master = await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log('Masterchain seqno:');
        console.log(master.last.seqno);

        const result = await client.getConfig(master.last);

        const config43 = result.config.get(43);

        if (!config43) {
            console.log('Config 43 NOT FOUND');
            return;
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== CONFIG 43 ROOT =====');

        console.log('Bits:', config43.bits.length);
        console.log('Refs:', config43.refs.length);
        console.log('Type:', config43.type);

        console.log('Hash:');
        console.log(
            config43.hash().toString('hex')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== CONFIG 43 ROOT BITS =====');

        console.log(
            config43.bits.toString()
        );

        console.log('');

        for (let i = 0; i < config43.refs.length; i++) {
            const ref = config43.refs[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(`===== CONFIG 43 REF ${i} =====`);

            console.log('Bits:', ref.bits.length);
            console.log('Refs:', ref.refs.length);
            console.log('Type:', ref.type);

            console.log('Hash:');
            console.log(
                ref.hash().toString('hex')
            );

            console.log('Bits string:');
            console.log(
                ref.bits.toString()
            );

            console.log('BOC:');
            console.log(
                Buffer.from(
                    ref.toBoc({ idx: false })
                ).toString('base64')
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== CONFIG 43 BOC =====');

        console.log(
            Buffer.from(
                config43.toBoc({ idx: false })
            ).toString('base64')
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('===== DONE =====');

    } finally {
        for (const e of engines) {
            e.close();
        }
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {
    const part1 = int & 255;
    const part2 = (int >> 8) & 255;
    const part3 = (int >> 16) & 255;
    const part4 = (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {
    console.error('');
    console.error('===== ERROR =====');
    console.error(error);
    process.exit(1);
});

export async function run() {
    await main();
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 CONNECTED WALLET IDENTITY CHECK');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log(' NO WALLET SIGNATURE');
    console.log(' NO send() CALL');
    console.log('======================================================================');
    console.log('');

    const sender = provider.sender();

    if (!sender.address) {
        console.log('CONNECTED WALLET ADDRESS: NOT AVAILABLE');
        console.log('');
        console.log('RESULT: FAIL');
        console.log('Cannot identify the connected wallet.');
        return;
    }

    const connected = sender.address;

    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('1. CONNECTED WALLET');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log('CONNECTED FRIENDLY:');
    console.log(connected.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('CONNECTED RAW:');
    console.log(connected.toRawString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('2. EXPECTED USER');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log('EXPECTED FRIENDLY:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED RAW:');
    console.log(USER.toRawString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('3. RAW ADDRESS COMPARISON');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    const sameRaw =
        connected.toRawString() === USER.toRawString();

    console.log('CONNECTED RAW = EXPECTED RAW:', sameRaw ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL');

    console.log('');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log('4. ADDRESS FORMAT ANALYSIS');
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'Connected friendly:',
        connected.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected friendly:',
        USER.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Same underlying TON address:',
        sameRaw ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');

    if (sameRaw) {
        console.log('======================================================================');
        console.log(' RESULT: PASS');
        console.log('======================================================================');
        console.log('');
        console.log('The connected wallet and USER are the SAME underlying TON address.');
        console.log('');
        console.log('The different EQ / 0Q representation is only address formatting.');
        console.log('');
        console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT');
        console.log('NO WALLET SIGNATURE');
        console.log('NO FUNDS MOVED');
        console.log('');
    } else {
        console.log('======================================================================');
        console.log(' RESULT: FAIL');
        console.log('======================================================================');
        console.log('');
        console.log('The connected wallet is NOT the expected USER address.');
        console.log('');
        console.log('DO NOT SIGN OR SEND ANY TRANSACTION.');
        console.log('');
    }
}

FILE: scripts\inspectCp0PushcontV22.ts

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const CP0_PATH = path.join(
    process.cwd(),
    'node_modules',
    '@tact-lang',
    'opcode',
    'dist',
    'spec',
    'cp0.json'
);

export async function run(_provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' @TACT-LANG/OPCODE CP0 PUSHCONT INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - LOCAL FILE ONLY');
    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log();

    console.log(`File: ${CP0_PATH}`);
    console.log();

    if (!fs.existsSync(CP0_PATH)) {
        throw new Error(`cp0.json not found: ${CP0_PATH}`);
    }

    const cp0 = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(CP0_PATH, 'utf8'));

    console.log(`Instruction count: ${cp0.instructions?.length ?? 0}`);
    console.log();

    const names = [
        'PUSHCONT',
        'PUSHCONT_SHORT',
    ];

    for (const name of names) {
        console.log('================================================================');
        console.log(` SEARCH: ${name}`);
        console.log('================================================================');

        const matches = (cp0.instructions ?? []).filter(
            (inst: any) =>
                inst?.mnemonic === name ||
                inst?.mnemonic?.includes(name)
        );

        console.log(`Matches: ${matches.length}`);
        console.log();

        for (let i = 0; i < matches.length; i++) {
            const inst = matches[i];

            console.log(`--- Match ${i + 1} ---`);
            console.log(JSON.stringify(inst, null, 2));
            console.log();
        }
    }

    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' SEARCH PREFIXES AROUND 0x50C6A654');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log();

    const target = '50C6A654';

    for (const inst of cp0.instructions ?? []) {
        const prefix = inst?.bytecode?.prefix;

        if (typeof prefix !== 'string') {
            continue;
        }

        const normalized = prefix
            .replace(/[^0-9A-Fa-f]/g, '')
            .toUpperCase();

        if (
            normalized.startsWith(target.slice(0, 2)) ||
            target.startsWith(normalized.slice(0, 2))
        ) {
            console.log(JSON.stringify(inst, null, 2));
            console.log();
        }
    }

    console.log('========================================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log('========================================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectCurrentJettonNotifyResponse.ts
====================================================================================================
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v3';

const ROUTER_RAW =
    '0:92E1411AE546892F33B2C8A89EA90390D8FF4CFBB917A643B91E73F706FDB9D1';

const TX_LT =
    '99491281000007';

const DELAY_MS =
    1300;

function sleep(ms: number) {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

async function getJson(
    url: string,
    label: string
): Promise<any> {

    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 5; attempt++) {

        const response = await fetch(url);

        if (response.ok) {
            return await response.json();
        }

        if (response.status === 429) {
            const wait =
                DELAY_MS * attempt;

            console.log(
                `429 for ${label}; waiting ${wait} ms...`
            );

            await sleep(wait);
            continue;
        }

        throw new Error(
            `${label}: HTTP ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`
        );
    }

    throw new Error(
        `${label}: too many 429 responses`
    );
}

async function main(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {

    void provider;

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' CURRENT ROUTER JETTON_NOTIFY RESPONSE INSPECTOR'
    );
    console.log(
        ' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION'
    );
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );

    console.log('');
console.log(
    'Router raw:',
    ROUTER_RAW
);

    console.log(
        'Target LT:',
        TX_LT
    );

    /*
     * Find the exact Router transaction.
     */

    const txParams =
        new URLSearchParams();

    txParams.set(
        'account',
        ROUTER_RAW
    );

    txParams.set(
        'limit',
        '20'
    );

    txParams.set(
        'offset',
        '0'
    );

    const txUrl =
        `${TONCENTER}/transactions?${txParams}`;

    const txData =
        await getJson(
            txUrl,
            'ROUTER TRANSACTIONS'
        );

    const txs =
        txData.transactions ?? [];

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'Router transactions returned:',
        txs.length
    );

    const tx =
        txs.find(
            (x: any) =>
                String(x.lt) === TX_LT
        );

    if (!tx) {
        throw new Error(
            `Router transaction LT ${TX_LT} not found`
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TARGET ROUTER TRANSACTION FOUND'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TX HASH:',
        tx.hash ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'LT:',
        tx.lt ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'TRACE:',
        tx.trace_id ?? 'N/A'
    );

    /*
     * Exact input message.
     */

    const msg =
        tx.in_msg;

    if (!msg) {
        throw new Error(
            'Router transaction has no input message'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '========== INPUT MESSAGE =========='
    );

    console.log(
        'HASH:',
        msg.hash ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'SOURCE:',
        msg.source ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'DESTINATION:',
        msg.destination ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        msg.value ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'OPCODE:',
        msg.opcode ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'DECODED OPCODE:',
        msg.decoded_opcode ?? 'N/A'
    );

    /*
     * Print the COMPLETE raw message object.
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '========== RAW MESSAGE OBJECT =========='
    );

    console.log(
        JSON.stringify(
            msg,
            null,
            2
        )
    );

    /*
     * Specifically inspect every possible body field.
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '========== BODY FIELD INSPECTION =========='
    );

    console.log(
        'msg.body:',
        typeof msg.body,
        msg.body ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'msg.boc:',
        typeof msg.boc,
        msg.boc ?? 'N/A'
    );

    console.log(
        'msg.message_content:',
        typeof msg.message_content
    );

    if (msg.message_content) {

        console.log(
            'message_content.body:',
            typeof msg.message_content.body,
            msg.message_content.body ?? 'N/A'
        );

        console.log(
            'message_content.boc:',
            typeof msg.message_content.boc,
            msg.message_content.boc ?? 'N/A'
        );

        console.log(
            'message_content.value:',
            typeof msg.message_content.value,
            msg.message_content.value ?? 'N/A'
        );
    }

    /*
     * Also print the exact input object keys.
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '========== MESSAGE KEYS =========='
    );

    console.log(
        Object.keys(msg)
    );

    if (msg.message_content) {

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'MESSAGE_CONTENT KEYS:'
        );

        console.log(
            Object.keys(
                msg.message_content
            )
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
    console.log(
        ' END - READ ONLY'
    );
    console.log(
        '================================================'
    );
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    await main(provider);
}



====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectCurrentPoolV22.ts
====================================================================================================
import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000009n;

function opcode(body: any): string {
    try {
        if (!body || body.bits.length < 32) return 'NONE';

        const s = body.beginParse();
        return '0x' + s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'ERROR';
    }
}

function typeOf(op: string): string {
    switch (op) {
        case '0x37c096df': return 'stonfi_provide_lp_v2';
        case '0x50c6a654': return 'stonfi_add_liquidity_v2';
        case '0x06ecd527': return 'stonfi_cb_add_liquidity_v2';
        case '0x178d4519': return 'jetton_internal_transfer';
        case '0x0f8a7ea5': return 'jetton_transfer';
        case '0x01f3835d': return 'pton_ton_transfer';
        case '0x7362d09c': return 'jetton_notify';
        default: return 'unknown';
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' CURRENT POOL TRANSACTION v2.2');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    const client = provider.api() as TonClient;

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        POOL,
        {
            limit: 30,
        }
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(`Fetched ${txs.length} transactions`);

    let found = false;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        if (tx.lt !== TARGET_LT) continue;

        found = true;

        console.log('');
        console.log('================================================');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION FOUND');
        console.log('================================================');

        console.log(`LT:   ${tx.lt.toString()}`);
        console.log(`HASH: ${tx.hash().toString('base64')}`);

        if (tx.now) {
            console.log(
                `TIME: ${new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()}`
            );
        }

        const inBody = tx.inMessage?.body;
        const inOp = opcode(inBody);

        console.log('');
        console.log('----- INPUT -----');
        console.log(`OPCODE: ${inOp}`);
        console.log(`TYPE:   ${typeOf(inOp)}`);

        if (tx.inMessage?.info.type === 'internal') {
            console.log(
                `FROM:   ${tx.inMessage.info.src.toString()}`
            );

            console.log(
                `TO:     ${tx.inMessage.info.dest.toString()}`
            );

            console.log(
                `VALUE:  ${tx.inMessage.info.value.coins.toString()}`
            );
        }

        if (inBody && inBody.bits.length > 0) {
            console.log('');
            console.log('INPUT BODY BOC:');
            console.log(
                inBody.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('----- OUTPUTS -----');
        console.log(`COUNT: ${tx.outMessagesCount}`);

        for (let i = 0; i < tx.outMessagesCount; i++) {
            const msg = tx.outMessages.get(i);
if (!msg) continue;
            const body = msg.body;
            const op = opcode(body);

            console.log('');
            console.log(`OUTPUT #${i + 1}`);
            console.log(`OPCODE: ${op}`);
            console.log(`TYPE:   ${typeOf(op)}`);

            if (msg.info.type === 'internal') {
                console.log(
                    `FROM:  ${msg.info.src.toString()}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `TO:    ${msg.info.dest.toString()}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `VALUE: ${msg.info.value.coins.toString()}`
                );
            }

            if (body && body.bits.length > 0) {
                console.log('BODY BOC:');
                console.log(
                    body.toBoc().toString('base64')
                );
            }
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('----- FEES -----');

        if (tx.totalFees) {
            console.log(
                `TOTAL FEES: ${tx.totalFees.coins.toString()}`
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET LT NOT FOUND');
        console.log('');
        console.log('Available LT range:');

        if (txs.length > 0) {
            console.log(
                `${txs[0].lt.toString()} -> ${txs[txs.length - 1].lt.toString()}`
            );
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log(' Transaction submission: NONE');
    console.log('================================================');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectCurrentPtonV22.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000003n;
const RANGE = 15;

function getOpcode(body: any): string {
    try {
        if (!body || body.bits.length < 32) return 'NONE';

        const s = body.beginParse();
        return '0x' + s.loadUint(32).toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
    } catch {
        return 'DECODE_ERROR';
    }
}

function getType(opcode: string): string {
    switch (opcode) {
        case '0x01f3835d':
            return 'pton_ton_transfer';
        case '0x0f8a7ea5':
            return 'jetton_transfer';
        case '0x7362d09c':
            return 'jetton_notify';
        case '0x37c096df':
            return 'stonfi_provide_lp_v2';
        case '0x06ecd527':
            return 'stonfi_cb_add_liquidity_v2';
        case '0x50c6a654':
            return 'stonfi_add_liquidity_v2';
        default:
            return 'unknown';
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' CURRENT pTON WALLET TRANSACTIONS v2.2');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON Wallet:');
    console.log(PTON_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Target LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log(`Range: ${RANGE} transactions before/after`);
    console.log('');

    const client = provider.api() as TonClient;

    // Fetch recent transactions.
    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        PTON_WALLET,
        {
            limit: 50,
        }
    );

    // Sort newest -> oldest, as returned by TON.
    let targetIndex = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i < txs.length; i++) {
        if (txs[i].lt === TARGET_LT) {
            targetIndex = i;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (targetIndex === -1) {
        console.log('TARGET LT NOT FOUND IN FETCHED TRANSACTIONS');
        console.log('');
        console.log('Fetched LT range:');

        if (txs.length > 0) {
            console.log(
                `${txs[0].lt.toString()} -> ${txs[txs.length - 1].lt.toString()}`
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('No transaction was sent.');
        return;
    }

    const start = Math.max(0, targetIndex - RANGE);
    const end = Math.min(txs.length, targetIndex + RANGE + 1);

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(
        `SHOWING ${start + 1}-${end} OF ${txs.length}`
    );
    console.log('================================================');

    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
        const tx = txs[i];

        const hash = tx.hash().toString('base64');
        const opcode = getOpcode(tx.inMessage?.body);
        const type = getType(opcode);

        const time = tx.now
            ? new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
            : 'unknown';

        console.log('');
        console.log('-----------------------------------------------');
        console.log(`#${i + 1}`);
        console.log(`LT:       ${tx.lt.toString()}`);
        console.log(`TIME:     ${time}`);
        console.log(`HASH:     ${hash}`);
        console.log(`OPCODE:   ${opcode}`);
        console.log(`TYPE:     ${type}`);

        if (tx.inMessage?.info.type === 'internal') {
            console.log(
                `IN VALUE: ${tx.inMessage.info.value.coins.toString()}`
            );

            console.log(
                `IN FROM:  ${tx.inMessage.info.src.toString()}`
            );
        }

        if (tx.inMessage?.info.type === 'internal') {
            console.log(
                `IN TO:    ${tx.inMessage.info.dest.toString()}`
            );
        }

        if (tx.outMessagesCount > 0) {
            console.log(`OUT MSGS: ${tx.outMessagesCount}`);
        }

        // Print a larger body preview only for relevant operations.
        if (
            opcode === '0x01f3835d' ||
            opcode === '0x0f8a7ea5' ||
            opcode === '0x37c096df' ||
            opcode === '0x06ecd527'
        ) {
            try {
                const boc = tx.inMessage?.body
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64');

                console.log(`BODY BOC: ${boc}`);
            } catch {
                console.log('BODY BOC: <decode error>');
            }
        }
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================');
    console.log(' READ ONLY CHECK COMPLETE');
    console.log(' Transaction submission: NONE');
    console.log('================================================');
}

FILE: scripts\inspectData1953.ts
