import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [0x195350, 0x195354];

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function findSection(id: number) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];

        const s = readULEB(p);
        p = s.next;

        if (sectionId === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + s.value
            };
        }

        p += s.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

console.log("INSPECT DATA SECTION FOR 0x195350 / 0x195354");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const sec = findSection(11);

console.log(
    `DATA SECTION = 0x${sec.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${sec.end.toString(16)}`
);

let p = sec.start;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS = ${count.value}`);
console.log("");

let total = 0;

for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {

    const flags = readULEB(p);
    p = flags.next;

    let memoryIndex = 0;
    let offset = 0;

    if (flags.value === 0) {
        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op !== 0x41) {
            console.log(
                `SEGMENT ${seg}: unexpected offset opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
            );
            process.exit(1);
        }

        const x = readSLEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        offset = x.value >>> 0;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) {
            console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg}: missing offset end`);
            process.exit(1);
        }

    } else if (flags.value === 2) {

        const mi = readULEB(p);
        p = mi.next;

        memoryIndex = mi.value;

        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op !== 0x41) {
            console.log(
                `SEGMENT ${seg}: unexpected offset opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
            );
            process.exit(1);
        }

        const x = readSLEB(p);
        p = x.next;

        offset = x.value >>> 0;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) {
            console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg}: missing offset end`);
            process.exit(1);
        }

    } else if (flags.value === 1) {

        const size = readULEB(p);
        p = size.next;

        p += size.value;

        continue;

    } else if (flags.value === 3) {

        const mi = readULEB(p);
        p = mi.next;

        memoryIndex = mi.value;

    } else {

        console.log(
            `SEGMENT ${seg}: unsupported flags ${flags.value}`
        );

        continue;
    }

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const dataStart = p;
    const dataEnd = p + sizeInfo.value;

    console.log(
        `SEGMENT ${seg}: memory=${memoryIndex} ` +
        `offset=0x${offset.toString(16)} ` +
        `size=${sizeInfo.value}`
    );

    for (const target of TARGETS) {

        if (target < offset) continue;

        const rel = target - offset;

        if (rel < 0 || rel + 4 > sizeInfo.value) continue;

        const a = dataStart + rel;

        const b0 = wasm[a];
        const b1 = wasm[a + 1];
        const b2 = wasm[a + 2];
        const b3 = wasm[a + 3];

        const value =
            (b0 |
            (b1 << 8) |
            (b2 << 16) |
            (b3 << 24)) >>> 0;

        console.log("");
        console.log(
            `  TARGET 0x${target.toString(16)} (${target})`
        );
        console.log(
            `  DATA OFFSET = 0x${a.toString(16)}`
        );
        console.log(
            `  BYTES = ${[b0,b1,b2,b3]
                .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2,"0"))
                .join(" ")}`
        );
        console.log(
            `  VALUE = ${value} (0x${value.toString(16)})`
        );

        total++;
    }

    p = dataEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log(`TOTAL DATA MATCHES = ${total}`);
console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 0x0019534c;
const RANGE = 64;

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return {
        start,
        end,
        size: s.value,
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function dumpBytes(start: number, end: number) {
  const lines: string[] = [];

  for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
    const e = Math.min(p + 16, end);

    let hexes = "";

    for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
      hexes += wasm[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    let ascii = "";

    for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
      const b = wasm[q];

      ascii +=
        b >= 32 && b <= 126
          ? String.fromCharCode(b)
          : ".";
    }

    lines.push(
      `${hex(p)}  ${hexes.padEnd(48)} ${ascii}`
    );
  }

  return lines;
}

console.log("INSPECT DATA AROUND 0x19534C");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET MEMORY ADDRESS = ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log(`RANGE = ±${RANGE} BYTES`);
console.log("============================================================");

const data = findSection(11);

if (!data) {
  console.log("DATA SECTION NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(0);
}

console.log("");
console.log("DATA SECTION");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`START = ${hex(data.start)}`);
console.log(`END   = ${hex(data.end)}`);
console.log(`SIZE  = ${data.size}`);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW WASM BYTES NEAR DATA OFFSET 0x19534C");
console.log("============================================================");

/*
 * Important:
 *
 * The linear-memory address is not necessarily equal to the
 * file offset in the data section. Therefore this first pass
 * does NOT assume:
 *
 *     fileOffset == memoryAddress
 *
 * We simply search the entire data payload for literal patterns
 * that could initialize 0x19534C through a data segment.
 */

const targetBytes = [
  TARGET & 0xff,
  (TARGET >>> 8) & 0xff,
  (TARGET >>> 16) & 0xff,
  (TARGET >>> 24) & 0xff,
];

let literalHits = 0;

for (let p = data.start; p + 4 <= data.end; p++) {
  if (
    wasm[p] === targetBytes[0] &&
    wasm[p + 1] === targetBytes[1] &&
    wasm[p + 2] === targetBytes[2] &&
    wasm[p + 3] === targetBytes[3]
  ) {
    console.log(`TARGET ADDRESS BYTES @ ${hex(p)}`);
    literalHits++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log(`LITERAL 0x19534C HITS IN DATA SECTION = ${literalHits}`);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEARCH FOR LITTLE-ENDIAN 0x12B4");
console.log("============================================================");

let valueHits = 0;

for (let p = data.start; p + 4 <= data.end; p++) {
  const v =
    wasm[p] |
    (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 3] << 24);

  if ((v >>> 0) === 0x12b4) {
    console.log(`0x000012B4 @ FILE ${hex(p)}`);
    valueHits++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log(`0x12B4 DATA HITS = ${valueHits}`);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEARCH FOR 0x0000007F");
console.log("============================================================");

let sevenFHits = 0;

for (let p = data.start; p + 4 <= data.end; p++) {
  const v =
    wasm[p] |
    (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[p + 3] << 24);

  if ((v >>> 0) === 0x7f) {
    console.log(`0x0000007F @ FILE ${hex(p)}`);
    sevenFHits++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log(`0x7F DATA HITS = ${sevenFHits}`);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("IMPORTANT");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("Data-section file offsets are NOT assumed to equal");
console.log("linear-memory addresses.");
console.log("The next step, if necessary, is to decode DATA SEGMENTS");
console.log("and their initialization offsets.");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGET = 0x250fa0;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function u32(pos) {
    if (pos + 4 > buf.length)
        return null;

    return (
        buf[pos] |
        (buf[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DATA SEGMENT INSPECTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET : ${hex(TARGET)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let pos = 8;

let foundData = false;
let dataCount = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const sectionStart = pos;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    // Data section = 11
    if (sectionId !== 11) {
        pos = sectionEnd;
        continue;
    }

    foundData = true;

    const countInfo = readULEB(pos);
    dataCount = countInfo.value;
    pos = countInfo.next;

    console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS : ${dataCount}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < dataCount; i++) {

        const flagsInfo = readULEB(pos);
        const flags = flagsInfo.value;
        pos = flagsInfo.next;

        let memoryIndex = 0;
        let offset = null;

        if (flags === 0) {

            // active segment, memory 0
            // offset expression

            const opcode = buf[pos++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${i}: unsupported offset opcode ${hex(opcode, 2)}`
                );

                break;
            }

            const off = readULEB(pos);
            offset = off.value;
            pos = off.next;

            if (buf[pos++] !== 0x0b) {
                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${i}: missing end opcode`
                );
                break;
            }

        } else if (flags === 2) {

            const mem = readULEB(pos);
            memoryIndex = mem.value;
            pos = mem.next;

            const opcode = buf[pos++];

            if (opcode !== 0x41) {
                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${i}: unsupported offset opcode ${hex(opcode, 2)}`
                );
                break;
            }

            const off = readULEB(pos);
            offset = off.value;
            pos = off.next;

            if (buf[pos++] !== 0x0b) {
                console.log(
                    `SEGMENT ${i}: missing end opcode`
                );
                break;
            }

        } else if (flags === 1 || flags === 3) {

            const lenInfo = readULEB(pos);
            const len = lenInfo.value;
            pos = lenInfo.next;

            pos += len;

            console.log(
                `SEGMENT ${i}: passive/unsupported flags=${flags}`
            );

            continue;

        } else {

            console.log(
                `SEGMENT ${i}: unknown flags=${flags}`
            );

            break;
        }

        const lenInfo = readULEB(pos);
        const dataLen = lenInfo.value;
        pos = lenInfo.next;

        const dataStart = pos;
        const dataEnd = dataStart + dataLen;

        const covers =
            offset !== null &&
            TARGET >= offset &&
            TARGET < offset + dataLen;

        if (covers) {

            console.log("\n############################################################");
            console.log(`TARGET FOUND IN DATA SEGMENT ${i}`);
            console.log("############################################################");

            console.log(`MEMORY INDEX : ${memoryIndex}`);
            console.log(`SEG OFFSET   : ${hex(offset)}`);
            console.log(`DATA LENGTH  : ${dataLen}`);
            console.log(`TARGET DELTA  : ${TARGET - offset}`);

            const delta = TARGET - offset;

            console.log(
                `TARGET FILE OFFSET : ${hex(dataStart + delta)}`
            );

            const value = u32(dataStart + delta);

            console.log(
                `U32 @ TARGET       : ${
                    value === null ? "N/A" : hex(value)
                }`
            );

            const from = Math.max(
                0,
                delta - 32
            );

            const to = Math.min(
                dataLen,
                delta + 64
            );

            console.log("\nDATA AROUND TARGET:");

            for (let j = from; j < to; j += 16) {

                const a = dataStart + j;
                const e = Math.min(
                    dataEnd,
                    a + 16
                );

                const bytes = [];

                for (let q = a; q < e; q++) {
                    bytes.push(
                        buf[q]
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(2, "0")
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    `${hex(offset + j)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
                );
            }
        }

        pos = dataEnd;
    }

    break;
}

if (!foundData) {
    console.log("DATA SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const DESTINATION = Address.parse(
    'EQCEwfJtb6ZmPJdNw387eJwed2N51K6dFFbH82jTxe_cf31e'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99809465000012n;

const TONCENTER =
    'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC';

function hex(n: bigint, bytes = 4) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(bytes * 2, '0');
}

function dumpCell(cell: any, name: string, depth = 0) {
    const pad = ' '.repeat(depth * 2);

    console.log(`${pad}${name}`);

    try {
        console.log(`${pad}bits: ${cell.bits.length}`);
    } catch {}

    try {
        console.log(`${pad}refs: ${cell.refs.length}`);
    } catch {}

    try {
        console.log(`${pad}BOC(base64):`);
        console.log(
            `${pad}${cell.toBoc().toString('base64')}`
        );
    } catch {}

    try {
        const s = cell.beginParse();

        console.log(
            `${pad}remainingBits: ${s.remainingBits}`
        );

        console.log(
            `${pad}remainingRefs: ${s.remainingRefs}`
        );

        if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
            const op = s.loadUint(32);

            console.log(
                `${pad}opcode: ${hex(BigInt(op))}`
            );

            if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
                const qid = s.loadUintBig(64);

                console.log(
                    `${pad}query_id: ${qid}`
                );
            }
        }
    } catch (e) {
        console.log(
            `${pad}slice decode error: ${e}`
        );
    }

    try {
        for (let i = 0; i < cell.refs.length; i++) {
            dumpCell(
                cell.refs[i],
                `ref[${i}]`,
                depth + 1
            );
        }
    } catch {}
}

function printMessage(message: any, label: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );
    console.log(label);
    console.log(
        '============================================================'
    );

    const info: any = message.info;

    try {
        console.log(`src: ${info.src?.toString()}`);
    } catch {}

    try {
        console.log(`dest: ${info.dest?.toString()}`);
    } catch {}

    try {
        console.log(
            `value: ${info.value.coins} nanoTON`
        );

        console.log(
            `value TON: ${(Number(info.value.coins) / 1e9).toFixed(9)}`
        );
    } catch {}

    try {
        console.log(
            `createdLt: ${info.createdLt}`
        );
    } catch {}

    try {
        console.log(
            `createdAt: ${info.createdAt}`
        );
    } catch {}

    console.log('');

    if (message.body) {
        dumpCell(
            message.body,
            'BODY'
        );
    } else {
        console.log('BODY: empty');
    }
}

async function main() {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 DESTINATION TRANSACTION INSPECTOR');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        `Destination: ${DESTINATION.toString()}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Target LT: ${TARGET_LT}`
    );

    console.log('');

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint: TONCENTER,
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(
        DESTINATION,
        {
            limit: 20,
        }
    );

    console.log(
        `Transactions returned: ${txs.length}`
    );

    console.log('');

    let found = false;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        let lt: bigint;

        try {
            lt = BigInt(tx.lt);
        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        console.log(`Available LT: ${lt}`);

        if (lt !== TARGET_LT) {
            continue;
        }

        found = true;

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            '############################################################'
        );
        console.log(
            'TARGET DESTINATION TRANSACTION FOUND'
        );
        console.log(
            '############################################################'
        );

        console.log(`LT: ${tx.lt}`);

        try {
            console.log(
                `hash: ${Buffer.from(tx.hash()).toString('base64')}`
            );
        } catch {}

        console.log('');

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Incoming message
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (tx.inMessage) {
            printMessage(
                tx.inMessage,
                'INCOMING MESSAGE'
            );
        } else {
            console.log('No incoming message.');
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Outgoing messages
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        console.log('');

        if (!tx.outMessages || tx.outMessages.size === 0) {
            console.log(
                'No outgoing messages.'
            );
        } else {
            console.log(
                `Outgoing messages: ${tx.outMessages.size}`
            );

            let i = 0;

            for (const [, message] of tx.outMessages) {
                printMessage(
                    message,
                    `OUTGOING MESSAGE #${i}`
                );

                i++;
            }
        }

        break;
    }

    if (!found) {
        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' INSPECTION COMPLETE');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log('');
}

export async function run(
    provider: NetworkProvider
) {
    await main();
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_END   = 0x0018f512;

const RECORDS = [
  0x0018b520,
  0x0018b5ac,
  0x0018b638,
  0x0018b6c4,
  0x0018b818,
  0x0018b8a4,
  0x0018b948,
  0x0018ee8c,
  0x0018ef14,
  0x0018efa0,
];

const ANCHORS = [
  0x00189db4,
  0x00189df8,
  0x00189e10,
  0x00189e44,
  0x00189e5c,
  0x00189e90,
  0x00189ea8,

  0x0018a014,
  0x0018a878,

  0x0018a918,
];

function u32(pos: number): number {
  if (pos < 0 || pos + 4 > buf.length) return 0;
  return buf.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function in412(v: number): boolean {
  return v >= SEG412_START && v <= SEG412_END;
}

function in413(v: number): boolean {
  return v >= SEG413_START && v <= SEG413_END;
}

/*
 * Build a memory-image map from active WASM data segments.
 * Supports the common active forms:
 *   flags 0 = memory 0 + i32.const offset
 *   flags 2 = explicit memory index + i32.const offset
 */
function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
}

function parseDataSection() {
  // Known verified data section.
  const sectionStart = 0x00324ef5;
  const payloadStart = 0x00324ef9;

  if (buf[sectionStart] !== 11) {
    throw new Error("Data section header mismatch");
  }

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(sectionStart + 1);
  const payloadEnd = sectionStart + 1 +
    (sizeInfo.next - (sectionStart + 1)) +
    sizeInfo.value;

  let p = payloadStart;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  const memory = new Map<number, number>();

  for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
    const flags = buf[p++];

    let memoryIndex = 0;
    let offset = 0;

    if (flags === 0) {
      const opcode = buf[p++];
      if (opcode !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(`Unsupported offset expr at segment ${seg}`);
      }

      const offInfo = readULEB(p);
      offset = offInfo.value >>> 0;
      p = offInfo.next;

      if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(`Missing end expr at segment ${seg}`);
      }

    } else if (flags === 2) {
      const memInfo = readULEB(p);
      memoryIndex = memInfo.value;
      p = memInfo.next;

      const opcode = buf[p++];
      if (opcode !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(`Unsupported offset expr at segment ${seg}`);
      }

      const offInfo = readULEB(p);
      offset = offInfo.value >>> 0;
      p = offInfo.next;

      if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(`Missing end expr at segment ${seg}`);
      }

    } else {
      throw new Error(
        `Unsupported data segment flags=${flags} at segment ${seg}`
      );
    }

    const lenInfo = readULEB(p);
    const len = lenInfo.value;
    p = lenInfo.next;

    if (memoryIndex === 0) {
      for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        memory.set(offset + i, buf[p + i]);
      }
    }

    p += len;
  }

  console.log(`Data segments = ${count}`);
  console.log(`Data parser end = ${hex(p)}`);
  console.log(`Expected payload end = ${hex(payloadEnd)}`);
  console.log(`Memory bytes mapped = ${memory.size}`);

  return memory;
}

const memory = parseDataSection();

function memU32(addr: number): number | null {
  const b0 = memory.get(addr);
  const b1 = memory.get(addr + 1);
  const b2 = memory.get(addr + 2);
  const b3 = memory.get(addr + 3);

  if (
    b0 === undefined ||
    b1 === undefined ||
    b2 === undefined ||
    b3 === undefined
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    b0 |
    (b1 << 8) |
    (b2 << 16) |
    (b3 << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function classify(v: number): string {
  if (in412(v)) return "SEG412";
  if (in413(v)) return "SEG413";
  if (ANCHORS.includes(v)) return "ANCHOR";
  return "";
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DISPATCH 413 RECORD INSPECTOR");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");
console.log(`SEG412 ${hex(SEG412_START)} .. ${hex(SEG412_END)}`);
console.log(`SEG413 ${hex(SEG413_START)} .. ${hex(SEG413_END)}`);

console.log("");
console.log("TARGET ANCHORS");

for (const a of ANCHORS) {
  console.log(`  ${hex(a)}`);
}

for (const base of RECORDS) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`RECORD ${hex(base)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let interesting = 0;

  for (let off = 0; off <= 0x100; off += 4) {
    const addr = base + off;
    const value = memU32(addr);

    if (value === null) continue;

    const kind = classify(value);

    if (kind) {
      console.log(
        `+0x${off.toString(16).padStart(3, "0")}  ` +
        `${hex(addr)} : ${hex(value)}  [${kind}]`
      );
      interesting++;
    }
  }

  if (interesting === 0) {
    console.log("  No SEG412/SEG413/ANCHOR pointers in +0x000..+0x100");
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("REVERSE SCAN: SEGMENT 413 → TARGET ANCHORS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const anchor of ANCHORS) {
  const hits: number[] = [];

  for (
    let addr = SEG413_START;
    addr + 4 <= SEG413_END;
    addr += 4
  ) {
    const v = memU32(addr);

    if (v === anchor) {
      hits.push(addr);
    }
  }

  if (hits.length) {
    console.log("");
    console.log(`${hex(anchor)} <- ${hits.length} pointer(s)`);

    for (const h of hits) {
      console.log(`  ${hex(h)} -> ${hex(anchor)}`);
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("REVERSE SCAN: SEGMENT 413 → SEGMENT 412");
console.log("============================================================");

const reverse412 = new Map<number, number[]>();

for (
  let addr = SEG413_START;
  addr + 4 <= SEG413_END;
  addr += 4
) {
  const v = memU32(addr);

  if (v !== null && in412(v)) {
    const arr = reverse412.get(v) ?? [];
    arr.push(addr);
    reverse412.set(v, arr);
  }
}

console.log(`Unique SEG412 targets from SEG413 = ${reverse412.size}`);

let printed = 0;

for (const [target, sources] of reverse412) {
  console.log("");
  console.log(`${hex(target)} <- ${sources.length} pointer(s)`);

  for (const source of sources.slice(0, 8)) {
    console.log(`  ${hex(source)} -> ${hex(target)}`);
  }

  printed++;

  if (printed >= 80) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("OUTPUT LIMITED TO FIRST 80 TARGETS");
    break;
  }
}

console.log("");

console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchAux.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNCS = [113, 119, 2057];

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${idWanted} not found`);
}

/*
 * IMPORTS
 */
const importSec = findSection(2);

let importedFunctions = 0;

{
  let p = importSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = readUleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

/*
 * CODE
 */
const codeSec = findSection(10);

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

{
  let p = codeSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    bodies.set(importedFunctions + i, { start, end });

    p = end;
  }
}

function decodeFunc(func: number) {
  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}: BODY NOT FOUND`);
    return;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
  console.log(`SIZE = ${body.end - body.start}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  let p = body.start;

  while (p < body.end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} i32.const ${hex(x.value >>> 0)}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x42) {
      const x = readSleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} i64.const ${x.value}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(`${hex(at)} local.get ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(`${hex(at)} local.set ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(`${hex(at)} local.tee ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(`${hex(at)} call FUNC ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
      const t = readUleb(p);
      p = t.next;

      const table = readUleb(p);
      p = table.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} call_indirect TYPE ${t.value} TABLE ${table.value}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Memory loads/stores.
     */
    if (
      op >= 0x28 &&
      op <= 0x3e
    ) {
      const a = readUleb(p);
      p = a.next;

      const off = readUleb(p);
      p = off.next;

      let name = `memop ${hex(op, 2)}`;

      switch (op) {
        case 0x28: name = "i32.load"; break;
        case 0x29: name = "i64.load"; break;
        case 0x2a: name = "f32.load"; break;
        case 0x2b: name = "f64.load"; break;
        case 0x2c: name = "i32.load8_s"; break;
        case 0x2d: name = "i32.load8_u"; break;
        case 0x2e: name = "i32.load16_s"; break;
        case 0x2f: name = "i32.load16_u"; break;
        case 0x30: name = "i64.load8_s"; break;
        case 0x31: name = "i64.load8_u"; break;
        case 0x32: name = "i64.load16_s"; break;
        case 0x33: name = "i64.load16_u"; break;
        case 0x34: name = "i64.load32_s"; break;
        case 0x35: name = "i64.load32_u"; break;
        case 0x36: name = "i32.store"; break;
        case 0x37: name = "i64.store"; break;
        case 0x38: name = "f32.store"; break;
        case 0x39: name = "f64.store"; break;
        case 0x3a: name = "i32.store8"; break;
        case 0x3b: name = "i32.store16"; break;
        case 0x3c: name = "i64.store8"; break;
        case 0x3d: name = "i64.store16"; break;
        case 0x3e: name = "i64.store32"; break;
      }

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${name} offset=${hex(off)}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Control flow.
     */
    if (op === 0x00) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} unreachable`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x01) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} nop`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      const bt = wasm[p++];

      const name =
        op === 0x02 ? "block" :
        op === 0x03 ? "loop" :
        "if";

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} ${name} blocktype=${hex(bt, 2)}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} else`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} end`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(`${hex(at)} br ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(`${hex(at)} br_if ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0e) {
      const n = readUleb(p);
      p = n.next;

      const labels: number[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const x = readUleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        labels.push(x.value);
      }

      const d = readUleb(p);
      p = d.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} br_table [${labels.join(",")}] default=${d.value}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} return`);
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Common numeric operations.
     */
    const simple: Record<number, string> = {
      0x1a: "drop",
      0x45: "i32.eqz",
      0x46: "i32.eq",
      0x47: "i32.ne",
      0x48: "i32.lt_s",
      0x49: "i32.lt_u",
      0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
      0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
      0x4c: "i32.le_s",
      0x4d: "i32.le_u",
      0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
      0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
      0x6a: "i32.add",
      0x6b: "i32.sub",
      0x71: "i32.and",
      0x72: "i32.or",
      0x73: "i32.xor",
      0x74: "i32.shl",
      0x75: "i32.shr_s",
      0x76: "i32.shr_u",
    };

    if (simple[op]) {
      console.log(`${hex(at)} ${simple[op]}`);
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Extended opcode prefix.
     * Do not interpret semantics here.
     */
    if (op === 0xfc) {
      const x = readUleb(p);
      p = x.next;

      console.log(
        `${hex(at)} 0xfc subopcode=${hex(x.value, 4)}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Local declaration bytes / other raw bytes.
     */
    console.log(
      `${hex(at)} opcode=${hex(op, 2)}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("INSPECT DISPATCH AUX FUNCTIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const f of TARGET_FUNCS) {
  decodeFunc(f);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchConstantMemory.ts

import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const ROOTS = [
    1634244,
    1633988,
    1633928,
    1634268,
    1634408,
    1634128,
    1634104,
    1617084
];

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
    [2053, "Function 8952"],
    [2101, "Function 8927"],
    [2612, "Function 8637"]
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readU32(buf: Buffer, off: number) {
    if (off < 0 || off + 4 > buf.length)
        return null;

    return buf.readUInt32LE(off);
}

/*
 * Parse sections.
 */
let p = 8;

let dataStart = 0;
let dataEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    const payload = s.next;
    const end = payload + s.value;

    if (id === 11) {
        dataStart = payload;
        dataEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!dataStart)
    throw new Error("Data section not found");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH CONSTANT MEMORY INSPECTOR");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Data section : ${hex(dataStart)} .. ${hex(dataEnd)}`);
console.log();

/*
 * Build WASM linear-memory image from active data segments.
 */
const memory = Buffer.alloc(0x2000000);

p = dataStart;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

console.log(`Data segments : ${count.value}`);
console.log();

type Segment = {
    id: number;
    memStart: number;
    size: number;
    fileStart: number;
};

const segments: Segment[] = [];

for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {

    const flags = uleb(p);
    p = flags.next;

    let memOffset = 0;

    if (flags.value === 0) {

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error("Expected i32.const");

        const off = uleb(p);
        p = off.next;

        memOffset = off.value;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Expected end");

    } else if (flags.value === 2) {

        const memidx = uleb(p);
        p = memidx.next;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error("Expected i32.const");

        const off = uleb(p);
        p = off.next;

        memOffset = off.value;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Expected end");

    } else {

        /*
         * Passive / unsupported for this analysis.
         */
        if (flags.value === 1) {
            const sz = uleb(p);
            p = sz.next + sz.value;
            continue;
        }

        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported data flags ${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    const size = uleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const fileStart = p;

    if (
        memOffset + size.value <= memory.length
    ) {
        wasm.copy(
            memory,
            memOffset,
            fileStart,
            fileStart + size.value
        );
    }

    segments.push({
        id: seg,
        memStart: memOffset,
        size: size.value,
        fileStart
    });

    p += size.value;
}

function findSegment(addr: number) {
    return segments.find(
        s =>
            addr >= s.memStart &&
            addr < s.memStart + s.size
    );
}

function inspectAddress(addr: number, label: string) {

    console.log(
        "------------------------------------------------------------"
    );

    console.log(`${label}`);
    console.log(`Address : ${addr} (${hex(addr)})`);

    const seg = findSegment(addr);

    if (seg) {

        console.log(
            `Segment : ${seg.id}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Segment memory : ${hex(seg.memStart)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Segment end    : ${hex(seg.memStart + seg.size)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `Relative       : ${hex(addr - seg.memStart)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `File offset    : ${hex(
                seg.fileStart + (addr - seg.memStart)
            )}`
        );

    } else {

        console.log("Segment : NOT FOUND");
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("Nearby i32 values:");

    const base = addr & ~3;

    for (let d = -32; d <= 64; d += 4) {

        const a = base + d;
        const v = readU32(memory, a);

        if (v === null)
            continue;

        let tag = "";

        if (TARGETS.has(v)) {
            tag =
                `  <== TARGET ${TARGETS.get(v)}`;
        }

        const pointed = findSegment(v);

        if (pointed) {
            tag +=
                `  -> Segment ${pointed.id} @ ${hex(v)}`;
        }

        console.log(
            `  ${hex(a)} : ${v.toString().padStart(10)} ${hex(v)}${tag}`
        );
    }

    console.log();
}

/*
 * Inspect every static root.
 */
for (const root of ROOTS) {

    inspectAddress(
        root,
        `STATIC ROOT ${root}`
    );

    const first = readU32(memory, root);

    if (first !== null) {

        console.log(
            `FIRST LOAD = ${first} (${hex(first)})`
        );

        if (TARGETS.has(first)) {
            console.log(
                `*** DIRECT TARGET: ${TARGETS.get(first)} ***`
            );
        }

        const seg = findSegment(first);

        if (seg) {

            console.log(
                `FIRST LOAD points into Segment ${seg.id}`
            );

            /*
             * Second-level load used by:
             *
             * MEM[MEM[root]+40]
             * MEM[MEM[root]+16]
             */
            for (const off of [0, 16, 40]) {

                const a = first + off;
                const v = readU32(memory, a);

                if (v === null)
                    continue;

                console.log(
                    `  MEM[${hex(first)}+${off}] = ` +
                    `${v} (${hex(v)})`
                );

                if (TARGETS.has(v)) {

                    console.log(
                        `    *** TARGET ${TARGETS.get(v)} ***`
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    console.log();
}

/*
 * Explicitly inspect the three known target locations.
 */
console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log("KNOWN TARGET MEMORY LOCATIONS");

);

const known = [
    0x00189dec,

for (const addr of known) {

    const v = readU32(memory, addr);

    console.log(
        `${hex(addr)} -> ${v} (${v !== null ? hex(v) : "N/A"})`
    );

    const seg = findSegment(addr);

    if (seg) {
        console.log(
            `  Segment ${seg.id}, relative ${hex(addr - seg.memStart)}`
        );

}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGETS = [
    { index: 2053, offset: 0x4a9035 },
    { index: 2101, offset: 0x4a91e5 },
    { index: 2612, offset: 0x4a9af9 },
];

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function dump(offset: number, radius = 96) {
    const start = Math.max(0, offset - radius);
    const end = Math.min(wasm.length, offset + radius + 4);

    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const rowEnd = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = wasm.subarray(p, rowEnd);

        const hexBytes = Array.from(bytes)
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
            .join(" ");

        const ascii = Array.from(bytes)
            .map(b => (b >= 32 && b <= 126 ? String.fromCharCode(b) : "."))
            .join("");

        const marker =
            offset >= p && offset < rowEnd ? "  <--- TARGET" : "";

        console.log(
            `${hex(p)}  ${hexBytes.padEnd(47)}  ${ascii}${marker}`
        );
    }
}

function u32(offset: number): number {
    if (offset < 0 || offset + 4 > wasm.length) return 0;
    return wasm.readUInt32LE(offset);
}

function u16(offset: number): number {
    if (offset < 0 || offset + 2 > wasm.length) return 0;
    return wasm.readUInt16LE(offset);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH DATA STRUCTURE INSPECTION");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`TARGET INDEX ${t.index}`);
    console.log(`DATA OFFSET ${hex(t.offset)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    console.log("");
    console.log("NEIGHBORHOOD ±96:");
    dump(t.offset, 96);

    console.log("");
    console.log("32-BIT WORDS AROUND TARGET:");

    const start = t.offset - 64;
    const end = t.offset + 68;

    for (let p = start; p <= end; p += 4) {
        const value = u32(p);
        const rel = p - t.offset;

        console.log(
            `  ${hex(p)}  rel=${rel.toString().padStart(4)}  u32=${value} (${hex(value)})`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("16-BIT WORDS AROUND TARGET:");

    for (let p = t.offset - 32; p <= t.offset + 32; p += 2) {
        const value = u16(p);

        console.log(
            `  ${hex(p)}  rel=${(p - t.offset)
                .toString()
                .padStart(4)}  u16=${value} (${hex(value, 4)})`
        );
    }

    console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECTION COMPLETE");

FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchFamily.js

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
  8948,
  8949,
  8950,
  8951,
  8952,
  8953,
  8954,
  8955,
  8956
];

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(pos++);

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return { value, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b;

  while (true) {
    b = u8(pos++);

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos
  };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function opcodeName(op) {
  const names = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x1a: "drop",
    0x1b: "select",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",

    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",

    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",

    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",

    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul"
  };

  return names[op] ||
    `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function parseSections() {

  let p = 8;

  const types = [];
  const funcTypes = [];
  const bodies = [];

  while (p < buf.length) {

    const sectionId = u8(p++);

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const end = p + size;

    if (sectionId === 1) {

      const nInfo = readULEB(p);
      p = nInfo.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < nInfo.value; i++) {

        const form = u8(p++);

        if (form !== 0x60) {
          throw new Error("Unexpected type form");
        }

        const np = readULEB(p);
        p = np.next;

        const params = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < np.value; j++) {
          params.push(u8(p++));
        }

        const nr = readULEB(p);
        p = nr.next;

        const results = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < nr.value; j++) {
          results.push(u8(p++));
        }

        types.push({
          params,
          results
        });
      }
    }

    else if (sectionId === 3) {

      const nInfo = readULEB(p);
      p = nInfo.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < nInfo.value; i++) {

        const t = readULEB(p);
        p = t.next;

        funcTypes.push(t.value);
      }
    }

    else if (sectionId === 10) {

      const nInfo = readULEB(p);
      p = nInfo.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < nInfo.value; i++) {

        const bodyInfo = readULEB(p);
        const bodySize = bodyInfo.value;

        p = bodyInfo.next;

        const start = p;
        const endBody = p + bodySize;

        bodies.push({
          start,
          end: endBody
        });

        p = endBody;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return {
    types,
    funcTypes,
    bodies
  };
}

function decodeBody(start, end) {

  let p = start;

  const localInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = localInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localInfo.value; i++) {

    const count = readULEB(p);
    p = count.next;

    p++;
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (p < end) {

    const offset = p;
    const op = u8(p++);

    const ins = {
      offset,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op)
    };

    if (op === 0x41) {

      const x = readSLEB(p);

      ins.value = x.value;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x42) {

      const x = readSLEB(p);

      ins.value = x.value;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {

      const x = readULEB(p);

      ins.func = x.value;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {

      const t = readULEB(p);
      p = t.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      ins.type = t.value;
      ins.table = table.value;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x20 ||
      op === 0x21 ||
      op === 0x22
    ) {

      const x = readULEB(p);

      ins.local = x.value;
      p = x.next;
    }

    else if (
      op === 0x28 ||
      op === 0x29 ||
      op === 0x2c ||
      op === 0x2d ||
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x3a
    ) {

      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offsetImm = readULEB(p);
      p = offsetImm.next;

      ins.align = align.value;
      ins.offset = offsetImm.value;
    }

    instructions.push(ins);

    if (op === 0x0b && p >= end) {
      break;
    }
  }

  return instructions;
}

const parsed = parseSections();

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH FAMILY 8948..8956");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

for (const funcId of TARGETS) {

  const definedIndex =
    funcId - IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS;

  const body =
    parsed.bodies[definedIndex];

  const typeIndex =
    parsed.funcTypes[definedIndex];

  const type =
    parsed.types[typeIndex];

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`FUNC ${funcId}`);

  console.log(
    `BODY : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );

  console.log(
    `TYPE : ${typeIndex}`
  );

  console.log(
    `SIG  : (${type.params.map(x => hex(x, 2)).join(",")}) -> ` +
    `(${type.results.map(x => hex(x, 2)).join(",")})`
  );

  const instructions =
    decodeBody(body.start, body.end);

  console.log();
  console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  for (const ins of instructions) {

    let line =
      `${hex(ins.offset)}  ${ins.name}`;

    if (ins.value !== undefined) {
      line += ` ${ins.value}`;
    }

    if (ins.local !== undefined) {
      line += ` local=${ins.local}`;
    }

    if (ins.func !== undefined) {
      line += ` func=${ins.func}`;
    }

    if (ins.type !== undefined) {
      line +=
        ` type=${ins.type} table=${ins.table}`;
    }

    if (ins.offset !== undefined &&
        (
          ins.name === "i32.load" ||
          ins.name === "i64.load" ||
          ins.name === "i32.load8_u" ||
          ins.name === "i32.store" ||
          ins.name === "i64.store" ||
          ins.name === "i32.store8"
        )) {
      line += ` mem_offset=${ins.offset}`;
    }

    console.log(line);
  }

  const direct =
    instructions.filter(x => x.name === "call");

  const indirect =
    instructions.filter(x => x.name === "call_indirect");

  console.log();
  console.log(
    `DIRECT CALLS    : ${direct.length}`
  );

  for (const x of direct) {
    console.log(`  → FUNC ${x.func}`);
  }

  console.log(
    `INDIRECT CALLS  : ${indirect.length}`
  );

  for (const x of indirect) {
    console.log(
      `  → TYPE ${x.type}, TABLE ${x.table}`
    );
  }

  console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchFinal.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readUleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function section(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted)
      return { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("section not found");
}

/* imports */

const imp = section(2);

let importedFunctions = 0;

{
  let p = imp.start;
  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = readUleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const f = readUleb(p);
      p = f.next;
      const a = readUleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      if (f.value & 1) {
        const b = readUleb(p);
        p = b.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const f = readUleb(p);
      p = f.next;
      const a = readUleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      if (f.value & 1) {
        const b = readUleb(p);
        p = b.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

/* types */

const typeSec = section(1);

const types: {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
}[] = [];

{
  let p = typeSec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const form = wasm[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60)
      throw new Error("bad function type");

    const pc = readUleb(p);
    p = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
      params.push(wasm[p++]);

    const rc = readUleb(p);
    p = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
      results.push(wasm[p++]);

    types.push({ params, results });
  }
}

/* function section */

const funcSec = section(3);

const typeIndex: number[] = [];

{
  let p = funcSec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const x = readUleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    typeIndex.push(x.value);
  }
}

function getType(func: number) {
  const i = func - importedFunctions;

  if (i < 0 || i >= typeIndex.length)
    return null;

  const ti = typeIndex[i];

  return {
    index: ti,
    type: types[ti]
  };
}

/* code */

const codeSec = section(10);

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

{
  let p = codeSec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    bodies.set(importedFunctions + i, { start, end });

    p = end;
  }
}

console.log("DISPATCH FINAL CHECK");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const f of [83, 113, 119, 122, 656, 787]) {
  const t = getType(f);

  console.log("");
  console.log(`FUNC ${f}`);

  if (!t) {
    console.log("TYPE NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${t.index}`);

  console.log(
    `PARAM COUNT = ${t.type.params.length}`
  );

  console.log(
    `PARAMS = [${t.type.params.map(x => hex(x, 2)).join(", ")}]`
  );

  console.log(
    `RESULT COUNT = ${t.type.results.length}`
  );

  console.log(
    `RESULTS = [${t.type.results.map(x => hex(x, 2)).join(", ")}]`
  );
}

/*
 * Search all code for 0xFC 0x0B.
 * This tells us whether this operation is unique
 * and which functions use it.
 */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SEARCH 0xFC 0x0B");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (const [func, body] of bodies) {
  for (let p = body.start; p + 1 < body.end; p++) {
    if (wasm[p] === 0xfc && wasm[p + 1] === 0x0b) {
      console.log(
        `FUNC ${func} @ ${hex(p)}`
      );
      hits++;
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log(`TOTAL 0xFC 0x0B HITS = ${hits}`);

console.log("");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchFunc113.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 113;

function uleb(pos:number) {
  let v=0, shift=0, p=pos;
  while(p<wasm.length) {
    const b=wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if(!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return {value:v>>>0,next:p};
}

let p=8;
let imported=0;
let start=0,end=0;

while(p<wasm.length) {
  const id=wasm[p++];
  const s=uleb(p);
  p=s.next;
  const secEnd=p+s.value;

  if(id===2) {
    let q=p;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.next;

    for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
      const ml=uleb(q); q=ml.next+ml.value;
      const nl=uleb(q); q=nl.next+nl.value;
      const kind=wasm[q++];

      if(kind===0) {
        q=uleb(q).next;
        imported++;
      } else if(kind===1) {
        q++;
        q=uleb(q).next;
        q=uleb(q).next;
      } else if(kind===2) {
        const f=uleb(q); q=f.next;
        q=uleb(q).next;
        if(f.value&1) q=uleb(q).next;
      } else if(kind===3) {
        q+=2;
      }
    }
  }

  if(id===10) {
    let q=p;
    const n=uleb(q); q=n.next;

    for(let i=0;i<n.value;i++) {
      const bs=uleb(q); q=bs.next;
      const st=q;
      const en=q+bs.value;

      if(imported+i===TARGET) {
        start=st;
        end=en;
      }

      q=en;
    }
    break;
  }

  p=secEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 113 DISPATCH ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : 0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${end.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

for(let q=start;q<end;) {
  const pos=q;
  const op=wasm[q++];
  let imm="";

  if(
    op===0x20 || op===0x21 || op===0x22 ||
    op===0x10 || op===0x0c || op===0x0d ||
    op===0x41 || op===0x42
  ) {
    const x=uleb(q);
    imm=` ${x.value}`;
    q=x.next;
  }
  else if(op>=0x28 && op<=0x3e) {
    const a=uleb(q); q=a.next;
    const o=uleb(q); q=o.next;
    imm=` align=${a.value} offset=0x${o.value.toString(16)}`;
  }
  else if(op===0x11) {
    const t=uleb(q); q=t.next;
    const tab=uleb(q); q=tab.next;
    imm=` type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
  }

  let mark="";
  if(op>=0x28 && op<=0x35) mark=" <<< LOAD";
  if(op>=0x36 && op<=0x3e) mark=" <<< STORE";
  if(op===0x10) mark=" <<< CALL";
  if(op===0x11) mark=" <<< CALL_INDIRECT";

  console.log(
    `0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
    `${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}${imm}${mark}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchHandlers.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNCS = [122, 656, 91, 787];
const TARGET_ADDRS = [
  0x195348,
  0x19534c,
  0x195350,
  0x195354,
  0x195358,
];

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8): string {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${idWanted} not found`);
}

console.log("INSPECT DISPATCH HANDLERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  "TARGET FUNCS = " + TARGET_FUNCS.join(", ")
);

console.log(
  "TARGET ADDRS = " +
  TARGET_ADDRS.map(x => hex(x)).join(", ")
);

const importSec = findSection(2);
const funcSec = findSection(3);
const codeSec = findSection(10);

let importedFunctionCount = 0;

//
// Count imported functions
//
{
  let p = importSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = readUleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      importedFunctionCount++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p += 1;

      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

//
// Get code body offsets
//
let codeBodies: {
  func: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

{
  let p = codeSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    codeBodies.push({
      func: importedFunctionCount + i,
      start,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }
}

function getBody(func: number) {
  return codeBodies.find(x => x.func === func);
}

function scanFunction(func: number) {
  const body = getBody(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}: BODY NOT FOUND`);
    return;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
  );
  console.log(
    `SIZE = ${body.end - body.start}`
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let hits = 0;

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {
    const b = wasm[p];

    //
    // Detect literal i32.const 0x195xxx
    //
    if (b === 0x41) {
      const x = readUleb(p + 1);

      if (TARGET_ADDRS.includes(x.value)) {
        console.log(
          `${hex(p)} i32.const ${hex(x.value)}`
        );
        hits++;
      }
    }

    //
    // Detect direct call to one of the four handlers
    //
    if (b === 0x10) {
      const x = readUleb(p + 1);

      if (TARGET_FUNCS.includes(x.value)) {
        console.log(
          `${hex(p)} call FUNC ${x.value}`
        );
        hits++;
      }
    }
  }

  if (hits === 0) {
    console.log("NO DIRECT TARGET HITS");
  }
}

for (const func of TARGET_FUNCS) {
  scanFunction(func);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("DISPATCH MAP:");
console.log("0x12B4 -> FUNC 122");
console.log("0x12B5 -> FUNC 656");
console.log("0x12B6 -> FUNC 91");
console.log("0x12B7 -> FUNC 787");

console.log("");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const wanted = new Set([2034, 813, 2053, 1533]);
    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const mod = wabt.readWasm(fs.readFileSync(file), {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
    let func = null;
    let body = [];

    function printFunction(id, content) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("================================================");
        console.log("FUNCTION:", id);
        console.log("WAT LINES:", content.length);
        console.log("================================================");

        const limit = 180;
        const shown = content.slice(0, limit);

        shown.forEach((line, i) => {
            console.log(line);
        });

        if (content.length > limit) {
            console.log(
                `... TRUNCATED: ${content.length - limit} more lines`
            );
        }
    }

    for (const line of lines) {
        const header = line.match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);

        if (header) {
            if (func !== null && wanted.has(func)) {
                printFunction(func, body);
            }

            func = Number(header[1]);
            body = [line];
            continue;
        }

        if (func !== null) {
            body.push(line);

            if (/^\s*\)\s*$/.test(line)) {
                if (wanted.has(func)) {
                    printFunction(func, body);
                }

                func = null;
                body = [];
            }
        }
    }

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchHelpers.mjs

import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const sourcePath = path.resolve("scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs");
const source = fs.readFileSync(sourcePath, "utf8");
const marker = "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";
const markerIndex = source.indexOf(marker);

if (markerIndex < 0) {
  throw new Error("Decoder bootstrap marker not found.");
}

const tempPath = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `rooster-dispatch-${process.pid}.mjs`);

fs.writeFileSync(
  tempPath,
  source.slice(0, markerIndex) + "\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n",
  "utf8"
);

try {
  const { bodies, decodeFunction } = await import(pathToFileURL(tempPath).href);

  function decode(id) {
    try {
      return decodeFunction(id);
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  function printFunction(id) {
    const result = decode(id);

    console.log(`\n${"=".repeat(72)}\nFUNC ${id}`);

    if (!result) {
      console.log("DECODE FAILED");
      return;
    }

    console.log(`INSTRUCTION COUNT: ${result.instructions.length}`);

    for (let i = 0; i < result.instructions.length; i++) {
      console.log(
        `FUNC ${id} #${String(i).padStart(5)} | ${result.instructions[i].text}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("ROOSTER WASM — DISPATCH HELPER INSPECTION");
  console.log("READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION");
  console.log("NO TRANSACTIONS SENT");

  // The helper immediately preceding the dispatch-cell read,
  // plus the related helpers already seen in the call paths.
  for (const id of [259, 2498, 2499]) {
    printFunction(id);
  }

  console.log(`\n${"=".repeat(72)}\nDIRECT CALLERS OF FUNC 259`);

  let callers = 0;
  let decoded = 0;
  let failed = 0;

  for (const id of bodies.keys()) {
    const result = decode(id);

    if (!result) {
      failed++;
      continue;
    }

    decoded++;

    const ins = result.instructions;

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
      if (!/^\s*call\s+259(?:\s|$)/.test(ins[i].text)) continue;

      callers++;

      console.log(`\nCALLER FUNC ${id}, instruction #${i}`);

      const start = Math.max(0, i - 4);
      const end = Math.min(ins.length, i + 6);

      for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {
        console.log(
          `${j === i ? ">>> " : "    "}FUNC ${id} #${String(j).padStart(5)} | ${ins[j].text}`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("\nSUMMARY");
  console.log(`FUNC 259 DIRECT CALL SITES: ${callers}`);
  console.log(`FUNCTIONS DECODED: ${decoded}`);
  console.log(`DECODE FAILURES: ${failed}`);
  console.log("COMPLETE — STATIC ANALYSIS ONLY");
} finally {
  try {
    fs.unlinkSync(tempPath);
  } catch {}
}

FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchLifecycle.js
