(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const wanted = new Set([
        1079, 2034, 2053, 2061,
        3314, 3315, 3316, 3319,
        3336, 6991
    ]);

    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const mod = wabt.readWasm(fs.readFileSync(file), {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
    const bodies = new Map();

    let currentId = null;
    let currentBody = [];

    function saveCurrent() {
        if (currentId !== null && wanted.has(currentId)) {
            bodies.set(currentId, currentBody.slice());
        }
    }

    for (const line of lines) {
        const match = line.match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);

        if (match) {
            saveCurrent();

            currentId = Number(match[1]);
            currentBody = [line];
            continue;
        }

        if (currentId !== null) {
            currentBody.push(line);
        }
    }

    saveCurrent();

    console.log("DISPATCH LIFECYCLE INSPECTION");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

    for (const id of wanted) {
        const content = bodies.get(id);

        console.log("");
        console.log("================================================");
        console.log("FUNCTION:", id);

        if (!content) {
            console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
            continue;
        }

        console.log("BODY LINES:", content.length);

        const selected = new Set();

        for (let i = 0; i < content.length; i++) {
            const line = content[i];

            if (
                /1659720|1659724|1659728|1659732|1659736/.test(line) ||
                /\bcall(?:_indirect)?\b/.test(line) ||
                /\b(?:i32|i64|f32|f64|v128)\.store(?:8|16|32|64)?\b/.test(line)
            ) {
                for (
                    let j = Math.max(0, i - 2);
                    j <= Math.min(content.length - 1, i + 2);
                    j++
                ) {
                    selected.add(j);
                }
            }
        }

        let previous = -2;

        for (const i of [...selected].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
            if (i > previous + 1) {
                console.log("...");
            }

            console.log(`${i + 1}: ${content[i].trim()}`);
            previous = i;
        }
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("FUNCTIONS FOUND:", bodies.size);

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchMemory.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET_FUNCS = [113, 119, 122, 2057, 656];

const TARGET_ADDRS = [
    0x19534c,
    0x195350,
    0x195354,
    0x195358,
];

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSections() {
    let p = 8;

    const sections: {
        id: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
    }[] = [];

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const s = uleb(p);
        p = s.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = p + s.value;

        sections.push({ id, start, end });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const funcSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
    throw new Error("Function/code section not found");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Function section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = funcSec.start;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.next;

const definedCount = fc.value;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const cc = uleb(p);
p = cc.next;

if (cc.value !== definedCount) {
    throw new Error(
        `Function/code count mismatch: ${definedCount} vs ${cc.value}`
    );
}

const functions: {
    globalFunc: number;
    ordinal: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < cc.value; ordinal++) {
    const bs = uleb(p);
    p = bs.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + bs.value;

    functions.push({
        globalFunc: 83 + ordinal,
        ordinal,
        start,
        end,
    });

    p = end;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INSPECT DISPATCH MEMORY REGION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("MEMORY REGION");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const addr of TARGET_ADDRS) {
    console.log(
        `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Helper: dump function
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function dumpFunction(globalFunc: number) {

    const fn = functions.find(x => x.globalFunc === globalFunc);

    if (!fn) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${globalFunc} NOT FOUND`);
        return;
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNC ${globalFunc}`);
    console.log(`ORDINAL = ${fn.ordinal}`);
    console.log(
        `BODY    = 0x${fn.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${fn.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
    console.log(`SIZE    = ${fn.end - fn.start}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end; off += 16) {

        const e = Math.min(off + 16, fn.end);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let i = off; i < e; i++) {
            bytes.push(
                buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            `0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
        );
    }

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Detect all i32.const targets
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end; off++) {

        if (buf[off] !== 0x41)
            continue;

        const x = uleb(off + 1);

        if (!TARGET_ADDRS.includes(x.value))
            continue;

        console.log(
            `CONST TARGET 0x${x.value.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
            `@ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );

        const c0 = Math.max(fn.start, off - 32);
        const c1 = Math.min(fn.end, off + 80);

        console.log("  CONTEXT:");

        for (let c = c0; c < c1; c += 16) {

            const e = Math.min(c + 16, c1);

            const bytes: string[] = [];

            for (let i = c; i < e; i++) {
                bytes.push(
                    buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
                );
            }

            console.log(
                `  0x${c.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
            );
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Detect all memory stores
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log("MEMORY STORE OPCODES:");

    const stores = new Set([
        0x36, // i32.store
        0x37, // i64.store
        0x38, // f32.store
        0x39, // f64.store
        0x3a, // i32.store8
        0x3b, // i32.store16
        0x3c, // i64.store8
        0x3d, // i64.store16
        0x3e, // i64.store32
    ]);

    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end; off++) {

        if (!stores.has(buf[off]))
            continue;

        const a = uleb(off + 1);
        const o = uleb(a.next);

        console.log(
            `  STORE @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
            `opcode=0x${buf[off].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} ` +
            `align=${a.value} ` +
            `offset=0x${o.value.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // Calls
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    console.log("DIRECT CALLS:");

    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end; off++) {

        if (buf[off] !== 0x10)
            continue;

        const x = uleb(off + 1);

        console.log(
            `  call FUNC ${x.value} @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
    }

    console.log("CALL_INDIRECT:");

    for (let off = fn.start; off < fn.end; off++) {

        if (buf[off] !== 0x11)
            continue;

        const x = uleb(off + 1);

        console.log(
            `  call_indirect @ 0x${off.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
            `type=${x.value}`
        );
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Execute inspection
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (const f of TARGET_FUNCS) {
    dumpFunction(f);
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const DATA_SEGMENT = 412;

const TARGETS = [
    { name: "2053", mem: 0x189dec },
    { name: "2101", mem: 0x189f9c },
    { name: "2612", mem: 0x18a8b0 },
];

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSection(pos: number) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const len = readULEB(pos);

    return {
        id,
        start: len.next,
        end: len.next + len.value,
    };
}

function u32(p: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[p] |
        (wasm[p + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[p + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" DISPATCH RECORD STRUCTURE INSPECTOR");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

let p = 8;
let dataStart = 0;
let dataEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const s = readSection(p);

    if (s.id === 11) {
        dataStart = s.start;
        dataEnd = s.end;
        break;
    }

    p = s.end;
}

if (!dataStart)
    throw new Error("Data section not found");

let q = dataStart;

const segCount = readULEB(q);
q = segCount.next;

let segmentFileStart = 0;
let segmentMemStart = 0;
let segmentSize = 0;

for (let seg = 0; seg < segCount.value; seg++) {

    const flags = readULEB(q);
    q = flags.next;

    let memStart = 0;

    if (flags.value === 0) {

        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(`Bad offset expression at segment ${seg}`);

        const off = readULEB(q);
        q = off.next;

        memStart = off.value;

        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(`Missing end at segment ${seg}`);
    }
    else if (flags.value === 2) {

        const memoryIndex = readULEB(q);
        q = memoryIndex.next;

        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(`Bad offset expression at segment ${seg}`);

        const off = readULEB(q);
        q = off.next;

        memStart = off.value;

        if (wasm[q++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(`Missing end at segment ${seg}`);
    }
    else if (flags.value === 1) {
        memStart = 0;
    }
    else {
        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported flags ${flags.value} at segment ${seg}`
        );
    }

    const size = readULEB(q);
    q = size.next;

    const bytesStart = q;
    const bytesEnd = q + size.value;

    if (seg === DATA_SEGMENT) {
        segmentFileStart = bytesStart;
        segmentMemStart = memStart;
        segmentSize = size.value;
    }

    q = bytesEnd;
}

console.log(`Data segment      = ${DATA_SEGMENT}`);
console.log(`File start        = ${hex(segmentFileStart)}`);
console.log(`Memory start      = ${hex(segmentMemStart)}`);
console.log(`Size              = ${hex(segmentSize)}`);
console.log();

if (!segmentFileStart)
    throw new Error("Target data segment not found");

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET RECORD WINDOWS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const target of TARGETS) {

    const relative =
        target.mem - segmentMemStart;

    const fileOffset =
        segmentFileStart + relative;

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `TARGET ${target.name}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  memory   = ${hex(target.mem)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  relative = ${hex(relative)}`
    );
    console.log(
        `  file     = ${hex(fileOffset)}`
    );

    /*
       Print 0x100 bytes before and after.
    */

    const from =
        Math.max(
            segmentFileStart,
            fileOffset - 0x100
        );

    const to =
        Math.min(
            segmentFileStart + segmentSize,
            fileOffset + 0x100
        );

    console.log();
    console.log(
        `WINDOW ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`
    );

    /*
       16-byte rows with:
       file offset
       memory address
       four u32 values
    */

    for (
        let off = from;
        off + 16 <= to;
        off += 16
    ) {

        const mem =
            segmentMemStart +
            (off - segmentFileStart);

        console.log(
            `${hex(off)}  MEM ${hex(mem)}  ` +
            `${hex(u32(off))} ` +
            `${hex(u32(off + 4))} ` +
            `${hex(u32(off + 8))} ` +
            `${hex(u32(off + 12))}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    /*
       Search the local window for values which look like
       function indices near the target.
    */

    console.log("NEAR FUNCTION-INDEX VALUES:");

    const low =
        Number(target.name) - 16;

    const high =
        Number(target.name) + 16;

    let count = 0;

    for (
        let off = from;
        off + 4 <= to;
        off += 4
    ) {

        const value = u32(off);

        if (
            value >= low &&
            value <= high
        ) {

            const mem =
                segmentMemStart +
                (off - segmentFileStart);

            console.log(
                `  MEM ${hex(mem)} ` +
                `FILE ${hex(off)} ` +
                `VALUE ${value}`
            );

            count++;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `  COUNT = ${count}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("POINTER-LIKE VALUES IN SEGMENT 412");
console.log("============================================================");

/*
   Search entire segment for values pointing back into the same
   segment memory range.
*/

const memBegin = segmentMemStart;
const memEnd =
    segmentMemStart + segmentSize;

let pointerCount = 0;

for (
    let off = segmentFileStart;
    off + 4 <= segmentFileStart + segmentSize;
    off += 4
) {

    const value = u32(off);

    if (
        value >= memBegin &&
        value < memEnd &&
        (value & 3) === 0
    ) {

        const mem =
            segmentMemStart +
            (off - segmentFileStart);

        console.log(
            `MEM ${hex(mem)} ` +
            `-> ${hex(value)} ` +
            `delta=${value - memBegin}`
        );

        pointerCount++;

        if (pointerCount >= 200) {
            console.log(
                "... first 200 pointer-like values shown"
            );
            break;
        }
    }
}

console.log();
console.log(
    `POINTER-LIKE COUNT SHOWN = ${pointerCount}`
);

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SCAN COMPLETE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchStateRefs.js

(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const bytes = fs.readFileSync(file);

    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const mod = wabt.readWasm(bytes, {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);

    const targets = new Set([
        1659720,
        1659724,
        1659728,
        1659732,
        1659736
    ]);

    let func = "unknown";
    let hits = 0;

    console.log("DISPATCH STATE REFERENCES");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("================================================");

    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const header =
            lines[i].match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);

        if (header) {
            func = header[1];
        }

        const match = lines[i].match(
            /\bi32\.const\s+(-?\d+)\b/
        );

        if (!match) continue;

        const value = Number(match[1]);

        if (!targets.has(value)) continue;

        hits++;

        console.log(
            "\n--- FUNC " + func +
            " | ADDRESS " + value +
            " | WAT LINE " + (i + 1) + " ---"
        );

        const start = Math.max(0, i - 12);
        const end = Math.min(lines.length - 1, i + 16);

        for (let j = start; j <= end; j++) {
            console.log(
                String(j + 1).padStart(8) +
                " | " + lines[j]
            );
        }
    }

    console.log("\n================================================");
    console.log("TOTAL REFERENCES:", hits);
    console.log("================================================");

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error.stack || error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchTableRange.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const wasmPath = path.resolve(dir, '..', 'emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm');
const outputPath = path.join(dir, 'inspectDispatchTableRange_output.txt');

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const lines = [];
const out = value => lines.push(String(value ?? ''));

const MIN_SLOT = 4755;
const MAX_SLOT = 4805;

if (wasm.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex') !== '0061736d') {
    throw new Error('Invalid WASM magic header');
}

function readULEB(s) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (s.p < s.end) {
        const b = wasm[s.p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) return value;

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('ULEB too large');
    }

    throw new Error('Unexpected end of ULEB');
}

function readSLEB(s) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    do {
        if (s.p >= s.end) throw new Error('Unexpected end of SLEB');

        b = wasm[s.p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;
    } while (b & 0x80);

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) value -= 2 ** shift;

    return value;
}

function readByte(s) {
    if (s.p >= s.end) throw new Error('Unexpected end of section');
    return wasm[s.p++];
}

function readConstExpr(s) {
    let firstConst = null;

    while (s.p < s.end) {
        const op = readByte(s);

        if (op === 0x0b) return firstConst;

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const value = readSLEB(s);
            if (firstConst === null) firstConst = value;
        } else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0xd2) {
            readULEB(s);
        } else if (op === 0x42) {
            readSLEB(s);
        } else if (op === 0x43) {
            s.p += 4;
        } else if (op === 0x44) {
            s.p += 8;
        } else if (op === 0xd0) {
            readSLEB(s);
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported const-expression opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    throw new Error('Unterminated constant expression');
}

function readExpressionFunc(s) {
    let func = null;

    while (s.p < s.end) {
        const op = readByte(s);

        if (op === 0x0b) return func;

        if (op === 0xd2) {
            func = readULEB(s);
        } else if (op === 0xd0) {
            readSLEB(s);
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported element expression opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    throw new Error('Unterminated element expression');
}

const mappings = new Map();
let pos = 8;
let elementSections = 0;
let segmentCount = 0;
let warnings = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];
    const sizeState = { p: pos, end: wasm.length };
    const sectionSize = readULEB(sizeState);
    const sectionStart = sizeState.p;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionEnd > wasm.length) {
        throw new Error('Section extends beyond WASM file');
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;

    if (sectionId !== 9) continue;

    elementSections++;

    const s = { p: sectionStart, end: sectionEnd };
    const count = readULEB(s);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
        segmentCount++;

        const flags = readULEB(s);
        let tableIndex = null;
        let baseSlot = null;

        if (flags === 0 || flags === 2 || flags === 4 || flags === 6) {
            if (flags === 2 || flags === 6) {
                tableIndex = readULEB(s);
            } else {
                tableIndex = 0;
            }

            baseSlot = readConstExpr(s);
        }

        if (flags === 0 || flags === 2) {
            const n = readULEB(s);

            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                const func = readULEB(s);
                const slot = baseSlot === null ? null : baseSlot + i;

                if (
                    tableIndex === 0 &&
                    slot !== null &&
                    slot >= MIN_SLOT &&
                    slot <= MAX_SLOT
                ) {
                    mappings.set(slot, func);
                }
            }
        } else if (flags === 1 || flags === 3) {
            readByte(s); // elemkind
            const n = readULEB(s);

            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) readULEB(s);
        } else if (flags === 4 || flags === 6) {
            readByte(s); // reftype
            const n = readULEB(s);

            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                const func = readExpressionFunc(s);
                const slot = baseSlot === null ? null : baseSlot + i;

                if (
                    tableIndex === 0 &&
                    slot !== null &&
                    slot >= MIN_SLOT &&
                    slot <= MAX_SLOT
                ) {
                    mappings.set(slot, func);
                }
            }
        } else if (flags === 5 || flags === 7) {
            readByte(s); // reftype
            const n = readULEB(s);

            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) readExpressionFunc(s);
        } else {
            warnings.push(`Unknown element flags ${flags} in segment ${seg}`);
            break;
        }
    }
}

out('ROOSTER WASM — DISPATCH TABLE RANGE');
out('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
out('====================================================');
out(`TABLE: 0`);
out(`SLOT RANGE: ${MIN_SLOT}..${MAX_SLOT}`);
out(`ELEMENT SECTIONS: ${elementSections}`);
out(`SEGMENTS: ${segmentCount}`);
out('');

for (let slot = MIN_SLOT; slot <= MAX_SLOT; slot++) {
    const func = mappings.get(slot);

    if (func === undefined) {
        out(`TABLE[${slot}] = <not mapped by parsed active element segment>`);
    } else {
        let note = '';

        if (slot === 4769) note = '  <== TARGET: function 7006 expected';
        if (slot === 4790) note = '  <== DISPATCH COMPARISON VALUE';

        out(`TABLE[${slot}] = function ${func}${note}`);
    }
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out('PARSER WARNINGS');

if (warnings.length === 0) {
    out('None');
} else {
    for (const warning of warnings) out(warning);
}

out('');
out('Interpretation: an unmapped slot is not automatically proof of an invalid call.');
out('Only active element segments were used to build this range.');
out('No WASM execution performed.');

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join('\n') + '\n', 'utf8');

console.log(`Element sections: ${elementSections}`);
console.log(`Segments: ${segmentCount}`);
console.log(`Mapped slots in requested range: ${mappings.size}`);
console.log(`Warnings: ${warnings.length}`);
console.log(`Output: ${outputPath}`);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectDispatchTargetRecords.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

const SEG_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const TARGETS = [
    {
        index: 2053,
        address: 0x00189dec,
        fn: 8952
    },
    {
        index: 2101,
        address: 0x00189f9c,
        fn: 8927
    },
    {
        index: 2612,
        address: 0x0018a8b0,
        fn: 8637
    }
];

function uleb(pos: number) {

    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {

        const b = wasm[p++];

        value +=
            (b & 0x7f) *
            2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value,
        next: p
    };
}

function hex(v: number) {

    return (
        "0x" +
        v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")
    );
}

/*
 * Build linear memory from data segments.
 */
let p = 8;

let dataStart = 0;
let dataEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);

    const start = s.next;
    const end =
        start + s.value;

    if (id === 11) {

        dataStart = start;
        dataEnd = end;

        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!dataStart)
    throw new Error(
        "Data section not found"
    );

const memory =
    Buffer.alloc(0x02000000);

p = dataStart;

const segCount = uleb(p);

p = segCount.next;

for (
    let seg = 0;
    seg < segCount.value;
    seg++
) {

    const flags = uleb(p);

    p = flags.next;

    let memStart = 0;

    if (
        flags.value === 0
    ) {

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(
                "Expected i32.const"
            );

        const off = uleb(p);

        p = off.next;

        memStart =
            off.value;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(
                "Expected end"
            );

    } else if (
        flags.value === 2
    ) {

        const memidx = uleb(p);

        p = memidx.next;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41)
            throw new Error(
                "Expected i32.const"
            );

        const off = uleb(p);

        p = off.next;

        memStart =
            off.value;

        if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error(
                "Expected end"
            );

    } else if (
        flags.value === 1
    ) {

        const sz = uleb(p);

        p =
            sz.next +
            sz.value;

        continue;

    } else {

        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported flags ${flags.value}`
        );
    }

    const size = uleb(p);

    p = size.next;

    const fileStart = p;

    if (
        memStart + size.value <=
        memory.length
    ) {

        wasm.copy(
            memory,
            memStart,
            fileStart,
            fileStart + size.value
        );
    }

    p += size.value;
}

function readU32(addr: number) {

    if (
        addr < 0 ||
        addr + 4 >
        memory.length
    )
        return null;

    return memory.readUInt32LE(
        addr
    );
}

function isPointer(v: number) {

    return (
        v >= 0x1000 &&
        v < 0x02000000
    );
}

function region(v: number) {

    if (
        v >= 0x0018744c &&
        v < 0x0018ab8d
    )
        return "SEG412";

    if (
        v >= 0x0018ab98 &&
        v < 0x0018f512
    )
        return "SEG413";

    if (
        v < 0x00100000
    )
        return "LOW";

    return "OTHER";
}

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log(
    " DISPATCH TARGET RECORD INSPECTOR"
);

console.log(
    " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
);

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log();

console.log(
    `Segment 412 : ${hex(SEG_START)} .. ${hex(SEG_END)}`
);

console.log();

/*
 * Search target exact address and index.
 */
for (const target of TARGETS) {

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    console.log(
        `TARGET ${target.index} -> Function ${target.fn}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Address = ${hex(target.address)}`
    );

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );

    /*
     * Dump 0x80 bytes around target.
     */
    const start =
        target.address - 0x80;

    const end =
        target.address + 0x80;

    console.log();
    console.log(
        "RAW WORD WINDOW"
    );
    console.log();

    for (
        let addr = start;
        addr < end;
        addr += 4
    ) {

        const v =
            readU32(addr);

        if (v === null)
            continue;

        let tag = "";

        if (
            addr ===
            target.address
        ) {
            tag =
                " <=== TARGET";
        }

        if (
            v === target.index
        ) {
            tag +=
                " [TARGET_INDEX]";
        }

        if (
            v === 2053 ||
            v === 2101 ||
            v === 2612
        ) {
            tag +=
                " [DISPATCH_INDEX]";
        }

        if (isPointer(v)) {

            const r =
                region(v);

            if (
                r !== "OTHER"
            ) {
                tag +=
                    ` -> ${r}`;
            }
        }

        console.log(
            `${hex(addr)} : ` +
            `${v.toString().padStart(10)} ` +
            `${hex(v)}${tag}`
        );
    }

    /*
     * Find the nearest run of sequential integers.
     */
    console.log();
    console.log(
        "SEQUENTIAL INDEX RUN"
    );
    console.log();

    let runStart =
        target.address;

    while (true) {

        const prev =
            readU32(runStart - 4);

        if (
            prev === null ||
            prev + 1 !==
            readU32(runStart)
        )
            break;

        runStart -= 4;
    }

    let runEnd =
        target.address;

    while (true) {

        const cur =
            readU32(runEnd);

        const next =
            readU32(runEnd + 4);

        if (
            cur === null ||
            next === null ||
            next !== cur + 1
        )
            break;

        runEnd += 4;
    }

    console.log(
        `RUN ${hex(runStart)} .. ${hex(runEnd)}`
    );

    for (
        let addr = runStart;
        addr <= runEnd;
        addr += 4
    ) {

        const v =
            readU32(addr);

        if (v !== null) {

            console.log(
                `  ${hex(addr)} = ${v}`
            );
        }
    }

    /*
     * Search Segment 412 for pointers
     * close to this target record.
     */
    console.log();
    console.log(
        "POINTERS INTO TARGET NEIGHBORHOOD"
    );
    console.log();

    let hits = 0;

    for (
        let addr = SEG_START;
        addr + 4 <= SEG_END;
        addr += 4
    ) {

        const v =
            readU32(addr);

        if (v === null)
            continue;

        if (
            v >= target.address - 0x100 &&
            v <= target.address + 0x100
        ) {

            console.log(
                `${hex(addr)} -> ${hex(v)} ` +
                `(delta ${v - target.address})`
            );

            hits++;
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `TOTAL = ${hits}`
    );

    /*
     * Search Segment 412 for target index
     * and print its surrounding pointer fields.
     */
    console.log();
    console.log(
        "TARGET INDEX STRUCTURE"
    );
    console.log();

    const index =
        target.index;

    for (
        let addr = SEG_START;
        addr + 4 <= SEG_END;
        addr += 4
    ) {

        const v =
            readU32(addr);

        if (
            v !== index
        )
            continue;

        console.log(
            `INDEX ${index} @ ${hex(addr)}`
        );

        for (
            let d = -0x40;
            d <= 0x40;
            d += 4
        ) {

            const a =
                addr + d;

            const x =
                readU32(a);

            if (x === null)
                continue;

            let tag = "";

            if (
                isPointer(x)
            ) {

                const r =
                    region(x);

                tag =
                    ` -> ${r}`;
            }

            console.log(
                `  ${hex(a)} ` +
                `${d >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${d} : ` +
                `${x} ${hex(x)}${tag}`
            );
        }

        console.log();
    }
}

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

console.log(
    "END"
);

console.log(
    "============================================================"
);

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNCS = [83, 91, 113, 119, 122, 656, 787];

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${idWanted} not found`);
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
 * IMPORTS
 * --------------------------------------------------------- */

const importSec = findSection(2);

let importedFunctions = 0;

{
  let p = importSec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = readUleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
 * FUNCTION TYPES
 * --------------------------------------------------------- */

const typeSec = findSection(1);

const funcTypes: {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
}[] = [];

{
  let p = typeSec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const form = wasm[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(`Unexpected function type form ${hex(form, 2)}`);
    }

    const pc = readUleb(p);
    p = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
      params.push(wasm[p++]);
    }

    const rc = readUleb(p);
    p = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
      results.push(wasm[p++]);
    }

    funcTypes.push({ params, results });
  }
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
 * FUNCTION SECTION
 * --------------------------------------------------------- */

const funcSec = findSection(3);

const definedTypeIndices: number[] = [];

{
  let p = funcSec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const t = readUleb(p);
    p = t.next;
    definedTypeIndices.push(t.value);
  }
}

function funcType(func: number) {
  if (func < importedFunctions) return null;

  const idx = func - importedFunctions;

  if (idx < 0 || idx >= definedTypeIndices.length) {
    return null;
  }

  const typeIndex = definedTypeIndices[idx];

  return {
    typeIndex,
    type: funcTypes[typeIndex]
  };
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
 * CODE SECTION
 * --------------------------------------------------------- */

const codeSec = findSection(10);

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

{
  let p = codeSec.start;

  const c = readUleb(p);
  p = c.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    bodies.set(importedFunctions + i, { start, end });

    p = end;
  }
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
 * TYPE SUMMARY
 * --------------------------------------------------------- */

console.log("DISPATCH TERMINAL / TYPE SUMMARY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const f of TARGET_FUNCS) {
  const ft = funcType(f);
  const body = bodies.get(f);

  console.log("");
  console.log(`FUNC ${f}`);

  if (body) {
    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE = ${body.end - body.start}`);
  }

  if (ft) {
    console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${ft.typeIndex}`);
    console.log(
      `PARAMS = [${ft.type.params.map(x => hex(x, 2)).join(", ")}]`
    );
    console.log(
      `RESULTS = [${ft.type.results.map(x => hex(x, 2)).join(", ")}]`
    );
  }
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------
 * EXACT RAW BODY FOR FUNC83 AND FUNC787
 * --------------------------------------------------------- */

function dumpRaw(func: number) {
  const body = bodies.get(func);

  if (!body) {
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}: BODY NOT FOUND`);
    return;
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${func} RAW`);
  console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
  console.log(`SIZE = ${body.end - body.start}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (let p = body.start; p < body.end; p++) {
    console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${wasm[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);
  }
}

dumpRaw(83);
dumpRaw(787);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_TYPES = [3, 4, 6];
const TARGET_INDICES = [0x12b4, 0x12b5, 0x12b6, 0x12b7];

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= -1 << shift;
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${idWanted} not found`);
}

/*
 * TYPE SECTION
 * ---------------------------------------------
 * 0x60 = function type
 */
const typeSec = findSection(1);

const types: {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
}[] = [];

{
  let p = typeSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const form = wasm[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unexpected type form at ${hex(p - 1, 8)} = ${hex(form, 2)}`
      );
    }

    const pc = readUleb(p);
    p = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
      params.push(wasm[p++]);
    }

    const rc = readUleb(p);
    p = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
      results.push(wasm[p++]);
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
  }
}

/*
 * FUNCTION SECTION
 * ---------------------------------------------
 * maps defined-function ordinal -> type index
 */
const funcSec = findSection(3);

const definedTypes: number[] = [];

{
  let p = funcSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const x = readUleb(p);
    p = x.next;
    definedTypes.push(x.value);
  }
}

/*
 * IMPORT SECTION
 * ---------------------------------------------
 */
const importSec = findSection(2);

let importedFunctions = 0;

{
  let p = importSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const t = readUleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      importedFunctions++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      p++;
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;
      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = readUleb(p);
      p = flags.next;
      const min = readUleb(p);
      p = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(p);
        p = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      p += 2;
    }
  }
}

function globalType(func: number): number | null {
  if (func < importedFunctions) {
    return null;
  }

  const ordinal = func - importedFunctions;

  if (ordinal < 0 || ordinal >= definedTypes.length) {
    return null;
  }

  return definedTypes[ordinal];
}

function sig(typeIndex: number) {
  const t = types[typeIndex];

  if (!t) return "UNKNOWN";

  return `PARAMS=[${t.params.join(",")}] RESULTS=[${t.results.join(",")}]`;
}

console.log("INSPECT DISPATCH TYPES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");
console.log(`TYPE COUNT = ${types.length}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${definedTypes.length}`);
console.log("");

/*
 * Find all functions having each target type.
 */
for (const typeIndex of TARGET_TYPES) {
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`TYPE ${typeIndex}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  console.log(sig(typeIndex));

  const matches: number[] = [];

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < definedTypes.length; ordinal++) {
    if (definedTypes[ordinal] === typeIndex) {
      matches.push(importedFunctions + ordinal);
    }
  }

  console.log(`DEFINED MATCHES = ${matches.length}`);

  if (matches.length > 0) {
    console.log(
      matches.map(x => `FUNC ${x}`).join("\n")
    );
  }
}

/*
 * CODE SECTION
 */
const codeSec = findSection(10);

const bodies = new Map<number, { start: number; end: number }>();

{
  let p = codeSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const size = readUleb(p);
    p = size.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + size.value;

    bodies.set(importedFunctions + i, { start, end });

    p = end;
  }
}

/*
 * ELEMENT SECTION
 * ---------------------------------------------
 * Resolve table[0] entries.
 */
const elemSec = findSection(9);

const tableMap = new Map<number, number>();

{
  let p = elemSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log("TABLE 0 MAPPING");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENTS = ${count.value}`);

  for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {
    const flags = readUleb(p);
    p = flags.next;

    /*
     * We only need the active table-0 function segment
     * used by this WASM.
     *
     * flags=0:
     *   table index omitted
     *   offset expr follows
     *   function indices follow
     */
    if (flags.value === 0) {
      const opcode = wasm[p++];

      if (opcode !== 0x41) {
        throw new Error(
          `Unexpected element offset opcode ${hex(opcode, 2)}`
        );
      }

      const off = readSleb(p);
      p = off.next;

      const endOpcode = wasm[p++];

      if (endOpcode !== 0x0b) {
        throw new Error(
          `Unexpected element offset end ${hex(endOpcode, 2)}`
        );
      }

      const n = readUleb(p);
      p = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const f = readUleb(p);
        p = f.next;

        tableMap.set(off.value + i, f.value);
      }
    } else if (flags.value === 1) {
      /*
       * passive element segment
       */
      const elemKind = wasm[p++];

      if (elemKind !== 0x00) {
        throw new Error(
          `Unexpected passive elemkind ${hex(elemKind, 2)}`
        );
      }

      const n = readUleb(p);
      p = n.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
        const f = readUleb(p);
        p = f.next;
      }
    } else {
      /*
       * Other segment formats are not expected in this file.
       */
      console.log(
        `SEGMENT ${seg}: flags=${flags.value} (not decoded)`
      );
    }
  }
}

/*
 * Known selector mappings
 */
console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("KNOWN DISPATCH SELECTORS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const index of TARGET_INDICES) {
  const f = tableMap.get(index);

  if (f === undefined) {
    console.log(`${hex(index, 4)} -> NOT FOUND`);
  } else {
    const t = globalType(f);

    console.log(
      `${hex(index, 4)} -> FUNC ${f} -> TYPE ${t ?? "IMPORT"}`
    );
  }
}

/*
 * For each TYPE, list all table indices pointing to functions
 * with that exact type.
 */
for (const typeIndex of TARGET_TYPES) {
  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`TABLE INDICES -> TYPE ${typeIndex}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let count = 0;

  for (const [index, func] of tableMap.entries()) {
    const t = globalType(func);

    if (t === typeIndex) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(index, 4)} -> FUNC ${func}`
      );
      count++;
    }
  }

  console.log(`TOTAL = ${count}`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

(async () => {
    const fs = require("fs");
    const wabt = await require("wabt")();

    const file =
        "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

    const wanted = new Set([91, 113, 119, 122, 656, 2057]);

    const features = {
        exceptions: true,
        mutable_globals: true,
        sat_float_to_int: true,
        sign_extension: true,
        simd: true,
        threads: true,
        function_references: true,
        multi_value: true,
        tail_call: true,
        bulk_memory: true,
        reference_types: true,
        annotations: true,
        code_metadata: true,
        gc: true,
        memory64: true,
        multi_memory: true,
        extended_const: true,
        relaxed_simd: true
    };

    const mod = wabt.readWasm(fs.readFileSync(file), {
        readDebugNames: false,
        ...features
    });

    mod.validate();

    const wat = mod.toText({
        foldExprs: false,
        inlineExport: false
    });

    const lines = wat.split(/\r?\n/);
    const bodies = new Map();

    let currentId = null;
    let currentBody = [];

    function saveCurrent() {
        if (currentId !== null && wanted.has(currentId)) {
            bodies.set(currentId, currentBody.slice());
        }
    }

    for (const line of lines) {
        const match = line.match(/^\s*\(func \(;(\d+);\)/);

        if (match) {
            saveCurrent();
            currentId = Number(match[1]);
            currentBody = [line];
        } else if (currentId !== null) {
            currentBody.push(line);
        }
    }

    saveCurrent();

    console.log("DISPATCH WRAPPER INSPECTION");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");

    for (const id of [...wanted].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {
        const body = bodies.get(id);

        console.log("");
        console.log("================================================");
        console.log("FUNCTION:", id);

        if (!body) {
            console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
            continue;
        }

        console.log("BODY LINES:", body.length);

        body.forEach((line, i) => {
            console.log(`${i + 1}: ${line.trimEnd()}`);
        });
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("FUNCTIONS FOUND:", bodies.size);

    mod.destroy();
})().catch(error => {
    console.error(error);
    process.exitCode = 1;
});

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readU32(buf, p) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hexByte(b) {
    return b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
}

function dump(buf, start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {

        const lineEnd = Math.min(p + 16, end);

        const bytes = [];

        for (let q = p; q < lineEnd; q++) {
            bytes.push(hexByte(buf[q]));
        }

        console.log(
            "0x" +
            p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0") +
            "  " +
            bytes.join(" ")
        );
    }
}

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readU32LE(buf: Buffer, p: number): number {
    return (
        buf[p] |
        (buf[p + 1] << 8) |
        (buf[p + 2] << 16) |
        (buf[p + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(
    buf: Buffer,
    p: number
): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
        }
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function dump(buf: Buffer, start: number, count: number) {
    for (let p = start; p < start + count; p += 16) {
        const end = Math.min(p + 16, start + count);

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let q = p; q < end; q++) {
            bytes.push(buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
        }

        console.log(
            hex(p),
            bytes.join(" ")
        );
    }
}

function parseSections(buf: Buffer) {
    const sections: any[] = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const size = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = size.next;

        const payloadStart = p;
        const payloadEnd = p + size.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            payloadStart,
            payloadEnd,
            size: size.value
        });

        p = payloadEnd;
    }

    return sections;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" ELEMENT SECTION RAW DECODER");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

    const sections = parseSections(buf);

    const sec = sections.find((x) => x.id === 9);

    if (!sec) {
        throw new Error("Element section not found");
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("WASM size :", buf.length.toLocaleString());
    console.log("Element section payload :", hex(sec.payloadStart));
    console.log("Element section size    :", sec.size);
    console.log("Element section end     :", hex(sec.payloadEnd));

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" RAW HEADER");
    console.log("============================================================");

    dump(
        buf,
        sec.payloadStart,
        Math.min(256, sec.size)
    );

    let p = sec.payloadStart;

    const count = readULEB(buf, p);
    p = count.next;

    console.log("");
    console.log("Element segment count :", count.value);

    for (let seg = 0; seg < count.value; seg++) {
        const segStart = p;

        const flags = readULEB(buf, p);
        p = flags.next;

        console.log("");
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
        console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENT ${seg}`);
        console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

        console.log(
            "segment start :",
            hex(segStart)
        );

        console.log(
            "flags         :",
            flags.value,
            hex(flags.value)
        );

        /*
         * Print a generous raw window immediately after flags.
         * This lets us identify the exact encoding before writing
         * a specialized parser.
         */

        console.log("");
        console.log("RAW AFTER FLAGS:");

        dump(
            buf,
            p,
            Math.min(128, sec.payloadEnd - p)
        );

        /*
         * Do not attempt to guess the remaining structure.
         * For this diagnostic pass we stop after the first segment.
         */

        break;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" END");
    console.log("============================================================");
}

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const wasmPath = path.resolve(dir, '..', 'emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm');
const outputPath = path.join(dir, 'inspectElementTable7006_output.txt');

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);
const TARGET = 7006;
const lines = [];
const out = value => lines.push(String(value));

let pos = 8;

if (
    wasm.length < 8 ||
    wasm.subarray(0, 4).toString('hex') !== '0061736d'
) {
    throw new Error('Invalid WASM magic header');
}

function readULEB(state) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (state.p < state.end) {
        const b = wasm[state.p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return value;
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error('ULEB too large');
        }
    }

    throw new Error('Unexpected end of ULEB');
}

function readSLEB(state) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    do {
        if (state.p >= state.end) {
            throw new Error('Unexpected end of SLEB');
        }

        b = wasm[state.p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;
    } while (b & 0x80);

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value -= 2 ** shift;
    }

    return value;
}

function readByte(state) {
    if (state.p >= state.end) {
        throw new Error('Unexpected end of section');
    }

    return wasm[state.p++];
}

function skipExpr(state) {
    const start = state.p;
    let firstConst = null;

    while (state.p < state.end) {
        const op = readByte(state);

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            return {
                start,
                end: state.p,
                firstConst
            };
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const value = readSLEB(state);
            if (firstConst === null) firstConst = value;
        } else if (op === 0x23 || op === 0xd2) {
            readULEB(state);
        } else if (op === 0x42) {
            readSLEB(state);
        } else if (op === 0x43) {
            state.p += 4;
        } else if (op === 0x44) {
            state.p += 8;
        } else if (op === 0xd0) {
            readSLEB(state);
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported const-expression opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    throw new Error('Unterminated constant expression');
}

function readElementExprFunc(state) {
    const start = state.p;
    let func = null;

    while (state.p < state.end) {
        const op = readByte(state);

        if (op === 0x0b) break;

        if (op === 0xd2) {
            func = readULEB(state);
        } else if (op === 0xd0) {
            readSLEB(state);
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported element-expression opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    return { func, start, end: state.p };
}

function readFuncVector(state, segment, tableIndex, offset) {
    const count = readULEB(state);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const func = readULEB(state);

        if (func === TARGET) {
            out(
                `MATCH: function ${TARGET}, segment=${segment}, ` +
                `table=${tableIndex}, offset=${offset}, element=${i}` +
                (offset === null ? '' : `, table slot=${offset + i}`)
            );
        }
    }

    return count;
}

function readExprVector(state, segment, tableIndex, offset) {
    const count = readULEB(state);

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const item = readElementExprFunc(state);

        if (item.func === TARGET) {
            out(
                `MATCH: function ${TARGET}, segment=${segment}, ` +
                `table=${tableIndex}, offset=${offset}, element=${i}` +
                (offset === null ? '' : `, table slot=${offset + i}`)
            );
        }
    }

    return count;
}

out('ROOSTER WASM — ELEMENT TABLE INSPECTION');
out('READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION');
out('====================================================');
out(`FILE: ${wasmPath}`);
out(`TARGET FUNCTION: ${TARGET}`);
out('');

let elementSections = 0;
let segments = 0;
let matches = 0;
const errors = [];

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];

    const sizeState = { p: pos, end: wasm.length };
    const sectionSize = readULEB(sizeState);
    const sectionStart = sizeState.p;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

    if (sectionEnd > wasm.length) {
        throw new Error('Section extends beyond file');
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;

    if (sectionId !== 9) continue;

    elementSections++;

    const state = {
        p: sectionStart,
        end: sectionEnd
    };

    try {
        const count = readULEB(state);
        out(`ELEMENT SEGMENTS: ${count}`);

        for (let s = 0; s < count; s++) {
            segments++;

            const flags = readULEB(state);
            let tableIndex = 0;
            let offset = null;

            if (flags === 0 || flags === 2 || flags === 4 || flags === 6) {
                if (flags === 2 || flags === 6) {
                    tableIndex = readULEB(state);
                }

                const expr = skipExpr(state);

                if (expr.firstConst !== null) {
                    offset = expr.firstConst;
                }
            }

            if (flags === 0 || flags === 2) {
                readFuncVector(state, s, tableIndex, offset);
            } else if (flags === 1 || flags === 3 || flags === 5 || flags === 7) {
                if (flags === 1 || flags === 3) {
                    readByte(state); // elemkind
                    readFuncVector(state, s, null, null);
                } else {
                    readByte(state); // reference type
                    readExprVector(state, s, null, null);
                }
            } else if (flags === 4 || flags === 6) {
                readExprVector(state, s, tableIndex, offset);
            } else {
                throw new Error(`Unknown element segment flags: ${flags}`);
            }
        }

        if (state.p !== state.end) {
            out(`WARNING: ${state.end - state.p} unparsed bytes remain`);
        }
    } catch (error) {
        errors.push(String(error));
        out(`PARSER WARNING: ${error.message}`);
    }
}

out('');
out('====================================================');
out(`ELEMENT SECTIONS FOUND: ${elementSections}`);
out(`SEGMENTS PROCESSED: ${segments}`);
out(`PARSER WARNINGS: ${errors.length}`);
out('NOTE: No match is not proof of absence if parsing warned.');
out('No WASM execution performed.');

fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, lines.join('\n') + '\n', 'utf8');

console.log(`Element sections: ${elementSections}`);
console.log(`Segments processed: ${segments}`);
console.log(`Parser warnings: ${errors.length}`);
console.log(`Output: ${outputPath}`);

const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u8(p) {
  return buf[p];
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = u8(pos++);

    value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      return {
        value,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readBytes(start, end) {
  const out = [];

  for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {
    out.push(buf[p].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  return out.join(" ");
}

  const sectionId = u8(p++);

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = p + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId !== 9) {
    p = payloadEnd;
    continue;
  }

  console.log("ELEMENT SECTION FOUND");

  console.log("SECTION ID       :", sectionId);
  console.log("SECTION START    :", hex(sectionStart));
  console.log("PAYLOAD START    :", hex(payloadStart));
  console.log("PAYLOAD END      :", hex(payloadEnd));
  console.log("PAYLOAD SIZE     :", sectionSize);

  console.log();
  console.log("RAW SECTION HEADER/PAYLOAD PREFIX");
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  const rawEnd = Math.min(payloadEnd, payloadStart + 256);

  for (let x = payloadStart; x < rawEnd; x += 16) {
    const e = Math.min(x + 16, rawEnd);

    console.log(
      hex(x) + "  " + readBytes(x, e)
    );
  }

  console.log();
  console.log("FIRST ULEB IN PAYLOAD");

  const countInfo = readULEB(payloadStart);

  console.log(

    countInfo.value
  );

  let q = countInfo.next;

  console.log();
  console.log("RAW BYTES AFTER SEGMENT COUNT");
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  for (let x = q; x < afterCountEnd; x += 16) {

    );

const fs = require("fs");

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

function u32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function i32(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) {
      if (s < 32 && (b & 0x40)) v |= (~0 << s);
      return { value: v | 0, next: p };
    }
  }
}

function hx(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const targets = [
  { fn: 9368, off: 0x00321b99 },
  { fn: 9479, off: 0x00323091 },
  { fn: 9479, off: 0x00323110 }
];

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const s = u32(p);
  p = s.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    codeEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

let q = codeStart;
const count = u32(q);
q = count.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const bs = u32(q);
  q = bs.next;

  bodies.push({
    fn: 83 + i,
    start: q,
    end: q + bs.value
  });

  q += bs.value;
}

function findBody(fn) {
  return bodies.find(x => x.fn === fn);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Inspect exact target regions
// ------------------------------------------------------------

for (const target of targets) {

  const body = findBody(target.fn);

  console.log("");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${target.fn} @ ${hx(target.off)}`);
  console.log("============================================================");

  if (!body) {
    console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  const start = Math.max(body.start, target.off - 32);
  const end = Math.min(body.end, target.off + 24);

  console.log(`BODY : ${hx(body.start)} .. ${hx(body.end)}`);
  console.log(`RANGE: ${hx(start)} .. ${hx(end)}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {

    const off = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hx(off)} local.get ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hx(off)} local.set ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hx(off)} local.tee ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = i32(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} i32.const ${x.value} (${hx(x.value >>> 0)})`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = u32(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = u32(p);
      p = o.next;

      console.log(
        `${hx(off)} i32.load offset=${o.value}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
      const a = u32(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = u32(a.next);

      if (o.value === 24) {
        console.log(
          `${hx(off)} i32.store offset=24  <=== TARGET`
        );
      } else {
        console.log(
          `${hx(off)} i32.store offset=${o.value}`
        );
      }

      p = o.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = u32(p);
      p = x.next;
      console.log(`${hx(off)} CALL ${x.value}`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x45) {
      console.log(`${hx(off)} i32.eqz`);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
      console.log(`${hx(off)} i32.add`);
      continue;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hx(off)} opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("INTERPRETATION");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("Look especially at the i32.const immediately before each");
console.log("i32.store offset=24.");
console.log("We need the EXACT constant written into object+24.");

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    do {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF');
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    } while (byte & 0x80);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function dump(start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const stop = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [...wasm.subarray(p, stop)]
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ');
        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes}`);
    }
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error('Code section not found');

const count = uleb(codeStart);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    bodies.set(83 + ordinal, {
        start,
        end,
        size: size.value
    });

    pos = end;
}

const targets = [7, 95, 1022, 1769];

console.log('FUNC 1022 CALL CHAIN INSPECTION');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${count.value}`);

for (const func of targets) {
    const body = bodies.get(func);

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC ${func}`);

    if (!body) {
        console.log('BODY NOT FOUND IN DEFINED FUNCTIONS');
        continue;
    }

    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE = ${body.size}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    dump(body.start, body.end);
}

console.log('\nDONE - READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');
