import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 104;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
  return (
    "0x" +
    (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0")
  );
}

function readMemArg(pos: number) {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

const sections: {
  id: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  sections.push({
    id,
    start: p,
    end: p + size.value,
  });

  p += size.value;
}

const importSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Missing FUNCTION/CODE section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  p = importSec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readULEB(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readULEB(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0x00) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      importedCount++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
      p++;

      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
      p += 2;
    } else if (kind === 0x04) {
      p++;
      p = readULEB(p).next;
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

p = funcSec.start;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    functionTypes.push(r.value);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND FUNC 104
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

let found = false;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

  const localDecls = readULEB(p);
  p = localDecls.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++;
  }

  const codeStart = p;
  const globalFunc = importedCount + ordinal;

  if (globalFunc !== TARGET_FUNC) {
    p = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  found = true;

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} RAW BODY`);
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${globalFunc}`);
  console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${ordinal}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${functionTypes[ordinal]}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY = ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`
  );
  console.log(`SIZE = ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let q = codeStart;

  while (q < bodyEnd) {
    const addr = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    let text =
      `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

    switch (op) {
      case 0x02: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `block type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x03: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `loop type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x04: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `if type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        text = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `br ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        text = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `call func=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const t = readULEB(q);
        q = t.next;

        const table = readULEB(q);
        q = table.next;

        text =
          `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        text = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        text =
          `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        text =
          `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x2d: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        text =
          `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;

      case 0x36: {

        q = m.next;

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0;
const TARGET = 106;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0, shift = 0;
    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        if (!(b & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: pos };
        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeCount = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        const c = uleb(p);
        codeCount = c.value;
        p = c.next;
        codeStart = p;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

let bodyStart = null;
let bodyEnd = null;

for (let i = 0; i < codeCount; i++) {
    const s = uleb(p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + size;

    if (i + 83 === TARGET) {
        bodyStart = start;
        bodyEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 106 / GLOBAL 0 FLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();

console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`BODY           : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`SIZE           : ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
console.log();

console.log("RAW BYTES");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

for (let a = bodyStart; a < bodyEnd; a += 16) {
    const e = Math.min(a + 16, bodyEnd);
    const bytes = [];

    for (let x = a; x < e; x++) {
        bytes.push(wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
    }

    console.log(`${hex(a)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`);
}

console.log();
console.log("GLOBAL / CALL REFERENCES");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

p = bodyStart;

// locals
const lc = uleb(p);
p = lc.next;

for (let i = 0; i < lc.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++;
}

while (p < bodyEnd) {
    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        console.log(`${hex(at)} GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`);

    } else if (op === 0x24) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        console.log(`${hex(at)} GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`);

    } else if (op === 0x10) {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);

    } else if (op === 0x11) {
        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;
        const tab = uleb(p);
        p = tab.next;
        console.log(`${hex(at)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`);

    } else if (op === 0x0f) {
        console.log(`${hex(at)} RETURN`);
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

import fs from "fs";

const buf = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_ORDINAL = 36;
const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_GLOBAL = TARGET_ORDINAL + IMPORTED;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
    });

    p += s.value;
}

const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!codeSec)
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

let c = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(c);
c = count.next;

let target:
    | {
        start: number;
        end: number;
        instrStart: number;
    }
    | null = null;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {

    const bodySize = readULEB(c);
    c = bodySize.next;

    const start = c;
    const end = c + bodySize.value;

    let x = start;

    const localGroups = readULEB(x);
    x = localGroups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(x);
        x = n.next;
        x++;
    }

    if (ordinal === TARGET_ORDINAL) {
        target = {
            start,
            end,
            instrStart: x
        };
        break;
    }

    c = end;
}

if (!target) {
    throw new Error(
        `TARGET CODE ORDINAL NOT FOUND: ${TARGET_ORDINAL}`
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 119 RAW BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${TARGET_ORDINAL}`);
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${TARGET_GLOBAL}`);
console.log(`BODY          = ${hex(target.start)} .. ${hex(target.end)}`);
console.log(`INSTR         = ${hex(target.instrStart)}`);
console.log(`SIZE          = ${target.end - target.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (
    let x = target.start;
    x < target.end;
    x += 16
) {
    const end = Math.min(x + 16, target.end);

    let line = `${hex(x)}  `;

    for (let i = x; i < end; i++) {
        line += buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(line);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspectFunc122Dispatch.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_FUNC = 122;
const TARGET_TYPE = 3;
const TARGET_TABLE = 0x12b4;

function readUleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8): string {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function findSection(idWanted: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readUleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === idWanted) {
      return {
        start,
        end,
        size: s.value,
      };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`Section ${idWanted} not found`);
}

function typeString(params: number[], results: number[]) {
  return `PARAMS=[${params.join(", ")}] RESULTS=[${results.join(", ")}]`;
}

console.log("INSPECT FUNC122 DISPATCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`TARGET FUNC  = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`TARGET TYPE  = ${TARGET_TYPE}`);
console.log(`TABLE INDEX  = ${hex(TARGET_TABLE)}`);

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE SECTION");
console.log("============================================================");

const typeSec = findSection(1);

let p = typeSec.start;

const typeCount = readUleb(p);
p = typeCount.next;

console.log(`TYPE COUNT = ${typeCount.value}`);

const types: {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
}[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.value; i++) {
  const form = wasm[p++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    throw new Error(
      `TYPE ${i}: expected 0x60, got ${hex(form, 2)}`
    );
  }

  const pc = readUleb(p);
  p = pc.next;

  const params: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
    params.push(wasm[p++]);
  }

  const rc = readUleb(p);
  p = rc.next;

  const results: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
    results.push(wasm[p++]);
  }

  types.push({ params, results });

  if (i === TARGET_TYPE) {
    console.log(`TYPE ${i}`);
    console.log(`  ${typeString(params, results)}`);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION SECTION");
console.log("============================================================");

const importSec = findSection(2);
const funcSec = findSection(3);

let importedFunctionCount = 0;

{
  let q = importSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(q);
  q = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readUleb(q);
    q = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readUleb(q);
    q = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[q++];

    if (kind === 0) {
      const ti = readUleb(q);
      q = ti.next;
      importedFunctionCount++;
    } else if (kind === 1) {
      q += 1;

      const flags = readUleb(q);
      q = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(q);
      q = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(q);
        q = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 2) {
      const flags = readUleb(q);
      q = flags.next;

      const min = readUleb(q);
      q = min.next;

      if (flags.value & 1) {
        const max = readUleb(q);
        q = max.next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 3) {
      q += 2;
    } else {
      throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
    }
  }
}

let funcTypeIndex: number | null = null;

{
  let q = funcSec.start;

  const count = readUleb(q);
  q = count.next;

  console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTION COUNT = ${count.value}`);
  console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTION COUNT = ${importedFunctionCount}`);

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ti = readUleb(q);
    q = ti.next;

    const globalFunc = importedFunctionCount + i;

    if (globalFunc === TARGET_FUNC) {
      funcTypeIndex = ti.value;
      console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} TYPE INDEX = ${ti.value}`);
    }
  }
}

console.log("");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync(
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm"
);

const TARGET = 131;

function uleb(p) {
  let v=0,s=0;
  while(true) {
    const x=b[p++];
    v += (x&0x7f)*2**s;
    if(!(x&0x80)) return {value:v,next:p};
    s+=7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v=0,s=0,x;
  do {
    x=b[p++];
    v |= (x&0x7f)<<s;
    s+=7;
  } while(x&0x80);
  if(s<32 && (x&0x40)) v|=(~0<<s);
  return {value:v,next:p};
}

let p=8;
let typeSec=null, funcSec=null, codeSec=null;

while(p<b.length) {
  const id=b[p++];
  const z=uleb(p);
  const size=z.value;
  p=z.next;

  if(id===1) typeSec={start:p,end:p+size};
  if(id===3) funcSec={start:p,end:p+size};
  if(id===10) codeSec={start:p,end:p+size};

  p+=size;
}

function importedCount() {
  let p=8;

  while(p<b.length) {
    const id=b[p++];
    const z=uleb(p);
    const size=z.value;
    p=z.next;

    if(id!==2) {
      p+=size;
      continue;
    }

    const end=p+size;
    const c=uleb(p);
    p=c.next;

    let n=0;

    for(let i=0;i<c.value;i++) {
      let x=uleb(p); p=x.next+x.value;
      x=uleb(p); p=x.next+x.value;

      const kind=b[p++];

      if(kind===0) {
        x=uleb(p); p=x.next;
        n++;
      } else if(kind===1) {
        p+=4;
      } else if(kind===2) {
        x=uleb(p); p=x.next+x.value;
      } else if(kind===3) {
        p+=2;
      }
    }

    return n;
  }

  return 0;
}

const imp=importedCount();
const ord=TARGET-imp;

let fp=funcSec.start;
let fc=uleb(fp);
fp=fc.next;

let typeIndex=null;

for(let i=0;i<=ord;i++) {
  const x=uleb(fp);
  fp=x.next;
  if(i===ord) typeIndex=x.value;
}

let cp=codeSec.start;
let cc=uleb(cp);
cp=cc.next;

let body=null;

for(let i=0;i<cc.value;i++) {
  const x=uleb(cp);
  const size=x.value;
  cp=x.next;

  if(i===ord) {
    body={start:cp,end:cp+size};
    break;
  }

  cp+=size;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 131 EXACT BODY");

console.log(`IMPORTED COUNT : ${imp}`);

console.log(`BODY           : 0x${body.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`SIZE           : ${body.end-body.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function dec(p) {
  const a=p,op=b[p++];

  switch(op) {
    case 0x01:return{next:p,s:"NOP"};
    case 0x0b:return{next:p,s:"END"};
    case 0x0f:return{next:p,s:"RETURN"};

    case 0x10:{
      const x=uleb(p);
      return{next:x.next,s:`CALL FUNC ${x.value}`};
    }

    case 0x11:{
      const t=uleb(p);p=t.next;
      const tb=uleb(p);p=tb.next;
      return{next:p,s:`CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`};
    }

    case 0x20:{
      const x=uleb(p);
      return{next:x.next,s:`LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`};
    }

    case 0x21:{
      const x=uleb(p);
      return{next:x.next,s:`LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`};
    }

    case 0x22:{
      const x=uleb(p);
      return{next:x.next,s:`LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`};
    }

    case 0x23:{
      const x=uleb(p);
      return{next:x.next,s:`GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`};
    }

    case 0x24:{
      const x=uleb(p);
      return{next:x.next,s:`GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`};
    }

    case 0x28:{
      const al=uleb(p);p=al.next;
      const of=uleb(p);p=of.next;
      return{next:p,s:`I32.LOAD align=${al.value} offset=${of.value}`};
    }

    case 0x2d:{
      const al=uleb(p);p=al.next;
      const of=uleb(p);p=of.next;
      return{next:p,s:`I32.LOAD8_U align=${al.value} offset=${of.value}`};
    }

    case 0x36:{
      const al=uleb(p);p=al.next;
      const of=uleb(p);p=of.next;
      return{next:p,s:`I32.STORE align=${al.value} offset=${of.value}`};
    }

    case 0x41:{
      const x=sleb(p);
      return{next:x.next,s:`I32.CONST ${x.value} (0x${(x.value>>>0).toString(16)})`};
    }

    case 0x45:return{next:p,s:"I32.EQZ"};
    case 0x46:return{next:p,s:"I32.EQ"};
    case 0x47:return{next:p,s:"I32.NE"};
    case 0x48:return{next:p,s:"I32.LT_S"};
    case 0x4a:return{next:p,s:"I32.GT_S"};

    case 0x6a:return{next:p,s:"I32.ADD"};
    case 0x6b:return{next:p,s:"I32.SUB"};
    case 0x71:return{next:p,s:"I32.AND"};
    case 0x72:return{next:p,s:"I32.OR"};

    default:
      return{next:p,s:`OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}`};
  }
}

let q=body.start;

while(q<body.end) {
  const x=dec(q);
  console.log(`0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ${x.s}`);
  q=x.next;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("REFERENCES TO 0x19534C / 0x250FB8 / 0x121E7B4");

for(let q=body.start;q<body.end;q++) {
  if(b[q]===0x41) {
    const x=sleb(q+1);

    if(
      x.value===0x19534c ||
      x.value===0x250fb8 ||
      x.value===0x121e7b4
    ) {
      console.log(
        `0x${q.toString(16)} CONST ${x.value} = 0x${(x.value>>>0).toString(16)}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "node:fs";
import os from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { pathToFileURL } from "node:url";

const decoderPath = path.resolve(
  "scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs"
);

const decoderSource = fs.readFileSync(decoderPath, "utf8");

const marker =
  "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";

const markerIndex = decoderSource.indexOf(marker);

if (markerIndex < 0) {
  throw new Error(
    "Decoder bootstrap marker not found. Original decoder was not modified."
  );
}

const tempDecoderPath = path.join(
  os.tmpdir(),
  `rooster-decoder-${process.pid}.mjs`
);

const bootstrap =
  decoderSource.slice(0, markerIndex) +
  "\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n";

fs.writeFileSync(tempDecoderPath, bootstrap, "utf8");

try {
  const { bodies, decodeFunction } = await import(
    pathToFileURL(tempDecoderPath).href
  );

  function decodeSafely(funcId) {
    try {
      return decodeFunction(funcId);
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  function printFunction(funcId, radius = 0, focusIndex = null) {
    const result = decodeSafely(funcId);

    if (!result) {
      console.log(`FUNC ${funcId}: DECODE FAILED`);
      return;
    }

    const instructions = result.instructions;

    console.log(`\nFUNC ${funcId}`);
    console.log(`BODY OFFSET: 0x${result.body.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
    console.log(`INSTRUCTIONS: ${instructions.length}`);
    console.log("-".repeat(76));

    let start = 0;
    let end = instructions.length;

    if (focusIndex !== null) {
      start = Math.max(0, focusIndex - radius);
      end = Math.min(instructions.length, focusIndex + radius + 1);
    }

    for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
      const ins = instructions[i];
      const offset = Number(ins.offset);

      console.log(
        `FUNC ${funcId} #${String(i).padStart(5)} ` +
        `0x${offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | ${ins.text}`
      );
    }

    if (start > 0 || end < instructions.length) {
      console.log(
        `... showing instructions ${start} through ${end - 1}`
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("ROOSTER WASM — FUNCTION 1413 ANALYSIS");
  console.log("READ ONLY / OFFLINE / NO WASM EXECUTION");
  console.log("NO TRANSACTIONS SENT");
  console.log("=".repeat(76));

  // 1. Inspect the complete function 1413.
  printFunction(1413);

  // 2. Find all direct callers and print context around each call.
  console.log("\n\nDIRECT CALLERS OF FUNC 1413");
  console.log("=".repeat(76));

  let callerCount = 0;
  let decodedCount = 0;
  let failedCount = 0;

  for (const funcId of bodies.keys()) {
    const result = decodeSafely(funcId);

    if (!result) {
      failedCount++;
      continue;
    }

    decodedCount++;

    const instructions = result.instructions;

    for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
      if (!/^\s*call\s+1413(?:\s|$)/.test(instructions[i].text)) {
        continue;
      }

      callerCount++;

      console.log(
        `\nCALLER FUNC ${funcId}, CALL INSTRUCTION #${i}`
      );

      const start = Math.max(0, i - 8);
      const end = Math.min(instructions.length, i + 10);

      for (let j = start; j < end; j++) {
        const ins = instructions[j];
        const offset = Number(ins.offset);
        const pointer = j === i ? " >>> " : "     ";

        console.log(
          `${pointer}FUNC ${funcId} #${String(j).padStart(5)} ` +
          `0x${offset.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} | ${ins.text}`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("\n\nSUMMARY");
  console.log("=".repeat(76));
  console.log(`FUNCTION 1413 DECODED: ${decodeSafely(1413) ? "YES" : "NO"}`);
  console.log(`DIRECT CALL SITES: ${callerCount}`);
  console.log(`FUNCTIONS DECODED DURING SCAN: ${decodedCount}`);
  console.log(`FUNCTION DECODE FAILURES: ${failedCount}`);
  console.log("ANALYSIS COMPLETE — NO WASM EXECUTION");
} finally {
  try {
    fs.unlinkSync(tempDecoderPath);
  } catch {
    // Temporary-file cleanup only.
  }
}

import fs from "node:fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 1533;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32LE(pos: number): number {
  return (
    wasm[pos] |
    (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readS32(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Parse WASM sections and recover code bodies.
 */
let p = 8;
let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const sectionId = wasm[p++];
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = p + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeSectionStart = payloadStart;
    codeSectionEnd = payloadEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (codeSectionStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let q = codeSectionStart;
const countInfo = readULEB(q);
q = countInfo.next;

let targetBodyStart = -1;
let targetBodyEnd = -1;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < countInfo.value; ordinal++) {
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(q);
  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  q = sizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  const globalFunc = ordinal + 83;

  if (globalFunc === TARGET) {
    targetBodyStart = bodyStart;
    targetBodyEnd = bodyEnd;
    break;
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

if (targetBodyStart < 0) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET} NOT FOUND`);
}

/*
 * Skip locals vector.
 */
let x = targetBodyStart;
const localCountInfo = readULEB(x);
x = localCountInfo.next;

for (let i = 0; i < localCountInfo.value; i++) {
  const n = readULEB(x);
  x = n.next;

  x++; // valtype
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 1533 RAW BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`BODY        = ${hex(targetBodyStart)} .. ${hex(targetBodyEnd)}`);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const opNames: Record<number, string> = {
  0x00: "unreachable",
  0x01: "nop",
  0x02: "block",
  0x03: "loop",
  0x04: "if",
  0x05: "else",
  0x0b: "end",
  0x0c: "br",
  0x0d: "br_if",
  0x0e: "br_table",
  0x0f: "return",
  0x10: "call",
  0x11: "call_indirect",
  0x1a: "drop",
  0x20: "local.get",
  0x21: "local.set",
  0x22: "local.tee",
  0x23: "global.get",
  0x24: "global.set",
  0x28: "i32.load",
  0x29: "i64.load",
  0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
  0x36: "i32.store",
  0x3a: "i32.store8",
  0x41: "i32.const",
  0x45: "i32.eqz",
  0x46: "i32.eq",
  0x47: "i32.ne",
  0x48: "i32.lt_s",
  0x49: "i32.lt_u",
  0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
  0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
  0x4c: "i32.le_s",
  0x4d: "i32.le_u",
  0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
  0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
  0x6a: "i32.add",
  0x6b: "i32.sub",
  0x6c: "i32.mul",
  0x71: "i32.and",
  0x72: "i32.or",
  0x73: "i32.xor",
  0x74: "i32.shl",
  0x75: "i32.shr_s",
  0x76: "i32.shr_u",
};

function readMemArg(pos: number) {
  const align = readULEB(pos);
  const offset = readULEB(align.next);
  return {
    align: align.value,
    offset: offset.value,
    next: offset.next,
  };
}

let pos = x;

while (pos < targetBodyEnd) {
  const at = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  let text = opNames[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {
    const m = readMemArg(pos);
    pos = m.next;
    text += ` align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
  } else if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24 ||
    op === 0x10 ||
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {
    const n = readULEB(pos);
    pos = n.next;

    if (op === 0x10) {
      text += ` func=${n.value}`;
    } else {
      text += ` ${n.value}`;
    }
  } else if (op === 0x41) {
    const n = readS32(pos);
    pos = n.next;
    text += ` ${hex(n.value >>> 0)}`;
  } else if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = readULEB(pos);
    pos = t.next;
    const table = readULEB(pos);
    pos = table.next;
    text += ` type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`;
  }

  console.log(`${hex(at)}  ${text}`);
}

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

const targets = [0x2530a4, 0x252d04];

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF in ULEB');
        const byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    do {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF in SLEB');
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;
    } while (byte & 0x80);

    if (byte & 0x40) value -= 2 ** shift;

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

let pos = 8;
let dataSection = null;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const size = readULEB(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (end > wasm.length) throw new Error('Invalid section size');

    if (id === 11) {
        dataSection = { start, end };
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

console.log('FUNC 1769 MEMORY INSPECTION');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${wasm.length}`);

if (!dataSection) {
    console.log('DATA SECTION NOT FOUND');
    process.exit(0);
}

pos = dataSection.start;
const count = readULEB(pos);
pos = count.next;

const segments = [];
let activeCount = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const flags = readULEB(pos);
    pos = flags.next;

    let active = false;
    let offset = null;

    if (flags.value === 0) {
        active = true;
        if (wasm[pos++] !== 0x41) throw new Error('Expected i32.const offset');
        const expr = readSLEB(pos);
        offset = expr.value;
        pos = expr.next;
        if (wasm[pos++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error('Expected end of offset expression');
    } else if (flags.value === 1) {
        active = false;
    } else if (flags.value === 2) {
        active = true;
        const memidx = readULEB(pos);
        pos = memidx.next;
        if (wasm[pos++] !== 0x41) throw new Error('Expected i32.const offset');
        const expr = readSLEB(pos);
        offset = expr.value;
        pos = expr.next;
        if (wasm[pos++] !== 0x0b) throw new Error('Expected end of offset expression');
    } else {
        throw new Error(`Unsupported data segment flags: ${flags.value}`);
    }

    const length = readULEB(pos);
    pos = length.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + length.value;

    if (payloadEnd > dataSection.end) throw new Error('Data segment exceeds section');

    if (active) {
        activeCount++;
        segments.push({
            index: i,
            offset: offset >>> 0,
            length: length.value,
            payloadStart,
            payloadEnd
        });
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS = ${count.value}`);
console.log(`ACTIVE SEGMENTS = ${activeCount}`);

for (const target of targets) {
    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`TARGET LINEAR MEMORY ADDRESS ${hex(target)}`);

    const matches = segments.filter(s =>
        target >= s.offset && target + 16 <= s.offset + s.length
    );

    if (matches.length === 0) {
        console.log('No single active data segment fully covers 16 bytes here.');
        const partial = segments.filter(s =>
            target < s.offset + s.length && target + 16 > s.offset
        );

        if (partial.length) {
            for (const s of partial) {
                console.log(
                    `OVERLAP: segment=${s.index}, base=${hex(s.offset)}, length=${s.length}`
                );
            }
        } else {
            console.log('No active data segment overlaps this address.');
        }
        continue;
    }

    for (const s of matches) {
        const relative = target - s.offset;
        const from = s.payloadStart + relative;
        const bytes = wasm.subarray(from, from + 16);

        console.log(`SEGMENT = ${s.index}`);
        console.log(`BASE = ${hex(s.offset)}`);
        console.log(`LENGTH = ${s.length}`);
        console.log(`RELATIVE OFFSET = ${hex(relative)}`);
        console.log(
            [...bytes].map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join(' ')
        );

        console.log('ASCII: ' + [...bytes]
            .map(b => b >= 32 && b <= 126 ? String.fromCharCode(b) : '.')
            .join(''));
    }
}

console.log('\nDONE - READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');

import fs from "fs";

const buf = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_GLOBAL = 1876;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

    start: number;

    p = s.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
    });

    p += s.value;
}

    if (ordinal + IMPORTED === TARGET_GLOBAL) {
        targetOrdinal = ordinal;
        targetStart = start;
        targetEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    c = end;
}

if (targetStart < 0)
    throw new Error("TARGET FUNCTION NOT FOUND");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 1968;

let p = 8;

function u() {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return v >>> 0;
}

let typeStart = 0;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const size = u();
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 1) typeStart = start;
  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = start;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

// function section: maps defined function index -> type index
p = codeStart;
const bodyCount = u();

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const size = u();
  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  // Skip locals
  const groups = u();
  for (let g = 0; g < groups; g++) {
    u();
    p++;
  }

  bodies.push({
    func: i + 91,
    start,
    end
  });

  p = end;
}

const body = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 1968 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

if (!body) {
  console.log("FUNC NOT FOUND");
  process.exit(1);
}

console.log(
  "BODY:",
  "0x" + body.start.toString(16),
  ".. 0x" + body.end.toString(16)
);

console.log("");

for (let q = body.start; q < body.end; q += 16) {
  const z = Math.min(body.end, q + 16);

  console.log(
    "0x" + q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
    [...b.slice(q, z)]
      .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join(" ")
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc1974Real.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const BODY_START = 0x0008f17c;
const BODY_END   = 0x0008f1a1;

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function uleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (true) {
    byte = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

type Instr = {
  addr: number;
  op: number;
  name: string;
  imm?: number;
};

function decode(start: number, end: number): Instr[] {
  const out: Instr[] = [];
  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    switch (op) {

      case 0x00:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "unreachable" });
        break;

      case 0x01:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "nop" });
        break;

      case 0x02:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "block" });
        p += 1;
        break;

      case 0x03:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "loop" });
        p += 1;
        break;

      case 0x04:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "if" });
        p += 1;
        break;

      case 0x05:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "else" });
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "end" });
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "br",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "br_if",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "return" });
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "call",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        p += 1;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "call_indirect",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "drop" });
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "local.get",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "local.set",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "local.tee",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "global.get",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "global.set",
          imm: x.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "i32.load",
          imm: o.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "i32.store",
          imm: o.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x3a: {
        const a = uleb(p);
        p = a.next;

        const o = uleb(p);
        p = o.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "i32.store8",
          imm: o.value,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const x = sleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "i32.const",
          imm: x.value >>> 0,
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.eqz" });
        break;

      case 0x46:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.eq" });
        break;

      case 0x47:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.ne" });
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.ge_u" });
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.add" });
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.sub" });
        break;

      case 0x71:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.and" });
        break;

      case 0x72:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.or" });
        break;

      case 0x74:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.shl" });
        break;

      case 0x7c:
        out.push({ addr, op, name: "i32.add" });
        break;

      case 0xa7:
        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name: "i32.wrap_i64",
        });
        break;

      default:
        out.push({
          addr,
          op,
          name:
            `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        });
        break;
    }
  }

  return out;
}

console.log("INSPECT REAL FUNC1974");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(60));

console.log(`FUNC = 1974`);
console.log(`BODY = ${hex(BODY_START)} .. ${hex(BODY_END)}`);
console.log();

const ins = decode(BODY_START, BODY_END);

console.log(`INSTRUCTIONS = ${ins.length}`);
console.log();

for (const x of ins) {

  let extra = "";

  if (
    x.name === "local.get" ||
    x.name === "local.set" ||
    x.name === "local.tee"
  ) {
    extra = ` local${x.imm}`;
  }

  if (
    x.name === "i32.const"
  ) {
    extra = ` ${hex(x.imm!)}`;
  }

  if (
    x.name === "call" ||
    x.name === "br" ||
    x.name === "br_if"
  ) {
    extra = ` ${x.imm}`;
  }

  if (
    x.name === "i32.load" ||
    x.name === "i32.store" ||
    x.name === "i32.store8"
  ) {
    extra = ` offset=${hex(x.imm ?? 0)}`;
  }

  if (x.name === "call_indirect") {
    extra = ` type=${x.imm}`;
  }

  console.log(
    `${hex(x.addr)}  ${x.name}${extra}`
  );
}

console.log();
console.log("-".repeat(60));

const stores = ins.filter(
  x =>
    x.name === "i32.store" ||
    x.name === "i32.store8"
);

const loads = ins.filter(
  x => x.name === "i32.load"
);

    x.name === "call" ||
    x.name === "call_indirect"
);

console.log(`LOADS  = ${loads.length}`);
console.log(`STORES = ${stores.length}`);
console.log(`CALLS  = ${calls.length}`);

console.log();
console.log("TARGET ADDRESS LITERALS");

const targets = [
  0x0019534c,
  0x00195350,
  0x00195354,
  0x00195358,
];

for (const x of ins) {
  if (
    x.name === "i32.const" &&
    targets.includes(x.imm!)
  ) {
    console.log(
      `>>> TARGET ${hex(x.imm!)} @ ${hex(x.addr)}`
    );
  }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc2034.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 2034;
const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readU32LE(pos: number): number {
  return (
    wasm[pos] |
    (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
    (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
    (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readString(pos: number): { value: string; next: number } {
  const r = readULEB(pos);
  const end = r.next + r.value;

  return {
    value: wasm.subarray(r.next, end).toString("utf8"),
    next: end,
  };
}

function readSections() {
  const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    payloadStart: number;
    end: number;
  }[] = [];

  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = p;
    const end = payloadStart + sizeInfo.value;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: payloadStart,
      payloadStart,
      end,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const typeSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 1);
const importSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find((s) => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Missing FUNCTION or CODE section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  let p = importSec.payloadStart;
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const mod = readString(p);
    p = mod.next;

    const name = readString(p);
    p = name.next;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    switch (kind) {
      case 0x00: {
        // function
        readULEB(p);
        p = readULEB(p).next;
        importedCount++;
        break;
      }

      case 0x01: {
        // table
        const elemType = wasm[p++];
        void elemType;

        const flags = wasm[p++];
        p = readULEB(p).next;

        if (flags & 1) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }
        break;
      }

      case 0x02: {
        // memory
        const flags = wasm[p++];
        p = readULEB(p).next;

        if (flags & 1) {
          p = readULEB(p).next;
        }
        break;
      }

      case 0x03: {
        // global
        p += 2;
        break;
      }

      case 0x04: {
        // tag
        p++;
        p = readULEB(p).next;
        break;
      }

      default:
        throw new Error(`Unknown import kind 0x${kind.toString(16)}`);
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let functionTypes: number[] = [];

{
  let p = funcSec.payloadStart;
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  functionTypes = new Array(count.value);

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;
    functionTypes[i] = r.value;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

interface Body {
  globalFunc: number;
  ordinal: number;
  typeIndex: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  bodySize: number;
  codeStart: number;
  codeEnd: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

{
  let p = codeSec.payloadStart;
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;

    const localDeclCount = readULEB(p);
    p = localDeclCount.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount.value; i++) {
      p = readULEB(p).next; // local count
      p++; // value type
    }

    const codeStart = p;
    const codeEnd = bodyEnd;

    const globalFunc = importedCount + ordinal;

    bodies.push({
      globalFunc,
      ordinal,
      typeIndex: functionTypes[ordinal],
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      bodySize: sizeInfo.value,
      codeStart,
      codeEnd,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }
}

const target = bodies.find((b) => b.globalFunc === TARGET_FUNC);

if (!target) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// BASIC OPCODE DECODER
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readI32Const(p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  // signed i32
  if (shift < 32 && (result & (1 << shift)) !== 0) {
    result |= (-1 << (shift + 1));
  }

  return {
    value: result | 0,
    next: p,
  };
}

function readMemArg(p: number): {
  align: number;
  offset: number;
  next: number;
} {
  const a = readULEB(p);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} RAW BODY`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${target.globalFunc}`);

console.log(`BODY        = ${hex(target.bodyStart)} .. ${hex(target.bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`SIZE        = ${target.bodySize}`);

let p = target.codeStart;

while (p < target.codeEnd) {
  const at = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  let line = `${hex(at)}  `;

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00:
      line += "unreachable";
      break;

    case 0x01:
      line += "nop";
      break;

    case 0x02:
      line += "block";

      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        line += ` type=${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x03:
      line += "loop";

      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        line += ` type=${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x04:
      line += "if";

      {
        const r = readULEB(p);
        p = r.next;
        line += ` type=${r.value}`;
      }
      break;

    case 0x05:
      line += "else";
      break;

    case 0x0b:
      line += "end";
      break;

    case 0x0c: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `br ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `br_if ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0e: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;

      const labels: number[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < r.value; i++) {
        const x = readULEB(p);
        p = x.next;
        labels.push(x.value);
      }

      const def = readULEB(p);
      p = def.next;

      line += `br_table [${labels.join(", ")}] default=${def.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f:
      line += "return";
      break;

    case 0x10: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `call func=${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const type = readULEB(p);
      p = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      line += `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x1a:
      line += "drop";
      break;

    case 0x20: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `local.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `local.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `local.tee ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `global.get ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `global.set ${r.value}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;
      line += `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;
      line += `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;
      line += `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x2c: {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;
      line += `i32.load8_s align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;
      line += `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: {
      const m = readMemArg(p);
      p = m.next;
      line += `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const r = readI32Const(p);
      p = r.next;
      line += `i32.const ${hex(r.value)}`;
      break;
    }

    case 0x45:
      line += "i32.eqz";
      break;

    case 0x46:
      line += "i32.eq";
      break;

    case 0x47:
      line += "i32.ne";
      break;

    case 0x48:
      line += "i32.lt_s";
      break;

    case 0x49:
      line += "i32.lt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4a:
      line += "i32.gt_s";
      break;

    case 0x4b:
      line += "i32.gt_u";
      break;

    case 0x4c:
      line += "i32.le_s";
      break;

    case 0x4d:
      line += "i32.le_u";
      break;

    case 0x4e:
      line += "i32.ge_s";
      break;

    case 0x4f:
      line += "i32.ge_u";
      break;

    case 0x6a:
      line += "i32.add";
      break;

    case 0x6b:
      line += "i32.sub";
      break;

    case 0x6c:
      line += "i32.mul";
      break;

    case 0x71:
      line += "i32.and";
      break;

    case 0x72:
      line += "i32.or";
      break;

    case 0x73:
      line += "i32.xor";
      break;

    case 0x74:
      line += "i32.shl";
      break;

    case 0x75:
      line += "i32.shr_s";
      break;

    case 0x76:
      line += "i32.shr_u";
      break;

    default:
      line += `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
      break;
  }

  console.log(line);
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const TARGET = 2055;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value:v, next:p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;

const imported = (() => {
  let q = 8;
  const count = uleb(q); q = count.next;
  return count.value;
})();

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_FUNC = 2057;

function readU32LE(pos: number): number {
  return wasm.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

function readU8(pos: number): number {
  return wasm[pos];
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = readU8(pos++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = readU8(pos++);
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

/*
 * Locate code section and function bodies.
 * This is a read-only structural parser.
 */

let p = 8;

let codeStart = -1;
let codeSize = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = readU8(p++);
  const sz = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sz.value;
  p = sz.next;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    codeSize = sectionSize;
    break;
  }

  p += sectionSize;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let q = codeStart;

const count = readULEB(q);
q = count.next;

const bodies: {
  func: number;
  ordinal: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const bodySize = readULEB(q);
  q = bodySize.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize.value;

  const globalFunc = 83 + ordinal;

  bodies.push({
    func: globalFunc,
    ordinal,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd,
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

const body = bodies.find(x => x.func === TARGET_FUNC);

if (!body) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} NOT FOUND`);
}

console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`SIZE = ${body.end - body.start}`);

const stack: string[] = [];

function push(v: string) {
  stack.push(v);
}

function pop(): string {
  return stack.length ? stack.pop()! : "<UNKNOWN>";
}

function showStack() {
  console.log(
    "    STACK = [" +
      stack.map((x, i) => `${i}:${x}`).join(" | ") +
      "]"
  );
}

while (pos < body.end) {
  const at = pos;
  const op = readU8(pos++);

  console.log(`${hex(at)}  opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`);

  switch (op) {
    case 0x0b: // end
      console.log("          end");
      break;

    case 0x0c: { // br
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      console.log(`          br ${x.value}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x0d: { // br_if
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      const cond = pop();
      console.log(`          br_if ${x.value}  cond=${cond}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x0f: // return
      console.log("          return");
      break;

    case 0x1a: { // drop
      const v = pop();
      console.log(`          drop ${v}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x20: { // local.get
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      const v = `local${x.value}`;
      push(v);
      console.log(`          local.get ${x.value} -> ${v}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x21: { // local.set
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      const v = pop();
      console.log(`          local.set ${x.value} <- ${v}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x22: { // local.tee
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      const v = stack.length ? stack[stack.length - 1] : "<UNKNOWN>";
      console.log(`          local.tee ${x.value} = ${v}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x23: { // global.get
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      const v = `global${x.value}`;
      push(v);
      console.log(`          global.get ${x.value}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x24: { // global.set
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;
      const v = pop();
      console.log(`          global.set ${x.value} <- ${v}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x28: { // i32.load
      const align = readULEB(pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(pos);
      pos = off.next;

      const addr = pop();

      const expr =
        off.value === 0
          ? `MEM32[${addr}]`
          : `MEM32[${addr} + ${hex(off.value)}]`;

      push(expr);

      console.log(
        `          i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${hex(
          off.value
        )} addr=${addr}`
      );

      if (addr.includes("0x0019534c")) {
        console.log("          >>> READS TARGET 0x0019534C");
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x2d: { // i32.load8_u
      const align = readULEB(pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(pos);
      pos = off.next;

      const addr = pop();

      const expr =
        off.value === 0
          ? `MEM8[${addr}]`
          : `MEM8[${addr} + ${hex(off.value)}]`;

      push(expr);

      console.log(
        `          i32.load8_u align=${align.value} offset=${hex(
          off.value
        )} addr=${addr}`
      );

      break;
    }

    case 0x36: { // i32.store
      const align = readULEB(pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(pos);
      pos = off.next;

      const value = pop();
      const addr = pop();

      console.log(
        `          i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${hex(
          off.value
        )}`
      );
      console.log(`          ADDRESS = ${addr}`);
      console.log(`          VALUE   = ${value}`);

      if (
        addr.includes("0x0019534c") ||
        addr.includes("MEM32[0x0019534c]")
      ) {
        console.log(
          "          >>> POSSIBLE WRITE TO TARGET 0x0019534C"
        );
      }

      if (addr.includes("local") || addr.includes("TARGET")) {
        console.log("          >>> DYNAMIC ADDRESS");
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x3a: { // i32.store8
      const align = readULEB(pos);
      pos = align.next;

      const off = readULEB(pos);
      pos = off.next;

      const value = pop();
      const addr = pop();

      console.log(
        `          i32.store8 align=${align.value} offset=${hex(
          off.value
        )}`
      );
      console.log(`          ADDRESS = ${addr}`);
      console.log(`          VALUE   = ${value}`);

      break;
    }

    case 0x41: { // i32.const
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;

      const v = hex(x.value);
      push(v);

      console.log(`          i32.const ${v}`);

      if ((x.value >>> 0) === 0x0019534c) {
        console.log(
          "          >>> TARGET CONSTANT 0x0019534C"
        );
      }

      break;
    }

    case 0x45: { // i32.eqz
      const v = pop();
      push(`eqz(${v})`);
      console.log(`          i32.eqz ${v}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x46: { // i32.eq
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();
      push(`(${a} == ${b})`);
      console.log(`          i32.eq ${a}, ${b}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x47: { // i32.ne
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();
      push(`(${a} != ${b})`);
      console.log(`          i32.ne ${a}, ${b}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x4b: { // i32.gt_u
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();
      push(`(${a} >u ${b})`);
      console.log(`          i32.gt_u ${a}, ${b}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x6a: { // i32.add
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();
      push(`(${a} + ${b})`);
      console.log(`          i32.add ${a}, ${b}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x6b: { // i32.sub
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();
      push(`(${a} - ${b})`);
      console.log(`          i32.sub ${a}, ${b}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x72: { // i32.or
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();
      push(`(${a} | ${b})`);
      console.log(`          i32.or ${a}, ${b}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x74: { // i32.shl
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();
      push(`(${a} << ${b})`);
      console.log(`          i32.shl ${a}, ${b}`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x10: { // call
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      pos = x.next;

      console.log(`          call func=${x.value}`);

      // We deliberately do not attempt to execute or infer
      // the callee's runtime result.
      push(`call${x.value}(...)`);
      break;
    }

    case 0x11: { // call_indirect
      const type = readULEB(pos);
      pos = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(pos);
      pos = table.next;

      console.log(
        `          call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`
      );

      push("call_indirect(...)");
      break;
    }

    case 0x04: { // if
      console.log("          if");
      break;
    }

    case 0x05:
      console.log("          else");
      break;

    case 0x02: { // block
      const bt = readU8(pos++);
      console.log(`          block type=0x${bt.toString(16)}`);
      break;
    }

    default:
      console.log(
        `          [unhandled opcode 0x${op
          .toString(16)
          .padStart(2, "0")}]`
      );
      break;
  }

  if (at >= 0x00093e60 && at <= 0x00093ea5) {
    showStack();
  }
}

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const GLOBAL_FUNC = 2057;
const IMPORTED = 83;
const CODE_ORDINAL = GLOBAL_FUNC - IMPORTED;

const TARGETS = new Set([
  0x195348,
  0x19534c,
  0x195350,
  0x195354,
  0x195358,
]);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function readU32LE(pos: number): number {
  return (
    buf[pos] |
    (buf[pos + 1] << 8) |
    (buf[pos + 2] << 16) |
    (buf[pos + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function findCodeSection(): { start: number; end: number } {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number): {
  text: string;
  next: number;
} {
  const op = buf[pos++];

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00:
      return { text: "unreachable", next: pos };

    case 0x01:
      return { text: "nop", next: pos };

    case 0x02:
      return { text: "block", next: pos + 1 };

    case 0x03:
      return { text: "loop", next: pos + 1 };

    case 0x04:
      return { text: "if", next: pos };

    case 0x05:
      return { text: "else", next: pos };

    case 0x0b:
      return { text: "end", next: pos };

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `br ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `br_if ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x0f:
      return { text: "return", next: pos };

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `call ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      const table = readULEB(x.next);
      return {
        text: `call_indirect type=${x.value} table=${table.value}`,
        next: table.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.get ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.set ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `local.tee ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `global.get ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return { text: `global.set ${x.value}`, next: x.next };
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const off = readULEB(a.next);
      return {
        text: `i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${off.value}`,
        next: off.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = readULEB(pos);
      const off = readULEB(a.next);
      return {
        text: `i32.store align=${a.value} offset=${off.value}`,
        next: off.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      return {
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
        next: x.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      return {
        text: `i64.const ${x.value}`,
        next: x.next,
      };
    }

    case 0x45:
      return { text: "i32.eqz", next: pos };

    case 0x46:
      return { text: "i32.eq", next: pos };

    case 0x47:
      return { text: "i32.ne", next: pos };

    case 0x6a:
      return { text: "i32.add", next: pos };

    case 0x6b:
      return { text: "i32.sub", next: pos };

    case 0x6c:
      return { text: "i32.mul", next: pos };

    case 0x6d:
      return { text: "i32.div_s", next: pos };

    case 0x6e:
      return { text: "i32.div_u", next: pos };

    case 0x71:
      return { text: "i32.and", next: pos };

    case 0x72:
      return { text: "i32.or", next: pos };

    case 0x73:
      return { text: "i32.xor", next: pos };

    case 0x74:
      return { text: "i32.shl", next: pos };

    case 0x75:
      return { text: "i32.shr_s", next: pos };

    case 0x76:
      return { text: "i32.shr_u", next: pos };

    case 0xa7:
      return { text: "i32.wrap_i64", next: pos };

    case 0xad:
      return { text: "i64.extend_i32_u", next: pos };

    default:
      return {
        text: `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
        next: pos,
      };
  }
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;
const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + sizeInfo.value;

  if (ordinal === CODE_ORDINAL) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("TARGET FUNCTION BODY NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("INSPECT FUNC2057 STORE DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${GLOBAL_FUNC}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL  = ${CODE_ORDINAL}`);

  `BODY          = 0x${bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${bodyEnd
    .toString(16)
    .padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log("");

const instructions: {
  pos: number;
  text: string;
}[] = [];

let q = bodyStart;

while (q < bodyEnd) {
  const pos = q;
  const d = decodeInstruction(q);
  instructions.push({ pos, text: d.text });

  if (d.next <= q) break;
  q = d.next;

    `0x${ins.pos.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins.text}`
  );
