import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
const TARGET = 2075;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

let p = 8;
let funcSec = null;
let codeSec = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 3)
    funcSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  p += size;
}

const ordinal = TARGET - IMPORTED;

let fp = funcSec.start;
const fc = uleb(fp);
fp = fc.next;

let typeIndex = null;

for (let i = 0; i <= ordinal; i++) {
  const x = uleb(fp);
  fp = x.next;

  if (i === ordinal)
    typeIndex = x.value;
}

let cp = codeSec.start;
const cc = uleb(cp);
cp = cc.next;

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
  const z = uleb(cp);
  const size = z.value;
  cp = z.next;

  const start = cp;
  const end = cp + size;

  if (i === ordinal) {
    body = { start, end };
    break;
  }

  cp = end;
}

function decode(p) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  const R = (next, s) => ({
    next,
    text:
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${s}`
  });

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00: return R(p, "UNREACHABLE");
    case 0x01: return R(p, "NOP");
    case 0x05: return R(p, "ELSE");
    case 0x0b: return R(p, "END");
    case 0x0f: return R(p, "RETURN");
    case 0x1a: return R(p, "DROP");
    case 0x1b: return R(p, "SELECT");

    case 0x02:
      return R(p + 1, `BLOCK type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x03:
      return R(p + 1, `LOOP type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x04:
      return R(p + 1, `IF type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `BR ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `BR_IF ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      const tb = uleb(p);
      p = tb.next;
      return R(
        p,
        `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      return R(
        p,
        `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      return R(
        p,
        `I64.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      return R(
        p,
        `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      return R(
        x.next,
        `I32.CONST ${x.value} (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
      );
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `I64.CONST ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x45: return R(p, "I32.EQZ");
    case 0x46: return R(p, "I32.EQ");
    case 0x47: return R(p, "I32.NE");
    case 0x48: return R(p, "I32.LT_S");
    case 0x49: return R(p, "I32.LT_U");
    case 0x4a: return R(p, "I32.GT_S");
    case 0x4b: return R(p, "I32.GT_U");
    case 0x4c: return R(p, "I32.LE_S");
    case 0x4d: return R(p, "I32.GE_U");

    case 0x6a: return R(p, "I32.ADD");
    case 0x6b: return R(p, "I32.SUB");
    case 0x71: return R(p, "I32.AND");
    case 0x72: return R(p, "I32.OR");
    case 0x73: return R(p, "I32.XOR");
    case 0x74: return R(p, "I32.SHL");
    case 0x75: return R(p, "I32.SHR_S");
    case 0x76: return R(p, "I32.SHR_U");

    default:
      return R(
        p,
        `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 2075 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX     : ${typeIndex}`);
console.log(
  `BODY           : 0x${body.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`
);
console.log(`SIZE           : ${body.end - body.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let q = body.start;

while (q < body.end) {
  const x = decode(q);
  console.log(x.text);
  q = x.next;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("CALLS / INDIRECT CALLS / CONSTANTS");

q = body.start;

while (q < body.end) {
  const addr = q;
  const op = b[q++];

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = uleb(q);
    q = t.next;
    const tb = uleb(q);
    q = tb.next;
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = sleb(q);
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)} I32.CONST ${x.value} ` +
      `(0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
    );
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24 ||
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {
    q = uleb(q).next;
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x36
  ) {
    q = uleb(q).next;
    q = uleb(q).next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04)
    q++;
}

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const dir = path.dirname(decodeURIComponent(new URL(import.meta.url).pathname).replace(/^\/([A-Za-z]:)/, '$1'));
const scannerPath = path.join(dir, 'disassembleAddressUsers.mjs');
const tempDecoderPath = path.join(
    dir,
    '__traceFunc215_decoder_temp.mjs'
);

const scannerSource = fs.readFileSync(scannerPath, 'utf8');
const marker =
    "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";

const cut = scannerSource.indexOf(marker);

if (cut < 0) {
    throw new Error('Could not find decoder bootstrap marker');
}

const decoderBootstrap = scannerSource.slice(0, cut);

let decoder;

try {
    fs.writeFileSync(
        tempDecoderPath,
        decoderBootstrap +
            '\nexport { wasm, bodies, decodeFunction, hex };\n',
        'utf8'
    );

    decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempDecoderPath).href +
        '?inspectFunc215'
    );
} finally {
    fs.rmSync(tempDecoderPath, { force: true });
}

const { bodies, decodeFunction, hex } = decoder;
const target = 215;

if (!bodies.has(target)) {
    throw new Error(`Function ${target} has no defined body`);
}

for (const ins of instructions) {
    const text = String(ins.text);
    const callMatch = text.match(/\bcall\s+(\d+)\b/);
    const callTarget = callMatch ? Number(callMatch[1]) : null;

    const addressHit =
        /0x0*(?:195348|250f30|250f5c)\b/i.test(text) ||
        /\b(?:1659720|2428720|2428764)\b/.test(text);

    const importantCall =
        callTarget !== null && interestingCalls.has(callTarget);

    const prefix =
        addressHit ? '>>> ' :
        importantCall ? '*** ' :
        '    ';

    console.log(`${prefix}${hex(ins.start)}  ${ins.raw}  ${text}`);
}

console.log('\n====================================================');
console.log('TARGETED CALL SUMMARY');
console.log('====================================================');

for (const ins of instructions) {
    const text = String(ins.text);
    const callMatch = text.match(/\bcall\s+(\d+)\b/);

    if (callMatch && interestingCalls.has(Number(callMatch[1]))) {
        console.log(`${hex(ins.start)}  ${text}`);
    }
}

console.log('\nInspection complete. No WASM execution performed.');

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 23;
const IMPORTED = 83;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 23 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${IMPORTED}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function u32leb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function s32leb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function sectionHeader(pos: number) {
    const id = buf[pos];
    const size = u32leb(pos + 1);

    return {
        id,
        size: size.value,
        payloadStart: size.next,
        payloadEnd: size.next + size.value,
    };
}

let functionStart = -1;
let functionEnd = -1;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const s = sectionHeader(p);

    if (s.id === 3) {
        functionStart = s.payloadStart;
        functionEnd = s.payloadEnd;
    }

    if (s.id === 10) {
        codeStart = s.payloadStart;
        codeEnd = s.payloadEnd;
    }

    p = s.payloadEnd;
}

if (functionStart < 0 || codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("Required sections not found");
}

console.log(`FUNCTION SECTION = ${hex(functionStart)} .. ${hex(functionEnd)}`);
console.log(`CODE SECTION     = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);

let fp = functionStart;

const functionCount = u32leb(fp);
fp = functionCount.next;

const definedCount = functionCount.value;
const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
    const r = u32leb(fp);
    fp = r.next;
    typeIndices.push(r.value);
}

const ordinal = TARGET_FUNC - IMPORTED;

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${definedCount}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL      = ${ordinal}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX        = ${typeIndices[ordinal]}`);

let cp = codeStart;

const bodyCountInfo = u32leb(cp);
cp = bodyCountInfo.next;

const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;
let bodySize = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = u32leb(cp);

    const start = sizeInfo.next;
    const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

    if (i === ordinal) {
        bodyStart = start;
        bodyEnd = end;
        bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
        break;
    }

    cp = end;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("FUNC23 body not found");
}

console.log(`BODY START        = ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
console.log(`BODY END          = ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE         = ${bodySize}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let q = bodyStart;

const groups = u32leb(q);
q = groups.next;

console.log(`LOCAL DECL GROUPS = ${groups.value}`);

for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
    const count = u32leb(q);
    q = count.next;

    const type = buf[q++];

    console.log(
        `  GROUP ${i}: COUNT=${count.value} TYPE=0x${type
            .toString(16)
            .padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log(`INSTRUCTION START = ${hex(q)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let pos = q;

while (pos < bodyEnd) {
    const at = pos;
    const op = buf[pos++];

    function line(bytes: string, text: string) {
        console.log(
            `${hex(at)}  ${bytes.padEnd(28)} ${text}`
        );
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        line(`20 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `LOCAL.GET ${r.value}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        line(`21 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `LOCAL.SET ${r.value}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        line(`22 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `LOCAL.TEE ${r.value}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        line(`23 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `GLOBAL.GET ${r.value}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        line(`24 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `GLOBAL.SET ${r.value}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = s32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;

        line(
            `41 ${(r.value >>> 0).toString(16)}`,
            `I32.CONST ${r.value < 0 ? r.value : hex(r.value)}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
        const a = u32leb(pos);
        pos = a.next;

        const o = u32leb(pos);
        pos = o.next;

        line(
            `28 ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
            `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x2c) {
        const a = u32leb(pos);
        pos = a.next;

        const o = u32leb(pos);
        pos = o.next;

        line(
            `2c ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
            `I32.LOAD8_S align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x2d) {
        const a = u32leb(pos);
        pos = a.next;

        const o = u32leb(pos);
        pos = o.next;

        line(
            `2d ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
            `I32.LOAD8_U align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36) {
        const a = u32leb(pos);
        pos = a.next;

        const o = u32leb(pos);
        pos = o.next;

        line(
            `36 ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
            `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x3a) {
        const a = u32leb(pos);
        pos = a.next;

        const o = u32leb(pos);
        pos = o.next;

        line(
            `3a ${a.value.toString(16)} ${o.value.toString(16)}`,
            `I32.STORE8 align=${a.value} offset=${hex(o.value)}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x45) {
        line("45", "I32.EQZ");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x46) {
        line("46", "I32.EQ");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x47) {
        line("47", "I32.NE");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x48) {
        line("48", "I32.LT_S");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x49) {
        line("49", "I32.LT_U");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x4f) {
        line("4f", "I32.GE_U");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
        line("6a", "I32.ADD");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
        line("6b", "I32.SUB");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x71) {
        line("71", "I32.AND");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x72) {
        line("72", "I32.OR");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x73) {
        line("73", "I32.XOR");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x74) {
        line("74", "I32.SHL");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x75) {
        line("75", "I32.SHR_S");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x76) {
        line("76", "I32.SHR_U");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x04) {
        const t = buf[pos++];
        line(`04 ${t.toString(16)}`, `IF type=0x${t.toString(16)}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x05) {
        line("05", "ELSE");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {
        line("0b", "END");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0c) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        line(`0c ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR ${r.value}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        line(`0d ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR_IF ${r.value}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x0f) {
        line("0f", "RETURN");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x1a) {
        line("1a", "DROP");
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;
        line(`10 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `CALL FUNC${r.value}`);
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const t = u32leb(pos);
        pos = t.next;

        const table = u32leb(pos);
        pos = table.next;

        line(
            `11 ${t.value.toString(16)} ${table.value.toString(16)}`,
            `CALL_INDIRECT TYPE${t.value} TABLE${table.value}`
        );
        continue;
    }

    if (op === 0xfc) {
        const r = u32leb(pos);
        pos = r.next;

        let name = `EXTENDED 0xfc ${r.value}`;

        if (r.value === 0x0a) {
            name = "MEMORY.COPY";
        }

        if (r.value === 0x0b) {
            name = "MEMORY.FILL";
        }

        line(
            `fc ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
            name
        );
        continue;
    }

    line(
        op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
        `UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log("TARGET SCRATCH  = 0x255a6c");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET = 2431;
const IMPORTED = 83;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

function i32uleb(pos) {
  return uleb(pos);
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];
    const sz = uleb(p);
    p = sz.next;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start: p, end: p + sz.value };
    }

    p += sz.value;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE section not found");
}

function skipBlockType(p) {
  const x = b[p];
  if (x === 0x40 || x === 0x7f || x === 0x7e ||
      x === 0x7d || x === 0x7c || x === 0x7b) {
    return p + 1;
  }
  return uleb(p).next;
}

function scanBody(bodyStart, bodyEnd) {
  let p = bodyStart;
  const hits = [];

  while (p < bodyEnd) {
    const opPos = p;
    const op = b[p++];

    switch (op) {
      case 0x00: // unreachable
      case 0x01: // nop
      case 0x05: // else
      case 0x0b: // end
      case 0x0f: // return
      case 0x1a: // drop
      case 0x1b: // select
      case 0x45: // i32.eqz
      case 0x46: // i32.eq
      case 0x47: // i32.ne

      case 0x49: // i32.lt_u
      case 0x4a: // i32.gt_s
      case 0x4b: // i32.gt_u

      case 0x4e: // i32.ge_s
      case 0x4f: // i32.ge_u
      case 0x50: // i64.eqz
      case 0x51:
      case 0x52:
      case 0x53:
      case 0x54:
      case 0x55:
      case 0x56:
      case 0x57:
      case 0x58:
      case 0x59:
      case 0x5a:
      case 0x5b:

      case 0x7e:

      case 0xd6:
      case 0xd7:
      case 0xd8:
      case 0xd9:
      case 0xda:
      case 0xdb:
      case 0xdc:
      case 0xdd:
      case 0xde:
      case 0xdf:
      case 0xe0:
      case 0xe1:
      case 0xe2:
      case 0xe3:
      case 0xe4:
      case 0xe5:
      case 0xe6:
      case 0xe7:
      case 0xe8:
      case 0xe9:
      case 0xea:
      case 0xeb:
      case 0xec:
      case 0xed:
      case 0xee:
      case 0xef:
      case 0xf0:
      case 0xf1:
      case 0xf2:
      case 0xf3:
      case 0xf4:
      case 0xf5:
      case 0xf6:
      case 0xf7:
      case 0xf8:
      case 0xf9:
      case 0xfa:
      case 0xfb:
      case 0xfc:
      case 0xfd:
      case 0xfe:
      case 0xff:
        break;

      case 0x02: // block
      case 0x03: // loop
      case 0x04: // if
        p = skipBlockType(p);
        break;

      case 0x0c: { // br
        p = uleb(p).next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: { // br_if
        p = uleb(p).next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x10: { // call
        const x = uleb(p);
        const f = x.value;
        p = x.next;
        hits.push({
          addr: opPos,
          type: "CALL",
          value: f
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x11: { // call_indirect
        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;
        const table = uleb(p);
        p = table.next;
        hits.push({
          addr: opPos,
          type: "CALL_INDIRECT",
          typeidx: t.value,
          tableidx: table.value
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x20: { // local.get
        p = uleb(p).next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: { // local.set
        p = uleb(p).next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: { // local.tee
        p = uleb(p).next;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: { // global.get
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        hits.push({
          addr: opPos,
          type: "GLOBAL.GET",
          value: x.value
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: { // global.set
        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        hits.push({
          addr: opPos,
          type: "GLOBAL.SET",
          value: x.value
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: // i32.load
      case 0x29: // i64.load
      case 0x2a:
      case 0x2b:
      case 0x2c:
      case 0x2d:
      case 0x2e:
      case 0x2f:
      case 0x30:
      case 0x31:
      case 0x32:
      case 0x33:
      case 0x34:
      case 0x35:
      case 0x36: // store
      case 0x37:
      case 0x38:
      case 0x39:
      case 0x3a:
      case 0x3b:
      case 0x3c:
      case 0x3d:
      case 0x3e:
      case 0x3f:
      case 0x40: {
        p = uleb(p).next; // alignment
        p = uleb(p).next; // offset
        break;
      }

      case 0x41: { // i32.const
        const x = i32uleb(p);
        p = x.next;
        hits.push({
          addr: opPos,
          type: "I32.CONST",
          value: x.value
        });
        break;
      }

      case 0x42: { // i64.const
        p = uleb(p).next;
        break;
      }

      default:
        break;
    }
  }

  return hits;
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;
const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

let target = null;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const bodySize = uleb(p);
  p = bodySize.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize.value;

  const funcIndex = IMPORTED + i;

  if (funcIndex === TARGET) {
    target = {
      funcIndex,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      size: bodySize.value
    };
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (!target) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET} not found`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 2431 GLOBAL / RETURN FLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${target.funcIndex}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL   : ${target.funcIndex - IMPORTED}`);
console.log(`BODY           : 0x${target.bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${target.bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE      : ${target.size}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const hits = scanBody(target.bodyStart, target.bodyEnd);

for (const h of hits) {
  const a = "0x" + h.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");

  if (h.type === "CALL") {
    console.log(`${a}  CALL FUNC ${h.value}`);
  } else if (h.type === "CALL_INDIRECT") {
    console.log(
      `${a}  CALL_INDIRECT type=${h.typeidx} table=${h.tableidx}`
    );
  } else if (h.type === "GLOBAL.GET") {
    console.log(`${a}  GLOBAL.GET ${h.value}`);
  } else if (h.type === "GLOBAL.SET") {
    console.log(`${a}  GLOBAL.SET ${h.value}`);
  } else if (h.type === "I32.CONST") {
    console.log(
      `${a}  I32.CONST ${h.value} (0x${h.value.toString(16)})`
    );
  }
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("GLOBAL 0 SUMMARY");

const g0get = hits.filter(x =>
  x.type === "GLOBAL.GET" && x.value === 0
);

const g0set = hits.filter(x =>
  x.type === "GLOBAL.SET" && x.value === 0
);

console.log(`GLOBAL.GET 0 : ${g0get.length}`);
console.log(`GLOBAL.SET 0 : ${g0set.length}`);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("DIRECT CALLS");

for (const h of hits.filter(x => x.type === "CALL")) {
  console.log(
    `0x${h.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  FUNC ${TARGET} -> FUNC ${h.value}`
  );
}

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    do {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF');
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    } while (byte & 0x80);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function dump(start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const stop = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [...wasm.subarray(p, stop)]
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ');
        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes}`);
    }
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error('Code section not found');

const count = uleb(codeStart);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = uleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    bodies.set(83 + ordinal, { start, end, size: size.value });
    pos = end;
}

for (const func of [2524, 2525, 2526, 2527, 2528]) {
    const body = bodies.get(func);

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);

    if (!body) {
        console.log('BODY NOT FOUND');
        continue;
    }

    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE = ${body.size}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    dump(body.start, body.end);
}

console.log('\nDONE - READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION');

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET = 259;

function uleb(pos) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: v, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v, n = 8) {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeCount = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const sectionId = wasm[p++];

    const si = uleb(p);
    p = si.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + si.value;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        const ci = uleb(p);
        codeCount = ci.value;
        p = ci.next;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("FUNC 259 RAW / CALL FLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log();

console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`CODE COUNT     : ${codeCount}`);
console.log();

let bodyStart = null;
let bodyEnd = null;

for (let i = 0; i < codeCount; i++) {

    const si = uleb(p);
    const size = si.value;
    p = si.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + size;

    if (i + 83 === TARGET) {
        bodyStart = start;
        bodyEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (bodyStart === null) {
    console.log("FUNC 259 NOT FOUND");
    process.exit(1);
}

console.log(`BODY : ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`SIZE : ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
console.log();

console.log("RAW BYTES");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

for (let a = bodyStart; a < bodyEnd; a += 16) {

    const e = Math.min(a + 16, bodyEnd);

    const bytes = [];

    for (let x = a; x < e; x++) {
        bytes.push(
            wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );
    }

    console.log(
        `${hex(a)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("CALL REFERENCES");
console.log("-".repeat(72));

p = bodyStart;

// Skip local declaration vector.
const localCountInfo = uleb(p);
p = localCountInfo.next;

for (let i = 0; i < localCountInfo.value; i++) {
    const n = uleb(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++; // value type
}

while (p < bodyEnd) {

    const at = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];

    if (op === 0x10) {

        const x = uleb(p);
        p = x.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)} CALL FUNC ${x.value}`
        );

    } else if (op === 0x11) {

        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;

        const table = uleb(p);
        p = table.next;

        console.log(
            `${hex(at)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`
        );

    } else if (op === 0x0f) {

        console.log(`${hex(at)} RETURN`);

    } else if (op === 0x0b) {

        console.log(`${hex(at)} END`);
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("=".repeat(72));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(72));

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc265Body.ts

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function readWasmSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections: any[] = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = readULEB(p);
    const size = r.value;
    p = r.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + size,
      size
    });

    p += size;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = readWasmSections();

const funcSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
const codeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!funcSection || !codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Function/code section not found");
}

let p = funcSection.start;

const fr = readULEB(p);
const funcCount = fr.value;
p = fr.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < funcCount; i++) {
  const r = readULEB(p);

  typeIndices.push(r.value);

  p = r.next;
}

const targetGlobalFunc = 265;
const importedFunctions = 83;

if (targetGlobalFunc < importedFunctions) {
  throw new Error("FUNC265 is imported, unexpected");
}

const codeOrdinal = targetGlobalFunc - importedFunctions;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 265 BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC     = ${targetGlobalFunc}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCS  = ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL    = ${codeOrdinal}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX      = ${typeIndices[codeOrdinal]}`);
console.log("");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSection.start;

const cr = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = cr.value;
p = cr.next;

let targetStart = -1;
let targetEnd = -1;
let targetSize = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sr = readULEB(p);
  const bodySize = sr.value;

  p = sr.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

  if (i === codeOrdinal) {
    targetStart = bodyStart;
    targetEnd = bodyEnd;
    targetSize = bodySize;
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (targetStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("FUNC265 body not found");
}

console.log(
  `BODY = 0x${targetStart
    .toString(16)
    .padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${(targetEnd - 1)
    .toString(16)
    .padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log(`SIZE = ${targetSize}`);
console.log("");

console.log("RAW BODY");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let q = targetStart; q < targetEnd; q++) {
  console.log(
    `0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${b[q]
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc265Structure.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 265;
const IMPORTED = 83;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 265 EXACT STRUCTURE ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${IMPORTED}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function u32leb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function s32leb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function sectionHeader(pos: number) {
  const id = buf[pos];
  const size = u32leb(pos + 1);

  return {
    id,
    size: size.value,
    payloadStart: size.next,
    payloadEnd: size.next + size.value,
  };
}

let functionStart = -1;
let functionEnd = -1;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const s = sectionHeader(p);

  if (s.id === 3) {
    functionStart = s.payloadStart;
    functionEnd = s.payloadEnd;
  }

  if (s.id === 10) {
    codeStart = s.payloadStart;
    codeEnd = s.payloadEnd;
  }

  p = s.payloadEnd;
}

if (functionStart < 0 || codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

console.log(`FUNCTION SECTION = ${hex(functionStart)} .. ${hex(functionEnd)}`);
console.log(`CODE SECTION     = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);

//
// Parse function section.
//

let fp = functionStart;

const functionCountInfo = u32leb(fp);
fp = functionCountInfo.next;

const definedCount = functionCountInfo.value;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < definedCount; i++) {
  const r = u32leb(fp);
  fp = r.next;
  typeIndices.push(r.value);
}

const codeOrdinal = TARGET_FUNC - IMPORTED;

if (codeOrdinal < 0 || codeOrdinal >= definedCount) {
  throw new Error(`Invalid code ordinal: ${codeOrdinal}`);
}

//
// Parse code section.
//

let cp = codeStart;

const bodyCountInfo = u32leb(cp);
cp = bodyCountInfo.next;

const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;

let bodyStart = -1;
let bodyEnd = -1;
let bodySize = -1;

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sizeInfo = u32leb(cp);

  const start = sizeInfo.next;
  const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

  if (i === codeOrdinal) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    break;
  }

  cp = end;
}

if (bodyStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("FUNC265 body not found");
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${definedCount}`);
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL      = ${codeOrdinal}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX        = ${typeIndices[codeOrdinal]}`);
console.log(`BODY START        = ${hex(bodyStart)}`);
console.log(`BODY END          = ${hex(bodyEnd)}`);
console.log(`BODY SIZE         = ${bodySize}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

//
// Local declarations.
//

let q = bodyStart;

const localGroupsInfo = u32leb(q);
q = localGroupsInfo.next;

console.log(`LOCAL DECL GROUPS = ${localGroupsInfo.value}`);

let extraLocals = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < localGroupsInfo.value; i++) {
  const countInfo = u32leb(q);
  q = countInfo.next;

  const type = buf[q++];

  extraLocals += countInfo.value;

  console.log(
    `  GROUP ${i}: COUNT=${countInfo.value} TYPE=0x${type
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log(`TOTAL EXTRA LOCALS = ${extraLocals}`);
console.log(`INSTRUCTION START  = ${hex(q)}`);

//
// FUNC265 has TYPE2 from previous analysis:
// (i32) -> i32
//
// Therefore local 0 is the input structure pointer.
//

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("EXPECTED SIGNATURE: TYPE2 = (i32) -> i32");
console.log("LOCAL 0 = STRUCTURE POINTER / INPUT");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const knownFields = [
  0x04,
  0x0c,
  0x14,
  0x18,
  0x1c,
  0x24,
  0x2c,
  0x30,
  0x34,
  0x38,
];

function fieldName(offset: number) {
  if (knownFields.includes(offset)) {
    return ` <<< FIELD +0x${offset.toString(16)}`;
  }

  return "";
}

function emit(pos: number, bytes: string, text: string) {
  console.log(
    `${hex(pos)}  ${bytes.padEnd(22)} ${text}`
  );
}

let instr = q;

while (instr < bodyEnd) {
  const pos = instr;
  const op = buf[instr++];

  //
  // local.get
  //
  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `20 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `LOCAL.GET ${r.value}${r.value === 0 ? " <<< STRUCT PTR" : ""}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // local.set
  //
  if (op === 0x21) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `21 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `LOCAL.SET ${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // local.tee
  //
  if (op === 0x22) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `22 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `LOCAL.TEE ${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // global.get
  //
  if (op === 0x23) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `23 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `GLOBAL.GET ${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // global.set
  //
  if (op === 0x24) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `24 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `GLOBAL.SET ${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.const
  //
  if (op === 0x41) {
    const r = s32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `41 ${(r.value >>> 0).toString(16)}`,
      `I32.CONST ${r.value < 0 ? r.value : hex(r.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.load
  //
  if (op === 0x28) {
    const align = u32leb(instr);
    instr = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(instr);
    instr = offset.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `28 ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}${fieldName(
        offset.value
      )}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.load8_s
  //
  if (op === 0x2c) {
    const align = u32leb(instr);
    instr = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(instr);
    instr = offset.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `2c ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD8_S align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.load8_u
  //
  if (op === 0x2d) {
    const align = u32leb(instr);
    instr = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(instr);
    instr = offset.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `2d ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD8_U align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.load16_s
  //
  if (op === 0x2e) {
    const align = u32leb(instr);
    instr = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(instr);
    instr = offset.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `2e ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD16_S align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.load16_u
  //
  if (op === 0x2f) {
    const align = u32leb(instr);
    instr = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(instr);
    instr = offset.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `2f ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.LOAD16_U align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.store
  //
  if (op === 0x36) {
    const align = u32leb(instr);
    instr = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(instr);
    instr = offset.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `36 ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.STORE align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}${fieldName(
        offset.value
      )}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.store8
  //
  if (op === 0x3a) {
    const align = u32leb(instr);
    instr = align.next;

    const offset = u32leb(instr);
    instr = offset.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `3a ${align.value.toString(16)} ${offset.value.toString(16)}`,
      `I32.STORE8 align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.add
  //
  if (op === 0x6a) {
    emit(pos, "6a", "I32.ADD");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.sub
  //
  if (op === 0x6b) {
    emit(pos, "6b", "I32.SUB");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.and
  //
  if (op === 0x71) {
    emit(pos, "71", "I32.AND");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.or
  //
  if (op === 0x72) {
    emit(pos, "72", "I32.OR");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.xor
  //
  if (op === 0x73) {
    emit(pos, "73", "I32.XOR");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.shl
  //
  if (op === 0x74) {
    emit(pos, "74", "I32.SHL");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.shr_s
  //
  if (op === 0x75) {
    emit(pos, "75", "I32.SHR_S");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.shr_u
  //
  if (op === 0x76) {
    emit(pos, "76", "I32.SHR_U");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.rotl
  //
  if (op === 0x77) {
    emit(pos, "77", "I32.ROTL");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // i32.rotr
  //
  if (op === 0x78) {
    emit(pos, "78", "I32.ROTR");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // comparisons
  //
  if (op === 0x45) {
    emit(pos, "45", "I32.EQZ");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x46) {
    emit(pos, "46", "I32.EQ");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x47) {
    emit(pos, "47", "I32.NE");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x48) {
    emit(pos, "48", "I32.LT_S");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x49) {
    emit(pos, "49", "I32.LT_U");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x4a) {
    emit(pos, "4a", "I32.GT_S");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x4b) {
    emit(pos, "4b", "I32.GT_U");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x4c) {
    emit(pos, "4c", "I32.LE_S");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x4d) {
    emit(pos, "4d", "I32.LE_U");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x4e) {
    emit(pos, "4e", "I32.GE_S");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x4f) {
    emit(pos, "4f", "I32.GE_U");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // control
  //
  if (op === 0x00) {
    emit(pos, "00", "UNREACHABLE");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x01) {
    emit(pos, "01", "NOP");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02) {
    const type = buf[instr++];
    emit(
      pos,
      `02 ${type.toString(16)}`,
      `BLOCK type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x03) {
    const type = buf[instr++];
    emit(
      pos,
      `03 ${type.toString(16)}`,
      `LOOP type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x04) {
    const type = buf[instr++];
    emit(
      pos,
      `04 ${type.toString(16)}`,
      `IF type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    emit(pos, "05", "ELSE");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    emit(pos, "0b", "END");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `0c ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `BR ${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `0d ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `BR_IF ${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0f) {
    emit(pos, "0f", "RETURN");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // stack
  //
  if (op === 0x1a) {
    emit(pos, "1a", "DROP");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x1b) {
    emit(pos, "1b", "SELECT");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // call
  //
  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `10 ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      `CALL FUNC${r.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // call_indirect
  //
  if (op === 0x11) {
    const type = u32leb(instr);
    instr = type.next;

    const table = u32leb(instr);
    instr = table.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `11 ${type.value.toString(16)} ${table.value.toString(16)}`,
      `CALL_INDIRECT TYPE${type.value} TABLE${table.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  //
  // memory.size
  //
  if (op === 0x3f) {
    const mem = buf[instr++];
    emit(pos, `3f ${mem.toString(16)}`, "MEMORY.SIZE");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // memory.grow
  //
  if (op === 0x40) {
    const mem = buf[instr++];
    emit(pos, `40 ${mem.toString(16)}`, "MEMORY.GROW");
    continue;
  }

  //
  // Extended instructions.
  //
  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    let name = `EXTENDED 0xfc ${r.value.toString(16)}`;

    if (r.value === 0x0a) {
      name = "MEMORY.COPY";
    } else if (r.value === 0x0b) {
      name = "MEMORY.FILL";
    }

    emit(
      pos,
      `fc ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      name
    );
    continue;
  }

  emit(
    pos,
    op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
    `UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC265 FIELD TARGETS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const off of knownFields) {
  console.log(
    `+0x${off.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}  READ/WRITE TRACE TARGET`
  );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("NO TRANSACTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc265Type.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  const sections: any[] = [];

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    sections.push({
      id,
      start: p,
      end: p + r.value
    });

    p += r.value;
  }

  return sections;
}

const sections = readSections();

const typeSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);
const funcSection = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);

if (!typeSection || !funcSection) {
  throw new Error("Required sections not found");
}

const typeCount = tr.value;
p = tr.next;

const types: any[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount; i++) {
  const form = b[p++];

  const pc = readULEB(p);
  const paramCount = pc.value;
  p = pc.next;

  const params: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < paramCount; j++) {
    params.push(b[p++]);
  }

  const rc = readULEB(p);
  const resultCount = rc.value;
  p = rc.next;

  const results: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < resultCount; j++) {
    results.push(b[p++]);
  }

  types.push({
    form,
    params,
    results
  });
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = funcSection.start;

const fr = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = fr.value;
p = fr.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {
  const r = readULEB(p);
  typeIndices.push(r.value);
  p = r.next;
}

const target = 265;
const imported = 83;
const ordinal = target - imported;
const typeIndex = typeIndices[ordinal];

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC265 TYPE");

console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${typeIndex}`);

  JSON.stringify(types[typeIndex], null, 2)
);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc270.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 270;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8): string {
  return (
    "0x" +
    (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0")
  );
}

function readMemArg(pos: number) {
  const a = readULEB(pos);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SECTIONS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const sections: {
  id: number;
  start: number;
  end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  sections.push({
    id,
    start: p,
    end: p + size.value,
  });

  p += size.value;
}

const importSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 2);
const funcSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 3);
const codeSec = sections.find(s => s.id === 10);

if (!funcSec || !codeSec) {
  throw new Error("Missing FUNCTION/CODE section");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// IMPORT COUNT
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let importedCount = 0;

if (importSec) {
  p = importSec.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const ml = readULEB(p);
    p = ml.next + ml.value;

    const nl = readULEB(p);
    p = nl.next + nl.value;

    const kind = wasm[p++];

    if (kind === 0x00) {
      p = readULEB(p).next;
      importedCount++;
    } else if (kind === 0x01) {
      p++;

      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x02) {
      const flags = wasm[p++];

      p = readULEB(p).next;

      if (flags & 1) {
        p = readULEB(p).next;
      }
    } else if (kind === 0x03) {
      p += 2;
    } else if (kind === 0x04) {
      p++;
      p = readULEB(p).next;
    }
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FUNCTION SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const functionTypes: number[] = [];

p = funcSec.start;

{
  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const r = readULEB(p);
    p = r.next;

    functionTypes.push(r.value);
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// FIND TARGET
// ------------------------------------------------------------

p = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

let found = false;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + size.value;

  const localDecls = readULEB(p);
  p = localDecls.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
    const n = readULEB(p);
    p = n.next;
    p++;
  }

  const codeStart = p;
  const globalFunc = importedCount + ordinal;

  if (globalFunc !== TARGET_FUNC) {
    p = bodyEnd;
    continue;
  }

  found = true;

  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} RAW BODY`);
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${globalFunc}`);
  console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${ordinal}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX = ${functionTypes[ordinal]}`);
  console.log(
    `BODY = ${hex(bodyStart)} .. ${hex(bodyEnd)}`
  );
  console.log(`SIZE = ${bodyEnd - bodyStart}`);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let q = codeStart;

  while (q < bodyEnd) {
    const addr = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    let text =
      `opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

    switch (op) {
      case 0x02: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `block type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x03: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `loop type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x04: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `if type=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x05:
        text = "else";
        break;

      case 0x0b:
        text = "end";
        break;

      case 0x0c: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `br ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0d: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `br_if ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x0f:
        text = "return";
        break;

      case 0x10: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `call func=${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x1a:
        text = "drop";
        break;

      case 0x20: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x21: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x22: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x23: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `global.get ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x24: {
        const r = readULEB(q);
        q = r.next;
        text = `global.set ${r.value}`;
        break;
      }

      case 0x28: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        text =
          `i32.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x29: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        text =
          `i64.load align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x2d: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        text =
          `i32.load8_u align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x36: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        text =
          `i32.store align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x3a: {
        const m = readMemArg(q);
        q = m.next;

        text =
          `i32.store8 align=${m.align} offset=${hex(m.offset)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x41: {
        const r = readSLEB(q);
        q = r.next;

        text =
          `i32.const ${hex(r.value)}`;

        break;
      }

      case 0x45:
        text = "i32.eqz";
        break;

      case 0x46:
        text = "i32.eq";
        break;

      case 0x47:
        text = "i32.ne";
        break;

      case 0x48:
        text = "i32.lt_s";
        break;

      case 0x49:
        text = "i32.lt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4a:
        text = "i32.gt_s";
        break;

      case 0x4b:
        text = "i32.gt_u";
        break;

      case 0x4c:
        text = "i32.le_s";
        break;

      case 0x4d:
        text = "i32.le_u";
        break;

      case 0x4e:
        text = "i32.ge_s";
        break;

      case 0x4f:
        text = "i32.ge_u";
        break;

      case 0x6a:
        text = "i32.add";
        break;

      case 0x6b:
        text = "i32.sub";
        break;

      case 0x6c:
        text = "i32.mul";
        break;

      case 0x74:
        text = "i32.shl";
        break;

      case 0x75:
        text = "i32.shr_s";
        break;

      case 0x76:
        text = "i32.shr_u";
        break;
    }

    console.log(`${hex(addr)}  ${text}`);
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
  console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
  console.log("============================================================");

  break;
}

if (!found) {
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET_FUNC} not found`);
}

import * as nodefs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = nodefs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0;
  let s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNCTION 2827 SIGNATURE v35");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

//
// WASM HEADER
//
console.log(
  `Magic : ${b.subarray(0, 4).toString("hex")}`
);

//
// FIND TYPE + FUNCTION SECTIONS
//
let p = 8;

let typeStart = -1;
let typeEnd = -1;

let functionStart = -1;
let functionEnd = -1;

while (p < b.length) {

  const sectionId = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const sectionSize = s.v;
  p = s.p;

  const payloadStart = p;
  const payloadEnd = p + sectionSize;

  console.log(
    `section id=${sectionId} ` +
    `start=${hex(payloadStart)} ` +
    `end=${hex(payloadEnd)} ` +
    `size=${sectionSize}`
  );

  if (sectionId === 1) {
    typeStart = payloadStart;
    typeEnd = payloadEnd;
  }

  if (sectionId === 3) {
    functionStart = payloadStart;
    functionEnd = payloadEnd;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

console.log();

if (typeStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Type section not found");

if (functionStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Function section not found");

//
// PARSE TYPE SECTION
//
p = typeStart;

const typeCount = uleb(p);
p = typeCount.p;

const types: {
  params: number[];
  results: number[];
}[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < typeCount.v; i++) {

  const form = b[p++];

  if (form !== 0x60) {
    throw new Error(
      `Bad function type at ${hex(p - 1)}`
    );
  }

  const np = uleb(p);
  p = np.p;

  const params: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < np.v; j++)
    params.push(b[p++]);

  const nr = uleb(p);
  p = nr.p;

  const results: number[] = [];

  for (let j = 0; j < nr.v; j++)
    results.push(b[p++]);

  types.push({
    params,
    results
  });
}

//
// PARSE FUNCTION SECTION
//
p = functionStart;

const functionCount = uleb(p);
p = functionCount.p;

let typeIndex2827 = -1;

for (let fn = 0; fn < functionCount.v; fn++) {

  const r = uleb(p);
  p = r.p;

  if (fn === 2827)
    typeIndex2827 = r.v;
}

console.log("============================================================");

  throw new Error("Function 2827 not found");

console.log();

  console.log(
    `param[${i}] = 0x${t.params[i].toString(16)}`
  );

import fs from "fs";

const buf = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_ORDINAL = 30;
const IMPORTED = 83;
const TARGET_GLOBAL = TARGET_ORDINAL + IMPORTED;

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: pos
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(n: number): string {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const sections: {
    id: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {

    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    sections.push({
        id,
        start: p,
        end: p + s.value
    });

    p += s.value;
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

if (!codeSec) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let c = codeSec.start;

const count = readULEB(c);
c = count.next;

let target:
    | {
        start: number;
        end: number;
        instrStart: number;
    }
    | null = null;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {

    const bodySize = readULEB(c);
    c = bodySize.next;

    const start = c;
    const end = c + bodySize.value;

    let x = start;

    const localGroups = readULEB(x);
    x = localGroups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {

        const n = readULEB(x);
        x = n.next;

        // local value type
        x++;
    }

    if (ordinal === TARGET_ORDINAL) {

        target = {
            start,
            end,
            instrStart: x
        };

        break;
    }

    c = end;
}

if (!target) {
    throw new Error(
        `TARGET CODE ORDINAL NOT FOUND: ${TARGET_ORDINAL}`
    );
}

//
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Output
// ------------------------------------------------------------
//

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 30 RAW BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE ORDINAL = ${TARGET_ORDINAL}`);
console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC   = ${TARGET_GLOBAL}`);
console.log(`BODY          = ${hex(target.start)} .. ${hex(target.end)}`);
console.log(`INSTR         = ${hex(target.instrStart)}`);
console.log(`SIZE          = ${target.end - target.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (
    let x = target.start;
    x < target.end;
    x += 16
) {

    const end = Math.min(x + 16, target.end);

    let line = `${hex(x)}  `;

    for (let i = x; i < end; i++) {
        line += buf[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(line);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\inspectFunc306.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");
const TARGET = 306;

function uleb(pos:number) {
  let v = 0, shift = 0, p = pos;
  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    shift += 7;
  }
  return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
}

let p = 8;
let imported = 0;
let start = 0, end = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
  const id = wasm[p++];
  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.next;
  const secEnd = p + s.value;

  if (id === 2) {
    let q = p;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const ml = uleb(q); q = ml.next + ml.value;
      const nl = uleb(q); q = nl.next + nl.value;
      const kind = wasm[q++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        q = uleb(q).next;
        imported++;
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        q++;
        q = uleb(q).next;
        q = uleb(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        q = uleb(q).next;
        q = uleb(q).next;
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        q += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  if (id === 10) {
    let q = p;
    const n = uleb(q); q = n.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
      const bs = uleb(q); q = bs.next;
      const st = q;
      const en = q + bs.value;

      if (imported + i === TARGET) {
        start = st;
        end = en;
        break;
      }

      q = en;
    }
    break;
  }

  p = secEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 306 DISPATCH ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY : 0x${start.toString(16)} .. 0x${end.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

for (let q = start; q < end;) {
  const pos = q;
  const op = wasm[q++];
  let imm = "";

  if (
    op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x10 || op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d ||
    op === 0x41 || op === 0x42 ||
    op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04
  ) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    imm = ` ${x.value}`;
    q = x.next;
  }
  else if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(q); q = a.next;
    const o = uleb(q); q = o.next;
    imm = ` align=${a.value} offset=0x${o.value.toString(16)}`;
  }
  else if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = uleb(q); q = t.next;
    const tab = uleb(q); q = tab.next;
    imm = ` type=${t.value} table=${tab.value}`;
  }

  let mark = "";

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) mark = " <<< LOAD";
  if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) mark = " <<< STORE";
  if (op === 0x10) mark = " <<< CALL";
  if (op === 0x11) mark = " <<< CALL_INDIRECT";

  console.log(
    `0x${pos.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}  ` +
    `${op.toString(16).padStart(2,"0")}${imm}${mark}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET = 32;

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { value, next: pos };

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSections() {
  let p = 8;
  let code = null;
  let imported = 0;

  while (p < b.length) {
    const id = b[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 2) {
      let q = start;
      const c = uleb(q);
      q = c.next;

      imported = 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
        const ml = uleb(q);
        q = ml.next + ml.value;

        const nl = uleb(q);
        q = nl.next + nl.value;

        const kind = b[q++];

        if (kind === 0) {
          const t = uleb(q);
          q = t.next;
          imported++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
          q += 1;
          const flags = uleb(q);
          q = flags.next;

          if (flags.value & 1) {
            const x = uleb(q);
            q = x.next;
          }

          const y = uleb(q);
          q = y.next;
        } else if (kind === 2) {
          const flags = uleb(q);
          q = flags.next;

          const x = uleb(q);
          q = x.next;

          if (flags.value & 1) {
            const y = uleb(q);
            q = y.next;
          }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
          q += 1;
        }
      }
    }

    if (id === 10)
      code = { start, end };

    p = end;
  }

  return { code, imported };
}

const { code, imported } = readSections();

let p = code.start;

const count = uleb(p);
p = count.next;

let target = null;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {

  const bodySize = uleb(p);
  p = bodySize.next;

  const bodyStart = p;
  const bodyEnd = p + bodySize.value;

  const funcIndex = imported + ordinal;

  if (funcIndex === TARGET) {
    target = {
      funcIndex,
      ordinal,
      start: bodyStart,
      end: bodyEnd,
      size: bodySize.value
    };
    break;
  }

  p = bodyEnd;
}

if (!target)
  throw new Error(`FUNC ${TARGET} not found`);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 32 RAW BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
  `FUNCTION INDEX : ${target.funcIndex}`
);

console.log(
  `CODE ORDINAL   : ${target.ordinal}`
);

console.log(
  `BODY           : 0x${target.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} .. 0x${target.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log(
  `SIZE           : ${target.size}`
);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let p = target.start; p < target.end; p += 16) {

  const e = Math.min(p + 16, target.end);

  const bytes = [];

  for (let q = p; q < e; q++)
    bytes.push(
      b[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
    );

  console.log(
    `0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
  );
}

FILE: scripts\inspectFunc3837Atomic.mjs
