import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';

const sourcePath = path.resolve(
    'scripts/disassembleAddressUsers.mjs'
);

const outputPath = path.resolve(
    'scripts/inspectFunc3837Atomic_output.txt'
);

const targetFunction = 3837;
const targetOffset = 0x0019570B;
const contextCount = 20;

const source = fs.readFileSync(sourcePath, 'utf8');

const marker =
    "console.log('\\nROOSTER WASM - FUNC 2034 AND CALLERS');";

const markerIndex = source.indexOf(marker);

if (markerIndex < 0) {
    throw new Error(
        'Could not locate the reporting marker in the original decoder.'
    );
}

/*
 * Keep only the original decoder definitions.
 * Do not execute its reporting code.
 */
let bootstrap = source.slice(0, markerIndex);

/*
 * Add support for the WebAssembly 0xFE atomic prefix.
 *
 * Atomic instructions with memory arguments use memarg.
 * atomic.fence uses a single reserved immediate byte.
 * Unsupported subopcodes fail explicitly rather than
 * guessing instruction boundaries.
 */
const feHandler = String.raw`
        if (op === 0xfe) {
            const sub = readU(p, body.end);
            p = sub.next;

            let text = '';

            if (sub.value === 3) {
                needBytes(p, 1, body.end, 'atomic.fence');
                const reserved = wasm[p++];
                text = 'atomic.fence reserved=' + reserved;
            } else if (
                sub.value <= 2 ||
                (sub.value >= 0x10 && sub.value <= 0x4e)
            ) {
                const mem = readMemArg(p, body.end);
                p = mem.next;
                text =
                    'atomic.subop=' + sub.value +
                    ' ' + mem.text;
            } else {
                throw new Error(
                    'Unsupported 0xFE subopcode ' +
                    sub.value + ' at ' + hex(start)
                );
            }

            instructions.push({
                start,
                end: p,
                text,
                raw: readBytes(start, p),
            });

            continue;
        }

`;

const fcPattern = /(\s*)if \(op === 0xfc\) \{/;

if (!fcPattern.test(bootstrap)) {
    throw new Error(
        'Could not locate the 0xFC decoder branch; original file unchanged.'
    );
}

bootstrap = bootstrap.replace(
    fcPattern,
    (_match, indent) =>
        feHandler.replace(/^        /gm, indent) +
        indent + 'if (op === 0xfc) {'
);

const tempPath = path.join(
    os.tmpdir(),
    'rooster_decoder_func3837_atomic.mjs'
);

fs.writeFileSync(
    tempPath,
    bootstrap + '\nexport { bodies, decodeFunction };\n',
    'utf8'
);

const output = [];

function log(value = '') {
    output.push(String(value));
}

function hex(value) {
    return '0x' + value.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

try {
    const decoder = await import(
        pathToFileURL(tempPath).href +
        '?run=' + Date.now()
    );

    const { bodies, decodeFunction } = decoder;
    const body = bodies.get(targetFunction);

    if (!body) {
        throw new Error(
            `Function ${targetFunction} has no decoded body range.`
        );
    }

    log('FUNC 3837 ATOMIC-AWARE INSPECTION');
    log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE - NO WASM EXECUTION');
    log('============================================================');
    log(`TARGET FUNCTION : ${targetFunction}`);
    log(`TARGET OFFSET   : ${hex(targetOffset)}`);
    log(`BODY START      : ${hex(body.start)}`);
    log(`BODY END        : ${hex(body.end)}`);
    log(`BODY SIZE       : ${body.end - body.start}`);
    log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    if (
        targetOffset < body.start ||
        targetOffset >= body.end
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'Target offset is outside the function body.'
        );
    }

    const decoded = decodeFunction(targetFunction);
    const instructions = decoded.instructions;

    let targetIndex = instructions.findIndex(
        ins =>
            ins.start <= targetOffset &&
            targetOffset < ins.end
    );

    if (targetIndex < 0) {
        log('TARGET OFFSET IS NOT INSIDE A DECODED INSTRUCTION.');
        log('This may indicate a decoder gap or incorrect instruction boundary.');
    } else {
        log('');
        log('INSTRUCTIONS AROUND TARGET');
        log('------------------------------------------------------------');

        const from = Math.max(0, targetIndex - contextCount);
        const to = Math.min(
            instructions.length - 1,
            targetIndex + contextCount
        );

        for (let i = from; i <= to; i++) {
            const ins = instructions[i];
            const mark =
                ins.start <= targetOffset &&
                targetOffset < ins.end
                    ? '>>> '
                    : '    ';

            log(
                `${mark}${hex(ins.start)}  ` +
                `${ins.raw.padEnd(38)} ${ins.text}`
            );
        }

        const targetIns = instructions[targetIndex];

        log('');
        log('TARGET BOUNDARY CHECK');
        log(`INSTRUCTION START : ${hex(targetIns.start)}`);
        log(`INSTRUCTION END   : ${hex(targetIns.end)}`);
        log(`INSTRUCTION TEXT  : ${targetIns.text}`);
        log(`EXACT START MATCH : ${targetIns.start === targetOffset}`);
    }

    log('');
    log('ALL DECODED CALL_INDIRECT INSTRUCTIONS');
    log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    const indirectCalls = instructions.filter(
        ins => ins.text.startsWith('call_indirect')
    );

    log(`COUNT: ${indirectCalls.length}`);

    for (const ins of indirectCalls) {
        log(
            `${hex(ins.start)}  ` +
            `${ins.raw.padEnd(24)} ${ins.text}`
        );
    }

    log('');
    log('ATOMIC INSTRUCTIONS DECODED');
    log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    const atomics = instructions.filter(
        ins => ins.raw.startsWith('fe ')
    );

    log(`COUNT: ${atomics.length}`);

    for (const ins of atomics) {
        log(
            `${hex(ins.start)}  ` +
            `${ins.raw.padEnd(24)} ${ins.text}`
        );
    }

    log('');
    log('LIMITATIONS');
    log('- Table membership alone does not prove an executed call path.');
    log('- call_indirect type and runtime table index must be established separately.');
    log('- This report does not execute WASM or modify the contract.');
} catch (error) {
    log('DECODING FAILED');
    log(String(error));
    log('');
    log('No call-path conclusion can be drawn from a failed decode.');
} finally {
    fs.writeFileSync(outputPath, output.join('\n') + '\n', 'utf8');
    console.log(output.join('\n'));
    console.log('');
    console.log(`REPORT: ${outputPath}`);
    console.log('READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTIONS');
}

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    do {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF in ULEB');
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('ULEB too large');
    } while (byte & 0x80);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function dump(start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const stop = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [...wasm.subarray(p, stop)]
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ');
        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes}`);
    }
}

const targetSection = 10;
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[pos++];
    const sizeInfo = uleb(pos);
    const sectionStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sizeInfo.value;

    if (id === targetSection) {
        codeStart = sectionStart;
        codeEnd = sectionEnd;
        break;
    }

    pos = sectionEnd;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error('Code section not found');

pos = codeStart;
const countInfo = uleb(pos);
const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
pos = countInfo.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount; ordinal++) {
    const sizeInfo = uleb(pos);
    const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + sizeInfo.value;
    const globalFunc = 83 + ordinal;

    bodies.set(globalFunc, {
        start: bodyStart,
        end: bodyEnd,
        size: sizeInfo.value
    });

    pos = bodyEnd;
}

console.log('FUNC 4099 TARGET INSPECTION');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`FILE SIZE       = ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`CODE BODY COUNT = ${bodyCount}`);
console.log(`CODE SECTION    = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);

const target = bodies.get(4099);

if (!target) {
    throw new Error('FUNC 4099 body not found');
}

console.log('\nTARGET FUNCTION 4099');
console.log(`BODY = ${hex(target.start)} .. ${hex(target.end)}`);
console.log(`SIZE = ${target.size}`);
console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
dump(target.start, target.end);

const checks = [
    { func: 3626, hit: 0x00118d40 },
    { func: 7610, hit: 0x002588f7 }
];

for (const item of checks) {
    const body = bodies.get(item.func);

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`CALLER FUNC ${item.func}`);
    console.log(`EXPECTED BODY = ${body ? `${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}` : 'NOT FOUND'}`);
    console.log(`MATCH OFFSET  = ${hex(item.hit)}`);

    if (!body) continue;

    if (item.hit < body.start || item.hit >= body.end) {
        console.log('WARNING: match offset is outside the mapped function body');
        continue;
    }

    const start = Math.max(body.start, item.hit - 48);
    const end = Math.min(body.end, item.hit + 64);

    console.log(`WINDOW = ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(end)}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    dump(start, end);
}

console.log('\nDONE - NO WASM EXECUTION');

import fs from 'node:fs';

const file = 'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';
const wasm = fs.readFileSync(file);

function readUleb(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte;

    do {
        if (pos >= wasm.length) throw new Error('Unexpected EOF');
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value += (byte & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error('Invalid ULEB');
    } while (byte & 0x80);

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function hex(n) {
    return '0x' + n.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function dump(start, end) {
    for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
        const stop = Math.min(p + 16, end);
        const bytes = [...wasm.subarray(p, stop)]
            .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
            .join(' ');
        console.log(`${hex(p)}  ${bytes}`);
    }
}

let pos = 8;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (pos < wasm.length) {
    const sectionId = wasm[pos++];
    const size = readUleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) throw new Error('Code section not found');

const count = readUleb(codeStart);
pos = count.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = readUleb(pos);
    const start = size.next;
    const end = start + size.value;

    bodies.set(83 + ordinal, { start, end, size: size.value });
    pos = end;
}

const targets = [2505, 2506, 2507, 2508, 2509, 2510, 2511, 2538, 2540];

console.log('FUNC 4099 HELPER INSPECTION');
console.log('READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(`FILE SIZE = ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`FUNCTION BODIES = ${count.value}`);

for (const func of targets) {
    const body = bodies.get(func);

    console.log('\n============================================================');
    console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC ${func}`);

    if (!body) {
        console.log('BODY NOT FOUND');
        continue;
    }

    console.log(`BODY = ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`);
    console.log(`SIZE = ${body.size}`);
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');

    if (body.size <= 220) {
        dump(body.start, body.end);
    } else {
        console.log('BODY TOO LARGE FOR FULL DUMP; FIRST 160 BYTES:');
        dump(body.start, Math.min(body.start + 160, body.end));
    }
}

console.log('\nDONE - NO WASM EXECUTION');


====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\inspectFunc445.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);
const TARGET = 445;
const IMPORTED = 83;

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 0x80);

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40))
    v |= (~0 << s);

  return { value: v, next: p };
}

let p = 8;
let funcSec = null;
let codeSec = null;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const z = uleb(p);
  const size = z.value;
  p = z.next;

  if (id === 3)
    funcSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  if (id === 10)
    codeSec = { start: p, end: p + size };

  p += size;
}

const ordinal = TARGET - IMPORTED;

let fp = funcSec.start;
const fc = uleb(fp);
fp = fc.next;

let typeIndex = null;

for (let i = 0; i <= ordinal; i++) {
  const x = uleb(fp);
  fp = x.next;

  if (i === ordinal)
    typeIndex = x.value;
}

let cp = codeSec.start;
const cc = uleb(cp);
cp = cc.next;

let body = null;

for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
  const z = uleb(cp);
  const size = z.value;
  cp = z.next;

  const start = cp;
  const end = cp + size;

  if (i === ordinal) {
    body = { start, end };
    break;
  }

  cp = end;
}

function decode(p) {
  const addr = p;
  const op = b[p++];

  const R = (next, s) => ({
    next,
    text:
      `0x${addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${s}`
  });

  switch (op) {
    case 0x00: return R(p, "UNREACHABLE");
    case 0x01: return R(p, "NOP");
    case 0x05: return R(p, "ELSE");
    case 0x0b: return R(p, "END");
    case 0x0f: return R(p, "RETURN");
    case 0x1a: return R(p, "DROP");
    case 0x1b: return R(p, "SELECT");

    case 0x02:
      return R(p + 1, `BLOCK type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x03:
      return R(p + 1, `LOOP type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x04:
      return R(p + 1, `IF type=0x${b[p].toString(16)}`);

    case 0x0c: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `BR ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x0d: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `BR_IF ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x10: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x11: {
      const t = uleb(p);
      p = t.next;
      const tb = uleb(p);
      p = tb.next;
      return R(
        p,
        `CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x20: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x21: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x22: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `LOCAL.TEE ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x23: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `GLOBAL.GET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x24: {
      const x = uleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `GLOBAL.SET ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x28: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      return R(
        p,
        `I32.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x29: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      return R(
        p,
        `I64.LOAD align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x36: {
      const a = uleb(p);
      p = a.next;
      const o = uleb(p);
      p = o.next;
      return R(
        p,
        `I32.STORE align=${a.value} offset=${o.value}`
      );
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      return R(
        x.next,
        `I32.CONST ${x.value} (0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
      );
    }

    case 0x42: {
      const x = sleb(p);
      return R(x.next, `I64.CONST ${x.value}`);
    }

    case 0x45: return R(p, "I32.EQZ");
    case 0x46: return R(p, "I32.EQ");
    case 0x47: return R(p, "I32.NE");
    case 0x48: return R(p, "I32.LT_S");
    case 0x49: return R(p, "I32.LT_U");
    case 0x4a: return R(p, "I32.GT_S");
    case 0x4b: return R(p, "I32.GT_U");
    case 0x4c: return R(p, "I32.LE_S");
    case 0x4f: return R(p, "I32.GE_S");

    case 0x6a: return R(p, "I32.ADD");
    case 0x6b: return R(p, "I32.SUB");
    case 0x71: return R(p, "I32.AND");
    case 0x72: return R(p, "I32.OR");
    case 0x73: return R(p, "I32.XOR");
    case 0x74: return R(p, "I32.SHL");
    case 0x75: return R(p, "I32.SHR_S");
    case 0x76: return R(p, "I32.SHR_U");

    default:
      return R(
        p,
        `OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 445 EXACT BODY");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${TARGET}`);
console.log(`TYPE INDEX     : ${typeIndex}`);
console.log(
  `BODY           : 0x${body.start.toString(16)} .. 0x${body.end.toString(16)}`
);
console.log(`SIZE           : ${body.end - body.start}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let q = body.start;

while (q < body.end) {
  const x = decode(q);
  console.log(x.text);
  q = x.next;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log("CALLS / CONSTANTS");

q = body.start;

while (q < body.end) {
  const addr = q;
  const op = b[q++];

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = uleb(q);
    console.log(`0x${addr.toString(16)} CALL FUNC ${x.value}`);
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = uleb(q);
    q = t.next;
    const tb = uleb(q);
    q = tb.next;
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)} CALL_INDIRECT type=${t.value} table=${tb.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = sleb(q);
    console.log(
      `0x${addr.toString(16)} I32.CONST ${x.value} ` +
      `(0x${(x.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`
    );
    q = x.next;
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24 ||
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {
    q = uleb(q).next;
    continue;
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x36
  ) {
    q = uleb(q).next;
    q = uleb(q).next;
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04)
    q++;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 484;
const IMPORTED = 83;

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 484 EXACT STRUCTURE ANALYSIS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS = ${IMPORTED}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function u32leb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[p++];
    value += (b & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function s32leb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    b = buf[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    shift += 7;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function sectionHeader(pos: number) {
  const id = buf[pos];
  const size = u32leb(pos + 1);

  return {
    id,
    size: size.value,
    payloadStart: size.next,
    payloadEnd: size.next + size.value,
  };
}

let functionStart = -1;
let functionEnd = -1;
let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

let p = 8;

while (p < buf.length) {
  const s = sectionHeader(p);

  if (s.id === 3) {
    functionStart = s.payloadStart;
    functionEnd = s.payloadEnd;
  }

  if (s.id === 10) {
    codeStart = s.payloadStart;
    codeEnd = s.payloadEnd;
  }

  p = s.payloadEnd;
}

if (functionStart < 0 || codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Required WASM sections not found");
}

console.log(`FUNCTION SECTION = ${hex(functionStart)} .. ${hex(functionEnd)}`);
console.log(`CODE SECTION     = ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);

fp = countInfo.next;

  fp = r.next;
  typeIndices.push(r.value);

for (let i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
  const sizeInfo = u32leb(cp);

  const start = sizeInfo.next;
  const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

  if (i === codeOrdinal) {
    bodyStart = start;
    bodyEnd = end;
    bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    break;
  }

  cp = end;

  throw new Error("FUNC484 body not found");

const fieldOffsets = [
  0x00,
  0x04,
  0x08,
  0x0c,
  0x14,
  0x18,
  0x1c,
  0x24,
  0x2c,
  0x30,
  0x34,
  0x38,
];

function fieldTag(offset: number) {
  if (fieldOffsets.includes(offset)) {
    return ` <<< POSSIBLE STRUCT FIELD +0x${offset.toString(16)}`;
  }

  return "";
}

function emit(pos: number, bytes: string, text: string) {
  console.log(`${hex(pos)}  ${bytes.padEnd(24)} ${text}`);
}

let instr = q;

  const pos = instr;
  const op = buf[instr++];

  if (op === 0x20) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(pos, "01", "NOP");
    continue;

    const type = buf[instr++];
    emit(pos, `02 ${type.toString(16)}`, `BLOCK type=0x${type.toString(16)}`);
    continue;

  if (op === 0x03) {
    const type = buf[instr++];
    emit(pos, `03 ${type.toString(16)}`, `LOOP type=0x${type.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x04) {
    const type = buf[instr++];
    emit(pos, `04 ${type.toString(16)}`, `IF type=0x${type.toString(16)}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x05) {
    emit(pos, "05", "ELSE");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0b) {
    emit(pos, "0b", "END");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0c) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(pos, `0c ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0d) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(pos, `0d ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `BR_IF ${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x0f) {
    emit(pos, "0f", "RETURN");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x1a) {
    emit(pos, "1a", "DROP");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x1b) {
    emit(pos, "1b", "SELECT");
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    emit(pos, `10 ${r.value.toString(16)}`, `CALL FUNC${r.value}`);
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const t = u32leb(instr);
    instr = t.next;

    const table = u32leb(instr);
    instr = table.next;

    emit(
      pos,
      `11 ${t.value.toString(16)} ${table.value.toString(16)}`,
      `CALL_INDIRECT TYPE${t.value} TABLE${table.value}`
    );
    continue;
  }

  if (op === 0xfc) {
    const r = u32leb(instr);
    instr = r.next;

    let name = `EXTENDED 0xfc ${r.value.toString(16)}`;

    if (r.value === 0x0a) name = "MEMORY.COPY";
    if (r.value === 0x0b) name = "MEMORY.FILL";

    emit(
      pos,
      `fc ${r.value.toString(16)}`,
      name
    );
    continue;
  }

  emit(
    pos,
    op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"),
    `UNKNOWN OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
  );

import * as nodefs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = nodefs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80))
      return { v, p };

    s += 7;
  }
}

let p = 8;

let typeStart = -1;
let functionStart = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  p = s.p;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + s.v;

  if (id === 1)
    typeStart = start;

  if (id === 3)
    functionStart = start;

  p = end;
}

if (typeStart < 0 || functionStart < 0)
  throw new Error("Required sections not found");

const types: { params: number[]; results: number[] }[] = [];

p = typeStart;

const tc = uleb(p);
p = tc.p;

for (let i = 0; i < tc.v; i++) {
  if (b[p++] !== 0x60)
    throw new Error("Invalid function type");

  const np = uleb(p);
  p = np.p;

  const params: number[] = [];
  for (let j = 0; j < np.v; j++)
    params.push(b[p++]);

  const nr = uleb(p);
  p = nr.p;

  const results: number[] = [];
  for (let j = 0; j < nr.v; j++)
    results.push(b[p++]);

  types.push({ params, results });
}

p = functionStart;

const fc = uleb(p);
p = fc.p;

let typeIndex = -1;

for (let fn = 0; fn < fc.v; fn++) {
  const r = uleb(p);
  p = r.p;

  if (fn === 4996)
    typeIndex = r.v;
}

  console.log(`param[${i}] = 0x${t.params[i].toString(16)}`);
